		public string MessageText
		{
			get
			{
				return TxtBlock.Text;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))

				}
				TxtBlock.Text = value;
			}

		public MDMessageBoxButton Button
		{
			get
			{
				return _button;
			}
			set
			{
				_button = value;
				string[] array = _button_Captions[Button];
				if (array.Length < 1)
				{
					return;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < _btns.Count; i++)
				{
					if (i >= array.Length)
					{
						_btns[i].Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
						continue;
					}
					_btns[i].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_btns[i].Content = array[i];
				}
				if (_showHelpButton)
				{
					_btns[array.Length].Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_btns[array.Length].Content = AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Help;
				}
			}
		}

		public MDMessageBoxImage MessageIcon
		{
			get
			{
				return _icon;
			}
			set
			{
				_icon = value;
				if (_icon == MDMessageBoxImage.None)
				{
					HeaderImage.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					return;
				}
				HeaderImage.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				ImageSource imageSource = null;
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(MDMessageBox).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/MessageBox");
				switch (_icon)
				{
				case MDMessageBoxImage.Error:
					stringBuilder.Append("Stop.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case MDMessageBoxImage.Exclamation:
					stringBuilder.Append("Exclamation.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case MDMessageBoxImage.Information:
					stringBuilder.Append("Asterisk.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				case MDMessageBoxImage.Question:
					stringBuilder.Append("Question.png");
					imageSource = new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute));
					break;
				}
				if (imageSource != null)
				{
					HeaderImage.Source = imageSource;
				}
			}
		}

		protected int DefaultButton
		{
			get
			{
				return _defaultButton;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value > 0 && value <= 6 && _btns[value - 1].Content != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_btns[value - 1].Content.ToString()))
				{
					_btns[value - 1].IsDefault = true;
					_btns[value - 1].Focus();
					_defaultButton = value;
				}
				else
				{
					_btns[0].IsDefault = true;
					_btns[0].Focus();
					_defaultButton = 1;
				}
			}
		}

		private void btn_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is System.Windows.Controls.Button { Content: not null } button)
			{
				if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Ok))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.OK;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Cancel))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Yes))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Yes;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._No))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.No;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Abort))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Abort;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Retry))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Retry;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Ignore))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Ignore;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._YesAll))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.YesAll;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._NoAll))
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.NoAll;
				}
				else if (button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Help))
				{
					ApplicationCommands.Help.Execute(this, button);
				}
				if (!button.Content.Equals(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources._Help))
				{
					Close();
				}
			}
		}

		private void MDMessageBox_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				if (Button == MDMessageBoxButton.OK)
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.OK;
				}
				else
				{
					_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
				}
				Close();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClosed(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_result == MDMessageBoxResult.None)
			{
				_result = MDMessageBoxResult.Cancel;
			}
			base.OnClosed(e);
		}

		private static MDMessageBoxResult InitializeMDMessageBox(Window owner, string messageBoxText, string caption, MDMessageBoxButton button, MDMessageBoxImage icon, bool showHelpButton, int defaultButton, string helpFile, int helpId)
		{
			MDMessageBox mDMessageBox = new MDMessageBox
			{
				MessageText = messageBoxText,
				Title = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(caption) ? "" : caption),
				Button = button,
				MessageIcon = icon,
				DefaultButton = defaultButton,
				ShowHelpButton = showHelpButton,
				HelpFile = helpFile,
				HelpId = helpId
			};
			if (owner != null)
			{
				mDMessageBox.Owner = owner;
			}
			else
			{
				WindowInteropHelper windowInteropHelper = new WindowInteropHelper(mDMessageBox);
				windowInteropHelper.Owner = GetActiveWindow();
				if (windowInteropHelper.Owner == IntPtr.Zero)
				{
					windowInteropHelper.Owner = GetForegroundWindow();
				}
			}
			mDMessageBox.ShowDialog();
			return mDMessageBox?._result ?? MDMessageBoxResult.OK;
		}

		public static MDMessageBoxResult Show(Window owner, string messageBoxText, string caption = "", MDMessageBoxButton button = MDMessageBoxButton.OK, MDMessageBoxImage icon = MDMessageBoxImage.None, bool showHelpButton = false, int defaultButton = 1, string helpFile = "apwn.chm", int helpId = -1)
		{
			MDMessageBoxResult result = MDMessageBoxResult.OK;
			if (System.Windows.Application.Current.Dispatcher != Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher)
			{
				System.Windows.Application.Current.Dispatcher.Invoke(delegate
				{
					result = InitializeMDMessageBox(owner, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultButton, helpFile, helpId);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				result = InitializeMDMessageBox(owner, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultButton, helpFile, helpId);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static MDMessageBoxResult Show(string messageBoxText, string caption = "", MDMessageBoxButton button = MDMessageBoxButton.OK, MDMessageBoxImage icon = MDMessageBoxImage.None, bool showHelpButton = false, int defaultResult = 1, string helpFile = "apwn.chm", int helpId = -1)
		{
			return Show(null, messageBoxText, caption, button, icon, showHelpButton, defaultResult, helpFile, helpId);
		}

		private class AtomPrimaryLetterConverter : IMultiValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (values.Length != 2)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				string text = (string)values[0];
				string text2 = (string)values[1];
				if (!(text == text2))
				{
					return text;
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}

			public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private class HydrogenPrimaryLetterConverter : IMultiValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (values.Length != 4)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[2] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[3] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				bool flag = (bool)values[0];
				int num = (int)values[1];
				string obj = (string)values[2];
				string text = (string)values[3];
				if (obj == text)
				{
					return text;
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (num > 0)
				{
					return text;
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}

			public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private class HydrogenBottomRightLetterConverter : IMultiValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (values.Length != 4)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[2] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[3] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				int num = (int)values[0];
				string obj = (string)values[1];
				string text = (string)values[2];
				bool flag = (bool)values[3];
				if (obj == text)
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					if (num <= 1)
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
					return num.ToString();
				}
				return string.Empty;
			}

			public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private class TopRightTextConverter : IMultiValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (values.Length != 2)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				_ = (int)values[0];
				int num = (int)values[1];
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (num > 1)
				{
					return num + "+";
				}
				return num switch
				{
					1 => "+", 
					-1 => "-", 
					_ => Math.Abs(num) + "-", 
				};
			}

			public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		public Atom Atom
		{
			get
			{
				return (Atom)GetValue(AtomProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AtomProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string HydrogenName

		public IEnumerable<BondElement> ConnectedBondElements
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in base.SymbolAnchors)
				{
					if (symbolAnchor.Consumer != null && typeof(BondElement).IsInstanceOfType(symbolAnchor.Consumer))
					{
						yield return symbolAnchor.Consumer as BondElement;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public event RoutedEventHandler AnchorCollectionChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(AnchorCollectionChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(AnchorCollectionChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static AtomElement()
		{
			AtomProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Atom", typeof(Atom), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnAtomPropertyChanged));
			HydrogenNameProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("HydrogenName", typeof(string), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("H", FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, OnHydrogenNamePropertyChanged));
			ShouldShowHydrogenProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ShouldShowHydrogen", typeof(bool), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
			LettersVisibilityProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LettersVisibility", typeof(Visibility), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible));
			CenterEllipseBrushProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("CenterEllipseBrush", typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent));
			WarningImageSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("WarningImageSource", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
			AnchorCollectionChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("AnchorCollectionChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(AtomElement));
			Diagram.CanResizeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanRotateProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanFlipProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(AtomElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public AtomElement()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		private void InitializeComponent()
		{
			Grid grid = new Grid
			{
				SnapsToDevicePixels = true
			};
			_transparentEllipse.SetBinding(Shape.FillProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(CenterEllipseBrushProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			grid.Children.Add(_transparentEllipse);
			_letterGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
			{
				Width = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
			});
			_letterGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
			{
				Width = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
			});
			_letterGrid.Children.Add(_primaryLetter);
			_letterGrid.Children.Add(_hydrogenLetter);
			_letterGrid.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(LettersVisibilityProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			grid.Children.Add(_letterGrid);
			_warningImage.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Image.SourceProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(WarningImageSourceProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_warningImage.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.AvailableLinksCount")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Converter = new WarningImageVisibilityConverter()
			});
			grid.Children.Add(_warningImage);
			base.Content = grid;
			_primaryLetter.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_hydrogenLetter.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			MultiBinding multiBinding = new MultiBinding
			{
				FallbackValue = "?",
				Converter = new AtomPrimaryLetterConverter()
			};
			multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.Name")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(HydrogenNameProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_primaryLetter.SetBinding(TrippleText.PrimaryTextProperty, multiBinding);
			MultiBinding multiBinding2 = new MultiBinding
			{
				FallbackValue = "?",
				Converter = new HydrogenPrimaryLetterConverter()
			};
			multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(ShouldShowHydrogenProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.HydrogenCount")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.Name")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(HydrogenNameProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_hydrogenLetter.SetBinding(TrippleText.PrimaryTextProperty, multiBinding2);
			MultiBinding multiBinding3 = new MultiBinding
			{
				FallbackValue = string.Empty,
				Converter = new HydrogenBottomRightLetterConverter()
			};
			multiBinding3.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.HydrogenCount")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding3.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.Name")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding3.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(HydrogenNameProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			multiBinding3.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(ShouldShowHydrogenProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_hydrogenLetter.SetBinding(TrippleText.BottomRightTextProperty, multiBinding3);
			InvalidateDisplayedText();
			base.Loaded += AtomElement_Loaded;
			base.SymbolAnchors.CollectionChanged += SymbolAnchors_CollectionChanged;
		}

		private void AtomElement_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayedText();
		}

		private void SymbolAnchors_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateDisplayedText();
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(AnchorCollectionChangedEvent));
		}

		public void InvalidateDisplayedText()
		{
			InvalidateHydrogenPosition();
			BindingTopRightText();
		}

		private void InvalidateHydrogenPosition()
		{
			List<BondElement> list = ConnectedBondElements.ToList();
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				List<SideOption> list2 = list.Select((BondElement be) => AngleHelper.GetBondElementPosition(be, this)).ToList();
				if (!list2.Contains(SideOption.None))
				{
					if (!list2.Contains(SideOption.Left))
					{
						Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 0);
						Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 1);
						return;
					}
					if (!list2.Contains(SideOption.Right))
					{
						Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 1);
						Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 0);
						return;
					}
				}
			}
			if (Atom == null)
			{
				Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 1);
				Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 0);
			}
			else if (Atom.Type == ParticleType.Ion)
			{
				Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 1);
				Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 0);
			}
			else if (Atom.DefaultHydrogenPosition == SideOption.None)
			{
				if (Atom.Valence >= 0)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 1);
					Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 0);
				}
				else
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 0);
					Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 1);
				}
			}
			else if (Atom.DefaultHydrogenPosition == SideOption.Left)
			{
				Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 0);
				Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 1);
			}
			else
			{
				Grid.SetColumn(_hydrogenLetter, 1);
				Grid.SetColumn(_primaryLetter, 0);
			}
		}

		private void BindingTopRightText()
		{
			if (Atom == null || Atom.Name == HydrogenName || Grid.GetColumn(_primaryLetter) == 0 || 1 == 0)
			{
				_primaryLetter.TopRightText = string.Empty;
				MultiBinding multiBinding = new MultiBinding
				{
					Converter = new TopRightTextConverter()
				};
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.AvailableLinksCount")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.ChargeCount")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				_hydrogenLetter.SetBinding(TrippleText.TopRightTextProperty, multiBinding);
			}
			else
			{
				_hydrogenLetter.TopRightText = string.Empty;
				MultiBinding multiBinding2 = new MultiBinding
				{
					Converter = new TopRightTextConverter()
				};
				multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.AvailableLinksCount")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding2.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.ChargeCount")
				{
					Source = this,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				_primaryLetter.SetBinding(TrippleText.TopRightTextProperty, multiBinding2);
			}
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeContent()
		{
			return false;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			if (arrangeBounds.IsEmpty || double.IsNaN(arrangeBounds.Width) || double.IsInfinity(arrangeBounds.Width) || double.IsNaN(arrangeBounds.Height) || double.IsInfinity(arrangeBounds.Height))
			{
				return base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
			}
			Rect finalRect = new Rect((0.0 - arrangeBounds.Width) / 2.0, (0.0 - arrangeBounds.Height) / 2.0, arrangeBounds.Width, arrangeBounds.Height);
			(base.Content as Grid).Arrange(finalRect);
			return arrangeBounds;
		}

		public DiagramAnchor CreateAnchor(BondElement bondElement)
		{
			SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor = new SymbolAnchor
			{
				Provider = this,
				RelativeAnchorPoint = new System.Windows.Point((0.0 - base.ActualWidth) / 2.0, (0.0 - base.ActualHeight) / 2.0),
				ProjectionDistance = 0.0,
				ProjectionAngle = 0.0,
				Consumer = bondElement
			};
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				symbolAnchor.ProjectionAngle += parentDiagram.GetElementRotation(this);
			}
			AddAnchor(symbolAnchor);
			return symbolAnchor;
		}

		public List<TextInfo> GetTextInformation()
		{
			List<TextInfo> list = new List<TextInfo>();
			if (LettersVisibility == Visibility.Visible)
			{
				Vector vector = _primaryLetter.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), this) - new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				foreach (TextInfo item in _primaryLetter.GetTextInformation())
				{
					list.Add(new TextInfo(item.RelativePoint + vector, item.Text, item.FontSize));
				}
				Vector vector2 = _hydrogenLetter.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), this) - new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				foreach (TextInfo item2 in _hydrogenLetter.GetTextInformation())
				{
					list.Add(new TextInfo(item2.RelativePoint + vector2, item2.Text, item2.FontSize));
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect((0.0 - base.ActualWidth) / 2.0, (0.0 - base.ActualHeight) / 2.0, base.ActualWidth, base.ActualHeight);
		}

		private static void OnAtomPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AtomElement).InvalidateDisplayedText();
		}

		private static void OnHydrogenNamePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as AtomElement).BindingTopRightText();
		}

	[TemplatePart(Name = "PART_BUTTON", Type = typeof(System.Windows.Controls.Button))]
	public class HalfEnabledButton : System.Windows.Controls.Control
	{
		private const string TemplatedButton = "PART_BUTTON";

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CommandParameterProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent ClickEvent;

		public ICommand Command
		{
			get
			{
				return (ICommand)GetValue(CommandProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object CommandParameter
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(CommandParameterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CommandParameterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event RoutedEventHandler Click
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(ClickEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(ClickEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static HalfEnabledButton()
		{
			CommandProperty = System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandProperty.AddOwner(typeof(HalfEnabledButton));
			CommandParameterProperty = System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ButtonBase.CommandParameterProperty.AddOwner(typeof(HalfEnabledButton));
			ContentProperty = ContentControl.ContentProperty.AddOwner(typeof(HalfEnabledButton));
			ClickEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("Click", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(HalfEnabledButton));
			FrameworkElement.DefaultStyleKeyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(HalfEnabledButton), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(typeof(HalfEnabledButton)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			if (base.Template.FindName("PART_BUTTON", this) is System.Windows.Controls.Button button)
			{
				button.Click += templatedButton_Click;
			}
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		private void templatedButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnClick();
		}

		protected virtual void OnClick()
		{
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ClickEvent, this));
		}
	}
	public class TextInfo
	{
		public string Text { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Point RelativePoint { get; set; }

		public double FontSize { get; set; }

		public TextInfo()
		{
		}

		public TextInfo(System.Windows.Point relativePoint, string text, double fontSize)
		{
			RelativePoint = relativePoint;
			Text = text;
			FontSize = fontSize;
		}
	}
	public class TrippleText : ContentControl
	{
		private class NumberConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				double num = (double)value;
				double num2 = (double)parameter;
				return num * num2;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				double num = (double)value;
				double num2 = (double)parameter;
				return num / num2;
			}
		}

		private readonly TextBlock _primaryTextBlock = new TextBlock();

		private readonly TextBlock _toprightTextBlock = new TextBlock
		{
			VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Top
		};

		private readonly TextBlock _bottomrightTextBlock = new TextBlock
		{
			VerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Bottom
		};

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PrimaryTextProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TopRightTextProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BottomRightTextProperty;

		public string PrimaryText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PrimaryTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PrimaryTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string TopRightText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(TopRightTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(TopRightTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string BottomRightText
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(BottomRightTextProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(BottomRightTextProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static TrippleText()
		{
			PrimaryTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PrimaryText", typeof(string), typeof(TrippleText));
			TopRightTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("TopRightText", typeof(string), typeof(TrippleText));
			BottomRightTextProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BottomRightText", typeof(string), typeof(TrippleText));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.PaddingProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TrippleText), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new Thickness(3.0, 0.0, 3.0, 0.0)));
			System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(TrippleText), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent));
		}

		public TrippleText()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeContent()
		{
			return false;
		}

		private void InitializeComponent()
		{
			Grid grid = new Grid
			{
				SnapsToDevicePixels = true
			};
			grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
			{
				Width = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
			});
			grid.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition
			{
				Width = new GridLength(0.0, GridUnitType.Auto)
			});
			grid.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Panel.BackgroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			TextBlock primaryTextBlock = _primaryTextBlock;
			primaryTextBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(PrimaryTextProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			Grid.SetColumn(primaryTextBlock, 0);
			grid.Children.Add(primaryTextBlock);
			TextBlock toprightTextBlock = _toprightTextBlock;
			toprightTextBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(TopRightTextProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			toprightTextBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontSizeProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Converter = new NumberConverter(),
				ConverterParameter = 0.5
			});
			Grid.SetColumn(toprightTextBlock, 1);
			grid.Children.Add(toprightTextBlock);
			TextBlock bottomrightTextBlock = _bottomrightTextBlock;
			bottomrightTextBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.TextProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(BottomRightTextProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			bottomrightTextBlock.SetBinding(TextBlock.FontSizeProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontSizeProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Converter = new NumberConverter(),
				ConverterParameter = 0.5
			});
			Grid.SetColumn(bottomrightTextBlock, 1);
			grid.Children.Add(bottomrightTextBlock);
			base.Content = grid;
		}

		public List<TextInfo> GetTextInformation()
		{
			List<TextInfo> list = new List<TextInfo>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_primaryTextBlock.Text))
			{
				System.Windows.Point relativePoint = _primaryTextBlock.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), this);
				list.Add(new TextInfo(relativePoint, _primaryTextBlock.Text, _primaryTextBlock.FontSize));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_toprightTextBlock.Text))
			{
				System.Windows.Point relativePoint2 = _toprightTextBlock.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), this);
				list.Add(new TextInfo(relativePoint2, _toprightTextBlock.Text, _toprightTextBlock.FontSize));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_bottomrightTextBlock.Text))
			{
				System.Windows.Point relativePoint3 = _bottomrightTextBlock.TranslatePoint(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), this);
				list.Add(new TextInfo(relativePoint3, _bottomrightTextBlock.Text, _bottomrightTextBlock.FontSize));
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class PointPair
	{
		public System.Windows.Point StartPoint { get; set; }

		public System.Windows.Point EndPoint { get; set; }

		public PointPair()
		{
		}

		public PointPair(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
		{
			StartPoint = startPoint;
			EndPoint = endPoint;
		}
	}
	public class BondElement : Shape, IAnchorConsumer, IServiceProvider, ISelectionOverlayService
	{
		private const double EndpointOffset = 2.0;

		private readonly GeometryGroup _cachedGeometry = new GeometryGroup();

		private readonly List<PointPair> _renderPointPairs = new List<PointPair>();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LinesIntervalThicknessProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BondProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty StartAnchorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EndAnchorProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultBondLengthProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CenterStartPointPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CenterStartPointProperty;

		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey CenterEndPointPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CenterEndPointProperty;

		public static readonly RoutedEvent AnchorPositionChangedEvent;

		public double LinesIntervalThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(LinesIntervalThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LinesIntervalThicknessProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Bond Bond
		{
			get
			{
				return (Bond)GetValue(BondProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(BondProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DiagramAnchor StartAnchor
		{
			get
			{
				return (DiagramAnchor)GetValue(StartAnchorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(StartAnchorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DiagramAnchor EndAnchor
		{
			get
			{
				return (DiagramAnchor)GetValue(EndAnchorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(EndAnchorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point CenterStartPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Point)GetValue(CenterStartPointProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(CenterStartPointPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point CenterEndPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Point)GetValue(CenterEndPointProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(CenterEndPointPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double DefaultBondLength
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(DefaultBondLengthProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultBondLengthProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public AtomElement StartAtomElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (StartAnchor != null && StartAnchor.Provider != null)
				{
					return StartAnchor.Provider.Element as AtomElement;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public AtomElement EndAtomElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (EndAnchor != null && EndAnchor.Provider != null)
				{
					return EndAnchor.Provider.Element as AtomElement;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		protected override Geometry DefiningGeometry => _cachedGeometry;

		IEnumerable<DiagramAnchor> IAnchorConsumer.CurrentAnchors
		{
			get
			{
				yield return StartAnchor;
				yield return EndAnchor;
			}
		}

		UIElement IAnchorConsumer.Element => this;

		UIElement ISelectionOverlayService.AdornedElement => this;

		public event RoutedEventHandler AnchorPositionChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(AnchorPositionChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(AnchorPositionChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static BondElement()
		{
			LinesIntervalThicknessProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("LinesIntervalThickness", typeof(double), typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(3.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnLinesIntervalThicknessPropertyChanged));
			BondProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Bond", typeof(Bond), typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnBondPropertyChanged));
			StartAnchorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("StartAnchor", typeof(DiagramAnchor), typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnStartAnchorPropertyChanged));
			EndAnchorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("EndAnchor", typeof(DiagramAnchor), typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnEndAnchorPropertyChanged));
			DefaultBondLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("DefaultBondLength", typeof(double), typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(50.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
			CenterStartPointPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CenterStartPoint", typeof(System.Windows.Point), typeof(BondElement), new PropertyMetadata());
			CenterStartPointProperty = CenterStartPointPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CenterEndPointPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("CenterEndPoint", typeof(System.Windows.Point), typeof(BondElement), new PropertyMetadata());
			CenterEndPointProperty = CenterEndPointPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			AnchorPositionChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("AnchorPositionChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(BondElement));
			Shape.FillProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent));
			Shape.StrokeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black));
			Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(2.0));
			System.Windows.Controls.Panel.ZIndexProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(-100));
			Diagram.CanMoveProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanResizeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanRotateProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanFlipProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(BondElement), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public void InvalidateGeometry()
		{
			if (StartAnchor == null || EndAnchor == null || Bond == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point absoluteProjectionPoint = StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
			System.Windows.Point absoluteProjectionPoint2 = EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
			Rect rect = new Rect(absoluteProjectionPoint, absoluteProjectionPoint2);
			DiagramLayer.SetLeft(this, rect.Left);
			DiagramLayer.SetTop(this, rect.Top);
			Canvas.SetLeft(this, rect.Left);
			Canvas.SetTop(this, rect.Top);
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(absoluteProjectionPoint.X - rect.X, absoluteProjectionPoint.Y - rect.Y);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point(absoluteProjectionPoint2.X - rect.X, absoluteProjectionPoint2.Y - rect.Y);
			_cachedGeometry.Children.Clear();
			bool num = StartAtomElement != null && AngleHelper.IsConnectionRoughlyOnALine(StartAtomElement);
			bool flag = EndAtomElement != null && AngleHelper.IsConnectionRoughlyOnALine(EndAtomElement);
			if (num)
			{
				Vector vector = point - point2;
				if (vector.Length > 2.0)
				{
					point = point2 + vector * (vector.Length - 2.0) / vector.Length;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				Vector vector2 = point2 - point;
				if (vector2.Length > 2.0)
				{
					point2 = point + vector2 * (vector2.Length - 2.0) / vector2.Length;
				}
			}
			_renderPointPairs.Clear();
			System.Windows.Point centerStart;
			System.Windows.Point centerEnd;
			if (Bond.Type == BondType.Single)
			{
				_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(point, point2));
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(point, point2));
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 2.0 + base.StrokeThickness, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
			}
			else if (Bond.Type == BondType.UpStereo)
			{
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreateTriangleGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 2.0 + base.StrokeThickness, filled: true, _renderPointPairs));
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 2.0 + base.StrokeThickness, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
			}
			else if (Bond.Type == BondType.DownStereo)
			{
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreateTriangleGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 2.0 + base.StrokeThickness, filled: false, _renderPointPairs));
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 2.0 + base.StrokeThickness, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
			}
			else if (Bond.Type == BondType.Double)
			{
				double startAngle;
				double endAngle;
				SideOption slaveLinePosition = AngleHelper.GetSlaveLinePosition(this, out startAngle, out endAngle);
				double num2 = ((startAngle == 0.0) ? 90.0 : (Math.Abs(startAngle) / 2.0));
				double num3 = ((endAngle == 0.0) ? 90.0 : (Math.Abs(endAngle) / 2.0));
				switch (slaveLinePosition)
				{
				case SideOption.None:
				{
					double interval = (LinesIntervalThickness + base.StrokeThickness) / 2.0;
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint5 = GetSidePoint(point, point2, 0.0 - num2, interval);
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint6 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, num3, interval);
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint5, sidePoint6));
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint7 = GetSidePoint(point, point2, num2, interval);
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint8 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, 0.0 - num3, interval);
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint7, sidePoint8));
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 3.0 + base.StrokeThickness * 2.0, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint5, sidePoint6));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint7, sidePoint8));
					break;
				}
				case SideOption.Left:
				{
					double num5 = LinesIntervalThickness + base.StrokeThickness;
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint3 = GetSidePoint(point, point2, 0.0 - num2, num5);
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint4 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, num3, num5);
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint3, sidePoint4));
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(point, point2));
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(GetSidePoint(point, point2, 0.0 - num2, num5 / 2.0), GetSidePoint(point2, point, num3, num5 / 2.0), LinesIntervalThickness * 3.0 + base.StrokeThickness * 2.0, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(point, point2));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint3, sidePoint4));
					break;
				}
				default:
				{
					double num4 = LinesIntervalThickness + base.StrokeThickness;
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(point, point2));
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint = GetSidePoint(point, point2, num2, num4);
					System.Windows.Point sidePoint2 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, 0.0 - num3, num4);
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint, sidePoint2));
					_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(GetSidePoint(point, point2, num2, num4 / 2.0), GetSidePoint(point2, point, 0.0 - num3, num4 / 2.0), LinesIntervalThickness * 3.0 + base.StrokeThickness * 2.0, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(point, point2));
					_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint, sidePoint2));
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AngleHelper.GetSlaveLinePosition(this, out var startAngle2, out var endAngle2);
				double num6 = ((startAngle2 == 0.0) ? 90.0 : (Math.Abs(startAngle2) / 2.0));
				double num7 = ((endAngle2 == 0.0) ? 90.0 : (Math.Abs(endAngle2) / 2.0));
				double interval2 = LinesIntervalThickness + base.StrokeThickness;
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(point, point2));
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint9 = GetSidePoint(point, point2, 0.0 - num6, interval2);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint10 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, num7, interval2);
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint9, sidePoint10));
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint11 = GetSidePoint(point, point2, num6, interval2);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint12 = GetSidePoint(point2, point, 0.0 - num7, interval2);
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint11, sidePoint12));
				_cachedGeometry.Children.Add(CreatePolygonGeometry(point, point2, LinesIntervalThickness * 4.0 + base.StrokeThickness * 3.0, out centerStart, out centerEnd));
				_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(point, point2));
				_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint9, sidePoint10));
				_renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint11, sidePoint12));
			}
			CenterStartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(centerStart.X + rect.X, centerStart.Y + rect.Y);
			CenterEndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(centerEnd.X + rect.X, centerEnd.Y + rect.Y);
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private Geometry CreateTriangleGeometry(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint, double width, bool filled, List<PointPair> renderPointPairs)
		{
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			Vector vector = endPoint - startPoint;
			double num = (filled ? base.StrokeThickness : (base.StrokeThickness * 2.0));
			for (double num2 = vector.Length; num2 > 0.0; num2 -= num)
			{
				double num3 = num2 / vector.Length;
				double num4 = width * num3;
				System.Windows.Point sp = startPoint + vector * num3;
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint = GetSidePoint(sp, startPoint, -90.0, num4 / 2.0);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint2 = GetSidePoint(sp, startPoint, 90.0, num4 / 2.0);
				renderPointPairs.Add(new PointPair(sidePoint, sidePoint2));
				geometryGroup.Children.Add(new LineGeometry(sidePoint, sidePoint2));
			}
			return geometryGroup;
		}

		private Geometry CreatePolygonGeometry(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint, double width, out System.Windows.Point centerStart, out System.Windows.Point centerEnd)
		{
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint = GetSidePoint(startPoint, endPoint, -90.0, width / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint2 = GetSidePoint(endPoint, startPoint, 90.0, width / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint3 = GetSidePoint(startPoint, endPoint, 90.0, width / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint4 = GetSidePoint(endPoint, startPoint, -90.0, width / 2.0);
			PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
			pathFigure.StartPoint = sidePoint;
			pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(sidePoint2, isStroked: false));
			pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(sidePoint4, isStroked: false));
			pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(sidePoint3, isStroked: false));
			pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(sidePoint, isStroked: false));
			pathFigure.IsClosed = true;
			pathFigure.IsFilled = true;
			PathGeometry result = new PathGeometry
			{
				Figures = { pathFigure }
			};
			centerStart = sidePoint + (sidePoint3 - sidePoint) / 2.0;
			centerEnd = sidePoint2 + (sidePoint4 - sidePoint2) / 2.0;
			return result;
		}

		public void ConnectStartTo(AtomElement element)
		{
			if (StartAtomElement == element)
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			AtomElement oldElement = StartAtomElement;
			AtomElement newElement = element;
			using (new TransactionContext(parentDiagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty, 257))
			{
				if (element != null)
				{
					StartAnchor = newElement.CreateAnchor(this);
				}
				else
				{
					StartAnchor = new PointAnchor(StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint);
				}
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(parentDiagram);
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && parentDiagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(this) && parentDiagram != null)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					ConnectStartTo(oldElement);
				}, delegate
				{
					ConnectStartTo(newElement);
				});
			}
		}

		public void ConnectEndTo(AtomElement element)
		{
			if (EndAtomElement == element)
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			AtomElement oldElement = EndAtomElement;
			AtomElement newElement = element;
			using (new TransactionContext(parentDiagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty, 257))
			{
				if (element != null)
				{
					EndAnchor = newElement.CreateAnchor(this);
				}
				else
				{
					EndAnchor = new PointAnchor(EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint);
				}
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(parentDiagram);
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && parentDiagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(this))
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					ConnectEndTo(oldElement);
				}, delegate
				{
					ConnectEndTo(newElement);
				});
			}
		}

		private void Bond_TypeChanged(object sender, BondTypeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			InvalidateGeometry();
		}

		private void OnAnchorChanged(DiagramAnchor oldAnchor, DiagramAnchor newAnchor, bool isStartPoint)
		{
			if (oldAnchor != null && oldAnchor.Provider != null)
			{
				if (oldAnchor.Provider.Anchors.Contains(oldAnchor))
				{
					oldAnchor.Provider.RemoveAnchor(oldAnchor);
				}
				if (Bond != null)
				{
					if (isStartPoint)
					{
						Bond.StartAtom = null;
					}
					else
					{
						Bond.EndAtom = null;
					}
				}
			}
			if (newAnchor != null && newAnchor.Provider != null && Bond != null && newAnchor.Provider.Element is AtomElement { Atom: not null } atomElement)
			{
				if (isStartPoint)
				{
					Bond.StartAtom = atomElement.Atom;
				}
				else
				{
					Bond.EndAtom = atomElement.Atom;
				}
			}
			InvalidateRelatedWhenAnchorPositionChanged();
		}

		private void InvalidateRelatedWhenAnchorPositionChanged()
		{
			InvalidateGeometry();
			if (StartAtomElement != null)
			{
				StartAtomElement.InvalidateDisplayedText();
			}
			if (EndAtomElement != null)
			{
				EndAtomElement.InvalidateDisplayedText();
			}
			if (StartAtomElement != null)
			{
				foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement in StartAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
				{
					if (connectedBondElement != this)
					{
						connectedBondElement.InvalidateGeometry();
					}
				}
			}
			if (EndAtomElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement2 in EndAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
			{
				if (connectedBondElement2 != this)
				{
					connectedBondElement2.InvalidateGeometry();
				}
			}
		}

		public List<PointPair> GetLinesInfo()
		{
			return _renderPointPairs;
		}

		public static void SetDefaultBondLength(DependencyObject element, double length)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			element.SetValue(DefaultBondLengthProperty, length);
		}

		public static double GetDefaultBondLength(DependencyObject element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return (double)element.GetValue(DefaultBondLengthProperty);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetSidePoint(System.Windows.Point sp, System.Windows.Point ep, double angle, double interval)
		{
			Vector vector = ep - sp;
			if (vector.Length == 0.0)
			{
				return sp;
			}
			double num = Math.Abs(interval / Math.Sin(Math.Abs(angle) * Math.PI / 180.0));
			Vector vector2 = new RotateTransform(angle).Value.Transform(vector);
			vector2 = vector2 * num / vector2.Length;
			return sp + vector2;
		}

		private static void OnLinesIntervalThicknessPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as BondElement).InvalidateGeometry();
		}

		private static void OnBondPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			BondElement bondElement = d as BondElement;
			if (e.OldValue is Bond bond)
			{
				bond.TypeChanged -= bondElement.Bond_TypeChanged;
			}
			if (e.NewValue is Bond bond2)
			{
				bond2.TypeChanged += bondElement.Bond_TypeChanged;
			}
			bondElement.InvalidateGeometry();
		}

		private static void OnStartAnchorPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as BondElement).OnAnchorChanged(e.OldValue as DiagramAnchor, e.NewValue as DiagramAnchor, isStartPoint: true);
		}

		private static void OnEndAnchorPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as BondElement).OnAnchorChanged(e.OldValue as DiagramAnchor, e.NewValue as DiagramAnchor, isStartPoint: false);
		}

		void IAnchorConsumer.AnchorPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
		}

		void IAnchorConsumer.AnchorProjectionPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			InvalidateRelatedWhenAnchorPositionChanged();
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(AnchorPositionChangedEvent));
		}

		bool IAnchorConsumer.ReconnectToAnchor(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			if (anchor == StartAnchor || anchor == EndAnchor)
			{
				InvalidateGeometry();
			}
			return true;
		}

		object IServiceProvider.GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(IAnchorConsumer))
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		void ISelectionOverlayService.AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			editor.AddAdorner(new BondElementAdorner(this));
		}

		Rect ISelectionOverlayService.GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			Rect result = new Rect(new Rect(EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint, StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint).Size);
			foreach (PointPair renderPointPair in _renderPointPairs)
			{
				result.Union(renderPointPair.StartPoint);
				result.Union(renderPointPair.EndPoint);
			}
			if (_renderPointPairs.Count == 1)
			{
				double interval = LinesIntervalThickness / 2.0;
				double num = 90.0;
				double num2 = 90.0;
				System.Windows.Point startPoint = _renderPointPairs[0].StartPoint;
				System.Windows.Point endPoint = _renderPointPairs[0].EndPoint;
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint = GetSidePoint(startPoint, endPoint, 0.0 - num, interval);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint2 = GetSidePoint(endPoint, startPoint, num2, interval);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint3 = GetSidePoint(startPoint, endPoint, num, interval);
				System.Windows.Point sidePoint4 = GetSidePoint(endPoint, startPoint, 0.0 - num2, interval);
				result.Union(sidePoint);
				result.Union(sidePoint2);
				result.Union(sidePoint3);
				result.Union(sidePoint4);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class FragmentPreviewElement : ContentControl
	{
		private readonly Canvas _canvas = new Canvas();

		private readonly List<AtomElement> _atomElements = new List<AtomElement>();

		private readonly List<BondElement> _bondElements = new List<BondElement>();

		public Fragment Fragment { get; private set; }

		public List<AtomElement> AtomElements => _atomElements;

		public List<BondElement> BondElements => _bondElements;

		public Vector FirstElementVector
		{
			get
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[0]);
				return new Vector(elementPosition.X, elementPosition.Y);
			}
		}

		public FragmentPreviewElement()
		{
			base.Content = _canvas;
			base.IsHitTestVisible = false;
			base.Opacity = 0.5;
		}

		public void LoadFragment(Fragment fragment, double shortestBondLength)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			Fragment = fragment;
			if (fragment.Atoms.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				AtomElement atomElement = new AtomElement
				{
					Atom = atom,
					LettersVisibility = Visibility.Hidden
				};
				AtomElements.Add(atomElement);
				_canvas.Children.Add(atomElement);
			}
			foreach (Bond bond in fragment.Bonds)
			{
				BondElement bondElement = new BondElement();
				bondElement.Bond = bond;
				bondElement.StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection(new double[2] { 4.0, 0.5 });
				BondElement bondElement2 = bondElement;
				BondElements.Add(bondElement2);
				_canvas.Children.Add(bondElement2);
			}
			Update(shortestBondLength, 0.0);
		}

		public void Update(double shortestBondLength, double rotateAngle)
		{
			foreach (AtomElement atomElement2 in AtomElements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point absolutePositionForAtom = GetAbsolutePositionForAtom(atomElement2.Atom);
				Canvas.SetLeft(atomElement2, absolutePositionForAtom.X);
				Canvas.SetTop(atomElement2, absolutePositionForAtom.Y);
			}
			double extendRatio = GetExtendRatio(shortestBondLength);
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[0]);
			for (int i = 1; i < AtomElements.Count; i++)
			{
				AtomElement atomElement = AtomElements[i];
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition2 = GetElementPosition(atomElement);
				System.Windows.Point projectionPosition = GetProjectionPosition(elementPosition, elementPosition2, extendRatio, rotateAngle);
				Canvas.SetLeft(atomElement, projectionPosition.X);
				Canvas.SetTop(atomElement, projectionPosition.Y);
			}
			double num = double.MaxValue;
			double num2 = double.MinValue;
			double num3 = double.MaxValue;
			double num4 = double.MinValue;
			foreach (AtomElement atomElement3 in AtomElements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition3 = GetElementPosition(atomElement3);
				num = Math.Min(num, elementPosition3.X);
				num3 = Math.Min(num3, elementPosition3.Y);
				num2 = Math.Max(num2, elementPosition3.X);
				num4 = Math.Max(num4, elementPosition3.Y);
			}
			base.Width = num2 - num;
			base.Height = num4 - num3;
			foreach (AtomElement atomElement4 in AtomElements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition4 = GetElementPosition(atomElement4);
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(elementPosition4.X - num, elementPosition4.Y - num3);
				Canvas.SetLeft(atomElement4, point.X);
				Canvas.SetTop(atomElement4, point.Y);
			}
			foreach (BondElement bondElement in BondElements)
			{
				bondElement.StartAnchor = new PointAnchor(GetElementPosition(AtomElements[Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bondElement.Bond)]));
				bondElement.EndAnchor = new PointAnchor(GetElementPosition(AtomElements[Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bondElement.Bond)]));
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetAtomElementPosition(AtomElement element, System.Windows.Point basePoint)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(element);
			return new System.Windows.Point(basePoint.X + elementPosition.X, basePoint.Y + elementPosition.Y);
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetProjectionPosition(System.Windows.Point basePoint, System.Windows.Point oldPosition, double ratio, double rotateAngle)
		{
			Vector vector = (oldPosition - basePoint) * ratio;
			vector = new RotateTransform(rotateAngle).Value.Transform(vector);
			return basePoint + vector;
		}

		private double GetExtendRatio(double shortestBondLength)
		{
			if (AtomElements.Count < 2)
			{
				return 1.0;
			}
			if (BondElements.Count > 0)
			{
				double num = double.PositiveInfinity;
				foreach (BondElement bondElement in BondElements)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bondElement.Bond)]);
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition2 = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bondElement.Bond)]);
					num = Math.Min(num, (elementPosition2 - elementPosition).Length);
				}
				return shortestBondLength / num;
			}
			double num2 = double.PositiveInfinity;
			for (int i = 0; i < AtomElements.Count; i++)
			{
				for (int j = i + 1; j < AtomElements.Count; j++)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition3 = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[i]);
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition4 = GetElementPosition(AtomElements[j]);
					num2 = Math.Min(num2, (elementPosition4 - elementPosition3).Length);
				}
			}
			return shortestBondLength / num2;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetAbsolutePositionForAtom(Atom atom)
		{
			AtomPosition position = Fragment.GetPosition(atom);
			if (position.Mode == AtomPositionMode.Absolute)
			{
				return position.AbsolutePoint;
			}
			AtomElement ae = AtomElements[position.RelativeAtomIndex];
			Vector vector = new Vector(position.RelativeDistance, 0.0);
			return GetElementPosition(ae) + new RotateTransform(position.RelativeAngle).Value.Transform(vector);
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetElementPosition(AtomElement ae)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Point(Canvas.GetLeft(ae), Canvas.GetTop(ae));
		}
	}
	public enum VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom
	{
		VisibleOnlyWhenIsolated,
		VisibleOnlyWhenAtEnd,
		VisibleAnytime
	}
	[ContentProperty("Elements")]
	public class MolecularDrawingCanvas : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private class AtomElementVisibilityConverter : IMultiValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (values.Length != 6)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (values[0] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[1] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[2] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[3] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[4] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue || values[5] == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				string obj = (string)values[0];
				VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom visibilityModeOfDefaultAtom = (VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom)values[1];
				string text = (string)values[2];
				_ = (int)values[3];
				int num = (int)values[4];
				bool flag = (bool)values[5];
				AtomElement atomElement = parameter as AtomElement;
				if (obj != text)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (num != 0)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				switch (visibilityModeOfDefaultAtom)
				{
				case VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom.VisibleAnytime:
					return Visibility.Visible;
				case VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom.VisibleOnlyWhenIsolated:
					if (atomElement.ConnectedBondElements.Count() > 0)
					{
						return Visibility.Hidden;
					}
					return Visibility.Visible;
				case VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom.VisibleOnlyWhenAtEnd:
					if (atomElement.ConnectedBondElements.Count() > 1)
					{
						return Visibility.Hidden;
					}
					return Visibility.Visible;
				default:
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
			}

			public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private Fragment _fragment;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveBondTypeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActiveBondType", typeof(BondType), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(BondType.Single));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveAtomKeyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActiveAtomKey", typeof(string), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultAtomNameProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultAtomName", typeof(string), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("C"));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultAtomVisibilityModeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultAtomVisibilityMode", typeof(VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(VisibilityModeOfDefaultAtom.VisibleOnlyWhenAtEnd));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinorBondLengthProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinorBondLength", typeof(double), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(20.0));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultBondLengthProperty = BondElement.DefaultBondLengthProperty.AddOwner(typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultLoadingPositionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultLoadingPosition", typeof(System.Windows.Point), typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new System.Windows.Point(100.0, 100.0)));

		private bool _contentLoaded;
