		public Diagram Diagram => base.Content as Diagram;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public UIElementCollection Elements => Diagram.DiagramElements;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			Diagram.PrimitiveContextMenuOption = DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.None;
			Diagram.ElementAdded += Diagram_ElementAdded;
			Diagram.PreviewElementRemoved += Diagram_PreviewElementRemoved;
			Diagram.CutExecuting += Diagram_CutExecuting;
			Diagram.CopyExecuting += Diagram_CopyExecuting;
			Diagram.PasteExecuting += Diagram_PasteExecuting;
			Diagram.PrimitiveContextMenuInvoked += Diagram_PrimitiveContextMenuInvoked;
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeContent()
		{
			return false;
		}

		public Atom FindResourceAtom()
		{
			return FindResourceAtomWithKey(ActiveAtomKey);
		}

		public Atom FindResourceAtomWithKey(string key)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TryFindResource(key) as Atom;
		}

		public Atom FindResourceAtomWithName(string name)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(name))
			{
				return null;
			}
			string text = name.Substring(0, 1).ToUpper() + name.Substring(1);
			return FindResourceAtomWithKey("Atom" + text);
		}

		public Fragment FindResourceFragment()
		{
			Fragment fragment = FindResourceFragmentWithKey(ActiveFragmentKey);
			for (int i = 0; i < fragment.Atoms.Count; i++)
			{
				Atom atom = fragment.Atoms[i];
				Atom atom2 = FindResourceAtomWithName(atom.Name);
				if (atom2 != null)
				{
					fragment.Atoms[i] = atom2.Clone();
					Fragment.SetPosition(fragment.Atoms[i], Fragment.GetPosition(atom));
				}
			}
			return fragment;
		}

		public Fragment FindResourceFragmentWithKey(string key)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return TryFindResource(key) as Fragment;
		}

		public AtomElement CreateAtomElement()
		{
			AtomElement atomElement = new AtomElement();
			Atom atom = FindResourceAtom();
			if (atom != null)
			{
				if (atom.Type == ParticleType.Ion)
				{
					atom.Valence = atom.CommonCharge;
				}
				atomElement.Atom = atom.Clone();
				return atomElement;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public Rect GetAtomElementRect(AtomElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(element);
			System.Windows.Size elementSize = Diagram.GetElementSize(element);
			return new Rect(elementPosition.X - elementSize.Width / 2.0, elementPosition.Y - elementSize.Height / 2.0, elementSize.Width, elementSize.Height);
		}

		public DrawingVisual CreateVectorImage(System.Windows.Media.Brush background, Thickness inflateSize, out System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			DrawingVisual drawingVisual = new DrawingVisual();
			List<AtomElement> list = Diagram.DiagramElements.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
			List<BondElement> list2 = Diagram.DiagramElements.OfType<BondElement>().ToList();
			System.Windows.Size size2;
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity);
				System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point(double.NegativeInfinity, double.NegativeInfinity);
				foreach (AtomElement item in list)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item);
					point.X = Math.Min(point.X, elementPosition.X);
					point.Y = Math.Min(point.Y, elementPosition.Y);
					point2.X = Math.Max(point2.X, elementPosition.X);
					point2.Y = Math.Max(point2.Y, elementPosition.Y);
				}
				System.Windows.Point point3 = new System.Windows.Point(point.X - inflateSize.Left, point.Y - inflateSize.Top);
				size2 = new System.Windows.Size(point2.X - point.X + inflateSize.Left + inflateSize.Right, point2.Y - point.Y + inflateSize.Top + inflateSize.Bottom);
				using DrawingContext drawingContext = drawingVisual.RenderOpen();
				System.Windows.Media.Pen pen = new System.Windows.Media.Pen();
				drawingContext.DrawRectangle(background, pen, new Rect(size2));
				foreach (BondElement item2 in list2)
				{
					Vector vector = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item2) - point3;
					List<PointPair> linesInfo = item2.GetLinesInfo();
					System.Windows.Media.Pen pen2 = new System.Windows.Media.Pen(item2.Stroke, item2.StrokeThickness);
					foreach (PointPair item3 in linesInfo)
					{
						drawingContext.DrawLine(pen2, item3.StartPoint + vector, item3.EndPoint + vector);
					}
				}
				foreach (AtomElement item4 in list)
				{
					Vector vector2 = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item4) - point3;
					List<TextInfo> textInformation = item4.GetTextInformation();
					if (textInformation.Count <= 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Rect rectangle = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point((0.0 - item4.ActualWidth) / 2.0, (0.0 - item4.ActualHeight) / 2.0) + vector2, item4.RenderSize);
					drawingContext.DrawRectangle(background, pen, rectangle);
					foreach (TextInfo item5 in textInformation)
					{
						FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(item5.Text, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, System.Windows.FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(base.FontFamily, FontStyles.Normal, FontWeights.Normal, default(FontStretch)), item5.FontSize, base.Foreground);
						drawingContext.DrawText(formattedText, item5.RelativePoint + vector2);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				size2 = new System.Windows.Size(inflateSize.Left + inflateSize.Right, inflateSize.Top + inflateSize.Bottom);
				using DrawingContext drawingContext2 = drawingVisual.RenderOpen();
				drawingContext2.DrawRectangle(background, new System.Windows.Media.Pen(), new Rect(size2));
			}
			size = size2;
			return drawingVisual;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.GetDiagramElement() is AtomElement atomElement && !Diagram.Selection.Contains(atomElement))
			{
				Diagram.UnselectAll();
				Diagram.Select(atomElement);
			}
			base.OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(e);
		}

		public void AddAtomElement(AtomElement element, System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			Diagram.AddElement(element, position);
		}

		public void TryReplaceAtomForElement(AtomElement element, Atom atom)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (atom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atom");
			}
			if (element.Atom == null || atom.AvailableLinksCount < element.Atom.Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds)
			{
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(Diagram);
			try
			{
				Atom oldAtom = element.Atom;
				Atom newAtom = atom;
				element.Atom = newAtom;
				foreach (Bond item in new List<Bond>(oldAtom.Bonds))
				{
					if (item.StartAtom == oldAtom)
					{
						item.StartAtom = newAtom;
					}
					else
					{
						item.EndAtom = newAtom;
					}
				}
				if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
				{
					transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
					{
						TryReplaceAtomForElement(element, oldAtom);
					}, delegate
					{
						TryReplaceAtomForElement(element, newAtom);
					});
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public BondElement ConnectAtomElements(AtomElement start, AtomElement end)
		{
			if (start == null || end == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			using (new TransactionContext(Diagram))
			{
				BondElement bondElement = new BondElement
				{
					Bond = new Bond
					{
						Type = ActiveBondType
					}
				};
				Diagram.AddElement(bondElement);
				bondElement.ConnectStartTo(start);
				bondElement.ConnectEndTo(end);
				return bondElement;
			}
		}

		public void TryIncreaseNeiborhood(AtomElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (element.Atom == null || element.Atom.AvailableLinksCount < ActiveBondType.Valence)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point positionToExtendNewAtomElement = AngleHelper.GetPositionToExtendNewAtomElement(element, DefaultBondLength, ActiveBondType);
			if (positionToExtendNewAtomElement.X == 0.0 || positionToExtendNewAtomElement.Y == 0.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			AtomElement atomElement = FindAtomElementAt(positionToExtendNewAtomElement);
			using (new TransactionContext(Diagram))
			{
				if (atomElement == null)
				{
					atomElement = CreateAtomElement();
					AddAtomElement(atomElement, positionToExtendNewAtomElement);
				}
				ConnectAtomElements(element, atomElement);
			}
		}

		public void IncreaseBondType(BondElement bondElement)
		{
			if (bondElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bondElement");
			}
			if (bondElement.Bond != null)
			{
				BondType nextAllowedTypeToChange = ValenceHelper.GetNextAllowedTypeToChange(bondElement.Bond);
				ChangeBondElementType(bondElement, nextAllowedTypeToChange);
			}
		}

		public void ChangeBondElementType(BondElement bondElement, BondType newType)
		{
			if (bondElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bondElement");
			}
			if (bondElement.Bond == null || bondElement.Bond.Type == newType)
			{
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(Diagram);
			BondType oldType = bondElement.Bond.Type;
			bondElement.Bond.Type = newType;
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					bondElement.Bond.Type = oldType;
				}, delegate
				{
					bondElement.Bond.Type = newType;
				});
			}
		}

		public void InterChangeBondAtoms(BondElement bondElement)
		{
			if (bondElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bondElement");
			}
			AtomElement startAtomElement = bondElement.StartAtomElement;
			AtomElement endAtomElement = bondElement.EndAtomElement;
			using (new TransactionContext(Diagram))
			{
				if (startAtomElement != null && endAtomElement != null)
				{
					bondElement.ConnectStartTo(null);
					bondElement.ConnectEndTo(null);
					bondElement.ConnectStartTo(endAtomElement);
					bondElement.ConnectEndTo(startAtomElement);
				}
			}
		}

		public AtomElement FindAtomElementAt(System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in Diagram.DiagramElements)
			{
				if (diagramElement is AtomElement atomElement && GetAtomElementRect(atomElement).Contains(position))
				{
					return atomElement;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public List<AtomElement> FindAtomElementsAt(System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			List<AtomElement> list = new List<AtomElement>();
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in Diagram.DiagramElements)
			{
				if (diagramElement is AtomElement atomElement && GetAtomElementRect(atomElement).Contains(position))
				{
					list.Add(atomElement);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public BondElement GetConnectedBondElement(AtomElement fromAtomElement, AtomElement toAtomElement)
		{
			if (fromAtomElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fromAtomElement");
			}
			if (toAtomElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("toAtomElement");
			}
			foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement in fromAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
			{
				foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement2 in toAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
				{
					if (connectedBondElement == connectedBondElement2)
					{
						return connectedBondElement;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void AddFragmentElement(FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (fragmentPreviewElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragmentPreviewElement");
			}
			using (new TransactionContext(Diagram))
			{
				List<AtomElement> list = new List<AtomElement>();
				List<AtomElement> list2 = new List<AtomElement>();
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement4 in fragmentPreviewElement.AtomElements)
				{
					System.Windows.Point atomElementPosition = fragmentPreviewElement.GetAtomElementPosition(atomElement4, point);
					AtomElement atomElement = FindAtomElementAt(atomElementPosition);
					if (atomElement == null)
					{
						Atom atom = atomElement4.Atom.Clone();
						atomElement = new AtomElement
						{
							Atom = atom
						};
						AddAtomElement(atomElement, atomElementPosition);
					}
					else
					{
						list2.Add(atomElement);
					}
					list.Add(atomElement);
				}
				foreach (Bond bond2 in fragmentPreviewElement.Fragment.Bonds)
				{
					AtomElement atomElement2 = list[Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bond2)];
					AtomElement atomElement3 = list[Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bond2)];
					if (GetConnectedBondElement(atomElement2, atomElement3) == null)
					{
						Bond bond = bond2.Clone();
						BondElement bondElement = new BondElement
						{
							Bond = bond
						};
						Diagram.AddElement(bondElement);
						bondElement.ConnectStartTo(atomElement2);
						bondElement.ConnectEndTo(atomElement3);
					}
				}
				if (fragmentPreviewElement.Fragment.ShouldAdjustBonds)
				{
					AdjustBonds(list, list2, fragmentPreviewElement);
				}
			}
		}

		public void InsertFragment(Fragment fragment, System.Windows.Point position, double shortestBondLength)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement = new FragmentPreviewElement();
			fragmentPreviewElement.LoadFragment(fragment, shortestBondLength);
			AddFragmentElement(fragmentPreviewElement, position);
		}

		public void ImportFragment(Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			Clear();
			InsertFragment(fragment, DefaultLoadingPosition, DefaultBondLength);
			Diagram.TransactionManager.ClearHistory();
			_fragment = fragment;
		}

		public Fragment ExportFragment(IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			Fragment fragment = new Fragment();
			fragment.Atoms.Clear();
			fragment.Bonds.Clear();
			List<AtomElement> list = elements.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
			List<BondElement> list2 = elements.OfType<BondElement>().ToList();
			foreach (BondElement item in list2)
			{
				if (!list.Contains(item.StartAtomElement))
				{
					list.Add(item.StartAtomElement);
				}
				if (!list.Contains(item.EndAtomElement))
				{
					list.Add(item.EndAtomElement);
				}
			}
			foreach (AtomElement item2 in list)
			{
				Atom atom = item2.Atom.Clone();
				Fragment.SetPosition(atom, new AtomPosition
				{
					Mode = AtomPositionMode.Absolute,
					AbsolutePoint = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item2)
				});
				fragment.Atoms.Add(atom);
			}
			foreach (BondElement item3 in list2)
			{
				Bond bond = item3.Bond.Clone();
				Fragment.SetStartAtomIndex(bond, list.IndexOf(item3.StartAtomElement));
				Fragment.SetEndAtomIndex(bond, list.IndexOf(item3.EndAtomElement));
				bond.StartAtom = fragment.Atoms[Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bond)];
				bond.EndAtom = fragment.Atoms[Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bond)];
				fragment.Bonds.Add(bond);
			}
			return fragment;
		}

		public Fragment ExportFragment()
		{
			Fragment fragment = ExportFragment(Diagram.DiagramElements.Cast<UIElement>());
			if (_fragment != null)
			{
				fragment.Name = _fragment.Name;
				fragment.Comment1 = _fragment.Comment1;
				fragment.Comment2 = _fragment.Comment2;
				fragment.Comment3 = _fragment.Comment3;
			}
			return fragment;
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			Diagram.Clear();
		}

		public void OpenChangePropertyWindow(Atom atom)
		{
			if (atom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atom");
			}
			int oldChargeCount = atom.ChargeCount;
			int oldValence = atom.Valence;
			AtomPropertyWindow atomPropertyWindow = new AtomPropertyWindow(atom);
			atomPropertyWindow.Owner = Window.GetWindow(this);
			atomPropertyWindow.ShowDialog();
			int newChargeCount = atom.ChargeCount;
			int newValence = atom.Valence;
			if (oldChargeCount == newChargeCount && oldValence == newValence)
			{
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(Diagram);
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					atom.ChargeCount = oldChargeCount;
					atom.Valence = oldValence;
				}, delegate
				{
					atom.ChargeCount = newChargeCount;
					atom.Valence = newValence;
				});
			}
		}

		private void AdjustBonds(List<AtomElement> atomElements, List<AtomElement> existings, FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement)
		{
			List<AtomElement> list = existings.Where((AtomElement ele) => ele.Atom.AvailableLinksCount < 0).ToList();
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (AtomElement item2 in list)
			{
				foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement in item2.ConnectedBondElements)
				{
					AtomElement item = ((connectedBondElement.StartAtomElement == item2) ? connectedBondElement.EndAtomElement : connectedBondElement.StartAtomElement);
					if (!existings.Contains(item) && atomElements.Contains(item))
					{
						connectedBondElement.Bond.Type = BondType.Single;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void Diagram_ElementAdded(object sender, ElementAddedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Element is AtomElement atomElement)
			{
				MultiBinding multiBinding = new MultiBinding
				{
					Converter = new AtomElementVisibilityConverter(),
					ConverterParameter = atomElement
				};
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = this,
					Path = new PropertyPath(DefaultAtomNameProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = this,
					Path = new PropertyPath(DefaultAtomVisibilityModeProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.Name")
				{
					Source = atomElement,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.AvailableLinksCount")
				{
					Source = atomElement,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Atom.ChargeCount")
				{
					Source = atomElement,
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = atomElement,
					Path = new PropertyPath(UIElement.IsMouseOverProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
				atomElement.SetBinding(AtomElement.LettersVisibilityProperty, multiBinding);
				atomElement.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BackgroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = Diagram,
					Path = new PropertyPath(Diagram.BackgroundProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
		}

		private void Diagram_PreviewElementRemoved(object sender, PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Element is AtomElement atomElement)
			{
				foreach (BondElement item in new List<BondElement>(atomElement.ConnectedBondElements))
				{
					Diagram.RemoveElement(item);
				}
			}
			if (e.Element is BondElement bondElement)
			{
				bondElement.ConnectStartTo(null);
				bondElement.ConnectEndTo(null);
			}
		}

		private void Diagram_CutExecuting(object sender, CopyExecutingEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Data = GetCopyData();
			Diagram.RemoveSelectedElements();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void Diagram_CopyExecuting(object sender, CopyExecutingEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Data = GetCopyData();
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void Diagram_PasteExecuting(object sender, PasteExecutingEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!typeof(KeyValuePair<Fragment, double>).IsInstanceOfType(e.Data))
			{
				return;
			}
			KeyValuePair<Fragment, double> keyValuePair = (KeyValuePair<Fragment, double>)e.Data;
			Fragment key = keyValuePair.Key;
			double value = keyValuePair.Value;
			if (key != null)
			{
				if (e.PastePosition.HasValue)
				{
					InsertFragment(key, e.PastePosition.Value, value);
				}
				else
				{
					InsertFragment(key, FindPastePoint(key, value), value);
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point FindPastePoint(Fragment fragment, double shortestBondLength)
		{
			FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement = new FragmentPreviewElement();
			fragmentPreviewElement.LoadFragment(fragment, shortestBondLength);
			System.Windows.Point defaultLoadingPosition = DefaultLoadingPosition;
			Vector vector = new Vector(shortestBondLength, shortestBondLength);
			for (List<Rect> reservedRects = (from x in Diagram.DiagramElements.OfType<AtomElement>()
				select GetAtomElementRect(x)).ToList(); !IsPositionOkToPaste(defaultLoadingPosition, fragmentPreviewElement, reservedRects); defaultLoadingPosition += vector)
			{
			}
			return defaultLoadingPosition;
		}

		private bool IsPositionOkToPaste(System.Windows.Point position, FragmentPreviewElement fpe, List<Rect> reservedRects)
		{
			Rect rect = new Rect(position, new System.Windows.Size(fpe.Width, fpe.Height));
			foreach (Rect reservedRect in reservedRects)
			{
				if (rect.IntersectsWith(reservedRect))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void Diagram_PrimitiveContextMenuInvoked(object sender, ContextMenuInvokedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemCollection items = e.ContextMenu.Items;

				InputGestureText = "",
				Command = ApplicationCommands.Paste,
				Icon = CreateContextMenuIcon(ImageResources.Paste),

			});

		private object CreateContextMenuIcon(ImageSource imageSource)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Controls.Image
			{
				Source = imageSource,
				Width = 16.0,
				Height = 16.0
			};
		}

		private object GetCopyData()
		{
			Fragment key = ExportFragment(Diagram.Selection);
			double shortestBondLength = GetShortestBondLength(Diagram.Selection);
			return new KeyValuePair<Fragment, double>(key, shortestBondLength);
		}

		private double GetShortestBondLength(IEnumerable<UIElement> uiElements)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>(uiElements);
			if (list.Count <= 1)
			{
				return DefaultBondLength;
			}
			List<BondElement> list2 = list.OfType<BondElement>().ToList();
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				return (from be in list2
					let length = (be.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - be.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint).Length
					orderby length
					select length).First();
			}
			List<AtomElement> list3 = list.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
			double num = double.PositiveInfinity;
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < list3.Count; num2++)
			{
				for (int num3 = num2 + 1; num3 < list3.Count; num3++)
				{
					num = Math.Min(num, (Diagram.GetElementPosition(list3[num2]) - Diagram.GetElementPosition(list3[num3])).Length);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private void ChangeProperty_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Diagram != null && Diagram.Selection.Count == 1 && Diagram.Selection[0] is AtomElement)
			{
				e.CanExecute = true;
			}
		}

		private void ChangeProperty_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Diagram != null && Diagram.Selection.Count == 1 && Diagram.Selection[0] is AtomElement)
			{
				OpenChangePropertyWindow((Diagram.Selection[0] as AtomElement).Atom);
			}
		}

		private void FlipTopToBottom_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Diagram != null)
			{
				if (Diagram.Selection.Count >= 2)
				{
					e.CanExecute = true;
				}
				else if (Diagram.Selection.OfType<BondElement>().Count() > 0)
				{
					e.CanExecute = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void FlipTopToBottom_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			FlipHelper.FlipTopToBottom(Diagram, Diagram.Selection);
		}

		private void FlipLeftToRight_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Diagram != null)
			{
				if (Diagram.Selection.Count >= 2)
				{
					e.CanExecute = true;
				}
				else if (Diagram.Selection.OfType<BondElement>().Count() > 0)
				{
					e.CanExecute = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void FlipLeftToRight_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			FlipHelper.FlipLeftToRight(Diagram, Diagram.Selection);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += ChangeProperty_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += ChangeProperty_Executed;
				break;

				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Source", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(ImageButton));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty KeyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Key", typeof(string), typeof(ImageButton), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnKeyPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly RoutedEvent IsCheckedChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("IsCheckedChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(ImageButton));

		public static readonly RoutedEvent KeyChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("KeyChanged", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(ImageButton));

		internal Border Part_Border;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Image Part_Image;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public bool IsChecked

			}
		}

		{
			Part_Image.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Image.SourceProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(SourceProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Source != null)
			{
				IsChecked = true;
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
		}

		private static void OnKeyPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as ImageButton).RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(KeyChangedEvent));
		}

		private static void OnIsCheckedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ImageButton imageButton = d as ImageButton;
			if (imageButton.Part_Border != null)
			{
				if (imageButton.IsChecked)
				{
					if (imageButton.Resources["Part_SelectedBrush"] is System.Windows.Media.Brush background)
					{
						imageButton.Part_Border.Background = background;
						imageButton.Part_Border.BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.CornflowerBlue;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					imageButton.Part_Border.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
					imageButton.Part_Border.BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
				}
			}
			imageButton.RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(IsCheckedChangedEvent));
		}

				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

				break;

				break;
			}
		}

	public static class MolecularDrawingCommands
	{
		public static RoutedUICommand Clear { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ChangeProperty { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand FlipTopToBottom { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand FlipLeftToRight { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand FlipOnAtomOrBond { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomIn { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomOut { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomToFit { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomToActualSize { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand Erase { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand IncreaseCharge { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand DecreaseCharge { get; private set; }

		static MolecularDrawingCommands()
		{
			Clear = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_Clear, "Clear", new KeyGesture(Key.R, ModifierKeys.Control));
			ChangeProperty = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_ChangeProperty, "ChangeProperty", null);
			FlipTopToBottom = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_FlipTopToBottom, "FlipTopToBottom", null);
			FlipLeftToRight = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_FlipLeftToRight, "FlipLeftToRight", null);
			FlipOnAtomOrBond = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_FlipOnAtomOrBond, "FlipLeftToRight", null);
			ZoomIn = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_ZoomIn, "ZoomIn", new KeyGesture(Key.Up, ModifierKeys.Control));
			ZoomOut = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_ZoomOut, "ZoomOut", new KeyGesture(Key.Down, ModifierKeys.Control));
			ZoomToFit = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_ZoomToFit, "ZoomToFit", new KeyGesture(Key.End, ModifierKeys.Control));
			ZoomToActualSize = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_ZoomToActualSize, "ZoomToActualSize", new KeyGesture(Key.Home, ModifierKeys.Control));
			Erase = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_Erase, "Erase", new KeyGesture(Key.Delete, ModifierKeys.Control));
			IncreaseCharge = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_IncreaseCharge, "IncreaseCharge", new KeyGesture(Key.Add, ModifierKeys.Control));
			DecreaseCharge = CreateCommand(Resources.CommandText_DecreaseCharge, "DecreaseCharge", new KeyGesture(Key.Subtract, ModifierKeys.Control));
		}

		private static RoutedUICommand CreateCommand(string text, string name, KeyGesture gesture)
		{
			RoutedUICommand routedUICommand = new RoutedUICommand(text, name, typeof(MolecularDrawingCommands));
			if (gesture != null)
			{
				routedUICommand.InputGestures.Add(gesture);
			}
			return routedUICommand;
		}
	}
	public class PeriocTableWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private readonly string _initializedAtomKey;

		private readonly Border _adornerBorder;

		internal Grid PrimaryTable;

		internal Grid AtomInfoGrid;

		internal Grid AssistTable;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ButtonOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ButtonCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string SelectedResourceKey { get; private set; }

		public ImageSource SelectedImageSource { get; private set; }

		public PeriocTableWindow(string initializedAtomKey)
		{
			_initializedAtomKey = initializedAtomKey;
			_adornerBorder = new Border
			{
				BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
				BorderThickness = new Thickness(2.0)
			};
			System.Windows.Controls.Panel.SetZIndex(_adornerBorder, 100);
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(Image_MouseLeftButtonDown));
			InitializeSelection();
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		private void InitializeSelection()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_initializedAtomKey))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UIElement child in PrimaryTable.Children)
			{
				if (child is System.Windows.Controls.Image { Tag: not null } image && image.Tag.ToString() == _initializedAtomKey)
				{
					TrySelectImage(image);
					return;
				}
			}
			foreach (UIElement child2 in AssistTable.Children)
			{
				if (child2 is System.Windows.Controls.Image { Tag: not null } image2 && image2.Tag.ToString() == _initializedAtomKey)
				{
					TrySelectImage(image2);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void TrySelectImage(System.Windows.Controls.Image img)
		{
			if (img != null)
			{
				string text = img.Tag as string;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && TryFindResource(text) is Atom dataContext)
				{
					AddAdornerBorder(img);
					AtomInfoGrid.DataContext = dataContext;
					AtomInfoGrid.Tag = img;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddAdornerBorder(System.Windows.Controls.Image img)
		{
			Grid grid = null;
			if (AtomInfoGrid.Tag is System.Windows.Controls.Image image)
			{
				grid = image.Parent as Grid;
			}
			if (img != null)
			{
				Grid grid2 = img.Parent as Grid;
				if (grid != null && grid.Children.Contains(_adornerBorder) && grid != grid2)
				{
					grid.Children.Remove(_adornerBorder);
				}
				if (grid2 != null && !grid2.Children.Contains(_adornerBorder))
				{
					grid2.Children.Add(_adornerBorder);
				}
				Grid.SetRow(_adornerBorder, Grid.GetRow(img));
				Grid.SetColumn(_adornerBorder, Grid.GetColumn(img));
			}
		}

		private void Image_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OriginalSource is System.Windows.Controls.Image img)
			{
				TrySelectImage(img);
			}
		}

		private void ButtonOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AtomInfoGrid.Tag is System.Windows.Controls.Image image)
			{
				SelectedResourceKey = image.Tag as string;
				SelectedImageSource = image.Source;
				Close();
			}
		}

		private void ButtonCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing;component/perioctablewindow.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				PrimaryTable = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				AtomInfoGrid = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				AssistTable = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				ButtonOK = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				ButtonOK.Click += ButtonOK_Click;
				break;
			case 5:
				ButtonCancel = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				ButtonCancel.Click += ButtonCancel_Click;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class AtomPropertyWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private Atom _atom;

		private int _oldChargeCount;

		private int _oldValence;

		private bool _isCanceled;

		internal Grid InformationBox;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox ComboBoxChargeCount;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox TextBoxChargeCount;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox ComboBoxValence;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ButtonOK;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.Button ButtonCancel;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public AtomPropertyWindow(Atom atom)
		{
			if (atom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atom");
			}
			_atom = atom;
			_oldChargeCount = atom.ChargeCount;
			_oldValence = atom.Valence;
			base.Loaded += AtomPropertyWindow_Loaded;
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			if (_atom.Type == ParticleType.Ion)
			{
				ComboBoxChargeCount.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			else
			{
				TextBoxChargeCount.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			RefreshChargeCount();
			foreach (double possibleValence in _atom.PossibleValences)
			{
				ComboBoxValence.Items.Add(new ComboBoxItem
				{
					Content = possibleValence,
					IsSelected = (possibleValence == (double)_atom.Valence)
				});
			}
		}

		private void RefreshChargeCount()
		{
			TextBoxChargeCount.Text = _atom.ChargeCount.ToString();
			foreach (ComboBoxItem item in (IEnumerable)ComboBoxChargeCount.Items)
			{
				if (item.Content.ToString() == _atom.ChargeCount.ToString())
				{
					item.IsSelected = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClosed(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (_isCanceled)
			{
				_atom.ChargeCount = _oldChargeCount;
				_atom.Valence = _oldValence;
			}
			base.OnClosed(e);
		}

		private void AtomPropertyWindow_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			InformationBox.DataContext = _atom;
		}

		private void ButtonOK_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Close();
		}

		private void ButtonCancel_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_isCanceled = true;
			Close();
		}

		private void ComboBoxValence_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_atom.Valence = int.Parse((ComboBoxValence.SelectedItem as ComboBoxItem).Content.ToString());
			RefreshChargeCount();
		}

		private void ComboBoxChargeCount_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_atom.ChargeCount = int.Parse((ComboBoxChargeCount.SelectedItem as ComboBoxItem).Content.ToString());
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing;component/atompropertywindow.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				InformationBox = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				ComboBoxChargeCount = (System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox)target;
				ComboBoxChargeCount.SelectionChanged += ComboBoxChargeCount_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 3:
				TextBoxChargeCount = (System.Windows.Controls.TextBox)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				ComboBoxValence = (System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox)target;
				ComboBoxValence.SelectionChanged += ComboBoxValence_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 5:
				ButtonOK = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				ButtonOK.Click += ButtonOK_Click;
				break;
			case 6:
				ButtonCancel = (System.Windows.Controls.Button)target;
				ButtonCancel.Click += ButtonCancel_Click;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class Toolbox : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private int _additionalAtomButtomIndex = -1;

		private IDrawingCanvasTool _activeTool;

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool SelectTool = new EditorSelectTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool ZoomTool = new EditorZoomTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool PanTool = new EditorPanTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool DrawingTool = new EditorDrawingTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool FlipTool = new EditorFlipTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool EraseTool = new EditorEraseTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool ChargeIncreaseTool = new EditorChargeIncreaseTool();

		public readonly IDrawingCanvasTool ChargeDecreaseTool = new EditorChargeDecreaseTool();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty EditorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Editor", typeof(MolecularDrawingCanvas), typeof(Toolbox), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnDrawingCanvasPropertyChanged));

		internal StackPanel PanelOfSelection;

		internal ImageButton SelectButton;

		internal Grid PanelOfBonds;

		internal ImageButton buttonSingleBond;

		internal Grid PanelOfAtoms;

		internal ImageButton buttonCarbon;

		internal ImageButton RandomAtom1;

		internal ImageButton RandomAtom2;

		internal HalfEnabledButton ButtonAtomMore;

		internal UniformGrid PanelOfFragments;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public IDrawingCanvasTool ActiveTool
		{
			get
			{
				return _activeTool;
			}
			set
			{
				IDrawingCanvasTool activeTool = _activeTool;
				if (value != activeTool)
				{
					RegisterDrawingCanvas(activeTool, value);
					_activeTool = value;
					OnActiveToolChanged(new PropertyChangedEventArgs<IDrawingCanvasTool>(activeTool, value));
				}
			}
		}

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas Editor
		{
			get
			{
				return (MolecularDrawingCanvas)GetValue(EditorProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(EditorProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<PropertyChangedEventArgs<IDrawingCanvasTool>> ActiveToolChanged;

		public Toolbox()
		{
			AddHandler(ImageButton.IsCheckedChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(ImageButton_IsCheckedChanged));
			AddHandler(ImageButton.KeyChangedEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(ImageButton_KeyChanged));
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public void ResetToDrawing()
		{
			ActiveTool = null;
			if (buttonSingleBond != null)
			{
				buttonSingleBond.IsChecked = true;
			}
			if (buttonCarbon != null)
			{
				buttonCarbon.IsChecked = true;
			}
			ActiveTool = DrawingTool;
		}

		public void ResetToSelect()
		{
			ActiveTool = null;
			if (SelectButton != null)
			{
				SelectButton.IsChecked = true;
			}
			ActiveTool = SelectTool;
		}

		public void EmptySelection()
		{
			EmptySelection(null);
		}

		public void EmptySelection(ImageButton exclusion)
		{
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfSelection))
			{
				if (childImageButton != exclusion)
				{
					childImageButton.IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton2 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfBonds))
			{
				if (childImageButton2 != exclusion)
				{
					childImageButton2.IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton3 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfAtoms))
			{
				if (childImageButton3 != exclusion)
				{
					childImageButton3.IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton4 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfFragments))
			{
				if (childImageButton4 != exclusion)
				{
					childImageButton4.IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			InitializeActiveTool();
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		protected virtual void OnActiveToolChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs<IDrawingCanvasTool> e)
		{
			if (this.ActiveToolChanged != null)
			{
				this.ActiveToolChanged(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeActiveTool()
		{
			if (ActiveTool != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfSelection))
			{
				if (childImageButton.IsChecked)
				{
					if (childImageButton.Key == "Zoom")
					{
						ActiveTool = ZoomTool;
					}
					else if (childImageButton.Key == "Pan")
					{
						ActiveTool = PanTool;
					}
					else
					{
						ActiveTool = SelectTool;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			if (ActiveTool == null)
			{
				ActiveTool = DrawingTool;
			}
		}

		private void ImageButton_KeyChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.OriginalSource is ImageButton imageButton) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(imageButton.Key))
			{
				return;
			}
			object obj = TryFindResource(imageButton.Key);
			if (obj is Atom atom)
			{
				imageButton.ToolTip = atom.FullName;
			}
			else if (obj is Fragment fragment)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(fragment.ToolTip))
				{
					imageButton.ToolTip = fragment.ToolTip;
				}
				else
				{
					imageButton.ToolTip = fragment.Name;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ImageButton_IsCheckedChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(e.OriginalSource is ImageButton { IsChecked: not false } imageButton))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (PanelOfSelection.IsAncestorOf(imageButton))
			{
				if (imageButton.Key == "Zoom")
				{
					ActiveTool = ZoomTool;
				}
				else if (imageButton.Key == "Pan")
				{
					ActiveTool = PanTool;
				}
				else
				{
					ActiveTool = SelectTool;
				}
				EmptySelection(imageButton);
				return;
			}
			if (imageButton.Key.EndsWith("Charge"))
			{
				if (imageButton.Key == "IncreaseCharge")
				{
					ActiveTool = ChargeIncreaseTool;
				}
				else if (imageButton.Key == "DecreaseCharge")
				{
					ActiveTool = ChargeDecreaseTool;
				}
				EmptySelection(imageButton);
				return;
			}
			bool flag = ActiveTool != DrawingTool;
			ActiveTool = DrawingTool;
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfSelection))
			{
				childImageButton.IsChecked = false;
			}
			if (PanelOfBonds.IsAncestorOf(imageButton))
			{
				if (Editor != null)
				{
					Editor.ActiveBondType = (BondType)new BondTypeTypeConverter().ConvertFromString(imageButton.Key);
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton2 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfBonds))
				{
					if (childImageButton2 != imageButton)
					{
						childImageButton2.IsChecked = false;
					}
				}
				if (!(Editor != null && flag))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Editor.ActiveAtomKey))
				{
					foreach (ImageButton childImageButton3 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfAtoms))
					{
						if (childImageButton3.Key == Editor.ActiveAtomKey)
						{
							childImageButton3.IsChecked = true;
						}
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Editor.ActiveFragmentKey))
				{
					return;
				}
				{
					foreach (ImageButton childImageButton4 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfFragments))
					{
						if (childImageButton4.Key == Editor.ActiveFragmentKey)
						{
							childImageButton4.IsChecked = true;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			if (PanelOfAtoms.IsAncestorOf(imageButton))
			{
				if (Editor != null)
				{
					Editor.ActiveAtomKey = imageButton.Key;
					Editor.ActiveFragmentKey = string.Empty;
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton5 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfAtoms))
				{
					if (childImageButton5 != imageButton)
					{
						childImageButton5.IsChecked = false;
					}
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton6 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfFragments))
				{
					childImageButton6.IsChecked = false;
				}
			}
			else if (PanelOfFragments.IsAncestorOf(imageButton))
			{
				if (Editor != null)
				{
					Editor.ActiveAtomKey = string.Empty;
					Editor.ActiveFragmentKey = imageButton.Key;
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton7 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfAtoms))
				{
					childImageButton7.IsChecked = false;
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton8 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfFragments))
				{
					if (childImageButton8 != imageButton)
					{
						childImageButton8.IsChecked = false;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!(Editor != null && flag))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = new BondTypeTypeConverter().ConvertToString(Editor.ActiveBondType);
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton9 in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfBonds))
			{
				if (childImageButton9.Key == text)
				{
					childImageButton9.IsChecked = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private IEnumerable<ImageButton> GetChildImageButtons(System.Windows.Controls.Panel panel)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (UIElement child in panel.Children)
			{
				if (typeof(ImageButton).IsInstanceOfType(child))
				{
					yield return child as ImageButton;
				}
			}
		}

		private void RegisterDrawingCanvas(IDrawingCanvasTool oldTool, IDrawingCanvasTool newTool)
		{
			if (Editor != null)
			{
				oldTool?.UnregisterDrawingCanvas(Editor);
				newTool?.RegisterDrawingCanvas(Editor);
			}
		}

		private static void OnDrawingCanvasPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Toolbox toolbox = d as Toolbox;
			if (toolbox.Editor != null)
			{
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton in toolbox.GetChildImageButtons(toolbox.PanelOfBonds))
				{
					if (childImageButton.IsChecked && !childImageButton.Key.Contains("Charge"))
					{
						toolbox.Editor.ActiveBondType = (BondType)new BondTypeTypeConverter().ConvertFromString(childImageButton.Key);
						break;
					}
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton2 in toolbox.GetChildImageButtons(toolbox.PanelOfAtoms))
				{
					if (childImageButton2.IsChecked)
					{
						toolbox.Editor.ActiveAtomKey = childImageButton2.Key;
						break;
					}
				}
				foreach (ImageButton childImageButton3 in toolbox.GetChildImageButtons(toolbox.PanelOfFragments))
				{
					if (childImageButton3.IsChecked)
					{
						toolbox.Editor.ActiveFragmentKey = childImageButton3.Key;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (e.OldValue is MolecularDrawingCanvas molecularDrawingCanvas)
			{
				molecularDrawingCanvas.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown -= toolbox.PrimaryLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
				molecularDrawingCanvas.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.PreviewKeyDown -= toolbox.PrimaryLayer_PreviewKeyDown;
			}
			if (e.NewValue is MolecularDrawingCanvas molecularDrawingCanvas2)
			{
				molecularDrawingCanvas2.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown += toolbox.PrimaryLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
				molecularDrawingCanvas2.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.PreviewKeyDown += toolbox.PrimaryLayer_PreviewKeyDown;
			}
			toolbox.RegisterDrawingCanvas(toolbox.ActiveTool, toolbox.ActiveTool);
		}

		private void PrimaryLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!SelectButton.IsChecked)
			{
				SelectButton.IsChecked = true;
			}
		}

		private void PrimaryLayer_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.A && (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl)) && !SelectButton.IsChecked)
			{
				SelectButton.IsChecked = true;
				Editor.Diagram.SelectAll();
			}
		}

		private void ButtonAtomMore_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			PeriocTableWindow periocTableWindow = new PeriocTableWindow(Editor.ActiveAtomKey)
			{
				Owner = Window.GetWindow(this)
			};
			bool? flag = periocTableWindow.ShowDialog();
			if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.HasValue || string.IsNullOrEmpty(periocTableWindow.SelectedResourceKey) || periocTableWindow.SelectedImageSource == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ImageButton childImageButton in GetChildImageButtons(PanelOfAtoms))
			{
				if (childImageButton.Key == periocTableWindow.SelectedResourceKey)
				{
					childImageButton.IsChecked = true;
					return;
				}
			}
			_additionalAtomButtomIndex = (_additionalAtomButtomIndex + 1) % 2;
			if (_additionalAtomButtomIndex == 0)
			{
				RandomAtom1.Key = periocTableWindow.SelectedResourceKey;
				RandomAtom1.Source = periocTableWindow.SelectedImageSource;
				RandomAtom1.IsChecked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				RandomAtom2.Key = periocTableWindow.SelectedResourceKey;
				RandomAtom2.Source = periocTableWindow.SelectedImageSource;
				RandomAtom2.IsChecked = true;
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing;component/toolbox.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		internal Delegate _CreateDelegate(Type delegateType, string handler)
		{
			return Delegate.CreateDelegate(delegateType, this, handler);
		}

		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				PanelOfSelection = (StackPanel)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				SelectButton = (ImageButton)target;
				break;
			case 3:
				PanelOfBonds = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				buttonSingleBond = (ImageButton)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				PanelOfAtoms = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				buttonCarbon = (ImageButton)target;
				break;
			case 7:
				RandomAtom1 = (ImageButton)target;
				break;
			case 8:
				RandomAtom2 = (ImageButton)target;
				break;
			case 9:
				ButtonAtomMore = (HalfEnabledButton)target;
				break;
			case 10:
				PanelOfFragments = (UniformGrid)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

	public class MainWindow : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private string HelpFile = "cmpnts.chm";

		private const int HelpCode = 14;

		private const double DefaultDpi = 96.0;

		private static readonly string MoleFileFilter = string.Format("{0} {1}", AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.Molfiles_Filter, "(*.mol)|*.mol");

		private static readonly string SmilesFileFilter = string.Format("{0} {1}", AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.SMILES_Files_Filter, "(*.smi)|*.smi");

		private static readonly string MolAndSmilesFilter = string.Format("{0} {1}", AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.Molecule_Files_Filter, "(*.mol, *.smi)|*.mol;*.smi");

		private string _currentFile;

		private string _externalFragmentName;

		private bool _hasImported;

		private readonly bool _openedIndivitually;

		internal ToggleButton EraseButton;

		internal ToggleButton FlipButton;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox ZoomRatioComboBox;

		internal Toolbox Toolbox;

		internal MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string ExternalFragmentName
		{
			get
			{
				return _externalFragmentName;
			}
			private set
			{
				_externalFragmentName = value;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_externalFragmentName))
				{
					base.Title = AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.MainWindow_Title;
				}
				else
				{
					base.Title = _externalFragmentName + " - " + AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.MainWindow_Title;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsForTDE { get; private set; }

		public string CurrentFile
		{
			get

				return _currentFile;
			}
			private set
			{
				_currentFile = value;
				base.Title = GetShortFileName(_currentFile) + " - " + AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.MainWindow_Title;
			}

		public bool IsDrawingCanvasDirty
		{
			get
			{
				if (DrawingCanvas != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram.TransactionManager != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram.TransactionManager.IsDirty)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public MainWindow()
		{
			_openedIndivitually = true;
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		public MainWindow(string externalFragmentName, string[] molefileContentAsLines, bool isForTDE, bool shouldCheckConsistencyWhenClosing)
		{
			ExternalFragmentName = externalFragmentName;
			ImportFragmentLines = molefileContentAsLines;
			IsForTDE = isForTDE;
			ShouldCheckConsistencyWhenClosing = shouldCheckConsistencyWhenClosing;
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.Title = AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.MainWindow_Title;
			base.Loaded += MainWindow_Loaded;
			Toolbox.ActiveToolChanged += Toolbox_ActiveToolChanged;
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ZoomScaleChanged += Diagram_ZoomScaleChanged;
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyUp(System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				Toolbox.ResetToDrawing();
			}
			base.OnKeyUp(e);
		}

		protected override void OnClosing(CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnClosing(e);
			if (_openedIndivitually && IsDrawingCanvasDirty)
			{
				switch (MDMessageBox.Show(this, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Closing_SaveChanges, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Title, MDMessageBoxButton.YesNoCancel))
				{
				case MDMessageBoxResult.Cancel:
					e.Cancel = true;
					return;
				case MDMessageBoxResult.Yes:
					if (!TrySaveChanges())
					{
						e.Cancel = true;
						return;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			Fragment fragment = DrawingCanvas.ExportFragment();
			ConsistencyOption consistency = fragment.Consistency;
			if (ShouldCheckConsistencyWhenClosing && IsDrawingCanvasDirty && (consistency == ConsistencyOption.BondInconsistent || consistency == ConsistencyOption.Disconnected) && MDMessageBox.Show(this, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Save_FragmentIsNotDone, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Title, MDMessageBoxButton.YesNo) != MDMessageBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				e.Cancel = true;
				return;
			}
			fragment.Name = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ExternalFragmentName) ? FragmentWriter.GetFragmentNameByAtoms(fragment, DrawingCanvas) : ExternalFragmentName);
			ExportFragmentLines = FragmentWriter.Save(fragment, DrawingCanvas);
			FragmentConsistency = consistency;
		}

		private void MainWindow_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsForTDE)
			{
				HalfEnabledBorder.SetIsHalfEnabled(Toolbox.ButtonAtomMore, isHalfEnabled: true);
			}
			TryLoadImportedFragments();
			UpdateZoomRatioComboBox();
		}

		private void Toolbox_ActiveToolChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs<IDrawingCanvasTool> e)
		{
			EraseButton.IsChecked = Toolbox.ActiveTool == Toolbox.EraseTool;
			FlipButton.IsChecked = Toolbox.ActiveTool == Toolbox.FlipTool;
		}

		private void Diagram_ZoomScaleChanged(object sender, ZoomScaleChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateZoomRatioComboBox();
		}

		private void UpdateZoomRatioComboBox()
		{
			if (DrawingCanvas != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram != null)
			{
				double num = DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ZoomScale * 100.0;
				ZoomRatioComboBox.Text = num.ToString("##.##") + "%";
			}
		}

		private void ZoomRatioComboBox_KeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				ZoomFromComboBox(ZoomRatioComboBox.Text);
			}
		}

		private void ZoomRatioComboBox_LostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ZoomFromComboBox(ZoomRatioComboBox.Text);
		}

		private void ZoomRatioComboBox_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ZoomFromComboBox(((ComboBoxItem)ZoomRatioComboBox.SelectedItem).Content.ToString());
		}

		private void ZoomFromComboBox(string comboValue)
		{
			if (DrawingCanvas == null || DrawingCanvas.Diagram == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(comboValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			comboValue = comboValue.Trim();
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(comboValue))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (comboValue == MolecularDrawingCommands.ZoomToFit.Text)
			{
				DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ZoomFitToScreen();
				return;
			}
			if (comboValue == MolecularDrawingCommands.ZoomToActualSize.Text)
			{
				DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ZoomActualSize();
				return;
			}
			bool flag = comboValue.EndsWith("%");
			string s = (flag ? comboValue.Substring(0, comboValue.Length - 1) : comboValue);
			double result = 0.0;
			if (double.TryParse(s, out result))
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					ZoomRatioComboBox.Text += "%";
				}
				result /= 100.0;
				DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ZoomToScale(result, new System.Windows.Point(DrawingCanvas.Diagram.HorizontalOffset + DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ViewportWidth / 2.0, DrawingCanvas.Diagram.VerticalOffset + DrawingCanvas.Diagram.ViewportHeight / 2.0));
			}
		}

		private void TryLoadImportedFragments()
		{
			if (_hasImported)
			{
				return;
			}
			_hasImported = true;
			if (ImportFragmentLines == null || ImportFragmentLines.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Fragment fragment = FragmentReader.Read(ImportFragmentLines, DrawingCanvas);
				if (fragment != null)
				{
					DrawingCanvas.ImportFragment(fragment);
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ExternalFragmentName))
				{
					ExternalFragmentName = FragmentWriter.GetFragmentNameByAtoms(fragment, DrawingCanvas);
				}
			}
			catch (FileFormatException ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateActiveToolFromToggleButton(ToggleButton button, IDrawingCanvasTool tool)
		{
			if (button != null)
			{
				if (button.IsChecked.HasValue && button.IsChecked.Value)
				{
					Toolbox.EmptySelection();
					Toolbox.ActiveTool = tool;
				}
				else
				{
					Toolbox.ResetToSelect();
				}
			}
		}

		private static string GetShortFileName(string filePath)
		{
			string text = filePath.Substring(filePath.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1);
			return text.Substring(0, text.LastIndexOf('.'));
		}

		private void Open_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = true;
		}

		private void Open_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsDrawingCanvasDirty && MDMessageBox.Show(this, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Open_FileChangedQuestion, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Title, MDMessageBoxButton.YesNo) != MDMessageBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				return;
			}
			Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog.RestoreDirectory = true;
			openFileDialog.Filter = MolAndSmilesFilter + "|" + MoleFileFilter + "|" + SmilesFileFilter;
			openFileDialog.ValidateNames = true;
			bool? flag = openFileDialog.ShowDialog(this);
			if (flag.HasValue && flag.HasValue && File.Exists(openFileDialog.FileName))
			{
				try
				{
					Fragment fragment = FragmentReader.Read(openFileDialog.FileName, DrawingCanvas);
					DrawingCanvas.ImportFragment(fragment);
					CurrentFile = openFileDialog.FileName;
					WasEverImportedAfterLoaded = true;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					MDMessageBox.Show(this, string.Format(AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Open_LoadingErrorMessage, openFileDialog.FileName), AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Title);
				}
			}
		}

		private void Save_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = DrawingCanvas != null && DrawingCanvas.Elements.Count > 0;
		}

		private void Save_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			TrySaveChanges();
		}

		private bool TrySaveChanges()
		{
			Fragment fragment = DrawingCanvas.ExportFragment();
			if (fragment.Consistency != ConsistencyOption.Done && MDMessageBox.Show(this, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Save_FragmentIsNotDone, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Title, MDMessageBoxButton.YesNo) != MDMessageBoxResult.Yes)
			{
				return false;
			}
			System.Windows.Forms.SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog.Filter = MoleFileFilter;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(CurrentFile))
			{
				saveFileDialog.FileName = CurrentFile;
			}
			saveFileDialog.RestoreDirectory = true;
			if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog().Equals(System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK))
			{
				fragment.Name = FragmentWriter.GetFragmentNameByAtoms(fragment, DrawingCanvas);
				FragmentWriter.Save(fragment, saveFileDialog.FileName, DrawingCanvas);
				DrawingCanvas.Diagram.TransactionManager.ResetIsDirty();
				CurrentFile = saveFileDialog.FileName;
				WasEverSaved = true;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void Print_CanExecute(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = DrawingCanvas != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram != null && DrawingCanvas.Diagram.DiagramElements.Count > 0;
		}

		private void Print_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			PrintDocument printDocument = new PrintDocument();
			printDocument.PrintPage += printDocument_PrintPage;
			if (new System.Windows.Forms.PrintDialog
			{
				Document = printDocument
			}.ShowDialog() == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK)
			{
				printDocument.Print();
			}
		}

		private void printDocument_PrintPage(object sender, PrintPageEventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				System.Windows.Size size;
				DrawingVisual visual = DrawingCanvas.CreateVectorImage(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White, new Thickness(20.0), out size);
				RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)size.Width, (int)size.Height, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Default);
				renderTargetBitmap.Render(visual);
				using MemoryStream stream = new MemoryStream();
				BmpBitmapEncoder bmpBitmapEncoder = new BmpBitmapEncoder();
				bmpBitmapEncoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(renderTargetBitmap));
				bmpBitmapEncoder.Save(stream);
				Bitmap image = new Bitmap(stream);
				float num = Math.Min((float)size.Width, e.MarginBounds.Width);
				float num2 = Math.Min((float)size.Height, e.MarginBounds.Height);
				if (size.Width / size.Height >= (double)(num / num2))
				{
					e.Graphics.DrawImage(image, e.MarginBounds.Left, e.MarginBounds.Top, num, num * (float)(size.Height / size.Width));
				}
				else
				{
					e.Graphics.DrawImage(image, e.MarginBounds.Left, e.MarginBounds.Top, num2 * (float)(size.Width / size.Height), num2);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				MDMessageBox.Show(this, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Print_ErrorMessage + "\n" + ex.Message, AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.FileDialog_Print_Title);
			}
		}

		private void Help_Executed(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			string location = GetType().Assembly.Location;
			location = location.Substring(0, location.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1);
			_ = location + HelpFile;
			SystemEnvironment.ShowHtmlHelp(HelpFile, 14);
		}

				e.Handled = true;
			}
			base.OnPreviewKeyDown(e);
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Open_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Open_Executed;
				break;
			case 2:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Save_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Save_Executed;
				break;
			case 3:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Print_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Print_Executed;
				break;
			case 4:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Help_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Help_Executed;
				break;
			case 5:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Clear_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Clear_Executed;
				break;
			case 6:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += FlipOnAtomOrBond_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += FlipOnAtomOrBond_Executed;
				break;
			case 7:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += ZoomIn_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += ZoomIn_Executed;
				break;
			case 8:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += ZoomOut_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += ZoomOut_Executed;
				break;
			case 9:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += ZoomToFit_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += ZoomToFit_Executed;
				break;
			case 10:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += ZoomToActualSize_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += ZoomToActualSize_Executed;
				break;
			case 11:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += Erase_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += Erase_Executed;
				break;
			case 12:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += IncreaseCharge_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += IncreaseCharge_Executed;
				break;
			case 13:
				((CommandBinding)target).CanExecute += DecreaseCharge_CanExecute;
				((CommandBinding)target).Executed += DecreaseCharge_Executed;
				break;
			case 14:
				EraseButton = (ToggleButton)target;
				break;
			case 15:
				FlipButton = (ToggleButton)target;
				break;
			case 16:
				ZoomRatioComboBox = (System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox)target;
				ZoomRatioComboBox.KeyUp += ZoomRatioComboBox_KeyUp;
				ZoomRatioComboBox.LostKeyboardFocus += ZoomRatioComboBox_LostKeyboardFocus;
				ZoomRatioComboBox.SelectionChanged += ZoomRatioComboBox_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			case 17:
				Toolbox = (Toolbox)target;
				break;
			case 18:
				DrawingCanvas = (MolecularDrawingCanvas)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ShowRulersConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if ((RulerOption)value != RulerOption.None)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if ((bool)value)
			{
				return RulerOption.All;
			}
			return RulerOption.None;
		}
	}

		public BondCollection Bonds => _bonds;

		public event EventHandler<PropertyChangedEventArgs<int>> HydrogenCountChanged;

		public event EventHandler<PropertyChangedEventArgs<int>> AvailableLinksCountChanged;

		public Atom()
		{
			Bonds.CollectionChanged += Bonds_CollectionChanged;
		}

		protected virtual void OnBondChanged()
		{
			if (Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				Valence = CalculateMostProperValence();
				HydrogenCount = CalculateHydrogenCount();
				AvailableLinksCount = CalculateAvailableLinksCount();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnTypeChanged(ParticleType oldType, ParticleType newType)
		{
			if (Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				HydrogenCount = CalculateHydrogenCount();
				AvailableLinksCount = CalculateAvailableLinksCount();
			}
			else
			{
				ChargeCount = Valence;
				HydrogenCount = 0;
				AvailableLinksCount = 1;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnValenceChanged(int oldValence, int newValence)
		{
			if (Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				HydrogenCount = CalculateHydrogenCount();
				AvailableLinksCount = CalculateAvailableLinksCount();
			}
			else
			{
				ChargeCount = Valence;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnChargeCountChanged(int oldChargeCount, int newChargeCount)
		{
			if (Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				HydrogenCount = CalculateHydrogenCount();
				AvailableLinksCount = CalculateAvailableLinksCount();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHydrogenCountChanged(int oldHydrogenCount, int newHydrogenCount)
		{
			if (this.HydrogenCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.HydrogenCountChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs<int>(oldHydrogenCount, newHydrogenCount));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAvailableLinksCountChanged(int oldAvailableLinksCount, int newAvailableLinksCount)
		{
			if (this.AvailableLinksCountChanged != null)
			{
				this.AvailableLinksCountChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs<int>(oldAvailableLinksCount, newAvailableLinksCount));
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsFlexibleChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				HydrogenCount = CalculateHydrogenCount();
				AvailableLinksCount = CalculateAvailableLinksCount();
			}
		}

		private int CalculateMostProperValence()
		{
			if (CalculateAvailableLinksCount() < 0)
			{
				foreach (double possibleValence in PossibleValences)
				{
					if (possibleValence > 0.0 && possibleValence - (double)Math.Abs(ChargeCount) - (double)Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds >= 0.0)
					{
						return (int)possibleValence;
					}
					if (possibleValence < 0.0 && 0.0 - possibleValence + (double)ChargeCount - (double)Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds >= 0.0)
					{
						return (int)possibleValence;
					}
				}
			}
			return Valence;
		}

		private int CalculateHydrogenCount()
		{
			if (IsFlexible)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (Valence == 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (Valence > 0)
			{
				return Math.Max(0, Valence - Math.Abs(ChargeCount) - Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds);
			}
			return Math.Max(0, -Valence + ChargeCount - Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds);
		}

		private int CalculateAvailableLinksCount()
		{
			if (IsFlexible)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			if (Valence >= 0)
			{
				return Valence - Math.Abs(ChargeCount) - Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds;
			}
			return -Valence + ChargeCount - Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds;
		}

		private void Bonds_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (Bond bond in Bonds)
			{
				bond.TypeChanged -= bond_TypeChanged;
				bond.TypeChanged += bond_TypeChanged;
			}
			OnBondChanged();
		}

		private void bond_TypeChanged(object sender, BondTypeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			OnBondChanged();
		}

		private static object OnPossibleValencesPropertyCoerced(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			if (value is DoubleCollection source)
			{
				List<double> list = source.ToList();
				if (!list.Contains(0.0))
				{
					list.Add(0.0);
				}
				list.Sort();
				DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
				{
					foreach (double item in list)
					{
						doubleCollection.Add(item);
					}
					return doubleCollection;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

			(d as Atom).OnTypeChanged((ParticleType)e.OldValue, (ParticleType)e.NewValue);
		}

		}

		}

	[TypeConverter(typeof(BondTypeTypeConverter))]
	public sealed class BondType
	{
		public static readonly BondType Single = new BondType(1, BondGeometry.SingleLine);

		public static readonly BondType Double = new BondType(2, BondGeometry.DoubleLine);

		public static readonly BondType Tripple = new BondType(3, BondGeometry.TrippleLine);

		public static readonly BondType UpStereo = new BondType(1, BondGeometry.UpStereo);

		public static readonly BondType DownStereo = new BondType(1, BondGeometry.DownStereo);

		public int Valence { get; private set; }

		public BondGeometry GeometryType { get; private set; }

		private BondType(int valence, BondGeometry geometryType)
		{
			Valence = valence;
			GeometryType = geometryType;
		}
	}
	public class BondTypeTypeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, BondType> _bondTypes;

		static BondTypeTypeConverter()
		{
			_bondTypes = new Dictionary<string, BondType>();
			_bondTypes.Add("Single", BondType.Single);
			_bondTypes.Add("Double", BondType.Double);
			_bondTypes.Add("Tripple", BondType.Tripple);
			_bondTypes.Add("UpStereo", BondType.UpStereo);
			_bondTypes.Add("DownStereo", BondType.DownStereo);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (sourceType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			string text = value as string;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && _bondTypes.ContainsKey(text))
			{
				return _bondTypes[text];
			}
			return base.ConvertFrom(context, culture, value);
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			BondType bondType = value as BondType;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, BondType> bondType2 in _bondTypes)
			{
				if (bondType == bondType2.Value)
				{
					return bondType2.Key;
				}
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
		}
	}
	public class BondTypeChangedEventArgs : EventArgs

			NewValue = newValue;
		}
	}

		private static void OnStartAtomPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Bond item = d as Bond;
			if (e.OldValue is Atom atom && atom.Bonds.Contains(item))
			{
				atom.Bonds.Remove(item);
			}
			if (e.NewValue is Atom atom2 && !atom2.Bonds.Contains(item))
			{
				atom2.Bonds.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private static void OnEndAtomPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Bond item = d as Bond;
			if (e.OldValue is Atom atom && atom.Bonds.Contains(item))
			{
				atom.Bonds.Remove(item);
			}
			if (e.NewValue is Atom atom2 && !atom2.Bonds.Contains(item))
			{
				atom2.Bonds.Add(item);
			}
		}
