			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(Comment3Property, value);
			}
		}

		public double AngleToDockAtom
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(AngleToDockAtomProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AngleToDockAtomProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double AngleToDockBond
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(AngleToDockBondProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AngleToDockBondProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldDockBond
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShouldDockBondProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShouldDockBondProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldAdjustBonds
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShouldAdjustBondsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShouldAdjustBondsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public AtomCollection Atoms => _atoms;

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public BondCollection Bonds => _bonds;

		public ConsistencyOption Consistency
		{
			get
			{
				UpdateConnections();
				if (Atoms.Count == 0)
				{
					return ConsistencyOption.Empty;
				}
				foreach (Atom atom in Atoms)
				{
					if (atom.AvailableLinksCount < 0)
					{
						return ConsistencyOption.BondInconsistent;
					}
				}
				if (!ValenceHelper.IsFragmentWholyConnected(this))
				{
					return ConsistencyOption.Disconnected;
				}
				return ConsistencyOption.Done;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateConnections()
		{
			foreach (Bond bond in Bonds)
			{
				int startAtomIndex = GetStartAtomIndex(bond);
				if (startAtomIndex >= 0 && startAtomIndex < Atoms.Count)
				{
					bond.StartAtom = Atoms[startAtomIndex];
				}
				int endAtomIndex = GetEndAtomIndex(bond);
				if (endAtomIndex >= 0 && endAtomIndex < Atoms.Count)
				{
					bond.EndAtom = Atoms[endAtomIndex];
				}
			}
		}

		public static AtomPosition GetPosition(Atom atom)
		{
			if (atom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atom");
			}
			return (AtomPosition)atom.GetValue(PositionProperty);
		}

		public static void SetPosition(Atom atom, AtomPosition position)
		{
			if (atom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atom");
			}
			atom.SetValue(PositionProperty, position);
		}

		public static int GetStartAtomIndex(Bond bond)
		{
			if (bond == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond");
			}
			return (int)bond.GetValue(StartAtomIndexProperty);
		}

		public static void SetStartAtomIndex(Bond bond, int atomIndex)
		{
			if (bond == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond");
			}
			bond.SetValue(StartAtomIndexProperty, atomIndex);
		}

		public static int GetEndAtomIndex(Bond bond)
		{
			if (bond == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond");
			}
			return (int)bond.GetValue(EndAtomIndexProperty);
		}

		public static void SetEndAtomIndex(Bond bond, int atomIndex)
		{
			if (bond == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond");
			}
			bond.SetValue(EndAtomIndexProperty, atomIndex);
		}

		object ICloneable.Clone()
		{
			return Clone();
		}

		public Fragment Clone()
		{
			return XamlReader.Parse(XamlWriter.Save(this)) as Fragment;
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Tools
{
	public abstract class EditorChargeTool : DiagramTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		private class EditorChargeToolHelper
		{
			private const double MinorDraggingDistance = 5.0;

			private readonly EditorChargeTool _editorChargeTool;

			public Diagram Diagram => _editorChargeTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram;

			public bool MouseDownClicked { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseDownPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseDownElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseUpPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseUpElement { get; set; }

			public EditorChargeToolHelper(EditorChargeTool editorChargeTool)
			{
				_editorChargeTool = editorChargeTool;
			}

			public AtomElement GetClickedAtomElement()
			{
				if (MouseUpElement == MouseDownElement)
				{
					return MouseUpElement as AtomElement;
				}
				return null;
			}

			public void TryCaptureMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured == null)
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null)
					{
						MouseDownElement.CaptureMouse();
					}
					else
					{
						Diagram.PrimaryLayer.CaptureMouse();
					}
				}
			}

			public void TryReleaseMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured != null)
				{
					Mouse.Captured.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				MouseDownClicked = false;
				MouseDownPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseDownElement = null;
				MouseUpPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseUpElement = null;
			}
		}

		private const int MinChargeCount = -8;

		private const int MaxChargeCount = 8;

		private readonly EditorChargeToolHelper _helper;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		public int ChargeIncreasement { get; private set; }

		protected EditorChargeTool(int chargeIncreasement)
		{
			ChargeIncreasement = chargeIncreasement;
			_helper = new EditorChargeToolHelper(this);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_helper.MouseDownElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_helper.MouseDownPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_helper.TryCaptureMouse();
			_helper.MouseDownClicked = true;
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_helper.MouseUpElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_helper.MouseUpPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			if (_helper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				AtomElement clickedAtomElement = _helper.GetClickedAtomElement();
				if (clickedAtomElement != null)
				{
					Atom atom = clickedAtomElement.Atom;
					if (atom != null)
					{
						CycleIncreaseCharge(atom);
					}
				}
				_helper.Reset();
			}
			_helper.TryReleaseMouse();
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
		}

		private void CycleIncreaseCharge(Atom atom)
		{
			if (atom.Type == ParticleType.Atom)
			{
				int num = Math.Abs(atom.Valence) - Math.Abs(atom.Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds);
				int temp = atom.ChargeCount + ChargeIncreasement;
				if (temp < -num)
				{
					temp = num;
				}
				else if (temp > num)
				{
					temp = -num;
				}
				int oldChargeCount = atom.ChargeCount;
				atom.ChargeCount = temp;
				using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(DrawingCanvas.Diagram);
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					atom.ChargeCount = oldChargeCount;
				}, delegate
				{
					atom.ChargeCount = temp;
				});
				return;
			}
			if (atom.PossibleValences == null || atom.PossibleValences.Count <= 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<int> list = (from x in atom.PossibleValences
				orderby x
				select (int)x).ToList();
			int temp2 = atom.Valence + ChargeIncreasement;
			if (!list.Contains(temp2))
			{
				if (temp2 < list[0])
				{
					temp2 = list[list.Count - 1];
				}
				else if (temp2 > list[list.Count - 1])
				{
					temp2 = list[0];
				}
				else
				{
					for (int num2 = 0; num2 < list.Count - 1; num2++)
					{
						if (temp2 > list[num2] && temp2 < list[num2 + 1])
						{
							if (ChargeIncreasement > 0)
							{
								temp2 = list[num2 + 1];
							}
							else
							{
								temp2 = list[num2];
							}
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			int oldValence = atom.Valence;
			atom.Valence = temp2;
			using TransactionContext transactionContext2 = new TransactionContext(DrawingCanvas.Diagram);
			transactionContext2.AddChangeRecord(delegate
			{
				atom.Valence = oldValence;
			}, delegate
			{
				atom.Valence = temp2;
			});
		}

		public void RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		public void UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			_helper.Reset();
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}

		}
	}

			: base(-1)

		}
	}
	public class EditorEraseTool : DiagramTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		private class EraseHelper
		{
			private const double MinorDraggingDistance = 5.0;

			private readonly EditorEraseTool _editorEraseTool;

			public Diagram Diagram => _editorEraseTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram;

			public bool MouseDownClicked { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseDownPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseDownElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseUpPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseUpElement { get; set; }

			public EraseHelper(EditorEraseTool editorEraseTool)
			{
				_editorEraseTool = editorEraseTool;
			}

			public IEnumerable<UIElement> GetSelectedElementsToErase()
			{
				if ((MouseUpPosition - MouseDownPosition).Length <= 5.0)
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null)
					{
						return new UIElement[1] { MouseDownElement };
					}
					return new List<UIElement>();
				}
				return _editorEraseTool._draggingHelper.ContainedElements;
			}

			public void TryCaptureMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured == null)
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null)
					{
						MouseDownElement.CaptureMouse();
					}
					else
					{
						Diagram.PrimaryLayer.CaptureMouse();
					}
				}
			}

			public void TryReleaseMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured != null)
				{
					Mouse.Captured.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				MouseDownClicked = false;
				MouseDownPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseDownElement = null;
				MouseUpPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseUpElement = null;
			}
		}

		private class DraggingHelper
		{
			private readonly EditorEraseTool _editorEraseTool;

			private readonly UIElement _rectangle;

			public Diagram Diagram => _editorEraseTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram;

			public IEnumerable<UIElement> ContainedElements
			{
				get
				{
					Rect rect = new Rect(Diagram.GetElementPosition(_rectangle), _rectangle.RenderSize);
					foreach (UIElement element in Diagram.DiagramElements)
					{
						if (element is AtomElement && rect.Contains(Diagram.GetElementPosition(element)))
						{
							yield return element;
						}
						if (element is BondElement bondElement && rect.Contains(new Rect(bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint, bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint)))
						{
							yield return element;
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public DraggingHelper(EditorEraseTool editorEraseTool)
			{
				_editorEraseTool = editorEraseTool;
				_rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
				{
					Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
					StrokeThickness = 1.0,
					StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection(new double[2] { 4.0, 1.0 })
				};
			}

			public void Update(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
			{
				Rect rect = new Rect(startPoint, endPoint);
				if (!Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rectangle))
				{
					Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_rectangle, rect.TopLeft);
				}
				Diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_rectangle, rect.TopLeft);
				Diagram.SetElementSize(_rectangle, rect.Size);
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				if (Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rectangle))
				{
					Diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_rectangle);
				}
			}
		}

		private static readonly System.Windows.Input.Cursor EraseCursor = new System.Windows.Input.Cursor(new MemoryStream(Resources.Erase));

		private readonly EraseHelper _eraseHelper;

		private readonly DraggingHelper _draggingHelper;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		public EditorEraseTool()
		{
			_eraseHelper = new EraseHelper(this);
			_draggingHelper = new DraggingHelper(this);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_eraseHelper.MouseDownElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_eraseHelper.MouseDownPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_eraseHelper.TryCaptureMouse();
			_eraseHelper.MouseDownClicked = true;
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_eraseHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				_draggingHelper.Update(_eraseHelper.MouseDownPosition, diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
			}
			base.OnMouseMove(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_eraseHelper.MouseUpElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_eraseHelper.MouseUpPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			if (_eraseHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				_draggingHelper.Update(_eraseHelper.MouseDownPosition, _eraseHelper.MouseUpPosition);
				List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>(_eraseHelper.GetSelectedElementsToErase());
				if (list.Count > 0)
				{
					using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram);
					try
					{
						foreach (UIElement item in list)
						{
							diagram.RemoveElement(item);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
						throw;
					}
				}
				_eraseHelper.Reset();
				_draggingHelper.Reset();
			}
			_eraseHelper.TryReleaseMouse();
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
		}

		public void RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.Cursor = EraseCursor;
		}

		public void UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.PrimaryLayer.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;
			_eraseHelper.Reset();
			_draggingHelper.Reset();
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}
	public class EditorFlipTool : DiagramTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		private UIElement _clickedElement;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_clickedElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_clickedElement != null && _clickedElement == e.GetDiagramElement())
			{
				FlipHelper.FlipOnElement(DrawingCanvas.Diagram, DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Selection, _clickedElement);
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
		}

		public void RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		public void UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}
	public class EditorDrawingTool : DiagramTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		private class FragmentHelper
		{
			private readonly EditorDrawingTool _drawingTool;

			private BondElement _tempBondElement;

			private BondElement _hostBondElement;

			private SideOption _lastDockSide;

			private string _lastFragmentKey;

			public FragmentPreviewElement FragmentElement { get; private set; }

			public FragmentHelper(EditorDrawingTool drawingTool)
			{
				_drawingTool = drawingTool;
			}

			public void InitializePreviewFragment()
			{
				if (FragmentElement == null || _lastFragmentKey != _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.ActiveFragmentKey)
				{
					Fragment fragment = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindResourceFragment();
					if (fragment != null)
					{
						FragmentElement = new FragmentPreviewElement();
						FragmentElement.LoadFragment(fragment, _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.DefaultBondLength);
						_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(FragmentElement);
						_lastFragmentKey = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.ActiveFragmentKey;
					}
				}
				else if (!_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(FragmentElement))
				{
					_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(FragmentElement);
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				if (FragmentElement != null)
				{
					CleanDockingAtomElement();
					CleanDockingBondElement();
					_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(FragmentElement);
					FragmentElement = null;
					_lastFragmentKey = string.Empty;
				}
			}

			public void MovePreviewTo(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				if (FragmentElement != null)
				{
					CleanDockingAtomElement();
					CleanDockingBondElement();
					FragmentElement.Update(_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.DefaultBondLength, 0.0);
					MovePreviewFragmentTo(position);
				}
			}

			public void DockToAtomElement(AtomElement ae)
			{
				if (FragmentElement != null && _tempBondElement == null)
				{
					CleanDockingBondElement();
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(ae);
					System.Windows.Point positionToExtendNewAtomElement = AngleHelper.GetPositionToExtendNewAtomElement(ae, _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.DefaultBondLength, _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.ActiveBondType);
					Vector toVector = positionToExtendNewAtomElement - elementPosition;
					double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(AngleHelper.GetVectorByAngle(FragmentElement.Fragment.AngleToDockAtom), toVector);
					FragmentElement.Update(_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.DefaultBondLength, angleBetween);
					MovePreviewFragmentTo(positionToExtendNewAtomElement);
					_tempBondElement = new BondElement
					{
						Bond = new Bond
						{
							Type = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.ActiveBondType
						},
						Opacity = 0.5,
						IsHitTestVisible = false,
						StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection(new double[2] { 4.0, 1.0 })
					};
					_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_tempBondElement);
					_tempBondElement.StartAnchor = new PointAnchor(elementPosition);
					_tempBondElement.EndAnchor = new PointAnchor(positionToExtendNewAtomElement);
				}
			}

			public void DockToBondElement(BondElement bondElement, System.Windows.Point point)
			{
				if (FragmentElement == null || !FragmentElement.Fragment.ShouldDockBond)
				{
					return;
				}
				SideOption pointSide = AngleHelper.GetPointSide(bondElement.CenterStartPoint, bondElement.CenterEndPoint, point);
				if (_hostBondElement != bondElement || _lastDockSide != pointSide)
				{
					CleanDockingAtomElement();
					Vector vector = bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
					if (pointSide == SideOption.Right)
					{
						FragmentElement.Update(vector.Length, FragmentElement.Fragment.AngleToDockBond + AngleHelper.GetAngle(vector));
						MovePreviewFragmentTo(bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint);
					}
					else
					{
						FragmentElement.Update(vector.Length, FragmentElement.Fragment.AngleToDockBond + AngleHelper.GetAngle(-vector));
						MovePreviewFragmentTo(bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint);
					}
					_hostBondElement = bondElement;
					_lastDockSide = pointSide;
				}
			}

			public void SnapFragmentElement(System.Windows.Point mousePosition)
			{
				if (FragmentElement == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				System.Windows.Point point = mousePosition - FragmentElement.FirstElementVector;
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement2 in FragmentElement.AtomElements)
				{
					System.Windows.Point atomElementPosition = FragmentElement.GetAtomElementPosition(atomElement2, point);
					AtomElement atomElement = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(atomElementPosition);
					if (atomElement != null)
					{
						Vector vector = Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement) - atomElementPosition;
						_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.PositionElement(FragmentElement, point + vector);
						break;
					}
				}
			}

			private void MovePreviewFragmentTo(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.PositionElement(FragmentElement, position - FragmentElement.FirstElementVector);
			}

			private void CleanDockingAtomElement()
			{
				if (_tempBondElement != null)
				{
					if (_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_tempBondElement))
					{
						_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_tempBondElement);
					}
					_tempBondElement = null;
				}
			}

			private void CleanDockingBondElement()
			{
				if (_hostBondElement != null)
				{
					_hostBondElement = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private class DrawingHelper
		{
			private BondElement _lastFilledBondElement;

			private readonly EditorDrawingTool _drawingTool;

			public bool MouseDownClicked { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseDownPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseDownElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseMovePosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseMoveElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseUpPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseUpElement { get; set; }

			public Vector MouseMovingDistance => MouseMovePosition - MouseDownPosition;

			public bool IsToAddNewFragmentIntoFreeSpace
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext && !IsToPreviewFragmentSnapped && IsInFreeSpace(MouseDownElement) && IsInFreeSpace(MouseUpElement) && MouseMovingDistance.Length < _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.MinorBondLength)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToPreviewFragmentDockAtAtomElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext && IsAtomElement(MouseMoveElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToDockFragmentAtAtomElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext && IsAtomElement(MouseUpElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToPreviewFragmentSnapped
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext)
					{
						FragmentPreviewElement fragmentElement = _drawingTool._fragmentHelper.FragmentElement;
						if (fragmentElement != null)
						{
							System.Windows.Point basePoint = MouseMovePosition - fragmentElement.FirstElementVector;
							foreach (AtomElement atomElement in fragmentElement.AtomElements)
							{
								System.Windows.Point atomElementPosition = fragmentElement.GetAtomElementPosition(atomElement, basePoint);
								if (_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(atomElementPosition) != null)
								{
									return true;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToPreviewFragmentDockAtBondElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext && IsBondElement(MouseMoveElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToDockFragmentAtBondElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsFragmentDrawingContext && IsBondElement(MouseUpElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToAddNewAtomElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsAtomDrawingContext && IsInFreeSpace(MouseDownElement) && IsInFreeSpace(MouseUpElement) && MouseMovingDistance.Length < _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.MinorBondLength)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToChangeAtom
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsAtomDrawingContext && IsAtomElement(MouseDownElement) && MouseDownElement == MouseUpElement)
					{
						AtomElement atomElement = MouseDownElement as AtomElement;
						if (atomElement.Atom != null)
						{
							Atom atom = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindResourceAtom();
							if (atom != null && atomElement.Atom.PeriodicTableId != atom.PeriodicTableId)
							{
								return true;
							}
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToIncreaseNeiborhood
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsAtomDrawingContext && IsAtomElement(MouseDownElement) && MouseDownElement == MouseUpElement)
					{
						AtomElement atomElement = MouseDownElement as AtomElement;
						if (atomElement.Atom != null)
						{
							Atom atom = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindResourceAtom();
							if (atom != null && atomElement.Atom.PeriodicTableId == atom.PeriodicTableId)
							{
								return true;
							}
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsToChangeBondType
			{
				get
				{
					if (_drawingTool.IsAtomDrawingContext && IsBondElement(MouseDownElement) && MouseDownElement == MouseUpElement)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsDragging
			{
				get
				{
					if (!MouseDownClicked)
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (!_drawingTool.IsAtomDrawingContext)
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (IsInFreeSpace(MouseDownElement))
					{
						return MouseMovingDistance.Length >= _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.MinorBondLength;
					}
					if (IsAtomElement(MouseDownElement))
					{
						if (!MouseDownElement.IsMouseOver)
						{
							return MouseMovingDistance.Length >= _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.MinorBondLength;
						}
						return false;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public DrawingHelper(EditorDrawingTool drawingTool)
			{
				_drawingTool = drawingTool;
			}

			public bool IsAtomElement(UIElement element)
			{
				if (element != null)
				{
					return element is AtomElement;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public bool IsBondElement(UIElement element)
			{
				if (element != null)
				{
					return element is BondElement;
				}
				return false;
			}

			public bool IsInFreeSpace(UIElement element)
			{
				if (element == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (element is AtomElement || element is BondElement)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}

			public void UpdateFillForBondElement()
			{
				if (MouseMoveElement != _lastFilledBondElement)
				{
					if (_lastFilledBondElement != null)
					{
						_lastFilledBondElement.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
						_lastFilledBondElement = null;
					}
					if (MouseMoveElement is BondElement bondElement)
					{
						bondElement.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.YellowGreen;
						_lastFilledBondElement = bondElement;
					}
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				MouseDownClicked = false;
				MouseDownPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseDownElement = null;
				MouseMovePosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseMoveElement = null;
				MouseUpPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseUpElement = null;
			}
		}

		private class DraggingHelper
		{
			private readonly EditorDrawingTool _drawingTool;

			public BondElement BondElement { get; private set; }

			public DraggingHelper(EditorDrawingTool drawingTool)
			{
				_drawingTool = drawingTool;
			}

			public void TryInitialize(AtomElement startAtomElement)
			{
				if (startAtomElement != null)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(startAtomElement);
					TryInitialize(elementPosition);
				}
			}

			public void TryInitialize(System.Windows.Point startPosition)
			{
				if (BondElement == null)
				{
					BondElement = new BondElement
					{
						Bond = new Bond
						{
							Type = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.ActiveBondType
						},
						IsHitTestVisible = false
					};
					_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(BondElement);
					BondElement.StartAnchor = new PointAnchor(startPosition);
				}
			}

			public void MoveEndTo(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				if (BondElement != null)
				{
					AtomElement atomElement = _drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(position);
					if (atomElement != null)
					{
						position = Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement);
					}
					if (BondElement.EndAnchor != null)
					{
						BondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteAnchorPoint = position;
						return;
					}
					BondElement.EndAnchor = new PointAnchor(position)
					{
						Consumer = BondElement
					};
				}
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				if (BondElement != null)
				{
					BondElement.StartAtomElement?.RemoveAnchor(BondElement.StartAnchor);
					_drawingTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(BondElement);
					BondElement = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private readonly DrawingHelper _drawingHelper;

		private readonly DraggingHelper _draggingHelper;

		private readonly FragmentHelper _fragmentHelper;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		private bool IsAtomDrawingContext => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingCanvas.ActiveAtomKey);

		private bool IsFragmentDrawingContext => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingCanvas.ActiveFragmentKey);

		public EditorDrawingTool()
		{
			_drawingHelper = new DrawingHelper(this);
			_draggingHelper = new DraggingHelper(this);
			_fragmentHelper = new FragmentHelper(this);
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			_drawingHelper.Reset();
			_draggingHelper.Reset();
			_fragmentHelper.Reset();
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
			_drawingHelper.MouseDownClicked = true;
			_drawingHelper.MouseDownElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_drawingHelper.MouseDownPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_drawingHelper.MouseMoveElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_drawingHelper.UpdateFillForBondElement();
			if (IsFragmentDrawingContext)
			{
				if (_drawingHelper.IsToPreviewFragmentDockAtAtomElement)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.DockToAtomElement(_drawingHelper.MouseMoveElement as AtomElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToPreviewFragmentDockAtBondElement)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.DockToBondElement(_drawingHelper.MouseMoveElement as BondElement, _drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToPreviewFragmentSnapped)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.SnapFragmentElement(_drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.MouseMoveElement == null)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.MovePreviewTo(_drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition);
				}
			}
			else if (IsAtomDrawingContext && _drawingHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				if (_drawingHelper.IsDragging)
				{
					if (_drawingHelper.IsInFreeSpace(_drawingHelper.MouseDownElement))
					{
						_draggingHelper.TryInitialize(_drawingHelper.MouseDownPosition);
					}
					else
					{
						_draggingHelper.TryInitialize(_drawingHelper.MouseDownElement as AtomElement);
					}
					_draggingHelper.MoveEndTo(_drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition);
				}
				else
				{
					_draggingHelper.Reset();
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseMove(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_drawingHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				DrawingHelper drawingHelper = _drawingHelper;
				UIElement mouseUpElement = (_drawingHelper.MouseMoveElement = e.GetDiagramElement());
				drawingHelper.MouseUpElement = mouseUpElement;
				DrawingHelper drawingHelper2 = _drawingHelper;
				System.Windows.Point mouseUpPosition = (_drawingHelper.MouseMovePosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
				drawingHelper2.MouseUpPosition = mouseUpPosition;
				if (_drawingHelper.IsToAddNewAtomElement)
				{
					AddNewAtomElementAtPosition(_drawingHelper.MouseUpPosition);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToChangeAtom)
				{
					TryChangeAtom(_drawingHelper.MouseDownElement as AtomElement, DrawingCanvas.ActiveAtomKey);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToIncreaseNeiborhood)
				{
					TryIncreaseNeiborhood(_drawingHelper.MouseDownElement as AtomElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsDragging)
				{
					_draggingHelper.MoveEndTo(_drawingHelper.MouseUpPosition);
					if (_draggingHelper.BondElement != null)
					{
						TryCreateConnection(_draggingHelper.BondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint, _draggingHelper.BondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint, _drawingHelper.MouseDownElement as AtomElement, _drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as AtomElement);
					}
					_draggingHelper.Reset();
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToChangeBondType)
				{
					TryChangeBondType(_drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as BondElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToAddNewFragmentIntoFreeSpace)
				{
					TryAddFragmentIntoDrawingCanvas(_fragmentHelper.FragmentElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToDockFragmentAtAtomElement)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.DockToAtomElement(_drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as AtomElement);
					TryDockFragmentAtAtomElement(_fragmentHelper.FragmentElement, _drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as AtomElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToDockFragmentAtBondElement)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.DockToBondElement(_drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as BondElement, _drawingHelper.MouseUpPosition);
					TryDockFragmentAtBondElement(_fragmentHelper.FragmentElement, _drawingHelper.MouseUpElement as BondElement);
				}
				else if (_drawingHelper.IsToPreviewFragmentSnapped)
				{
					_fragmentHelper.SnapFragmentElement(_drawingHelper.MouseUpPosition);
					TryAddFragmentIntoDrawingCanvas(_fragmentHelper.FragmentElement);
				}
				_drawingHelper.Reset();
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFragmentDrawingContext)
			{
				_fragmentHelper.InitializePreviewFragment();
			}
			base.OnMouseEnter(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (IsFragmentDrawingContext)
			{
				_fragmentHelper.Reset();
			}
			_drawingHelper.UpdateFillForBondElement();
			base.OnMouseLeave(diagram, e);
		}

		private AtomElement AddNewAtomElementAtPosition(System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			AtomElement atomElement = DrawingCanvas.CreateAtomElement();
			if (atomElement != null)
			{
				DrawingCanvas.AddAtomElement(atomElement, position);
			}
			return atomElement;
		}

		private void TryChangeAtom(AtomElement element, string atomKey)
		{
			if (element != null && element.Atom != null)
			{
				Atom atom = DrawingCanvas.FindResourceAtomWithKey(atomKey);
				if (atom != null && atom.AvailableLinksCount >= element.Atom.Bonds.TotalAsSingleBonds)
				{
					Atom atom2 = atom.Clone();
					DrawingCanvas.TryReplaceAtomForElement(element, atom2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void TryIncreaseNeiborhood(AtomElement element)
		{
			if (element != null)
			{
				DrawingCanvas.TryIncreaseNeiborhood(element);
			}
		}

		private void TryCreateConnection(System.Windows.Point startPosition, System.Windows.Point endPosition, AtomElement startAtomElement, AtomElement endAtomElement)
		{
			using (new TransactionContext(DrawingCanvas.Diagram))
			{
				if (startAtomElement == null)
				{
					AtomElement atomElement = DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(startPosition);
					startAtomElement = ((atomElement == null) ? AddNewAtomElementAtPosition(startPosition) : atomElement);
				}
				if (endAtomElement == null)
				{
					AtomElement atomElement2 = DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(endPosition);
					endAtomElement = ((atomElement2 == null) ? AddNewAtomElementAtPosition(endPosition) : atomElement2);
				}
				DrawingCanvas.ConnectAtomElements(startAtomElement, endAtomElement);
			}
		}

		private void TryChangeBondType(BondElement bondElement)
		{
			if (bondElement == null || bondElement.Bond == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (DrawingCanvas.ActiveBondType == BondType.UpStereo)
			{
				if (bondElement.Bond.Type != BondType.UpStereo)
				{
					DrawingCanvas.ChangeBondElementType(bondElement, BondType.UpStereo);
				}
				else
				{
					DrawingCanvas.InterChangeBondAtoms(bondElement);
				}
			}
			else if (DrawingCanvas.ActiveBondType == BondType.DownStereo)
			{
				if (bondElement.Bond.Type != BondType.DownStereo)
				{
					DrawingCanvas.ChangeBondElementType(bondElement, BondType.DownStereo);
				}
				else
				{
					DrawingCanvas.InterChangeBondAtoms(bondElement);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DrawingCanvas.IncreaseBondType(bondElement);
			}
		}

		private void TryAddFragmentIntoDrawingCanvas(FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement)
		{
			DrawingCanvas.AddFragmentElement(fragmentPreviewElement, Diagram.GetElementPosition(fragmentPreviewElement));
		}

		private void TryDockFragmentAtAtomElement(FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement, AtomElement element)
		{
			using (new TransactionContext(DrawingCanvas.Diagram))
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(fragmentPreviewElement);
				System.Windows.Point atomElementPosition = fragmentPreviewElement.GetAtomElementPosition(fragmentPreviewElement.AtomElements[0], elementPosition);
				DrawingCanvas.AddFragmentElement(fragmentPreviewElement, elementPosition);
				DrawingCanvas.ConnectAtomElements(element, DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(atomElementPosition));
			}
		}

		private void TryDockFragmentAtBondElement(FragmentPreviewElement fragmentPreviewElement, BondElement bondElement)
		{
			using (new TransactionContext(DrawingCanvas.Diagram))
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(fragmentPreviewElement);
				DrawingCanvas.AddFragmentElement(fragmentPreviewElement, elementPosition);
			}
		}
	}
	public class EditorPanTool : PanTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}
	public class EditorSelectTool : DiagramTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		private class SelectHelper
		{
			private const double MinorDraggingDistance = 5.0;

			private readonly EditorSelectTool _editorSelectTool;

			private readonly Dictionary<AtomElement, System.Windows.Point> _oldPositions = new Dictionary<AtomElement, System.Windows.Point>();

			public Diagram Diagram => _editorSelectTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram;

			public bool MouseDownClicked { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseDownPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseDownElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseMovePosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseMoveElement { get; set; }

			public System.Windows.Point MouseUpPosition { get; set; }

			public UIElement MouseUpElement { get; set; }

			public bool IsCtrlPressed
			{
				get
				{
					if (!Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl))
					{
						return Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl);
					}
					return true;
				}
			}

			public bool IsMovingSelection
			{
				get
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null && (MouseMovePosition - MouseDownPosition).Length >= 5.0)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsDraggingToSelect
			{
				get
				{
					if (MouseDownElement == null && (MouseMovePosition - MouseDownPosition).Length >= 5.0)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsSingleClickAtElement
			{
				get
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null && (MouseUpPosition - MouseDownPosition).Length < 5.0)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public bool IsSingleClickAtFreeSpace
			{
				get
				{
					if (MouseDownElement == null && (MouseUpPosition - MouseDownPosition).Length < 5.0)
					{
						return true;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}

			public SelectHelper(EditorSelectTool editorSelectTool)
			{
				_editorSelectTool = editorSelectTool;
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				_oldPositions.Clear();
				MouseDownClicked = false;
				MouseDownPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseDownElement = null;
				MouseMovePosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseMoveElement = null;
				MouseUpPosition = default(System.Windows.Point);
				MouseUpElement = null;
			}

			public void MoveElements(bool isPreview)
			{
				if (!Diagram.IsSelected(MouseDownElement))
				{
					Diagram.Select(MouseDownElement);
				}
				List<AtomElement> atomElements = Diagram.Selection.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
				if (_oldPositions.Count == 0)
				{
					foreach (BondElement item in Diagram.Selection.OfType<BondElement>().ToList())
					{
						if (!atomElements.Contains(item.StartAtomElement))
						{
							atomElements.Add(item.StartAtomElement);
						}
						if (!atomElements.Contains(item.EndAtomElement))
						{
							atomElements.Add(item.EndAtomElement);
						}
					}
					foreach (AtomElement item2 in atomElements)
					{
						_oldPositions.Add(item2, Diagram.GetElementPosition(item2));
					}
				}
				Vector vector = MouseMovePosition - MouseDownPosition;
				if (!IsCtrlPressed)
				{
					foreach (AtomElement item3 in atomElements)
					{
						System.Windows.Point point = _oldPositions[item3];
						System.Windows.Point newPosition = point + vector;
						AtomElement atomElement = FindAtomElementAt(newPosition, item3);
						if (atomElement != null)
						{
							vector = Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement) - point;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				using (new TransactionContext(Diagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty, 257))
				{
					foreach (AtomElement item4 in atomElements)
					{
						Diagram.PositionElement(item4, _oldPositions[item4] + vector);
					}
				}
				if (isPreview)
				{
					return;
				}
				using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(Diagram))
				{
					if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
					{
						transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
						{
							MoveElements(atomElements, -vector);
						}, delegate
						{
							MoveElements(atomElements, vector);
						});
					}
					foreach (AtomElement item5 in atomElements)
					{
						AtomElement atomElement2 = FindAtomElementAt(Diagram.GetElementPosition(item5), item5);
						if (atomElement2 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						foreach (BondElement item6 in item5.ConnectedBondElements.ToList())
						{
							if (item6.StartAtomElement == item5)
							{
								if (FindAtomElementAt(Diagram.GetElementPosition(item6.EndAtomElement), item6.EndAtomElement) == null)
								{
									item6.ConnectStartTo(atomElement2);
								}
							}
							else if (FindAtomElementAt(Diagram.GetElementPosition(item6.StartAtomElement), item6.StartAtomElement) == null)
							{
								item6.ConnectEndTo(atomElement2);
							}
						}
						Diagram.RemoveElement(item5);
					}
				}
				_oldPositions.Clear();
			}

			private AtomElement FindAtomElementAt(System.Windows.Point newPosition, AtomElement excluded)
			{
				foreach (AtomElement item in _editorSelectTool.DrawingCanvas.FindAtomElementsAt(newPosition))
				{
					if (item != excluded)
					{
						return item;
					}
				}
				return null;
			}

			private void MoveElements(List<AtomElement> atomElements, Vector vector)
			{
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement in atomElements)
				{
					Diagram.PositionElement(atomElement, Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement) + vector);
				}
			}

			public void TryCaptureMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured == null)
				{
					if (MouseDownElement != null)
					{
						MouseDownElement.CaptureMouse();
					}
					else
					{
						Diagram.PrimaryLayer.CaptureMouse();
					}
				}
			}

			public void TryReleaseMouse()
			{
				if (Mouse.Captured != null)
				{
					Mouse.Captured.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}

			public void SelectElementsByMousePosition(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				FindElementsCompositionBy(position)?.SelectMe(Diagram);
			}

			public void RevertSelectionByMousePosition(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				ElementsComposition elementsComposition = FindElementsCompositionBy(position);
				if (elementsComposition != null)
				{
					if (elementsComposition.HasSelection(Diagram))
					{
						elementsComposition.DeselectMe(Diagram);
					}
					else
					{
						elementsComposition.SelectMe(Diagram);
					}
				}
			}

			private ElementsComposition FindElementsCompositionBy(System.Windows.Point position)
			{
				List<AtomElement> list = Diagram.DiagramElements.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
				while (list.Count > 0)
				{
					ElementsComposition elementsComposition = FindElementsCompositionBy(list[0]);
					if (elementsComposition.Contains(position))
					{
						return elementsComposition;
					}
					foreach (AtomElement atomElement in elementsComposition.AtomElements)
					{
						if (list.Contains(atomElement))
						{
							list.Remove(atomElement);
						}
					}
				}
				return null;
			}

			private ElementsComposition FindElementsCompositionBy(AtomElement atomElement)
			{
				ElementsComposition elementsComposition = new ElementsComposition();
				AddConnectedAtomElements(atomElement, elementsComposition.AtomElements);
				return elementsComposition;
			}

			private void AddConnectedAtomElements(AtomElement atomElement, List<AtomElement> list)
			{
				if (list.Contains(atomElement))
				{
					return;
				}
				list.Add(atomElement);
				foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement in atomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
				{
					AtomElement atomElement2 = ((connectedBondElement.StartAtomElement == atomElement) ? connectedBondElement.EndAtomElement : connectedBondElement.StartAtomElement);
					AddConnectedAtomElements(atomElement2, list);
				}
			}
		}

		private class DraggingHelper
		{
			private readonly EditorSelectTool _editorSelectTool;

			private readonly UIElement _rectangle;

			public Diagram Diagram => _editorSelectTool.DrawingCanvas.Diagram;

			public IEnumerable<UIElement> ContainedElements
			{
				get
				{
					Rect rect = new Rect(Diagram.GetElementPosition(_rectangle), _rectangle.RenderSize);
					foreach (UIElement element in Diagram.DiagramElements)
					{
						if (element is AtomElement && rect.Contains(Diagram.GetElementPosition(element)))
						{
							yield return element;
						}
						if (element is BondElement bondElement && rect.Contains(new Rect(bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint, bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint)))
						{
							yield return element;
						}
					}
				}
			}

			public DraggingHelper(EditorSelectTool editorSelectTool)
			{
				_editorSelectTool = editorSelectTool;
				_rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
				{
					Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
					StrokeThickness = 1.0,
					StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection(new double[2] { 4.0, 1.0 })
				};
			}

			public void Update(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
			{
				Rect rect = new Rect(startPoint, endPoint);
				if (!Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rectangle))
				{
					Diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_rectangle, rect.TopLeft);
				}
				Diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_rectangle, rect.TopLeft);
				Diagram.SetElementSize(_rectangle, rect.Size);
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				if (Diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rectangle))
				{
					Diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_rectangle);
				}
			}
		}

		private class ElementsComposition
		{
			public readonly List<AtomElement> AtomElements = new List<AtomElement>();

			public IEnumerable<BondElement> BondElements
			{
				get
				{
					List<BondElement> list = new List<BondElement>();
					foreach (AtomElement atomElement in AtomElements)
					{
						foreach (BondElement connectedBondElement in atomElement.ConnectedBondElements)
						{
							if (!list.Contains(connectedBondElement))
							{
								list.Add(connectedBondElement);
							}
						}
					}
					return list;
				}
			}

			public bool Contains(System.Windows.Point point)
			{
				if (AtomElements.Count < 2)
				{
					return false;
				}
				Rect rect = new Rect(Diagram.GetElementPosition(AtomElements[0]), Diagram.GetElementPosition(AtomElements[1]));
				for (int i = 2; i < AtomElements.Count; i++)
				{
					rect.Union(Diagram.GetElementPosition(AtomElements[i]));
				}
				return rect.Contains(point);
			}

			public bool HasSelection(Diagram diagram)
			{
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement in AtomElements)
				{
					if (diagram.IsSelected(atomElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				foreach (BondElement bondElement in BondElements)
				{
					if (diagram.IsSelected(bondElement))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			public void SelectMe(Diagram diagram)
			{
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement in AtomElements)
				{
					diagram.Select(atomElement);
				}
				foreach (BondElement bondElement in BondElements)
				{
					diagram.Select(bondElement);
				}
			}

			public void DeselectMe(Diagram diagram)
			{
				foreach (AtomElement atomElement in AtomElements)
				{
					diagram.Unselect(atomElement);
				}
				foreach (BondElement bondElement in BondElements)
				{
					diagram.Unselect(bondElement);
				}
			}
		}

		private readonly SelectHelper _selectHelper;

		private readonly DraggingHelper _draggingHelper;

		private RotationAdorner _rotationAdorner;

		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		public EditorSelectTool()
		{
			_selectHelper = new SelectHelper(this);
			_draggingHelper = new DraggingHelper(this);
		}

		protected override void OnRegisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			diagram.ElementsSelected += diagram_ElementsSelected;
			diagram.ElementsUnselected += diagram_ElementsUnselected;
			base.OnRegisterWithDiagram(diagram);
		}

		protected override void OnUnregisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			Reset();
			diagram.ElementsSelected -= diagram_ElementsSelected;
			diagram.ElementsUnselected -= diagram_ElementsUnselected;
			base.OnUnregisterWithDiagram(diagram);
		}

		private void Reset()
		{
			if (_rotationAdorner != null)
			{
				_rotationAdorner.Hide();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.A && (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl)))
			{
				DrawingCanvas.Diagram.SelectAll();
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_selectHelper.MouseDownElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_selectHelper.MouseDownPosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_selectHelper.TryCaptureMouse();
			_selectHelper.MouseDownClicked = true;
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_selectHelper.MouseMoveElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			_selectHelper.MouseMovePosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			if (_selectHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				if (_selectHelper.IsMovingSelection)
				{
					_selectHelper.MoveElements(isPreview: true);
				}
				else if (_selectHelper.IsDraggingToSelect)
				{
					_draggingHelper.Update(_selectHelper.MouseDownPosition, _selectHelper.MouseMovePosition);
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseMove(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectHelper selectHelper = _selectHelper;
			UIElement mouseUpElement = (_selectHelper.MouseMoveElement = e.GetDiagramElement());
			selectHelper.MouseUpElement = mouseUpElement;
			SelectHelper selectHelper2 = _selectHelper;
			System.Windows.Point mouseUpPosition = (_selectHelper.MouseMovePosition = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
			selectHelper2.MouseUpPosition = mouseUpPosition;
			if (_selectHelper.MouseDownClicked)
			{
				if (_selectHelper.IsSingleClickAtFreeSpace)
				{
					if (!_selectHelper.IsCtrlPressed)
					{
						if (DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Selection.Count == 0)
						{
							_selectHelper.SelectElementsByMousePosition(_selectHelper.MouseUpPosition);
						}
						else
						{
							DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						_selectHelper.RevertSelectionByMousePosition(_selectHelper.MouseUpPosition);
					}
				}
				else if (_selectHelper.IsDraggingToSelect)
				{
					_draggingHelper.Update(_selectHelper.MouseDownPosition, _selectHelper.MouseUpPosition);
					if (!_selectHelper.IsCtrlPressed)
					{
						DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
						foreach (UIElement containedElement in _draggingHelper.ContainedElements)
						{
							DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Select(containedElement);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						foreach (UIElement containedElement2 in _draggingHelper.ContainedElements)
						{
							if (DrawingCanvas.Diagram.IsSelected(containedElement2))
							{
								DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Unselect(containedElement2);
							}
							else
							{
								DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Select(containedElement2);
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else if (_selectHelper.IsSingleClickAtElement)
				{
					if (!_selectHelper.IsCtrlPressed)
					{
						DrawingCanvas.Diagram.UnselectAll();
						DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Select(_selectHelper.MouseUpElement);
					}
					else if (DrawingCanvas.Diagram.IsSelected(_selectHelper.MouseUpElement))
					{
						DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Unselect(_selectHelper.MouseUpElement);
					}
					else
					{
						DrawingCanvas.Diagram.Select(_selectHelper.MouseUpElement);
					}
				}
				else if (_selectHelper.IsMovingSelection)
				{
					_selectHelper.MoveElements(isPreview: false);
				}
				_selectHelper.Reset();
				_draggingHelper.Reset();
			}
			_selectHelper.TryReleaseMouse();
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
			if (_rotationAdorner != null && !_rotationAdorner.IsMouseOver)
			{
				_rotationAdorner.Update();
			}
		}

		private void diagram_ElementsSelected(object sender, SelectionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_rotationAdorner != null)
			{
				_rotationAdorner.Hide();
			}
			_rotationAdorner = new RotationAdorner(DrawingCanvas);
			_rotationAdorner.Update();
		}

		private void diagram_ElementsUnselected(object sender, SelectionEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_rotationAdorner != null)
			{
				_rotationAdorner.Hide();
			}
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}
	public class EditorZoomTool : ZoomTool, IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		public MolecularDrawingCanvas DrawingCanvas { get; private set; }

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			DrawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			RegisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}

		void IDrawingCanvasTool.UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			UnregisterWithDiagram(drawingCanvas.Diagram);
		}
	}
	public interface IDrawingCanvasTool
	{
		void RegisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas);

		void UnregisterDrawingCanvas(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas);
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Serialization
{
	public class MoleFileReader : IFragmentReader
	{
		private const string V2000 = "V2000";

		private static readonly IFormatProvider MolFormatProvider = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

		Fragment IFragmentReader.Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			return Read(contents, reference);
		}

		private static Fragment Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (contents == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("contents");
			}
			try
			{
				MakeSure(contents.Length >= 4);
				Fragment fragment = new Fragment();
				fragment.Name = contents[0];
				int headerIndex = 0;
				int atomCount = 0;
				int bondCount = 0;
				GetHeaderLineInfo(contents, out headerIndex, out atomCount, out bondCount);
				int num = headerIndex + 1;
				MakeSure(contents.Length >= num + atomCount + bondCount);
				Dictionary<int, string> dictionary = TryReadAtomAliases(contents, num + atomCount + bondCount);
				for (int i = 0; i < atomCount; i++)
				{
					string atomAlias = (dictionary.ContainsKey(i + 1) ? dictionary[i + 1] : null);
					fragment.Atoms.Add(GetAtomFrom(contents[num + i], reference, atomAlias));
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < bondCount; j++)
				{
					fragment.Bonds.Add(GetBondFrom(contents[num + atomCount + j]));
				}
				for (int k = num + atomCount + bondCount; k < contents.Length; k++)
				{
					TryReadChargeCount(contents[k], fragment);
				}
				for (int l = num + atomCount + bondCount; l < contents.Length; l++)
				{
					TryReadIsotopeCount(contents[l], fragment);
				}
				foreach (Bond bond in fragment.Bonds)
				{
					bond.StartAtom = fragment.Atoms[Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bond)];
					bond.EndAtom = fragment.Atoms[Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bond)];
				}
				return fragment;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
				throw new FileFormatException(ex.Message);
			}
		}

		private static void GetHeaderLineInfo(string[] contents, out int headerIndex, out int atomCount, out int bondCount)
		{
			for (int num = Math.Min(contents.Length - 1, 10); num > 0; num--)
			{
				string text = contents[num];
				if (text.Length >= 39 && string.Equals(text.Substring(33, 6).Trim(), "V2000", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && GetHeaderInfo(text, out atomCount, out bondCount))
				{
					headerIndex = num;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (GetHeaderInfo(contents[3], out atomCount, out bondCount))
			{
				headerIndex = 3;
				return;
			}
			if (GetHeaderInfo(contents[2], out atomCount, out bondCount))
			{
				headerIndex = 2;
				return;
			}
			throw new FileFormatException(Resources.ErrorFailToFindHeadLine);
		}

		private static bool GetHeaderInfo(string line, out int atomCount, out int bondCount)
		{
			try
			{
				atomCount = GetInteger(line, 0, 3);
				bondCount = GetInteger(line, 3, 3);
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
				atomCount = 0;
				bondCount = 0;
				return false;
			}
		}

		private static Dictionary<int, string> TryReadAtomAliases(string[] contents, int startFrom)
		{
			Dictionary<int, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, string>();
			for (int i = startFrom; i < contents.Length; i++)
			{
				if (i < contents.Length - 1)
				{
					string text = contents[i];
					if (text.Length == 6 && GetString(text, 0, 3) == "A")
					{
						int integer = GetInteger(text, 3, 3);
						string value = contents[i + 1].Trim();
						dictionary.Add(integer, value);
						i++;
					}
				}
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private static Atom GetAtomFrom(string content, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, string atomAlias)
		{
			double x = GetDouble(content, 0, 10);
			double num = GetDouble(content, 10, 10);
			GetDouble(content, 20, 10);
			string name = GetString(content, 31, 3);
			int integer = GetInteger(content, 34, 2);
			int integer2 = GetInteger(content, 36, 3);
			int integer3 = GetInteger(content, 48, 3);
			Atom atom = CreateAtomFromResource(reference, atomAlias, name);
			Fragment.SetPosition(atom, new AtomPosition
			{
				Mode = AtomPositionMode.Absolute,
				AbsolutePoint = new System.Windows.Point(x, 0.0 - num)
			});
			if (integer != 0)
			{
				atom.Mass += integer;
			}
			if (integer2 != 0)
			{
				atom.ChargeCount = 4 - integer2;
			}
			if (integer3 >= 1 && integer3 <= 14)
			{
				atom.Valence = integer3;
			}
			else if (integer3 == 15)
			{
				atom.Valence = 0;
			}
			return atom;
		}

		private static Atom CreateAtomFromResource(MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, string atomAlias, string name)
		{
			Atom atom = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(atomAlias))
			{
				atom = reference.FindResourceAtomWithName(GetPossibleAtomName(atomAlias));
				if (atom == null)
				{
					atom = reference.FindResourceAtomWithName("X");
					atom.Name = atomAlias;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				atom = reference.FindResourceAtomWithName(GetPossibleAtomName(name));
			}
			return atom.Clone();
		}

		private static string GetPossibleAtomName(string atomName)
		{
			if (string.Equals(atomName, "R#", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return "R";
			}
			return atomName;
		}

		private static Bond GetBondFrom(string content)
		{
			int integer = GetInteger(content, 0, 3);
			int integer2 = GetInteger(content, 3, 3);
			int integer3 = GetInteger(content, 6, 3);
			int integer4 = GetInteger(content, 9, 3);
			Bond bond = new Bond();
			switch (integer3)
			{
			case 1:
				switch (integer4)
				{
				case 1:
					bond.Type = BondType.UpStereo;
					break;
				case 6:
					bond.Type = BondType.DownStereo;
					break;
				default:
					bond.Type = BondType.Single;
					break;
				}
				break;
			case 2:
				bond.Type = BondType.Double;
				break;
			case 3:
				bond.Type = BondType.Tripple;
				break;
			default:
				bond.Type = BondType.Single;
				break;
			}
			Fragment.SetStartAtomIndex(bond, integer - 1);
			Fragment.SetEndAtomIndex(bond, integer2 - 1);
			return bond;
		}

		private static void TryReadChargeCount(string content, Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (content.StartsWith("M  CHG"))
			{
				int integer = GetInteger(content, 6, 3);
				for (int i = 0; i < integer; i++)
				{
					int integer2 = GetInteger(content, 9 + i * 8, 4);
					int integer3 = GetInteger(content, 13 + i * 8, 4);
					fragment.Atoms[integer2 - 1].ChargeCount = integer3;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void TryReadIsotopeCount(string content, Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (content.StartsWith("M  ISO"))
			{
				int integer = GetInteger(content, 6, 3);
				for (int i = 0; i < integer; i++)
				{
					int integer2 = GetInteger(content, 9 + i * 8, 4);
					int integer3 = GetInteger(content, 13 + i * 8, 4);
					fragment.Atoms[integer2 - 1].Mass = integer3;
				}
			}
		}

		private static void TryReadValenceCount(string content, Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (content.StartsWith("V  "))
			{
				int integer = GetInteger(content, 3, 3);
				int integer2 = GetInteger(content, 6, 2);
				fragment.Atoms[integer - 1].Valence = integer2;
			}
		}

		private static void MakeSure(bool check)
		{
			if (!check)
			{
				throw new FileFormatException();
			}
		}

		private static int GetInteger(string line, int startIndex, int length)
		{
			return (int)GetDouble(line, startIndex, length);
		}

		private static double GetDouble(string line, int startIndex, int length)
		{
			MakeSure(line.Length >= startIndex + length);
			return double.Parse(line.Substring(startIndex, length).Trim(), MolFormatProvider);
		}

		private static string GetString(string line, int startIndex, int length)
		{
			MakeSure(line.Length >= startIndex + length);
			return line.Substring(startIndex, length).Trim();
		}
	}
	public class MoleFileWriter : IFragmentWriter
	{
		private const double ScaleFactor = 0.01;

		private const int CarbonPeriodicId = 6;

		private const int HydrogenPeriodicId = 1;

		private const double ZeroDifference = 0.001;

		private static readonly IFormatProvider MolFormatProvider = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

		string[] IFragmentWriter.Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			return Save(fragment, reference);
		}

		private static string[] Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			list.Add(GetHeaderLine1(fragment));
			list.Add(GetHeaderLine2(fragment));
			list.Add(GetHeaderLine3(fragment));
			list.Add(GetCountsLine(fragment));
			double baseX = 0.0;
			double baseY = 0.0;
			if (fragment.Atoms.Count > 0)
			{
				baseX = fragment.Atoms.Average((Atom atom) => Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint.X);
				baseY = fragment.Atoms.Average((Atom atom) => Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint.Y);
			}
			List<Atom> isotopeAtoms = new List<Atom>();
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				list.Add(GetAtomLine(atom, baseX, baseY, reference, isotopeAtoms));
			}
			foreach (Bond bond in fragment.Bonds)
			{
				list.Add(GetBondLine(bond));
			}
			foreach (Atom item in fragment.Atoms.Where((Atom x) => x.IsFlexible))
			{
				list.AddRange(GetAliasAtomLines(fragment, item));
			}
			list.AddRange(GetPropertyChargeLines(fragment));
			list.AddRange(GetPropertyIsotopeLines(fragment, isotopeAtoms));
			list.Add("M  END");
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		private static string GetHeaderLine1(Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (fragment.Name != null)
			{
				return fragment.Name;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private static string GetHeaderLine2(Fragment fragment)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("  ");
			stringBuilder.Append("AspenMD ");
			stringBuilder.Append(DateTime.Now.ToString("MMddyyHHmm"));
			stringBuilder.Append("2D");
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(1, 2));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0.01, 10, 5));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0.0, 12, 5));
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string GetHeaderLine3(Fragment fragment)
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private static string GetCountsLine(Fragment fragment)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(fragment.Atoms.Count, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(fragment.Bonds.Count, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			int num = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)fragment.Atoms.Where((Atom atom) => atom.ChargeCount != 0).Count() / 8.0);
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(num + 1, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format("V2000", 6, leftAlignment: false));
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string GetAtomLine(Atom atom, double baseX, double baseY, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, List<Atom> isotopeAtoms)
		{
			Atom atom2 = (atom.IsFlexible ? reference.FindResourceAtomWithName("X") : reference.FindResourceAtomWithName(atom.Name));
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(Format((Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint.X - baseX) * 0.01, 10, 4));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format((baseY - Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint.Y) * 0.01, 10, 4));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0.0, 10, 4));
			stringBuilder.Append(' ');
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(GetAtomName(atom), 3, leftAlignment: true));
			bool processed = false;
			int massDifferenceDigit = GetMassDifferenceDigit(atom, atom2, out processed);
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(massDifferenceDigit, 2));
			if (!processed && !isotopeAtoms.Contains(atom))
			{
				isotopeAtoms.Add(atom);
			}
			if (atom.ChargeCount == 0 || Math.Abs(atom.ChargeCount) > 3)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(4 - atom.ChargeCount, 3));
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			if (atom2 != null && atom2.Valence == atom.Valence)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			}
			else if (atom.Valence == 0)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(15, 3));
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(atom.Valence, 3));
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string GetAtomName(Atom atom)
		{
			if (atom.IsFlexible)
			{
				return "X";
			}
			if (string.Equals(atom.Name, "R", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
			{
				return "R#";
			}
			return atom.Name;
		}

		private static int GetMassDifferenceDigit(Atom atom, Atom defaultAtom, out bool processed)
		{
			processed = true;
			double num = atom.Mass - defaultAtom.Mass;
			int num2 = (int)Math.Round(num);
			double num3 = Math.Abs((double)num2 - num);
			if (num >= -3.0 && num <= 4.0 && num3 <= 0.001)
			{
				return num2;
			}
			processed = false;
			return 0;
		}

		private static string GetBondLine(Bond bond)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(Fragment.GetStartAtomIndex(bond) + 1, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(Fragment.GetEndAtomIndex(bond) + 1, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(bond.Type.Valence, 3));
			if (bond.Type == BondType.UpStereo)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(1, 3));
			}
			else if (bond.Type == BondType.DownStereo)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(6, 3));
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			stringBuilder.Append(Format(0, 3));
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetAliasAtomLines(Fragment fragment, Atom atom)
		{
			yield return "A  " + Format(fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(atom) + 1, 3);
			yield return atom.Name;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetPropertyChargeLines(Fragment fragment)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<Atom> list2 = fragment.Atoms.Where((Atom atom) => atom.ChargeCount != 0).ToList();
			int num = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)list2.Count / 8.0);
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < num; num2++)
			{
				int num3 = num2 * 8;
				int num4 = Math.Min(list2.Count - 1, num3 + 7);
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("M  CHG").Append(Format(num4 + 1 - num3, 3));
				for (int num5 = num3; num5 <= num4; num5++)
				{
					int num6 = fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(list2[num5]);
					int chargeCount = list2[num5].ChargeCount;
					stringBuilder.Append(Format(num6 + 1, 4)).Append(Format(chargeCount, 4));
				}
				list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static IEnumerable<string> GetPropertyIsotopeLines(Fragment fragment, List<Atom> isotopeAtoms)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int num = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)isotopeAtoms.Count / 8.0);
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				int num2 = i * 8;
				int num3 = Math.Min(isotopeAtoms.Count - 1, num2 + 7);
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("M  ISO").Append(Format(num3 + 1 - num2, 3));
				for (int j = num2; j <= num3; j++)
				{
					int num4 = fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(isotopeAtoms[j]);
					int number = (int)Math.Round(isotopeAtoms[j].Mass);
					stringBuilder.Append(Format(num4 + 1, 4)).Append(Format(number, 4));
				}
				list.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static string Format(int number, int length)
		{
			string text = number.ToString(MolFormatProvider);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(length - text.Length));
			stringBuilder.Append(text);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string Format(double number, int length, int dotLength)
		{
			string text = string.Format(MolFormatProvider, "{0:F" + dotLength + "}", number);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(length - text.Length));
			stringBuilder.Append(text);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string Format(string line, int length, bool leftAlignment)
		{
			if (line.Length > length)
			{
				return line.Substring(0, length);
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(GetEmptyString(length - line.Length));
			if (leftAlignment)
			{
				stringBuilder.Insert(0, line);
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(line);
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string GetEmptyString(int length)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(' ');
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class SmilesFileReader : IFragmentReader
	{
		private enum ChiralFlagOrientation
		{
			None,
			SingleClockwise,
			SingleAnticlockwise,
			DoubleClockwise,
			DoubleAnticlockwise
		}

		private class ChiralFlag
		{
			public int UsedConnections { get; set; }

			public int ParenthesesIndex { get; set; }

			public ChiralFlagOrientation Orientation { get; set; }

			public int AffectiveConnections
			{
				get
				{
					if (Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleClockwise)
					{
						return 3;
					}
					if (Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleAnticlockwise)
					{
						return 3;
					}
					if (Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleClockwise)
					{
						return 2;
					}
					if (Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleAnticlockwise)
					{
						return 2;
					}
					return 0;
				}
			}
		}

		private enum CisTransFlag
		{
			None,
			Slash,
			BackSlash
		}

		private class CisTransPair
		{
			public CisTransFlag StartFlag { get; set; }

			public CisTransFlag EndFlag { get; set; }

			public Atom FarStartAtom { get; set; }

			public Atom FarEndAtom { get; set; }

			public Atom StartAtom { get; set; }

			public Atom EndAtom { get; set; }

			public bool IsCompleted => EndAtom != null;

			public bool IsSameSide => StartFlag != EndFlag;
		}

		private class Ring
		{
			public List<Atom> Atoms { get; private set; }

			public BondType ClosedBondType { get; set; }

			public bool IsAromatic { get; set; }

			public double InnerAngle => (Atoms.Count - 2) * 180 / Atoms.Count;

			public Ring()
			{
				Atoms = new List<Atom>();
			}

			public bool Contains(Bond bond)
			{
				if (Atoms.Contains(bond.StartAtom) && Atoms.Contains(bond.EndAtom))
				{
					int num = Atoms.IndexOf(bond.StartAtom);
					int num2 = Atoms.IndexOf(bond.EndAtom);
					if (Math.Abs(num - num2) == 1)
					{
						return true;
					}
					if ((num == 0 && num2 == Atoms.Count - 1) || (num == Atoms.Count - 1 && num2 == 0))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}

			public Atom GetAdjacentAtom(Atom firstAtom, Atom secondAtom)
			{
				if (firstAtom != null && secondAtom != null && Atoms.Contains(firstAtom) && Atoms.Contains(secondAtom))
				{
					int num = Atoms.IndexOf(firstAtom);
					int num2 = Atoms.IndexOf(secondAtom) * 2 - num;
					num2 = (num2 + Atoms.Count) % Atoms.Count;
					return Atoms[num2];
				}
				return null;
			}

			public double? GetPlacedInnerAngle(Atom startAtom, Atom endAtom)
			{
				if (startAtom != null && endAtom != null && Atoms.Contains(startAtom) && Atoms.Contains(endAtom))
				{
					AtomPosition position = Fragment.GetPosition(startAtom);
					AtomPosition position2 = Fragment.GetPosition(endAtom);
					if (position != null && position2 != null)
					{
						AtomPosition position3 = Fragment.GetPosition(GetAdjacentAtom(endAtom, startAtom));
