						if (position3 != null)
						{
							double num = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(position3.AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint, position2.AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint);
							if (num > 180.0)
							{
								num = 360.0 - num;
							}
							return num;
						}
						AtomPosition position4 = Fragment.GetPosition(GetAdjacentAtom(startAtom, endAtom));
						if (position4 != null)
						{
							double num2 = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(position.AbsolutePoint - position2.AbsolutePoint, position4.AbsolutePoint - position2.AbsolutePoint);
							if (num2 > 180.0)
							{
								num2 = 360.0 - num2;
							}
							return num2;
						}
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		private class RingSegment
		{
			public Atom StartAtom { get; set; }

			public Atom EndAtom { get; set; }

			public int Power { get; set; }

			public RingSegment Parent { get; set; }
		}

		private class RingBridge
		{
			public BondType BondType { get; set; }

			public Atom StartAtom { get; set; }

			public Atom EndAtom { get; set; }
		}

		private class BondQuartrand
		{
			public Bond IndividualBond1 { get; set; }

			public Bond IndividualBond2 { get; set; }

			public Bond RingBond1 { get; set; }

			public Bond RingBond2 { get; set; }

			public bool Calculated1 { get; set; }

			public bool Calculated2 { get; set; }
		}

		private const int BondLength = 100;

		private static readonly Dictionary<string, int> AromaticAtomAllowedHydrogens = new Dictionary<string, int>
		{
			{ "B", 5 },
			{ "C", 4 },
			{ "N", 3 },
			{ "P", 3 }
		};

		private static readonly string[] OrganicElements = new string[10] { "B", "C", "N", "O", "P", "S", "F", "Cl", "Br", "I" };

		private static readonly string[] ChiralConfigurations = new string[5] { "TH", "AL", "SP", "TB", "OH" };

		private static readonly List<double> BondAngleOptions = new List<double>
		{
			120.0, 240.0, 60.0, 180.0, 300.0, 30.0, 90.0, 150.0, 210.0, 270.0,
			330.0, 15.0, 45.0, 75.0, 105.0, 135.0, 165.0, 195.0, 225.0, 255.0,
			285.0, 315.0, 345.0
		};

		Fragment IFragmentReader.Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (contents == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("contents");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			foreach (string text in contents)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return ReadFromString(text.Trim(), reference);
				}
			}
			throw new FileFormatException();
		}

		private static Fragment ReadFromString(string line, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			try
			{
				Fragment fragment = new Fragment();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(line))
				{
					Stack<ChiralFlag> stack = new Stack<ChiralFlag>();
					Stack<CisTransPair> stack2 = new Stack<CisTransPair>();
					List<CisTransPair> list = new List<CisTransPair>();
					int num = 0;
					Stack<List<Atom>> stack3 = new Stack<List<Atom>>();
					Dictionary<List<Atom>, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<List<Atom>, int>();
					List<Atom> list2 = new List<Atom>();
					stack3.Push(list2);
					dictionary.Add(list2, num);
					Dictionary<Atom, ChiralFlagOrientation> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<Atom, ChiralFlagOrientation>();
					List<RingBridge> list3 = new List<RingBridge>();
					Dictionary<int, RingBridge> bridgeDictionary = new Dictionary<int, RingBridge>();
					List<Atom> list4 = new List<Atom>();
					Dictionary<Bond, KeyValuePair<Bond, double>> bondRelativeAngles = new Dictionary<Bond, KeyValuePair<Bond, double>>();
					BondType lastBondType = null;
					Dictionary<Atom, int> dictionary3 = new Dictionary<Atom, int>();
					int nextIndex = 0;
					while (nextIndex < line.Length)
					{
						char c = line[nextIndex];
						switch (c)
						{
						case '.':
							list2 = new List<Atom>();
							stack3.Push(list2);
							dictionary.Add(list2, num);
							nextIndex++;
							continue;
						case ')':
							while (stack3.Count > 0)
							{
								List<Atom> key = stack3.Peek();
								if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key) || dictionary[key] != num)
								{
									break;
								}
								dictionary.Remove(key);
								stack3.Pop();
							}
							list2 = stack3.Peek();
							num--;
							nextIndex++;
							continue;
						}
						if (list2.Count == 0)
						{
							bool isAromatic = false;
							ChiralFlag chiralFlag;
							int? declaredHydrogenCount;
							Atom atom = ReadAtom(line, nextIndex, reference, lastBondType, ref nextIndex, ref isAromatic, out chiralFlag, out declaredHydrogenCount);
							if (chiralFlag != null)
							{
								chiralFlag.ParenthesesIndex = num;
								stack.Push(chiralFlag);
							}
							dictionary2.Add(atom, chiralFlag?.Orientation ?? ChiralFlagOrientation.None);
							if (isAromatic && !list4.Contains(atom))
							{
								list4.Add(atom);
							}
							if (declaredHydrogenCount.HasValue)
							{
								dictionary3.Add(atom, declaredHydrogenCount.Value);
							}
							nextIndex = ReadRingBridges(line, nextIndex, atom, bridgeDictionary, list3);
							list2.Add(atom);
							fragment.Atoms.Add(atom);
							continue;
						}
						bool flag = false;
						if (c == '(')
						{
							flag = true;
							num++;
							nextIndex++;
						}
						CisTransFlag cisTransFlag = CisTransFlag.None;
						double? chiralAngle = null;
						ChiralFlag chiralFlag2 = ((stack.Count > 0) ? stack.Peek() : null);
						BondType bondType = ReadBondType(line, nextIndex, reference, chiralFlag2, flag, num, ref nextIndex, ref cisTransFlag, out chiralAngle);
						if (chiralFlag2 != null && chiralFlag2.UsedConnections >= chiralFlag2.AffectiveConnections)
						{
							chiralFlag2.UsedConnections = 0;
							stack.Pop();
						}
						bool isAromatic2 = false;
						ChiralFlag chiralFlag3;
						int? declaredHydrogenCount2;
						Atom atom2 = ReadAtom(line, nextIndex, reference, lastBondType, ref nextIndex, ref isAromatic2, out chiralFlag3, out declaredHydrogenCount2);
						if (chiralFlag3 != null)
						{
							chiralFlag3.ParenthesesIndex = num;
							stack.Push(chiralFlag3);
						}
						dictionary2.Add(atom2, chiralFlag3?.Orientation ?? ChiralFlagOrientation.None);
						if (isAromatic2 && !list4.Contains(atom2))
						{
							list4.Add(atom2);
						}
						if (declaredHydrogenCount2.HasValue)
						{
							dictionary3.Add(atom2, declaredHydrogenCount2.Value);
						}
						nextIndex = ReadRingBridges(line, nextIndex, atom2, bridgeDictionary, list3);
						if (cisTransFlag != CisTransFlag.None)
						{
							Atom atom3 = (flag ? atom2 : list2[list2.Count - 1]);
							Atom atom4 = (flag ? list2[list2.Count - 1] : atom2);
							if (stack2.Count == 0)
							{
								stack2.Push(new CisTransPair
								{
									StartFlag = cisTransFlag,
									FarStartAtom = atom3,
									StartAtom = atom4
								});
							}
							else
							{
								CisTransPair cisTransPair = stack2.Peek();
								if (cisTransPair.IsCompleted)
								{
									stack2.Push(new CisTransPair
									{
										StartFlag = cisTransFlag,
										FarStartAtom = atom3,
										StartAtom = atom4
									});
								}
								else
								{
									Bond bond = FindBondInFragment(cisTransPair.StartAtom, atom4);
									if (bond == null)
									{
										bond = FindBondInFragment(cisTransPair.StartAtom, atom3);
										if (bond != null)
										{
											Atom atom5 = atom3;
											atom3 = atom4;
											atom4 = atom5;
										}
									}
									if (bond == null)
									{
										stack2.Push(new CisTransPair
										{
											StartFlag = cisTransFlag,
											FarStartAtom = atom3,
											StartAtom = atom4
										});
									}
									else
									{
										cisTransPair.EndAtom = atom4;
										cisTransPair.EndFlag = cisTransFlag;
										cisTransPair.FarEndAtom = atom3;
										if (!list.Contains(cisTransPair))
										{
											list.Add(cisTransPair);
										}
										stack2.Pop();
									}
								}
							}
						}
						Bond item = new Bond
						{
							Type = bondType,
							StartAtom = list2[list2.Count - 1],
							EndAtom = atom2
						};
						if (flag)
						{
							list2 = new List<Atom>();
							stack3.Push(list2);
							dictionary.Add(list2, num);
						}
						lastBondType = bondType;
						list2.Add(atom2);
						fragment.Atoms.Add(atom2);
						fragment.Bonds.Add(item);
					}
					foreach (RingBridge item2 in list3)
					{
						SetBondWithType(fragment, item2.StartAtom, item2.EndAtom, item2.BondType);
					}
					list3.Sort((RingBridge bx, RingBridge by) => fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(bx.StartAtom) - fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(by.StartAtom));
					List<Ring> list5 = new List<Ring>();
					foreach (RingBridge item3 in list3)
					{
						FindRingByRingBridge(fragment, item3, list5, list4);
					}
					foreach (Ring item4 in list5)
					{
						if (item4.IsAromatic)
						{
							SetupBondsForAromaticRing(fragment, item4, dictionary3);
							continue;
						}
						for (int num2 = 0; num2 < item4.Atoms.Count - 1; num2++)
						{
							SetBondWithDefaultType(fragment, item4.Atoms[num2], item4.Atoms[num2 + 1], BondType.Single);
						}
						SetBondWithDefaultType(fragment, item4.Atoms[item4.Atoms.Count - 1], item4.Atoms[0], BondType.Single);
					}
					List<Atom> list6 = new List<Atom>();
					Dictionary<List<Atom>, Rect> dictionary4 = new Dictionary<List<Atom>, Rect>();
					foreach (Atom atom6 in fragment.Atoms)
					{
						if (list6.Contains(atom6))
						{
							continue;
						}
						List<Atom> list7 = new List<Atom>();
						PlaceAtom(atom6, null, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), list7, list, fragment, bondRelativeAngles, list5, dictionary2);
						list6.AddRange(list7);
						Rect empty = Rect.Empty;
						foreach (Atom item5 in list7)
						{
							empty.Union(Fragment.GetPosition(item5).AbsolutePoint);
						}
						dictionary4.Add(list7, empty);
					}
					double num3 = 0.0;
					foreach (KeyValuePair<List<Atom>, Rect> item6 in dictionary4)
					{
						Vector vector = new System.Windows.Point(num3, 0.0) - item6.Value.TopLeft;
						foreach (Atom item7 in item6.Key)
						{
							Fragment.GetPosition(item7).AbsolutePoint += vector;
						}
						num3 += item6.Value.Width + 100.0;
					}
					foreach (Bond bond2 in fragment.Bonds)
					{
						Fragment.SetStartAtomIndex(bond2, fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(bond2.StartAtom));
						Fragment.SetEndAtomIndex(bond2, fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(bond2.EndAtom));
					}
				}
				return fragment;
			}
			catch (FileFormatException ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		private static void SetupBondsForAromaticRing(Fragment fragment, Ring ring, Dictionary<Atom, int> hydrogenNumbers)
		{
			BondType bondType = null;
			BondType bondType2 = null;
			List<Atom> saturatedAtoms = ring.Atoms.FindAll((Atom x) => !IsAtomAllowedDoubleBond(x));
			int num = ((saturatedAtoms.Count > 0) ? ring.Atoms.IndexOf(saturatedAtoms[0]) : 0);
			int atomsCount = ring.Atoms.Count;
			for (int num2 = 0; num2 < atomsCount; num2++)
			{
				int index = CorrectAtomIndex(num + num2 - 1);
				int num3 = CorrectAtomIndex(num + num2);
				Atom startAtom = ring.Atoms[index];
				Atom endAtom = ring.Atoms[num3];
				if (num2 == 0)
				{
					bondType = ResolveBondType(startAtom, endAtom, (num3 == 0) ? ring.ClosedBondType : BondType.Single);
					SetBondWithType(fragment, startAtom, endAtom, bondType);
					bondType2 = bondType;
					continue;
				}
				BondType bondType3 = ResolveBondType(startAtom, endAtom, (bondType2 == BondType.Single) ? BondType.Double : BondType.Single);
				if (num2 == atomsCount - 1 && bondType3 == BondType.Double && bondType == BondType.Double)
				{
					bondType3 = BondType.Single;
				}
				SetBondWithType(fragment, startAtom, endAtom, bondType3);
				bondType2 = bondType3;
			}
			int CorrectAtomIndex(int num4)
			{
				if (num4 < 0)
				{
					return num4 + atomsCount;
				}
				if (num4 >= atomsCount)
				{
					return num4 - atomsCount;
				}
				return num4;
			}
			bool IsAtomAllowedDoubleBond(Atom atom)
			{
				if (!AromaticAtomAllowedHydrogens.ContainsKey(atom.Name))
				{
					return false;
				}
				int num4 = AromaticAtomAllowedHydrogens[atom.Name];
				if (num4 < 3)
				{
					return false;
				}
				int num5 = atom.Bonds.Where((Bond x) => !ring.Atoms.Contains(x.StartAtom) || !ring.Atoms.Contains(x.EndAtom)).Sum((Bond y) => y.Type.Valence);
				int num6 = num4 - num5;
				if (num6 < 3)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!hydrogenNumbers.ContainsKey(atom))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return num6 - hydrogenNumbers[atom] >= 3;
			}
			BondType ResolveBondType(Atom item, Atom item2, BondType suggestedBondType)
			{
				if (suggestedBondType == BondType.Single)
				{
					return BondType.Single;
				}
				if (suggestedBondType != BondType.Double)
				{
					return BondType.Single;
				}
				if (saturatedAtoms.Contains(item) || saturatedAtoms.Contains(item2))
				{
					return BondType.Single;
				}
				return BondType.Double;
			}
		}

		private static int ReadRingBridges(string line, int index, Atom atom, Dictionary<int, RingBridge> bridgeDictionary, List<RingBridge> bridges)
		{
			List<BondType> list = new List<BondType>();
			List<int> list2 = ReadRingNumbers(line, ref index, list);
			for (int i = 0; i < list2.Count; i++)
			{
				int key = list2[i];
				if (bridgeDictionary.ContainsKey(key))
				{
					RingBridge ringBridge = bridgeDictionary[key];
					ringBridge.EndAtom = atom;
					if (ringBridge.BondType == BondType.Single)
					{
						ringBridge.BondType = list[i];
					}
					bridges.Add(ringBridge);
					bridgeDictionary.Remove(key);
				}
				else
				{
					bridgeDictionary.Add(key, new RingBridge
					{
						StartAtom = atom,
						BondType = list[i]
					});
				}
			}
			return index;
		}

		private static void PlaceAtom(Atom atom, Atom baseAtom, System.Windows.Point position, List<Atom> placedAtoms, List<CisTransPair> cisTransPairs, Fragment fragment, Dictionary<Bond, KeyValuePair<Bond, double>> bondRelativeAngles, List<Ring> rings, Dictionary<Atom, ChiralFlagOrientation> atomChirals)
		{
			if (atom == null || placedAtoms.Contains(atom))
			{
				return;
			}
			Fragment.SetPosition(atom, new AtomPosition
			{
				Mode = AtomPositionMode.Absolute,
				AbsolutePoint = position
			});
			placedAtoms.Add(atom);
			List<Bond> list = new List<Bond>();
			List<Bond> list2 = new List<Bond>();
			foreach (Bond bond in atom.Bonds)
			{
				if (rings.Exists((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(bond)))
				{
					list.Add(bond);
				}
				else
				{
					list2.Add(bond);
				}
			}
			List<Bond> list3 = new List<Bond>(list);
			list3.AddRange(list2);
			if (list3.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (baseAtom != null)
			{
				Bond baseBond = FindBondInFragment(baseAtom, atom);
				bool num = rings.Count((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(baseBond)) > 0;
				List<Bond> extendedBonds = new List<Bond>(list3);
				extendedBonds.Remove(baseBond);
				if (!num && extendedBonds.Count == 2 && rings.Exists((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(extendedBonds[0]) && rg.Contains(extendedBonds[1])))
				{
					Ring ring = rings.FirstOrDefault((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(extendedBonds[0]) && rg.Contains(extendedBonds[1]));
					double num2 = 180.0 - ring.InnerAngle / 2.0;
					SetRelativeBondAngle(extendedBonds[0], baseBond, num2, bondRelativeAngles);
					SetRelativeBondAngle(extendedBonds[1], baseBond, 0.0 - num2, bondRelativeAngles);
				}
				else
				{
					Ring baseRing = null;
					Bond anotherBondInBaseRing = null;
					Bond bond2 = null;
					Bond bond3 = null;
					Ring anotherExtensionRing = GetAnotherExtensionRing(baseBond, extendedBonds, rings, out baseRing, out anotherBondInBaseRing, out bond2, out bond3);
					if (anotherExtensionRing != null)
					{
						double? placedInnerAngle = baseRing.GetPlacedInnerAngle(baseAtom, atom);
						double num3 = (360.0 - anotherExtensionRing.InnerAngle - baseRing.InnerAngle) / 2.0;
						if (placedInnerAngle.HasValue)
						{
							if (placedInnerAngle > 0.0)
							{
								SetRelativeBondAngle(bond2, baseBond, 0.0 - num3, bondRelativeAngles);
								SetRelativeBondAngle(bond3, baseBond, 0.0 - num3 - anotherExtensionRing.InnerAngle, bondRelativeAngles);
							}
							else
							{
								SetRelativeBondAngle(bond2, baseBond, num3, bondRelativeAngles);
								SetRelativeBondAngle(bond3, baseBond, num3 + anotherExtensionRing.InnerAngle, bondRelativeAngles);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							SetRelativeBondAngle(anotherBondInBaseRing, baseBond, 0.0 - baseRing.InnerAngle, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(bond2, baseBond, num3, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(bond3, baseBond, num3 + anotherExtensionRing.InnerAngle, bondRelativeAngles);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						foreach (Bond item in extendedBonds)
						{
							Atom atom2 = ((item.StartAtom == atom) ? item.EndAtom : item.StartAtom);
							Bond processedRelativeRingBond = null;
							double? processedRelatveRingAngle = GetProcessedRelatveRingAngle(fragment, atom2, atom, rings, placedAtoms, ref processedRelativeRingBond);
							if (processedRelatveRingAngle.HasValue)
							{
								SetRelativeBondAngle(item, processedRelativeRingBond, processedRelatveRingAngle.Value, bondRelativeAngles);
							}
						}
						foreach (Bond item2 in extendedBonds)
						{
							Atom another = ((item2.StartAtom == atom) ? item2.EndAtom : item2.StartAtom);
							if (bondRelativeAngles.ContainsKey(item2))
							{
								continue;
							}
							Ring ring2 = rings.FirstOrDefault((Ring rg) => rg.Atoms.Contains(baseAtom) && rg.Atoms.Contains(atom) && rg.Atoms.Contains(another));
							if (ring2 == null)
							{
								continue;
							}
							bool flag = false;
							foreach (Ring item3 in rings.FindAll((Ring rg) => rg.Atoms.Contains(baseAtom) && rg.Atoms.Contains(atom) && !rg.Atoms.Contains(another)))
							{
								double? placedInnerAngle2 = item3.GetPlacedInnerAngle(baseAtom, atom);
								if (placedInnerAngle2.HasValue)
								{
									double angle = ((placedInnerAngle2 > 0.0) ? (0.0 - ring2.InnerAngle) : ring2.InnerAngle);
									SetRelativeBondAngle(item2, baseBond, angle, bondRelativeAngles);
									flag = true;
									break;
								}
							}
							if (!flag)
							{
								SetRelativeBondAngle(item2, baseBond, 0.0 - ring2.InnerAngle, bondRelativeAngles);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			BondQuartrand bondQuartrand = null;
			if (list3.Count == 4)
			{
				if (!list3.Exists((Bond bd) => rings.Exists((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(bd))))
				{
					if (baseAtom == null)
					{
						SetRelativeBondAngle(list3[1], list3[0], 90.0, bondRelativeAngles);
						SetRelativeBondAngle(list3[2], list3[0], -90.0, bondRelativeAngles);
						SetRelativeBondAngle(list3[3], list3[0], 180.0, bondRelativeAngles);
					}
					else
					{
						Bond bond4 = FindBondInFragment(baseAtom, atom);
						List<Bond> list4 = list3.Except(new Bond[1] { bond4 }).ToList();
						ChiralFlagOrientation chiralFlagOrientation = atomChirals[baseAtom];
						if (chiralFlagOrientation != ChiralFlagOrientation.None)
						{
							int num4 = ((chiralFlagOrientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleClockwise || chiralFlagOrientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleClockwise) ? 1 : (-1));
							SetRelativeBondAngle(list4[0], bond4, 90 * num4, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(list4[1], bond4, -90 * num4, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(list4[2], bond4, 180.0, bondRelativeAngles);
						}
						else
						{
							Bond bond5 = list4.OrderByDescending((Bond rb) => GetExtendedAtomsCount(fragment, atom, (rb.StartAtom == atom) ? rb.EndAtom : rb.StartAtom)).First();
							List<Bond> list5 = list4.Except(new Bond[1] { bond5 }).ToList();
							SetRelativeBondAngle(list5[0], bond4, 90.0, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(list5[1], bond4, -90.0, bondRelativeAngles);
							SetRelativeBondAngle(bond5, bond4, 180.0, bondRelativeAngles);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					List<Bond> list6 = list3.FindAll((Bond bd) => rings.Exists((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(bd)));
					if (list6.Count == 2)
					{
						IEnumerable<Bond> source = list3.Except(list6);
						Bond individualBond = source.First();
						Bond individualBond2 = source.Last();
						bondQuartrand = new BondQuartrand
						{
							IndividualBond1 = individualBond,
							IndividualBond2 = individualBond2,
							RingBond1 = list6[0],
							RingBond2 = list6[1]
						};
					}
				}
			}
			List<Bond> list7 = new List<Bond>();
			List<double> list8 = new List<double>();
			Vector vector = new Vector(0.0, -100.0);
			if (baseAtom != null)
			{
				vector = Fragment.GetPosition(baseAtom).AbsolutePoint - position;
			}
			foreach (Bond bond6 in list3)
			{
				Atom atom3 = ((bond6.StartAtom == atom) ? bond6.EndAtom : bond6.StartAtom);
				if (placedAtoms.Contains(atom3))
				{
					Vector toVector = Fragment.GetPosition(atom3).AbsolutePoint - position;
					double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(vector, toVector);
					angleBetween = GetFixedRelativeAngle(angleBetween);
					list8.Add(angleBetween);
					continue;
				}
				if (bondRelativeAngles.ContainsKey(bond6) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Key.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Key.EndAtom))
				{
					Atom atom4 = ((bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Key.StartAtom == atom) ? bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Key.EndAtom : bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Key.StartAtom);
					double value = bondRelativeAngles[bond6].Value;
					if (!list8.Contains(GetFixedRelativeAngle(value)))
					{
						Vector vector2 = new RotateTransform(value).Value.Transform(Fragment.GetPosition(atom4).AbsolutePoint - position);
						List<Ring> list9 = rings.FindAll((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(bond6));
						if (list9.Count == 1)
						{
							Atom adjacentAtom = list9[0].GetAdjacentAtom(atom, atom3);
							if (placedAtoms.Contains(adjacentAtom))
							{
								while (HasAtomInLine(fragment, position + vector2, Fragment.GetPosition(adjacentAtom).AbsolutePoint))
								{
									vector2 = vector2 * (vector2.Length + 100.0) / vector2.Length;
								}
							}
						}
						System.Windows.Point position2 = vector2 + position;
						if (GetAtomAtPosition(fragment, position2) == null)
						{
							PlaceAtom(atom3, atom, position2, placedAtoms, cisTransPairs, fragment, bondRelativeAngles, rings, atomChirals);
							value = GetFixedRelativeAngle(value);
							list8.Add(value);
							continue;
						}
					}
					bondRelativeAngles.Remove(bond6);
				}
				CisTransPair cisTransPair = cisTransPairs.FirstOrDefault((CisTransPair ctp) => (ctp.EndAtom == bond6.StartAtom && ctp.FarEndAtom == bond6.EndAtom) || (ctp.EndAtom == bond6.EndAtom && ctp.FarEndAtom == bond6.StartAtom));
				if (cisTransPair != null && placedAtoms.Contains(cisTransPair.FarStartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(cisTransPair.StartAtom))
				{
					double angleBetween2 = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(Fragment.GetPosition(cisTransPair.FarStartAtom).AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(cisTransPair.StartAtom).AbsolutePoint, Fragment.GetPosition(cisTransPair.EndAtom).AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(cisTransPair.StartAtom).AbsolutePoint);
					double num5 = (cisTransPair.IsSameSide ? angleBetween2 : (360.0 - angleBetween2));
					System.Windows.Point position3 = new RotateTransform(num5).Value.Transform(Fragment.GetPosition(cisTransPair.StartAtom).AbsolutePoint - position) + position;
					PlaceAtom(atom3, atom, position3, placedAtoms, cisTransPairs, fragment, bondRelativeAngles, rings, atomChirals);
					num5 = GetFixedRelativeAngle(num5);
					list8.Add(num5);
				}
				else
				{
					list7.Add(bond6);
				}
			}
			foreach (Bond item4 in list7)
			{
				Atom atom5 = ((item4.StartAtom == atom) ? item4.EndAtom : item4.StartAtom);
				if (!placedAtoms.Contains(atom5))
				{
					if (bondRelativeAngles.ContainsKey(item4) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondRelativeAngles[item4].Key.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondRelativeAngles[item4].Key.EndAtom))
					{
						Atom atom6 = ((bondRelativeAngles[item4].Key.StartAtom == atom) ? bondRelativeAngles[item4].Key.EndAtom : bondRelativeAngles[item4].Key.StartAtom);
						double value2 = bondRelativeAngles[item4].Value;
						System.Windows.Point position4 = new RotateTransform(value2).Value.Transform(Fragment.GetPosition(atom6).AbsolutePoint - position) + position;
						PlaceAtom(atom5, atom, position4, placedAtoms, cisTransPairs, fragment, bondRelativeAngles, rings, atomChirals);
						value2 = GetFixedRelativeAngle(value2);
						list8.Add(value2);
					}
					else
					{
						System.Windows.Point position5 = GetExtendRelativeAngle(fragment, atom, item4, vector, list3, list8, placedAtoms, rings, bondQuartrand, atomChirals) + position;
						PlaceAtom(atom5, atom, position5, placedAtoms, cisTransPairs, fragment, bondRelativeAngles, rings, atomChirals);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private static int GetExtendedAtomsCount(Fragment fragment, Atom baseAtom, Atom targetAtom)
		{
			List<Atom> list = new List<Atom>();
			TravelAlongTree(fragment, baseAtom, targetAtom, list);
			return list.Count;
		}

		private static void TravelAlongTree(Fragment fragment, Atom baseAtom, Atom targetAtom, List<Atom> travelledAtoms)
		{
			if (travelledAtoms.Contains(targetAtom))
			{
				return;
			}
			travelledAtoms.Add(targetAtom);
			foreach (Bond bond in targetAtom.Bonds)
			{
				Atom atom = ((bond.StartAtom == targetAtom) ? bond.EndAtom : bond.StartAtom);
				if (atom != baseAtom)
				{
					TravelAlongTree(fragment, targetAtom, atom, travelledAtoms);
				}
			}
		}

		private static Ring GetAnotherExtensionRing(Bond baseBond, List<Bond> extendedBonds, List<Ring> rings, out Ring baseRing, out Bond anotherBondInBaseRing, out Bond bond1, out Bond bond2)
		{
			baseRing = null;
			anotherBondInBaseRing = (bond1 = (bond2 = null));
			if (extendedBonds.Count == 3)
			{
				Bond bond3 = null;
				foreach (Ring ring2 in rings)
				{
					if (!ring2.Contains(baseBond))
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (Bond extendedBond in extendedBonds)
					{
						if (ring2.Contains(extendedBond))
						{
							bond3 = extendedBond;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (bond3 != null)
					{
						baseRing = ring2;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (bond3 != null)
				{
					List<Bond> remained = new List<Bond>(extendedBonds);
					remained.Remove(bond3);
					Ring ring = rings.FirstOrDefault((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(remained[0]) && rg.Contains(remained[1]));
					if (ring != null)
					{
						anotherBondInBaseRing = bond3;
						bond1 = remained[0];
						bond2 = remained[1];
						return ring;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static double? GetProcessedRelatveRingAngle(Fragment fragment, Atom atom, Atom baseAtom, List<Ring> rings, List<Atom> placedAtoms, ref Bond processedRelativeRingBond)
		{
			foreach (Ring item in rings.Where((Ring rg) => rg.Atoms.Contains(atom) && rg.Atoms.Contains(baseAtom)))
			{
				Atom adjacentAtom = item.GetAdjacentAtom(atom, baseAtom);
				if (placedAtoms.Contains(adjacentAtom))
				{
					Atom adjacentAtom2 = item.GetAdjacentAtom(baseAtom, adjacentAtom);
					if (placedAtoms.Contains(adjacentAtom2))
					{
						double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(Fragment.GetPosition(adjacentAtom2).AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(adjacentAtom).AbsolutePoint, Fragment.GetPosition(baseAtom).AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(adjacentAtom).AbsolutePoint);
						processedRelativeRingBond = FindBondInFragment(baseAtom, adjacentAtom);
						return angleBetween;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static void SetRelativeBondAngle(Bond targetBond, Bond baseBond, double angle, Dictionary<Bond, KeyValuePair<Bond, double>> bondRelativeAngles)
		{
			bondRelativeAngles[targetBond] = new KeyValuePair<Bond, double>(baseBond, angle);
		}

		private static void FindRingByRingBridge(Fragment fragment, RingBridge bridge, List<Ring> rings, List<Atom> aromaticAtoms)
		{
			List<RingSegment> item = new List<RingSegment>
			{
				new RingSegment
				{
					StartAtom = bridge.EndAtom,
					EndAtom = bridge.StartAtom,
					Power = 0
				}
			};
			List<RingSegment> item2 = new List<RingSegment>
			{
				new RingSegment
				{
					StartAtom = bridge.StartAtom,
					EndAtom = bridge.EndAtom,
					Power = 0
				}
			};
			Ring ring = FindRingInBroadFirst(fragment, bridge, new List<List<RingSegment>> { item }, new List<List<RingSegment>> { item2 }, rings);
			if (ring == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(ring.Atoms.First()) > fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(ring.Atoms.Last()))
			{
				List<Atom> list = ring.Atoms.ToList();
				ring.Atoms.Clear();
				foreach (Atom item3 in list)
				{
					ring.Atoms.Insert(0, item3);
				}
			}
			ring.ClosedBondType = bridge.BondType;
			ring.IsAromatic = ring.Atoms.Except(aromaticAtoms).Count() == 0;
			rings.Add(ring);
		}

		private static Ring FindRingInBroadFirst(Fragment fragment, RingBridge bridge, List<List<RingSegment>> startSegmentStack, List<List<RingSegment>> endSegmentStack, List<Ring> rings)
		{
			bool hasNew = false;
			Ring ring = FindRingFromTopSegments(fragment, bridge, startSegmentStack, endSegmentStack, rings, ref hasNew);
			if (ring != null)
			{
				return ring;
			}
			bool hasNew2 = false;
			Ring ring2 = FindRingFromTopSegments(fragment, bridge, endSegmentStack, startSegmentStack, rings, ref hasNew2);
			if (ring2 != null)
			{
				return ring2;
			}
			if (hasNew || hasNew2)
			{
				return FindRingInBroadFirst(fragment, bridge, startSegmentStack, endSegmentStack, rings);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static Ring FindRingFromTopSegments(Fragment fragment, RingBridge bridge, List<List<RingSegment>> startSegmentStack, List<List<RingSegment>> endSegmentStack, List<Ring> rings, ref bool hasNew)
		{
			hasNew = false;
			List<RingSegment> list = new List<RingSegment>();
			List<Atom> list2 = new List<Atom>(from ssl in startSegmentStack
				from sl in ssl
				select sl.EndAtom);
			List<Atom> list3 = new List<Atom>();
			foreach (RingSegment item in startSegmentStack[startSegmentStack.Count - 1])
			{
				foreach (Bond bond in item.EndAtom.Bonds)
				{
					if ((bond.StartAtom == item.StartAtom && bond.EndAtom == item.EndAtom) || (bond.StartAtom == item.EndAtom && bond.EndAtom == item.StartAtom))
					{
						continue;
					}
					Atom atom = ((bond.StartAtom == item.EndAtom) ? bond.EndAtom : bond.StartAtom);
					if (list2.Contains(atom) || list3.Contains(atom))
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (List<RingSegment> item2 in endSegmentStack)
					{
						foreach (RingSegment item3 in item2)
						{
							if (atom == item3.EndAtom)
							{
								Ring ring = new Ring();
								for (RingSegment ringSegment = item; ringSegment != null; ringSegment = ringSegment.Parent)
								{
									ring.Atoms.Insert(0, ringSegment.EndAtom);
								}
								for (RingSegment ringSegment2 = item3; ringSegment2 != null; ringSegment2 = ringSegment2.Parent)
								{
									ring.Atoms.Add(ringSegment2.EndAtom);
								}
								if (!rings.Exists((Ring rg) => rg.Atoms.Except(ring.Atoms).Count() == 0))
								{
									return ring;
								}
								break;
							}
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						list.Add(new RingSegment
						{
							StartAtom = item.EndAtom,
							EndAtom = atom,
							Power = item.Power + 1,
							Parent = item
						});
					}
				}
			}
			hasNew = list.Count > 0;
			if (hasNew)
			{
				startSegmentStack.Add(list);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static Vector GetExtendRelativeAngle(Fragment fragment, Atom baseAtom, Bond bond, Vector baseVector, List<Bond> bonds, List<double> usedAngles, List<Atom> placedAtoms, List<Ring> rings, BondQuartrand bondQuartrand, Dictionary<Atom, ChiralFlagOrientation> atomChirals)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			AtomPosition position = Fragment.GetPosition(baseAtom);
			Atom firstAtom = ((bond.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bond.EndAtom : bond.StartAtom);
			if (bonds.Count == 2)
			{
				Bond bond2 = ((bonds[0] == bond) ? bonds[1] : bonds[0]);
				if (bond2.Type.Valence + bond.Type.Valence == 4)
				{
					num = 180.0;
				}
				else
				{
					Atom atom = ((bond2.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bond2.EndAtom : bond2.StartAtom);
					if (placedAtoms.Contains(atom))
					{
						List<Bond> list = atom.Bonds.ToList();
						if (list.Count == 2)
						{
							Bond bond3 = ((list[0] == bond2) ? list[1] : list[0]);
							if (bond3.Type.Valence + bond2.Type.Valence != 4)
							{
								AtomPosition position2 = Fragment.GetPosition((bond3.StartAtom == atom) ? bond3.EndAtom : bond3.StartAtom);
								AtomPosition position3 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom);
								if (position2 != null && position3 != null)
								{
									Vector vector = position3.AbsolutePoint - position2.AbsolutePoint;
									if (GetAtomAtPosition(fragment, position.AbsolutePoint + vector) == null)
									{
										return vector;
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else if (list.Count == 4)
						{
							Vector fromVector = position.AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint;
							foreach (Bond item in list)
							{
								if (item == bond)
								{
									continue;
								}
								Atom atom2 = ((item.StartAtom == atom) ? item.EndAtom : item.StartAtom);
								if (!placedAtoms.Contains(atom2))
								{
									continue;
								}
								Vector toVector = Fragment.GetPosition(atom).AbsolutePoint - Fragment.GetPosition(atom2).AbsolutePoint;
								if (Math.Abs(AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(fromVector, toVector)) <= 5.0)
								{
									if (atom2.Bonds.Count == 2)
									{
										Bond bond4 = ((atom2.Bonds[0] == item) ? atom2.Bonds[1] : atom2.Bonds[0]);
										if (bond4.Type.Valence + item.Type.Valence != 4)
										{
											AtomPosition position4 = Fragment.GetPosition((bond4.StartAtom == atom2) ? bond4.EndAtom : bond4.StartAtom);
											AtomPosition position5 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom2);
											if (position4 != null && position5 != null)
											{
												Vector vector2 = position5.AbsolutePoint - position4.AbsolutePoint;
												if (GetAtomAtPosition(fragment, position.AbsolutePoint + vector2) == null)
												{
													return vector2;
												}
												break;
											}
											break;
										}
										break;
									}
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (bonds.Count == 3)
			{
				List<Bond> list2 = bonds.Except(new Bond[1] { bond }).ToList();
				Bond bond5 = list2[0];
				Bond bond6 = list2[1];
				if (placedAtoms.Contains(bond5.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bond5.EndAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bond6.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bond6.EndAtom))
				{
					Atom atom3 = ((bond5.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bond5.EndAtom : bond5.StartAtom);
					Atom atom4 = ((bond6.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bond6.EndAtom : bond6.StartAtom);
					Vector vector3 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom3).AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint;
					Vector vector4 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom4).AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint;
					Vector vector5 = -(vector3 + vector4);
					vector5 = vector5 * 100.0 / vector5.Length;
					List<Ring> list3 = rings.FindAll((Ring rg) => rg.Contains(bond));
					if (list3.Count == 1)
					{
						Ring ring = list3[0];
						Atom adjacentAtom = ring.GetAdjacentAtom(firstAtom, baseAtom);
						if (placedAtoms.Contains(adjacentAtom))
						{
							Bond bond7 = FindBondInFragment(adjacentAtom, baseAtom);
							if (bond7 == bond5 || bond7 == bond6)
							{
								Vector vector6 = ((bond7 == bond5) ? vector3 : vector4);
								double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(vector6, vector5);
								if (!AngleHelper.IsRoughlyEqual(Math.Abs(angleBetween), ring.InnerAngle))
								{
									return new RotateTransform((double)Math.Sign(angleBetween) * ring.InnerAngle).Value.Transform(vector6);
								}
							}
						}
					}
					if (GetAtomAtPosition(fragment, position.AbsolutePoint + vector5) != null)
					{
						vector5 = -vector5;
					}
					return vector5;
				}
			}
			if (bonds.Count == 4 && bondQuartrand != null && (bond == bondQuartrand.IndividualBond1 || bond == bondQuartrand.IndividualBond2) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondQuartrand.RingBond1.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondQuartrand.RingBond1.EndAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondQuartrand.RingBond2.StartAtom) && placedAtoms.Contains(bondQuartrand.RingBond2.EndAtom))
			{
				Atom atom5 = ((bondQuartrand.RingBond1.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bondQuartrand.RingBond1.EndAtom : bondQuartrand.RingBond1.StartAtom);
				Atom atom6 = ((bondQuartrand.RingBond2.StartAtom == baseAtom) ? bondQuartrand.RingBond2.EndAtom : bondQuartrand.RingBond2.StartAtom);
				Vector vector7 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom5).AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint;
				vector7 = vector7 * 100.0 / vector7.Length;
				Vector vector8 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom6).AbsolutePoint - position.AbsolutePoint;
				vector8 = vector8 * 100.0 / vector8.Length;
				double angleBetween2 = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(vector8, vector7);
				double num2 = (360.0 - Math.Abs(angleBetween2)) / 3.0;
				if (bond == bondQuartrand.IndividualBond1 && !bondQuartrand.Calculated1)
				{
					if (angleBetween2 > 0.0)
					{
						return new RotateTransform(num2).Value.Transform(vector7);
					}
					return new RotateTransform(0.0 - num2).Value.Transform(vector7);
				}
				if (bond == bondQuartrand.IndividualBond2 && !bondQuartrand.Calculated2)
				{
					if (angleBetween2 < 0.0)
					{
						return new RotateTransform(num2).Value.Transform(vector8);
					}
					return new RotateTransform(0.0 - num2).Value.Transform(vector8);
				}
			}
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				foreach (double bondAngleOption in BondAngleOptions)
				{
					if (!usedAngles.Contains(bondAngleOption))
					{
						Vector vector9 = new RotateTransform(bondAngleOption).Value.Transform(baseVector);
						if (GetAtomNearPosition(fragment, position.AbsolutePoint + vector9) == null)
						{
							usedAngles.Add(bondAngleOption);
							return vector9;
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (double bondAngleOption2 in BondAngleOptions)
				{
					if (!usedAngles.Contains(bondAngleOption2))
					{
						Vector vector10 = new RotateTransform(bondAngleOption2).Value.Transform(baseVector);
						if (GetAtomAtPosition(fragment, position.AbsolutePoint + vector10) == null)
						{
							usedAngles.Add(bondAngleOption2);
							return vector10;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				for (int num3 = 0; num3 < bonds.Count; num3++)
				{
					num = 180 * num3 / bonds.Count;
					if (!usedAngles.Contains(num))
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!usedAngles.Contains(num))
			{
				usedAngles.Add(num);
			}
			return new RotateTransform(num).Value.Transform(baseVector);
		}

		private static Atom GetAtomNearPosition(Fragment fragment, System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				AtomPosition position2 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom);
				if (position2 != null && (position2.AbsolutePoint - position).Length < 50.0)
				{
					return atom;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static Atom GetAtomAtPosition(Fragment fragment, System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				AtomPosition position2 = Fragment.GetPosition(atom);
				if (position2 != null && (position2.AbsolutePoint - position).Length < 1.0)
				{
					return atom;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static bool HasAtomInLine(Fragment fragment, System.Windows.Point startPosition, System.Windows.Point endPosition)
		{
			Vector vector = endPosition - startPosition;
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				AtomPosition position = Fragment.GetPosition(atom);
				if (position != null)
				{
					Vector vector2 = position.AbsolutePoint - startPosition;
					Vector vector3 = endPosition - position.AbsolutePoint;
					if (vector2.Length > 1.0 && vector3.Length > 1.0 && AngleHelper.IsRoughlyEqual(vector2.Length + vector3.Length, vector.Length))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static double GetFixedRelativeAngle(double relativeAngle)
		{
			relativeAngle = (relativeAngle % 360.0 + 360.0) % 360.0;
			double num = relativeAngle % 15.0;
			if (num <= 5.0)
			{
				return relativeAngle - num;
			}
			if (num >= 10.0)
			{
				return relativeAngle - num + 15.0;
			}
			return relativeAngle;
		}

		private static void SetBondRelativeAngle(Fragment fragment, Atom startAtom, Atom endAtom, Atom baseStartAtom, Atom baseEndAtom, double angle, Dictionary<Bond, KeyValuePair<Bond, double>> bondRelativeAngles)
		{
			Bond bond = FindBondInFragment(startAtom, endAtom);
			Bond bond2 = FindBondInFragment(baseStartAtom, baseEndAtom);
			if (bond != null && bond2 != null)
			{
				bondRelativeAngles[bond] = new KeyValuePair<Bond, double>(bond2, angle);
			}
		}

		private static void SetBondWithDefaultType(Fragment fragment, Atom startAtom, Atom endAtom, BondType bondType)
		{
			Bond bond = FindBondInFragment(startAtom, endAtom);
			if (bond == null)
			{
				bond = new Bond
				{
					StartAtom = startAtom,
					EndAtom = endAtom,
					Type = bondType
				};
				fragment.Bonds.Add(bond);
			}
		}

		private static void SetBondWithType(Fragment fragment, Atom startAtom, Atom endAtom, BondType bondType)
		{
			Bond bond = FindBondInFragment(startAtom, endAtom);
			if (bond == null)
			{
				bond = new Bond
				{
					StartAtom = startAtom,
					EndAtom = endAtom
				};
				fragment.Bonds.Add(bond);
			}
			bond.Type = bondType;
		}

		private static Bond FindBondInFragment(Atom startAtom, Atom endAtom)
		{
			return startAtom.Bonds.Intersect(endAtom.Bonds).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		private static List<int> ReadRingNumbers(string line, ref int index, List<BondType> connectingBondTypes)
		{
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			while (index < line.Length)
			{
				char c = line[index];
				BondType item = BondType.Single;
				if (c == '-' || c == '=' || c == ':' || c == '#')
				{
					char c2 = line[index + 1];
					if (char.IsNumber(c2) || c2 == '%')
					{
						switch (c)
						{
						case '=':
							item = BondType.Double;
							break;
						case '#':
							item = BondType.Tripple;
							break;
						}
						index++;
					}
				}
				c = line[index];
				if (char.IsNumber(c))
				{
					list.Add(int.Parse(c.ToString()));
					connectingBondTypes.Add(item);
					index++;
					continue;
				}
				if (c != '%')
				{
					break;
				}
				index++;
				char c3 = line[index];
				if (!char.IsNumber(c3))
				{
					break;
				}
				index++;
				char c4 = line[index];
				if (char.IsNumber(c4))
				{
					int num = int.Parse(c3.ToString());
					int num2 = int.Parse(c4.ToString());
					list.Add(num * 10 + num2);
					connectingBondTypes.Add(item);
					index++;
					continue;
				}
				int item2 = int.Parse(c3.ToString());
				list.Add(item2);
				connectingBondTypes.Add(item);
				if (c4 != '-' && c4 != '=' && c4 != ':' && c4 != '#')
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static Bond FindLastBond(Fragment fragment, List<Atom> currentAtomGroup)
		{
			if (currentAtomGroup.Count >= 2)
			{
				Atom startAtom = currentAtomGroup[currentAtomGroup.Count - 2];
				Atom endAtom = currentAtomGroup[currentAtomGroup.Count - 1];
				return fragment.Bonds.FirstOrDefault((Bond bd) => bd.StartAtom == startAtom && bd.EndAtom == endAtom);
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static Atom ReadAtom(string line, int startIndex, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, BondType lastBondType, ref int nextIndex, ref bool isAromatic, out ChiralFlag chiralFlag, out int? declaredHydrogenCount)
		{
			string text = line.Substring(startIndex, 1);
			string atomNameTwo = ((line.Length > startIndex + 1) ? line.Substring(startIndex, 2) : null);
			if (text == "[")
			{
				int num = line.IndexOf(']', startIndex);
				Ensure(num > startIndex);
				int num2 = 0;
				int i;
				for (i = startIndex + 1; i < num; i++)
				{
					char c = line[i];
					if (!char.IsNumber(c))
					{
						break;
					}
					num2 = num2 * 10 + int.Parse(c.ToString());
				}
				string atomName = line.Substring(i, 1);
				string atomNameTwo2 = ((num > i + 1) ? line.Substring(i, 2) : null);
				int charsCount = 0;
				bool isAromatic2 = false;
				Atom newAtomFromResource = GetNewAtomFromResource(reference, atomName, atomNameTwo2, out charsCount, out isAromatic2);
				if (num2 != 0)
				{
					newAtomFromResource.Mass = num2;
				}
				i += charsCount;
				isAromatic = isAromatic2;
				ChiralFlagOrientation chiralFlagOrientation = ChiralFlagOrientation.None;
				chiralFlag = null;
				if (line[i] == '@')
				{
					i++;
					bool flag = false;
					string text2 = ((num > i + 1) ? line.Substring(i, 2) : null);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && ChiralConfigurations.Contains(text2.ToUpper()))
					{
						i += 2;
						chiralFlagOrientation = ((int.Parse(line.Substring(i, 1)) % 2 != 1) ? ((lastBondType != BondType.Double) ? ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleClockwise : ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleClockwise) : ((lastBondType == BondType.Double) ? ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleAnticlockwise : ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleAnticlockwise));
						i++;
						flag = true;
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						int num3 = 1;
						for (; i < num && line[i] == '@'; i++)
						{
							num3++;
						}
						chiralFlagOrientation = ((num3 % 2 != 1) ? ((lastBondType != BondType.Double) ? ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleClockwise : ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleClockwise) : ((lastBondType == BondType.Double) ? ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleAnticlockwise : ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleAnticlockwise));
					}
					if (chiralFlagOrientation != ChiralFlagOrientation.None)
					{
						chiralFlag = new ChiralFlag
						{
							Orientation = chiralFlagOrientation
						};
					}
				}
				declaredHydrogenCount = null;
				if (line[i] == 'H')
				{
					if (chiralFlag != null)
					{
						chiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
					}
					declaredHydrogenCount = 1;
					i++;
					int num4 = i;
					for (; i < num && char.IsNumber(line[i]); i++)
					{
					}
					if (i > num4 && int.TryParse(line.Substring(num4, i - num4), out var result))
					{
						declaredHydrogenCount = result;
					}
				}
				if (line[i] == '-' || line[i] == '+')
				{
					int num5 = ((line[i] != '-') ? 1 : (-1));
					i++;
					if (char.IsNumber(line[i]))
					{
						int num6 = 0;
						for (; i < num; i++)
						{
							char c2 = line[i];
							if (!char.IsNumber(c2))
							{
								break;
							}
							num6 = num6 * 10 + int.Parse(c2.ToString());
						}
						num5 *= num6;
					}
					else
					{
						for (; i < num; i++)
						{
							switch (line[i])
							{
							case '-':
								num5--;
								continue;
							case '+':
								num5++;
								continue;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					newAtomFromResource.ChargeCount = num5;
				}
				nextIndex = num + 1;
				return newAtomFromResource;
			}
			int charsCount2 = 0;
			bool isAromatic3 = false;
			Atom newAtomFromResource2 = GetNewAtomFromResource(reference, text, atomNameTwo, out charsCount2, out isAromatic3);
			nextIndex = startIndex + charsCount2;
			isAromatic = isAromatic3;
			chiralFlag = null;
			declaredHydrogenCount = null;
			return newAtomFromResource2;
		}

		private static BondType ReadBondType(string line, int index, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, ChiralFlag lastChiralFlag, bool isNewBranch, int parentheses, ref int nextIndex, ref CisTransFlag cisTransFlag, out double? chiralAngle)
		{
			char c = line[index];
			cisTransFlag = CisTransFlag.None;
			chiralAngle = null;
			switch (c)
			{
			case '/':
				cisTransFlag = ((!isNewBranch) ? CisTransFlag.Slash : CisTransFlag.BackSlash);
				index++;
				break;
			case '\\':
				cisTransFlag = (isNewBranch ? CisTransFlag.Slash : CisTransFlag.BackSlash);
				index++;
				break;
			}
			switch (line[index])
			{
			case ':':
				nextIndex = index + 1;
				return BondType.Single;
			case '=':
				nextIndex = index + 1;
				return BondType.Double;
			case '#':
				nextIndex = index + 1;
				return BondType.Tripple;
			case '-':
				index++;
				break;
			}
			nextIndex = index;
			if (lastChiralFlag != null && lastChiralFlag.Orientation != ChiralFlagOrientation.None && ((isNewBranch && parentheses == lastChiralFlag.ParenthesesIndex + 1) || parentheses == lastChiralFlag.ParenthesesIndex))
			{
				if (lastChiralFlag.Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleAnticlockwise)
				{
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 0)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 240.0;
						return BondType.UpStereo;
					}
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 1)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 120.0;
						return BondType.Single;
					}
					lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
					chiralAngle = 180.0;
					return BondType.DownStereo;
				}
				if (lastChiralFlag.Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.SingleClockwise)
				{
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 0)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 240.0;
						return BondType.UpStereo;
					}
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 1)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 180.0;
						return BondType.DownStereo;
					}
					lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
					chiralAngle = 120.0;
					return BondType.Single;
				}
				if (lastChiralFlag.Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleAnticlockwise)
				{
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 0)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 120.0;
						return BondType.DownStereo;
					}
					lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
					chiralAngle = 240.0;
					return BondType.UpStereo;
				}
				if (lastChiralFlag.Orientation == ChiralFlagOrientation.DoubleClockwise)
				{
					if (lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections == 0)
					{
						lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
						chiralAngle = 240.0;
						return BondType.UpStereo;
					}
					lastChiralFlag.UsedConnections++;
					chiralAngle = 120.0;
					return BondType.DownStereo;
				}
			}
			return BondType.Single;
		}

		private static Atom GetNewAtomFromResource(MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, string atomName, string atomNameTwo, out int charsCount, out bool isAromatic)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(atomNameTwo))
			{
				if (atomNameTwo.Length < 2)
				{
					atomNameTwo = string.Empty;
				}
				else
				{
					char c = atomNameTwo[0];
					char c2 = atomNameTwo[1];
					if (c2 == 'H')
					{
						atomNameTwo = string.Empty;
					}
					else if (!char.IsLetter(c2))
					{
						atomNameTwo = string.Empty;
					}
					else if (!char.IsLower(c2))
					{
						atomNameTwo = string.Empty;
					}
					else if (char.IsLower(c) && OrganicElements.Count((string oe) => oe.ToUpper() == atomNameTwo.ToUpper()) == 0)
					{
						atomNameTwo = string.Empty;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(atomNameTwo))
			{
				return GetNewAtomFromResource(reference, atomNameTwo, out charsCount, out isAromatic);
			}
			return GetNewAtomFromResource(reference, atomName, out charsCount, out isAromatic);
		}

		private static Atom GetNewAtomFromResource(MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, string atomName, out int charsCount, out bool isAromatic)
		{
			Ensure(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(atomName));
			bool flag = char.IsLower(atomName[0]);
			if (atomName.Length == 1)
			{
				atomName = atomName.ToUpper();
			}
			else if (atomName.Length > 1 && char.IsLower(atomName[1]))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				string text = atomName;
				foreach (char c in text)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append((stringBuilder.Length == 0) ? char.ToUpper(c) : char.ToLower(c));
				}
				Atom atom = reference.FindResourceAtomWithName(stringBuilder.ToString());
				if (atom != null)
				{
					isAromatic = flag && OrganicElements.Contains(atom.Name);
					charsCount = 2;
					return atom.Clone();
				}
			}
			Atom atom2 = reference.FindResourceAtomWithName(atomName.Substring(0, 1).ToUpper());
			Ensure(atom2 != null);
			isAromatic = flag && OrganicElements.Contains(atom2.Name);
			charsCount = 1;
			return atom2.Clone();
		}

		private static void Ensure(bool check)
		{
			if (!check)
			{
				throw new FileFormatException();
			}
		}
	}

	{
		string[] IFragmentWriter.Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public enum FragmentFileFormat
	{
		MoleFile,
		SmilesFile
	}
	public interface IFragmentReader
	{
		Fragment Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference);
	}
	public static class FragmentReader
	{
		private const string MoleFileExtension = ".mol";

		private const string SmilesFileExtension = ".smi";

		public static Fragment Read(string filePath, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("filePath");
			}
			if (!File.Exists(filePath))
			{
				throw new FileNotFoundException();
			}
			if (filePath.ToLower().EndsWith(".smi"))
			{
				return Read(filePath, reference, FragmentFileFormat.SmilesFile);
			}
			return Read(filePath, reference, FragmentFileFormat.MoleFile);
		}

		public static Fragment Read(string filePath, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("filePath");
			}
			if (!File.Exists(filePath))
			{
				throw new FileNotFoundException();
			}
			return Read(File.ReadAllLines(filePath), reference, fileFormat);
		}

		public static Fragment Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (contents == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("contents");
			}
			return Read(contents, reference, FragmentFileFormat.MoleFile);
		}

		public static Fragment Read(string[] contents, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (contents == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("contents");
			}
			return GetFragmentReader(fileFormat).Read(contents, reference);
		}

		private static IFragmentReader GetFragmentReader(FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (fileFormat == FragmentFileFormat.SmilesFile)
			{
				return new SmilesFileReader();
			}
			return new MoleFileReader();
		}
	}
	public interface IFragmentWriter
	{
		string[] Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference);
	}
	public static class FragmentWriter
	{
		private const double ScaleFactor = 0.01;

		private const int CarbonPeriodicId = 6;

		private const int HydrogenPeriodicId = 1;

		public static void Save(Fragment fragment, string filePath, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("filePath");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			Save(fragment, filePath, reference, FragmentFileFormat.MoleFile);
		}

		public static void Save(Fragment fragment, string filePath, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filePath))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("filePath");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			if (File.Exists(filePath) && File.GetAttributes(filePath).HasFlag(FileAttributes.ReadOnly))
			{
				MDMessageBox.Show(filePath + "\n" + Resources.WriteReadonlyFileWarningMessage, Resources.WriteReadonlyFileWarningTitle, MDMessageBoxButton.OK, MDMessageBoxImage.Exclamation);
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				File.WriteAllLines(filePath, Save(fragment, reference, fileFormat));
			}
			catch (UnauthorizedAccessException ex)
			{
				MDMessageBox.Show(ex.Message);
			}
		}

		public static string[] Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			return Save(fragment, reference, FragmentFileFormat.MoleFile);
		}

		public static string[] Save(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference, FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			return GetFragmentWriter(fileFormat).Save(fragment, reference);
		}

		public static List<KeyValuePair<string, int>> GetFragmentAtomsCount(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			List<KeyValuePair<string, int>> list = new List<KeyValuePair<string, int>>();
			string hydrogenName = AtomElement.GetHydrogenName(reference);
			int hydrogenCount = GetHydrogenCount(fragment, hydrogenName);
			if (IsOrganicCompound(fragment))
			{
				IEnumerable<Atom> source = fragment.Atoms.Where((Atom atom) => atom.PeriodicTableId == 6);
				list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(source.First().Name, source.Count()));
				list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(hydrogenName, hydrogenCount));
				foreach (var item in from atom in fragment.Atoms
					group atom by atom.Name into g
					let m = new
					{
						Atom = g.FirstOrDefault(),
						Count = g.Count()
					}
					where m.Atom.PeriodicTableId != 6 && m.Atom.PeriodicTableId != 1
					orderby m.Atom.Name
					select m)
				{
					list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(item.Atom.Name, item.Count));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				var enumerable = from atom in fragment.Atoms
					group atom by atom.Name into g
					let m = new
					{
						Atom = g.FirstOrDefault(),
						Count = g.Count(),
						Type = g.FirstOrDefault().Type
					}
					where m.Atom.Name != hydrogenName
					orderby m.Atom.Name
					select m;
				if (enumerable.Count() <= 0)
				{
					list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(hydrogenName, hydrogenCount));
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (var item2 in enumerable)
					{
						if (item2.Type == ParticleType.Ion)
						{
							list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(item2.Atom.Name, item2.Count));
						}
					}
					if (hydrogenCount > 0)
					{
						list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(hydrogenName, hydrogenCount));
					}
					foreach (var item3 in enumerable)
					{
						if (item3.Type == ParticleType.Atom)
						{
							list.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, int>(item3.Atom.Name, item3.Count));
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static string GetFragmentNameByAtoms(Fragment fragment, MolecularDrawingCanvas reference)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (reference == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("reference");
			}
			List<KeyValuePair<string, int>> fragmentAtomsCount = GetFragmentAtomsCount(fragment, reference);
			StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, int> item in fragmentAtomsCount)
			{
				AppendAtomIntoFormular(ref sb, item.Key, item.Value);
			}
			return sb.ToString();
		}

		private static void AppendAtomIntoFormular(ref StringBuilder sb, string atomName, int count)
		{
			if (count > 0)
			{
				sb.Append(atomName);
			}
			if (count > 1)
			{
				sb.Append(count);
			}
		}

		private static bool IsOrganicCompound(Fragment fragment)
		{
			IEnumerable<int> source = fragment.Atoms.Select((Atom atom) => atom.PeriodicTableId);
			if (source.Contains(6))
			{
				if (source.Contains(1))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (fragment.Atoms.Count((Atom atom) => atom.HydrogenCount > 0) > 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static int GetHydrogenCount(Fragment fragment, string hydrogenName)
		{
			int num = fragment.Atoms.Sum((Atom atom) => atom.HydrogenCount);
			int num2 = fragment.Atoms.Count((Atom atom) => atom.Name == hydrogenName);
			return num + num2;
		}

		private static IFragmentWriter GetFragmentWriter(FragmentFileFormat fileFormat)
		{
			if (fileFormat == FragmentFileFormat.SmilesFile)
			{
				return new SmilesFileWriter();
			}
			return new MoleFileWriter();
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Interop
{
	public interface IMoleculeDrawingEngine
	{
		void SimplyOpenMolecularEditor();

		string[] OpenMolecularEditor(string[] molefileContentAsLines, bool forTDE, ref string fragmentName, out int fragmentConsistency, out bool changed);

		string[] CheckMolecularEditor(string[] molefileContentAsLines, out int fragmentConsistency);

		object CreateBitmapForMolefile(string[] molefileContentAsLines);

		object CreateBitmapForMolefileInSpecialSize(string[] molefileContentAsLines, double width, double height);

		void GenerateConnectionTable(string[] molefileContentAsLines, out int[] startAtomIndexes, out int[] endAtomIndexes, out string[] startAtomSymbols, out string[] endAtomSymbols, out int[] bondTypes, out string[] formulaAtomSymbols, out int[] formularAtomCounts);

		string[] GenerateMolStringsFromSMILES(string smiles);
	}
	internal class AspenMoleculeDrawingEngine : IMoleculeDrawingEngine
	{
		private class PictureAxHost : AxHost
		{
			private PictureAxHost(string clsid)
				: base(clsid)
			{
			}

			public static object GetIPictureFromImage(System.Drawing.Image drawingImage)
			{
				return AxHost.GetIPictureFromPicture(drawingImage);
			}
		}

		public void SimplyOpenMolecularEditor()
		{
			NativeMethods.ShowWindow(new MainWindow(), showAsDialog: true);
		}

		public string[] OpenMolecularEditor(string[] molefileContentAsLines, bool forTDE, ref string fragmentName, out int fragmentConsistency, out bool changed)
		{
			MainWindow mainWindow = new MainWindow(fragmentName, molefileContentAsLines, forTDE, shouldCheckConsistencyWhenClosing: true);
			NativeMethods.ShowWindow(mainWindow, showAsDialog: true);
			string[] exportFragmentLines = mainWindow.ExportFragmentLines;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(fragmentName) && exportFragmentLines.Length != 0)
			{
				fragmentName = exportFragmentLines[0];
			}
			fragmentConsistency = (int)mainWindow.FragmentConsistency;
			if (!mainWindow.IsDrawingCanvasDirty && !mainWindow.WasEverSaved && !mainWindow.WasEverImportedAfterLoaded)
			{
				changed = false;
			}
			else
			{
				changed = !AreLinesEqual(molefileContentAsLines, exportFragmentLines);
			}
			return exportFragmentLines;
			static bool AreLinesEqual(IEnumerable<string> firstLines, IEnumerable<string> secondLines)
			{
				if (firstLines == null && secondLines == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (firstLines == null || secondLines == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				List<string> list = firstLines.ToList();
				List<string> list2 = secondLines.ToList();
				int num = Math.Min(list.Count, list2.Count);
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					if (!AreStringsEqual(list[i], list2[i]))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				for (int j = num; j < list.Count; j++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(list[j]))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				for (int k = num; k < list2.Count; k++)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(list2[k]))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
			static bool AreStringsEqual(string first, string second)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(first) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(second))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(first) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(second))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return string.Equals(first.Trim(), second.Trim());
			}
		}

		public string[] CheckMolecularEditor(string[] molefileContentAsLines, out int fragmentConsistency)
		{
			MolecularDrawingCanvas reference = new MolecularDrawingCanvas();
			Fragment fragment = FragmentReader.Read(molefileContentAsLines, reference);
			fragmentConsistency = (int)fragment.Consistency;
			return null;
		}

		public object CreateBitmapForMolefile(string[] molefileContentAsLines)
		{
			using MemoryStream stream = new MemoryStream();
			IMoleculeImage moleculeImage = MolecularEditorNetEngine.CreateMoleculeRenderEngine().GetMoleculeImage(molefileContentAsLines);
			BmpBitmapEncoder bmpBitmapEncoder = new BmpBitmapEncoder();
			bmpBitmapEncoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(moleculeImage.BitmapImage));
			bmpBitmapEncoder.Save(stream);
			return PictureAxHost.GetIPictureFromImage(System.Drawing.Image.FromStream(stream));
		}

		public object CreateBitmapForMolefileInSpecialSize(string[] molefileContentAsLines, double width, double height)
		{
			using MemoryStream stream = new MemoryStream();
			IMoleculeImage moleculeImage = MolecularEditorNetEngine.CreateMoleculeRenderEngine().GetMoleculeImage(molefileContentAsLines);
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmapLessThanTargetSize = ImageHelper.GetRenderTargetBitmapLessThanTargetSize(moleculeImage.VectorImage, moleculeImage.Size, new System.Windows.Size(width, height));
			BmpBitmapEncoder bmpBitmapEncoder = new BmpBitmapEncoder();
			bmpBitmapEncoder.Frames.Add(BitmapFrame.Create(renderTargetBitmapLessThanTargetSize));
			bmpBitmapEncoder.Save(stream);
			return PictureAxHost.GetIPictureFromImage(System.Drawing.Image.FromStream(stream));
		}

		public void GenerateConnectionTable(string[] molefileContentAsLines, out int[] startAtomIndexes, out int[] endAtomIndexes, out string[] startAtomSymbols, out string[] endAtomSymbols, out int[] bondTypes, out string[] formulaAtomSymbols, out int[] formularAtomCounts)
		{
			MolecularDrawingCanvas reference = new MolecularDrawingCanvas();
			Fragment fragment = FragmentReader.Read(molefileContentAsLines, reference);
			FilterAtoms(fragment, (Atom x) => x.PeriodicTableId == 1, updateHydrogen: true);
			FilterAtoms(fragment, (Atom x) => x.PeriodicTableId < 0, updateHydrogen: false);
			List<int> list = new List<int>();
			List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
			List<int> list5 = new List<int>();
			List<string> list6 = new List<string>();
			List<int> list7 = new List<int>();
			foreach (Bond bond in fragment.Bonds)
			{
				list.Add(fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(bond.StartAtom) + 1);
				list2.Add(fragment.Atoms.IndexOf(bond.EndAtom) + 1);
				list3.Add(bond.StartAtom.Name);
				list4.Add(bond.EndAtom.Name);
				list5.Add(bond.Type.Valence);
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, int> item in FragmentWriter.GetFragmentAtomsCount(fragment, reference))
			{
				list6.Add(item.Key);
				list7.Add(item.Value);
			}
			startAtomIndexes = list.ToArray();
			endAtomIndexes = list2.ToArray();
			startAtomSymbols = list3.ToArray();
			endAtomSymbols = list4.ToArray();
			bondTypes = list5.ToArray();
			formulaAtomSymbols = list6.ToArray();
			formularAtomCounts = list7.ToArray();
		}

		private static void FilterAtoms(Fragment fragment, Func<Atom, bool> filter, bool updateHydrogen)
		{
			foreach (Atom item in fragment.Atoms.Where(filter).ToList())
			{
				List<Bond> list = new List<Bond>(item.Bonds);
				if (list.Count <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (Bond item2 in list)
				{
					if (updateHydrogen)
					{
						item2.StartAtom = null;
						item2.EndAtom = null;
					}
					fragment.Bonds.Remove(item2);
				}
				fragment.Atoms.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		public string[] GenerateMolStringsFromSMILES(string smiles)
		{
			MolecularDrawingCanvas reference = new MolecularDrawingCanvas();
			return FragmentWriter.Save(FragmentReader.Read(new string[1] { smiles }, reference, FragmentFileFormat.SmilesFile), reference);
		}
	}
	public interface IMoleculeRenderEngine
	{
		double FontSize { get; set; }

		IMoleculeImage GetMoleculeImage(string[] moleFileLines);

		IMoleculeImage GetMoleculeImage(string smilesNotation);
	}
	public interface IMoleculeImage
	{
		string[] MoleFileLines { get; }

		System.Windows.Size Size { get; }

		DrawingVisual VectorImage { get; }

		RenderTargetBitmap BitmapImage { get; }
	}
	internal class AspenMoleculeRenderEngine : IMoleculeRenderEngine
	{
		private class MoleculeImage : IMoleculeImage
		{
			public string[] MoleFileLines { get; private set; }

			public System.Windows.Size Size { get; private set; }

			public DrawingVisual VectorImage { get; private set; }

			public RenderTargetBitmap BitmapImage { get; private set; }

			public MoleculeImage(string[] moleFileLines, System.Windows.Size size, DrawingVisual vectorImage)
			{
				MoleFileLines = moleFileLines;
				Size = size;
				VectorImage = vectorImage;
				BitmapImage = GenerateBitmapImage();
			}

			private RenderTargetBitmap GenerateBitmapImage()
			{
				RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)Size.Width, (int)Size.Height, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Default);
				renderTargetBitmap.Render(VectorImage);
				return renderTargetBitmap;
			}
		}

		private const double MinFontSize = 8.0;

		private const double MaxFontSize = 32.0;

		private double _fontSize = 20.0;

		private static readonly System.Windows.Size MeasuredCanvasSize = new System.Windows.Size(1024.0, 768.0);

		private static readonly Thickness ImageMargin = new Thickness(20.0);

		private const double DefaultDpi = 96.0;

		public double FontSize
		{
			get
			{
				return _fontSize;
			}
			set
			{
				_fontSize = Math.Max(8.0, Math.Min(32.0, value));
			}
		}

		public IMoleculeImage GetMoleculeImage(string[] moleFileLines)
		{
			return GetImage(moleFileLines, FragmentFileFormat.MoleFile);
		}

		public IMoleculeImage GetMoleculeImage(string smilesNotation)
		{
			return GetImage(new string[1] { smilesNotation }, FragmentFileFormat.SmilesFile);
		}

		private IMoleculeImage GetImage(string[] moleculeLines, FragmentFileFormat fragmentFileFormat)
		{
			MolecularDrawingCanvas molecularDrawingCanvas = new MolecularDrawingCanvas
			{
				FontSize = FontSize
			};
			molecularDrawingCanvas.Measure(MeasuredCanvasSize);
			molecularDrawingCanvas.Arrange(new Rect(MeasuredCanvasSize));
			Fragment fragment = FragmentReader.Read(moleculeLines, molecularDrawingCanvas, fragmentFileFormat);
			molecularDrawingCanvas.ImportFragment(fragment);
			molecularDrawingCanvas.UpdateLayout();
			System.Windows.Size size;
			DrawingVisual vectorImage = molecularDrawingCanvas.CreateVectorImage(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White, ImageMargin, out size);
			return new MoleculeImage(moleculeLines, size, vectorImage);
		}
	}
	public static class MolecularEditorNetEngine
	{
		public static IMoleculeRenderEngine CreateMoleculeRenderEngine()
		{
			return new AspenMoleculeRenderEngine();
		}

		public static IMoleculeDrawingEngine CreateMoleculeDrawingEngine()
		{
			return new AspenMoleculeDrawingEngine();
		}
	}
