namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Helpers
{
	public static class FlipHelper
	{
		public static void FlipTopToBottom(Diagram diagram, IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			if (elements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("elements");
			}
			List<UIElement> elements2 = elements.ToList();
			List<AtomElement> list = FilterElements(elements);
			System.Windows.Point centerPoint = GetCenterPoint(list);
			using (new TransactionContext(diagram))
			{
				foreach (AtomElement item in list)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item);
					System.Windows.Point newPosition = new System.Windows.Point(elementPosition.X, GetContraPoint(elementPosition, centerPoint).Y);
					diagram.PositionElement(item, newPosition);
				}
				RefreshSelection(diagram, elements2);
			}
		}

		public static void FlipLeftToRight(Diagram diagram, IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			if (elements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("elements");
			}
			List<UIElement> elements2 = elements.ToList();
			List<AtomElement> list = FilterElements(elements);
			System.Windows.Point centerPoint = GetCenterPoint(list);
			using (new TransactionContext(diagram))
			{
				foreach (AtomElement item in list)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item);
					System.Windows.Point newPosition = new System.Windows.Point(GetContraPoint(elementPosition, centerPoint).X, elementPosition.Y);
					diagram.PositionElement(item, newPosition);
				}
				RefreshSelection(diagram, elements2);
			}
		}

		public static void FlipOnElement(Diagram diagram, IEnumerable<UIElement> elements, UIElement axis)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			if (elements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("elements");
			}
			if (axis == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("axis");
			}
			List<UIElement> elements2 = elements.ToList();
			List<AtomElement> list = FilterElements(elements);
			if (axis is BondElement bondElement)
			{
				System.Windows.Point absoluteProjectionPoint = bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
				System.Windows.Point absoluteProjectionPoint2 = bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
				using (new TransactionContext(diagram))
				{
					foreach (AtomElement item in list)
					{
						System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item);
						System.Windows.Point flippedPoint = GetFlippedPoint(absoluteProjectionPoint, absoluteProjectionPoint2, elementPosition);
						diagram.PositionElement(item, flippedPoint);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				using (new TransactionContext(diagram))
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition2 = Diagram.GetElementPosition(axis);
					foreach (AtomElement item2 in list)
					{
						System.Windows.Point elementPosition3 = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item2);
						diagram.PositionElement(item2, GetContraPoint(elementPosition3, elementPosition2));
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshSelection(diagram, elements2);
		}

		private static void RefreshSelection(Diagram diagram, IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			diagram.UnselectAll();
			diagram.Select(elements);
		}

		private static List<AtomElement> FilterElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			List<AtomElement> list = elements.OfType<AtomElement>().ToList();
			foreach (UIElement element in elements)
			{
				if (element is BondElement bondElement)
				{
					if (bondElement.StartAtomElement != null && !list.Contains(bondElement.StartAtomElement))
					{
						list.Add(bondElement.StartAtomElement);
					}
					if (bondElement.EndAtomElement != null && !list.Contains(bondElement.EndAtomElement))
					{
						list.Add(bondElement.EndAtomElement);
					}
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetCenterPoint(List<AtomElement> internalElements)
		{
			if (internalElements.Count == 0)
			{
				return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			if (internalElements.Count == 1)
			{
				return Diagram.GetElementPosition(internalElements[0]);
			}
			Rect rect = new Rect(Diagram.GetElementPosition(internalElements[0]), Diagram.GetElementPosition(internalElements[1]));
			for (int i = 2; i < internalElements.Count; i++)
			{
				rect.Union(Diagram.GetElementPosition(internalElements[i]));
			}
			return rect.TopLeft + (rect.BottomRight - rect.TopLeft) / 2.0;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetContraPoint(System.Windows.Point position, System.Windows.Point center)
		{
			return center + (center - position);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetFlippedPoint(System.Windows.Point beStart, System.Windows.Point beEnd, System.Windows.Point oldPosition)
		{
			Vector toVector = beEnd - beStart;
			Vector vector = oldPosition - beStart;
			if (AngleHelper.IsRoughlyZero(vector.Length) || AngleHelper.IsRoughlyZero(toVector.Length))
			{
				return oldPosition;
			}
			double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(vector, toVector);
			if (AngleHelper.IsRoughlyZero(angleBetween))
			{
				return oldPosition;
			}
			Vector vector2 = new RotateTransform(angleBetween * 2.0).Value.Transform(vector);
			return beStart + vector2;
		}
	}
	public static class ImageHelper
	{
		private class VectorImage : FrameworkElement
		{
			private readonly DrawingVisual _dv;

			protected override int VisualChildrenCount => 1;

			public VectorImage(DrawingVisual dv)
			{
				_dv = dv;
				AddVisualChild(_dv);
			}

			protected override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
			{
				return _dv;
			}
		}

		private const double DefaultDpi = 96.0;

		public static RenderTargetBitmap GetRenderTargetBitmapLessThanTargetSize(DrawingVisual drawingVisual, System.Windows.Size sourceSize, System.Windows.Size targetSize)
		{
			if (drawingVisual == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingVisual");
			}
			Viewbox viewbox = new Viewbox
			{
				Child = new VectorImage(drawingVisual)
				{
					Width = sourceSize.Width,
					Height = sourceSize.Height
				}
			};
			if (sourceSize.Width < targetSize.Width && sourceSize.Height < targetSize.Height)
			{
				viewbox.Width = sourceSize.Width;
				viewbox.Height = sourceSize.Height;
			}
			Grid grid = new Grid
			{
				Width = targetSize.Width,
				Height = targetSize.Height,
				Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White
			};
			grid.Children.Add(viewbox);
			grid.Measure(targetSize);
			grid.Arrange(new Rect(targetSize));
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)targetSize.Width, (int)targetSize.Height, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Default);
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(grid);
			return renderTargetBitmap;
		}
	}
	internal static class ImageResources
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<string, ImageSource> _imageSources = new Dictionary<string, ImageSource>();

		public static ImageSource Cut => GetImageSource("Tools/Cut.png");

		public static ImageSource Copy => GetImageSource("Tools/Copy.png");

		public static ImageSource Paste => GetImageSource("Tools/Paste.png");

		public static ImageSource Undo => GetImageSource("Tools/Undo.png");

		public static ImageSource Redo => GetImageSource("Tools/Redo.png");

		public static ImageSource Clear => GetImageSource("Tools/Clear16.ico");

		public static ImageSource Delete => GetImageSource("Tools/Delete16.ico");

		private static ImageSource GetImageSource(string imageFileName)
		{
			if (!_imageSources.ContainsKey(imageFileName))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("pack://application:,,,/");
				stringBuilder.Append(typeof(ImageResources).Assembly.FullName);
				stringBuilder.Append(";component/Images/");
				stringBuilder.Append(imageFileName);
				_imageSources.Add(imageFileName, new BitmapImage(new Uri(stringBuilder.ToString(), UriKind.RelativeOrAbsolute)));
			}
			return _imageSources[imageFileName];
		}
	}
	internal static class AngleHelper
	{
		private const double DefaultIncreaseAngle = 240.0;

		public static System.Windows.Point GetPositionToExtendNewAtomElement(AtomElement element, double defaultBondLength, BondType bondType)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(element);
			List<BondElement> list = element.ConnectedBondElements.ToList();
			if (list.Count == 0)
			{
				return elementPosition + new Vector(defaultBondLength, 0.0);
			}
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				BondElement bondElement = list[0];
				System.Windows.Point anotherBondEndpoint = GetAnotherBondEndpoint(bondElement, element);
				if (bondType.Valence + bondElement.Bond.Type.Valence == 4)
				{
					return elementPosition + (elementPosition - anotherBondEndpoint);
				}
				AtomElement atomElement = ((bondElement.StartAtomElement == element) ? bondElement.EndAtomElement : bondElement.StartAtomElement);
				List<BondElement> list2 = atomElement.ConnectedBondElements.ToList();
				if (list2.Count == 2)
				{
					BondElement bondElement2 = ((list2[0] == bondElement) ? list2[1] : list2[0]);
					if (bondElement.Bond.Type.Valence + bondElement2.Bond.Type.Valence != 4)
					{
						System.Windows.Point anotherBondEndpoint2 = GetAnotherBondEndpoint(bondElement2, atomElement);
						return elementPosition + GetLengthedVector(anotherBondEndpoint - anotherBondEndpoint2, defaultBondLength);
					}
				}
				return GetAnotherBondPosition(elementPosition, anotherBondEndpoint - elementPosition, 240.0, defaultBondLength);
			}
			if (list.Count == 2)
			{
				BondElement bondElement3 = list[0];
				BondElement bondElement4 = list[1];
				System.Windows.Point anotherBondEndpoint3 = GetAnotherBondEndpoint(bondElement3, element);
				System.Windows.Point anotherBondEndpoint4 = GetAnotherBondEndpoint(bondElement4, element);
				System.Windows.Point centerAngledPosition = GetCenterAngledPosition(elementPosition, anotherBondEndpoint3, anotherBondEndpoint4, defaultBondLength);
				return elementPosition + (elementPosition - centerAngledPosition);
			}
			IEnumerable<System.Windows.Point> points = list.Select((BondElement bondEle) => GetAnotherBondEndpoint(bondEle, element));
			return GetBestPositionFrom(elementPosition, points, defaultBondLength);
		}

		public static SideOption GetBondElementPosition(BondElement bondElement, AtomElement atomElement)
		{
			if (bondElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bondElement");
			}
			if (atomElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atomElement");
			}
			if (bondElement.StartAtomElement != null && bondElement.EndAtomElement != null)
			{
				if (bondElement.StartAtomElement == atomElement)
				{
					if (bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint.X >= bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint.X)
					{
						return SideOption.Left;
					}
					return SideOption.Right;
				}
				if (bondElement.EndAtomElement == atomElement)
				{
					if (bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint.X >= bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint.X)
					{
						return SideOption.Right;
					}
					return SideOption.Left;
				}
			}
			return SideOption.None;
		}

		public static double GetAngleBetween(Vector fromVector, Vector toVector)
		{
			double num = Math.Max(fromVector.Length, toVector.Length);
			fromVector = fromVector * num / fromVector.Length;
			toVector = toVector * num / toVector.Length;
			double val = fromVector * toVector / (fromVector.Length * toVector.Length);
			val = Math.Min(1.0, Math.Max(-1.0, val));
			double num2 = Math.Acos(val) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
			Vector vector = new RotateTransform(num2).Value.Transform(fromVector);
			if (IsRoughlyEqual(vector.X, toVector.X) && IsRoughlyEqual(vector.Y, toVector.Y))
			{
				return num2;
			}
			return 0.0 - num2;
		}

		public static Vector GetVectorByAngle(double angle)
		{
			double num = angle * Math.PI / 180.0;
			return new Vector(Math.Cos(num), Math.Sin(num));
		}

		public static double GetAngle(Vector vector)
		{
			return GetAngleBetween(new Vector(10.0, 0.0), vector);
		}

		public static SideOption GetPointSide(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint, System.Windows.Point targetPoint)
		{
			if (GetAngleBetween(endPoint - startPoint, targetPoint - startPoint) > 0.0)
			{
				return SideOption.Right;
			}
			return SideOption.Left;
		}

		public static bool IsConnectionRoughlyOnALine(AtomElement atomElement)
		{
			if (atomElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("atomElement");
			}
			List<BondElement> list = atomElement.ConnectedBondElements.ToList();
			if (list.Count == 2)
			{
				BondElement bondElement = list[0];
				BondElement bondElement2 = list[1];
				if (bondElement.StartAnchor != null && bondElement.EndAnchor != null && bondElement2.StartAnchor != null && bondElement2.EndAnchor != null)
				{
					Vector fromVector = ((bondElement.StartAtomElement == atomElement) ? (bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint) : (bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint));
					Vector toVector = ((bondElement.StartAtomElement == atomElement) ? (bondElement2.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement2.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint) : (bondElement2.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement2.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint));
					double num = Math.Abs(GetAngleBetween(fromVector, toVector));
					if (num <= 10.0 || Math.Abs(num - 180.0) <= 10.0)
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static SideOption GetSlaveLinePosition(BondElement bondElement, out double startAngle, out double endAngle)
		{
			if (bondElement == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bondElement");
			}
			startAngle = (endAngle = 180.0);
			if (bondElement.Bond == null)
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (bondElement.Bond.Type == BondType.Single)
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (bondElement.StartAtomElement == null || bondElement.EndAtomElement == null)
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (bondElement.StartAnchor == null || bondElement.EndAnchor == null)
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			List<BondElement> list = bondElement.StartAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements.Except(new BondElement[1] { bondElement }).ToList();
			List<BondElement> list2 = bondElement.EndAtomElement.ConnectedBondElements.Except(new BondElement[1] { bondElement }).ToList();
			if (list.Count == 0 && list2.Count == 0)
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			List<double> list3 = new List<double>();
			foreach (BondElement item in list)
			{
				if (item.StartAnchor != null && item.EndAnchor != null)
				{
					Vector fromVector = bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
					Vector toVector = ((item.StartAtomElement == bondElement.StartAtomElement) ? (item.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - item.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint) : (item.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - item.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint));
					list3.Add(GetAngleBetween(fromVector, toVector));
				}
			}
			List<double> list4 = new List<double>();
			foreach (BondElement item2 in list2)
			{
				if (item2.StartAnchor != null && item2.EndAnchor != null)
				{
					Vector fromVector2 = bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
					Vector toVector2 = ((item2.StartAtomElement == bondElement.EndAtomElement) ? (item2.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - item2.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint) : (item2.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint - item2.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint));
					list4.Add(GetAngleBetween(fromVector2, toVector2));
				}
			}
			double num = (from angle in list3
				where angle > 0.0
				orderby angle
				select angle).FirstOrDefault();
			double num2 = (from angle in list3
				where angle < 0.0
				orderby angle descending
				select angle).FirstOrDefault();
			double num3 = (from angle in list4
				where angle > 0.0
				orderby angle
				select angle).FirstOrDefault();
			double num4 = (from angle in list4
				where angle < 0.0
				orderby angle descending
				select angle).FirstOrDefault();
			if (bondElement.Bond.Type == BondType.Tripple)
			{
				if (IsRoughlyZero(num) && IsRoughlyZero(num2))
				{
					startAngle = 180.0;
				}
				else if (IsRoughlyZero(num))
				{
					startAngle = Math.Abs(num2);
				}
				else if (IsRoughlyZero(num2))
				{
					startAngle = num;
				}
				else
				{
					startAngle = Math.Min(num, Math.Abs(num2));
				}
				if (IsRoughlyZero(num3) && IsRoughlyZero(num4))
				{
					endAngle = 180.0;
				}
				else if (IsRoughlyZero(num3))
				{
					endAngle = Math.Abs(num4);
				}
				else if (IsRoughlyZero(num4))
				{
					endAngle = num3;
				}
				else
				{
					endAngle = Math.Min(num3, Math.Abs(num4));
				}
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (IsRoughlyZero(num) && IsRoughlyZero(num2) && IsRoughlyZero(num3) && IsRoughlyZero(num4))
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (((IsRoughlyZero(num) && IsRoughlyEqual(num2, -180.0)) || (IsRoughlyEqual(num, 180.0) && IsRoughlyZero(num2)) || (IsRoughlyZero(num) && IsRoughlyZero(num2))) && ((IsRoughlyZero(num3) && IsRoughlyEqual(num4, -180.0)) || (IsRoughlyEqual(num3, 180.0) && IsRoughlyZero(num4)) || (IsRoughlyZero(num3) && IsRoughlyZero(num4))))
			{
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (num == 0.0 && num2 == 0.0)
			{
				if (num3 == 0.0 || num3 > Math.Abs(num4))
				{
					startAngle = 180.0;
					endAngle = num4;
					return SideOption.Right;
				}
				if (IsRoughlyEqual(num3, Math.Abs(num4)))
				{
					return SideOption.None;
				}
				startAngle = -180.0;
				endAngle = num3;
				return SideOption.Left;
			}
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				if (num3 > 0.0 || Math.Abs(num2) < Math.Abs(num4))
				{
					startAngle = num2;
					endAngle = num3;
					return SideOption.Left;
				}
				startAngle = 180.0;
				endAngle = num4;
				return SideOption.Right;
			}
			if (num2 == 0.0)
			{
				if (num4 < 0.0 || num < num3)
				{
					startAngle = num;
					endAngle = num4;
					return SideOption.Right;
				}
				startAngle = -180.0;
				endAngle = num3;
				return SideOption.Left;
			}
			if (num3 == 0.0 && num4 == 0.0 && IsRoughlyEqual(num, Math.Abs(num2)))
			{
				startAngle = num;
				endAngle = 180.0;
				return SideOption.None;
			}
			if (num3 == 0.0 && num4 == 0.0)
			{
				if (num <= Math.Abs(num2))
				{
					startAngle = num;
					endAngle = -180.0;
					return SideOption.Right;
				}
				startAngle = num2;
				endAngle = 180.0;
				return SideOption.Left;
			}
			if (num3 == 0.0)
			{
				startAngle = num;
				endAngle = num4;
				return SideOption.Right;
			}
			if (num4 == 0.0)
			{
				startAngle = num2;
				endAngle = num3;
				return SideOption.Left;
			}
			if (num + Math.Abs(num4) <= Math.Abs(num2) + num3)
			{
				startAngle = num;
				endAngle = num4;
				return SideOption.Right;
			}
			startAngle = num2;
			endAngle = num3;
			return SideOption.Left;
		}

		public static bool IsRoughlyEqual(double first, double second)
		{
			return Math.Abs(first - second) <= 0.0005;
		}

		public static bool IsRoughlyZero(double value)
		{
			return IsRoughlyEqual(value, 0.0);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetBestPositionFrom(System.Windows.Point position, IEnumerable<System.Windows.Point> points, double length)
		{
			if (points.Count() < 3)
			{
				return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			List<System.Windows.Point> list = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			foreach (System.Windows.Point point in points)
			{
				list.Add(position + GetLengthedVector(point - position, length));
			}
			List<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>> list2 = new List<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>>();
			foreach (System.Windows.Point item in list)
			{
				foreach (System.Windows.Point item2 in list)
				{
					if (item2 != item && !list2.Contains(new KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>(item2, item)))
					{
						list2.Add(new KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>(item, item2));
					}
				}
			}
			List<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>> sortedVectors = list2.OrderBy((KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point> pp) => (pp.Value - pp.Key).Length).ToList();
			List<KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>> list3 = new List<KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>>();
			for (int num = 0; num < list.Count; num++)
			{
				KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>? keyValuePair = FindRestMinorAngle(position, sortedVectors, list3);
				if (keyValuePair.HasValue && keyValuePair.HasValue)
				{
					list3.Add(keyValuePair.Value);
				}
			}
			Dictionary<System.Windows.Point, int> usedTimes = new Dictionary<System.Windows.Point, int>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double> item3 in list3)
			{
				if (!usedTimes.ContainsKey(item3.Key.Key))
				{
					usedTimes.Add(item3.Key.Key, 1);
				}
				else
				{
					usedTimes[item3.Key.Key]++;
				}
				if (!usedTimes.ContainsKey(item3.Key.Value))
				{
					usedTimes.Add(item3.Key.Value, 1);
				}
				else
				{
					usedTimes[item3.Key.Value]++;
				}
			}
			List<System.Windows.Point> list4 = usedTimes.Keys.Where((System.Windows.Point p) => usedTimes[p] == 1).ToList();
			if (list3.Sum((KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double> t) => t.Value) > 180.0)
			{
				return GetCenterAngledPosition(position, list4[0], list4[1], length);
			}
			System.Windows.Point centerAngledPosition = GetCenterAngledPosition(position, list4[0], list4[1], length);
			return position + (position - centerAngledPosition);
		}

		private static KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>? FindRestMinorAngle(System.Windows.Point position, List<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>> sortedVectors, List<KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>> tripplets)
		{
			if (tripplets.Count == 0)
			{
				KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point> keyValuePair = sortedVectors[0];
				return new KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>(new KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>(keyValuePair.Key, keyValuePair.Value), Math.Abs(GetAngleBetween(keyValuePair.Key - position, keyValuePair.Value - position)));
			}
			KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double> keyValuePair2 = tripplets[tripplets.Count - 1];
			Vector vector = keyValuePair2.Key.Value - keyValuePair2.Key.Key;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point> sortedVector in sortedVectors)
			{
				System.Windows.Point key = sortedVector.Key;
				System.Windows.Point value = sortedVector.Value;
				if ((value - key).Length < vector.Length || (key == keyValuePair2.Key.Key && value == keyValuePair2.Key.Value) || (key == keyValuePair2.Key.Value && value == keyValuePair2.Key.Key))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = ExistingTimesIn(tripplets, key);
				if (num <= 1)
				{
					int num2 = ExistingTimesIn(tripplets, value);
					if (num2 <= 1 && (num != 1 || num2 != 1 || !IsCrossingPoints(key, value, tripplets)))
					{
						return new KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>(new KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>(key, value), Math.Abs(GetAngleBetween(key - position, value - position)));
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static bool IsCrossingPoints(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q, List<KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>> tripplets)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = p;
			System.Windows.Point point2 = point;
			for (int i = 0; i < tripplets.Count; i++)
			{
				if (point == q)
				{
					return true;
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double> tripplet in tripplets)
				{
					if (point == tripplet.Key.Key && point2 != tripplet.Key.Value)
					{
						point2 = point;
						point = tripplet.Key.Value;
						break;
					}
					if (point == tripplet.Key.Value && point2 != tripplet.Key.Key)
					{
						point2 = point;
						point = tripplet.Key.Key;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return point == q;
		}

		private static int ExistingTimesIn(List<KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double>> tripplets, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<KeyValuePair<System.Windows.Point, System.Windows.Point>, double> tripplet in tripplets)
			{
				if (tripplet.Key.Key == point || tripplet.Key.Value == point)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetAnotherBondEndpoint(BondElement bondElement, AtomElement atomElement)
		{
			if (bondElement.StartAtomElement != atomElement)
			{
				return bondElement.StartAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
			}
			return bondElement.EndAnchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetCenterAngledPosition(System.Windows.Point position, System.Windows.Point p1, System.Windows.Point p2, double length)
		{
			Vector vector = p1 - position;
			Vector lengthedVector = GetLengthedVector(p2 - position, vector.Length);
			System.Windows.Point point = position + lengthedVector;
			System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point((position.X + p1.X + point.X) / 3.0, (position.Y + p1.Y + point.Y) / 3.0);
			return position + GetLengthedVector(point2 - position, length);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetAnotherBondPosition(System.Windows.Point position, Vector vector, double angle, double length)
		{
			vector = GetLengthedVector(vector, length);
			return position + new RotateTransform(angle).Value.Transform(vector);
		}

		private static Vector GetLengthedVector(Vector vector, double length)
		{
			return vector * length / vector.Length;
		}
	}
	internal static class ValenceHelper
	{
		public static bool IsAtomElementAllowedToConnect(AtomElement element, BondType type)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (element.Atom != null && element.Atom.AvailableLinksCount >= type.Valence)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static BondType GetNextAllowedTypeToChange(Bond bond)
		{
			if (bond == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond");
			}
			if (bond.StartAtom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond.StartAtom");
			}
			if (bond.EndAtom == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("bond.EndAtom");
			}
			if (bond.Type == BondType.Single)
			{
				if (bond.StartAtom.AvailableLinksCount >= 1 && bond.EndAtom.AvailableLinksCount >= 1)
				{
					return BondType.Double;
				}
				return BondType.Single;
			}
			if (bond.Type == BondType.Double)
			{
				if (bond.StartAtom.AvailableLinksCount >= 1 && bond.EndAtom.AvailableLinksCount >= 1)
				{
					return BondType.Tripple;
				}
				return BondType.Single;
			}
			return BondType.Single;
		}

		public static bool IsFragmentWholyConnected(Fragment fragment)
		{
			if (fragment == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("fragment");
			}
			if (fragment.Atoms.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (fragment.Atoms.Count == 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (fragment.Bonds.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			List<List<Atom>> list = new List<List<Atom>>();
			foreach (Atom atom in fragment.Atoms)
			{
				if (!list.Exists((List<Atom> x) => x.Exists((Atom y) => y == atom)))
				{
					List<Atom> list2 = new List<Atom>();
					List<Bond> connectedBonds = new List<Bond>();
					GoThroughAtom(atom, list2, connectedBonds);
					list.Add(list2);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count == 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			List<int> list3 = list.Select((List<Atom> ag) => ag.Sum((Atom a) => a.ChargeCount)).ToList();
			if (list3.Sum() == 0 && !list3.Exists((int x) => x == 0))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void GoThroughAtom(Atom atom, List<Atom> connectedAtoms, List<Bond> connectedBonds)
		{
			if (atom == null || connectedAtoms.Contains(atom))
			{
				return;
			}
			connectedAtoms.Add(atom);
			foreach (Bond bond in atom.Bonds)
			{
				if (!connectedBonds.Contains(bond))
				{
					connectedBonds.Add(bond);
					GoThroughAtom((bond.StartAtom == atom) ? bond.EndAtom : bond.StartAtom, connectedAtoms, connectedBonds);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class NativeMethods
	{
		[DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, ExactSpelling = true)]
		private static extern IntPtr GetActiveWindow();

		public static void ShowWindow(Window window, bool showAsDialog)
		{
			new WindowInteropHelper(window).Owner = GetActiveWindow();
			if (showAsDialog)
			{
				window.ShowDialog();
			}
			else
			{
				window.Show();
			}
		}
	}

		private bool IsClipboardOperationCanceled(bool isCanExecute, ClipboardOperationType operationType)
		{
			ClipboardCancelEventArgs e = new ClipboardCancelEventArgs(operationType);
			if (isCanExecute)
			{
				OnClipboardCanExecuteInvoked(e);
			}
			else
			{
				OnClipboardExecutedInvoked(e);
			}
			return e.Cancel;
		}

		private void InitializeCommandBindings()
		{
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Cut, delegate
			{
				if (!IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: false, ClipboardOperationType.Cut))
				{
					ClipboardHelper.SetImageIntoClipboard(ImagingHelper.GetBitmapSourceFrom(Selection, Background, ZoomScale));
					CopyExecutingEventArgs e = new CopyExecutingEventArgs();
					OnCutExecuting(e);
					if (e.Handled)
					{
						ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData = e.Data;
					}
					else
					{
						StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
						foreach (UIElement item in Selection)
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(XamlWriter.Save(item));
						}
						ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData = stringBuilder.ToString();
						RemoveSelectedElements();
					}
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: true, ClipboardOperationType.Cut))
				{
					args.CanExecute = false;
				}
				else
				{
					args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 0;
				}
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Copy, delegate
			{
				if (!IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: false, ClipboardOperationType.Copy))
				{
					ClipboardHelper.SetImageIntoClipboard(ImagingHelper.GetBitmapSourceFrom(Selection, Background, ZoomScale));
					CopyExecutingEventArgs e = new CopyExecutingEventArgs();
					OnCopyExecuting(e);
					if (e.Handled)
					{
						ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData = e.Data;
					}
					else
					{
						StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
						foreach (UIElement item2 in Selection)
						{
							stringBuilder.AppendLine(XamlWriter.Save(item2));
						}
						ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData = stringBuilder.ToString();
					}
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: true, ClipboardOperationType.Copy))
				{
					args.CanExecute = false;
				}
				else
				{
					args.CanExecute = Selection.Count > 0;
				}
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Paste, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (!IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: false, ClipboardOperationType.Paste))
				{
					System.Windows.Point? pastePosition = null;
					if (args.Parameter != null)
					{
						pastePosition = (System.Windows.Point)args.Parameter;
					}
					PasteExecutingEventArgs e = new PasteExecutingEventArgs(ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData, pastePosition);
					OnPasteExecuting(e);
					if (!e.Handled)
					{
						string[] array = ClipboardHelper.ClipboardData.ToString().Split(new string[1] { Environment.NewLine }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
						IList<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
						string[] array2 = array;
						foreach (string xamlText in array2)
						{
							list.Add((UIElement)XamlReader.Parse(xamlText));
						}
						foreach (UIElement item3 in list)
						{
							AddElement(item3);
							item3.UpdateLayout();
						}
						Rect rect = new Rect(double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN, double.NaN);
						if (pastePosition.HasValue)
						{
							foreach (FrameworkElement item4 in list)
							{
								System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(item4);
								GetElementWidth(item4);
								GetElementHeight(item4);
								double y = elementPosition.Y;
								double x = elementPosition.X;
								double num = x + item4.Width;
								double num2 = y + item4.Height;
								if (double.IsNaN(rect.Left) || (!double.IsNaN(x) && x < rect.Left))
								{
									rect.X = x;
								}
								if (double.IsNaN(rect.Right) || (!double.IsNaN(num) && num > rect.Right))
								{
									rect.Width = num - rect.Left;
								}
								if (double.IsNaN(rect.Top) || (!double.IsNaN(y) && y < rect.Top))
								{
									rect.Y = y;
								}
								if (double.IsNaN(rect.Bottom) || (!double.IsNaN(num2) && num2 < rect.Bottom))
								{
									rect.Height = num2 - rect.Top;
								}
							}
						}
						if (pastePosition.HasValue)
						{
							foreach (UIElement item5 in list)
							{
								Vector vector = System.Windows.Point.Subtract(GetElementPosition(item5), rect.TopLeft);
								PositionElement(item5, System.Windows.Point.Add(pastePosition.Value, vector));
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (IsClipboardOperationCanceled(isCanExecute: true, ClipboardOperationType.Paste))
				{
					args.CanExecute = false;
				}
				else
				{
					args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && ClipboardHelper.HasData();
				}
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Delete, delegate
			{
				RemoveSelectedElements();
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Print, delegate
			{
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = true;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.PrintPreview, delegate
			{
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = true;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Undo, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (TransactionManager != null)
				{
					int undoCount = 1;
					if (args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is int)
					{
						undoCount = (int)args.Parameter;
					}
					TransactionManager.Undo(undoCount);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && TransactionManager != null && TransactionManager.CanUndo;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(ApplicationCommands.Redo, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (TransactionManager != null)
				{
					int redoCount = 1;
					if (args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is int)
					{
						redoCount = (int)args.Parameter;
					}
					TransactionManager.Redo(redoCount);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && TransactionManager != null && TransactionManager.CanRedo;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.ZoomIn, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point)
				{
					ZoomIn((System.Windows.Point)args.Parameter);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.ZoomOut, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point)
				{
					ZoomOut((System.Windows.Point)args.Parameter);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.ZoomFitToScreen, delegate
			{
				ZoomFitToScreen();
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = true;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.ZoomActualSize, delegate(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				if (args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point)
				{
					ZoomToScale(1.0, (System.Windows.Point)args.Parameter);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = args.Parameter != null && args.Parameter is System.Windows.Point;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.BringToFront, delegate
			{
				BringToFront(Selection);
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && GetOverlappedUIElements(FilterOrderingElements(Selection)).Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.SendToBack, delegate
			{
				SendToBack(Selection);
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && GetOverlappedUIElements(FilterOrderingElements(Selection)).Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.BringForward, delegate
			{
				BringForward(Selection);
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && GetOverlappedUIElements(FilterOrderingElements(Selection)).Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.SendBackward, delegate
			{
				SendBackward(Selection);
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && GetOverlappedUIElements(FilterOrderingElements(Selection)).Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.FlipHorizontal, delegate
			{
				foreach (UIElement item6 in Selection)
				{
					FlipElement(item6, FlipAxis.Horizontal);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.FlipVertical, delegate
			{
				foreach (UIElement item7 in Selection)
				{
					FlipElement(item7, FlipAxis.Vertical);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.RotateLeft, delegate
			{
				foreach (UIElement item8 in Selection)
				{
					RotateElement(item8, -90.0);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 0;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.RotateRight, delegate
			{
				foreach (UIElement item9 in Selection)
				{
					RotateElement(item9, 90.0);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.Group, delegate
			{
				GroupElements(Selection);
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count > 1;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.Ungroup, delegate
			{
				DiagramGroup diagramGroup = ((Selection.Count == 1) ? (Selection[0] as DiagramGroup) : null);
				if (diagramGroup != null)
				{
					UngroupElements(diagramGroup);
				}
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && Selection.Count == 1 && Selection[0] is DiagramGroup;
			}));
			CommandTarget.CommandBindings.Add(new CommandBinding(DiagramCommands.Regroup, delegate
			{
				RegroupElements();
			}, delegate(object sender, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs args)
			{
				args.CanExecute = !IsReadOnly && CanRegroup;
			}));
		}

		protected override void OnContextMenuOpening(ContextMenuEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnContextMenuOpening(e);
			if (e.Handled || base.ContextMenu != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = CreatePrimitiveContextMenu(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
			ContextMenuInvokedEventArgs e2 = new ContextMenuInvokedEventArgs(PrimitiveContextMenuInvokedEvent, contextMenu, mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
			OnPrimitiveContextMenuInvoked(e2);
			if (!e2.Handled && contextMenu.Items.Count > 0)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position = Mouse.GetPosition(PrimaryLayer);
				if (position.X < 0.0)
				{
					position.X = 0.0;
				}
				else if (position.X > ViewportWidth)
				{
					position.X = ViewportWidth;
				}
				if (position.Y < 0.0)
				{
					position.Y = 0.0;
				}
				else if (position.Y > ViewportHeight)
				{
					position.Y = ViewportHeight;
				}
				contextMenu.PlacementTarget = PrimaryLayer;
				contextMenu.Placement = PlacementMode.RelativePoint;
				contextMenu.HorizontalOffset = position.X;
				contextMenu.VerticalOffset = position.Y;
				contextMenu.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu CreatePrimitiveContextMenu(System.Windows.Point clickedPoint)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu = new System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu();
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Clipboard))
			{
				AddCopyPasteMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Print);
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.FliAndRotate))
			{
				EnsureSeparator(contextMenu);
				AddFlipAndRotateMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Ordering))
			{
				AddOrderingMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.AlignmentAndSizing))
			{
				AddAlignAndSizeMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Group))
			{
				EnsureSeparator(contextMenu);
				AddGroupMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Zoom))
			{
				EnsureSeparator(contextMenu);
				AddZoomMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.Connector))
			{
				AddConnectorMenuItem(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			if (Selection.Count == 1 && GetElementService(Selection[0], typeof(IContextMenuService)) is IContextMenuService contextMenuService)
			{
				contextMenuService.AddMenuItems(contextMenu, clickedPoint);
			}
			return contextMenu;
		}

		private bool HasPrimitiveContextMenuOf(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption primitiveContextMenuOption)
		{
			if ((PrimitiveContextMenuOption & primitiveContextMenuOption) == primitiveContextMenuOption)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void EnsureSeparator(System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu menu)
		{
			if (menu.Items.Count > 0 && !(menu.Items[menu.Items.Count - 1] is Separator))
			{
				menu.Items.Add(new Separator());
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.Image GetContextItemIcon(string strImage)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.Image image = new System.Windows.Controls.Image();
			string text = "pack://application:,,,/" + typeof(Diagram).Assembly.FullName + ";component/Images/Tools/";
			image.Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(text + strImage));
			return image;
		}
