		public static Diagram GetParentDiagram(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DependencyObject parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(element);
			Diagram diagram = parent as Diagram;
			while (diagram == null && parent != null)
			{
				parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(parent);
				diagram = parent as Diagram;
			}
			return diagram;
		}

		public static object GetElementService(UIElement element, Type serviceType)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (element is IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
			{
				obj = serviceProvider.GetService(serviceType);
			}
			if (obj == null)
			{
				obj = BuiltinElementServices.GetServiceForElement(element, serviceType);
			}
			return obj;
		}

		private static void HandleDoubleClick(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ClickCount == 2)
			{
				Diagram diagram = (Diagram)sender;
				MouseButtonEventArgs e2 = new MouseButtonEventArgs(e.MouseDevice, e.Timestamp, e.ChangedButton, e.StylusDevice);
				if (e.RoutedEvent == UIElement.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDownEvent || e.RoutedEvent == UIElement.PreviewMouseRightButtonDownEvent)
				{
					e2.RoutedEvent = PreviewMouseDoubleClickEvent;
					e2.Source = e.OriginalSource;
					diagram.OnPreviewMouseDoubleClick(e2);
				}
				else
				{
					e2.RoutedEvent = MouseDoubleClickEvent;
					e2.Source = e.OriginalSource;
					diagram.OnMouseDoubleClick(e2);
				}
				if (e2.Handled)
				{
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}

		static Diagram()
		{
			PrimitiveContextMenuInvokedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PrimitiveContextMenuInvoked", RoutingStrategy.Bubble, typeof(EventHandler<ContextMenuInvokedEventArgs>), typeof(Diagram));
			PreviewMouseDoubleClickEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("PreviewMouseDoubleClick", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(MouseButtonEventHandler), typeof(Diagram));
			MouseDoubleClickEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("MouseDoubleClick", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(MouseButtonEventHandler), typeof(Diagram));
			IdProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("Id", typeof(Guid), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(Guid.Empty));
			CanMoveProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CanMove", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			CanResizeProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CanResize", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnCanResizePropertyChanged));
			CanRotateProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CanRotate", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnCanRotatePropertyChanged));
			CanFlipProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("CanFlip", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnCanFlipPropertyChanged));
			ShouldShowQuickToolProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ShouldShowQuickTool", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnShouldShowQuickToolPropertyChanged));
			GroupIdProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("GroupId", typeof(Guid), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(Guid.Empty));
			MaximumDesignerNestingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("MaximumDesignerNesting", typeof(int), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(1));
			ShowPageBorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowPageBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram));
			BackgroundProperty = System.Windows.Controls.Panel.BackgroundProperty.AddOwner(typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Lavender, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.None));
			MinimumSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimumSize", typeof(System.Windows.Size), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new System.Windows.Size(500.0, 400.0), FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnMinimumSizePropertyChanged));
			ZoomScaleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ZoomScale", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnZoomScalePropertyChanged, OnZoomScalePropertyCoerced));
			ZoomStepFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ZoomStepFactor", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(1.25));
			ZoomFullMarginProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ZoomFullMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(new Thickness(25.0)));
			WheelZoomStepFactorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("WheelZoomStepFactor", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(1.1));
			MouseWheelZoomOptionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MouseWheelZoomOption", typeof(WheelZoomOption), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(WheelZoomOption.Enabled));
			MinimumZoomProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimumZoom", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(0.05));
			MaximumZoomProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MaximumZoom", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(20.0));
			IsReadOnlyProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsReadOnly", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnIsReadOnlyPropertyChanged));
			DefaultSymbolLabelUriProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultSymbolLabelUri", typeof(string), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata("Diagram.DisplayNameLabel"));
			DefaultConnectorLabelUriProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultConnectorLabelUri", typeof(string), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata("Diagram.DisplayNameLabel"));
			MinElementWidthToHideAdornerProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("MinElementWidthToHideAdorner", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(40.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange));
			MinElementHeightToHideAdornerProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("MinElementHeightToHideAdorner", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(40.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange));
			SnapToGridProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("SnapToGrid", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			ShowGridsProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ShowGrids", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnShowGridsPropertyChanged));
			ShowRulersProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("ShowRulers", typeof(RulerOption), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(RulerOption.All, OnShowRulesPropertyChanged));
			GridMinorSpacingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("GridMinorSpacing", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(10.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange));
			GridMajorSpacingProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("GridMajorSpacing", typeof(double), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(50.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsArrange));
			UndoAndRedoRaiseEventsProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("UndoAndRedoRaiseEvents", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			ShouldAvoidEmptyConnectionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShouldAvoidEmptyConnection", typeof(bool), typeof(Diagram), new UIPropertyMetadata(false));
			PrimitiveContextMenuOptionProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PrimitiveContextMenuOption", typeof(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption), typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DiagramPrimitiveContextMenuOption.All));
			FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(320.0));
			FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Diagram), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(180.0));
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Diagram), UIElement.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(HandleDoubleClick), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Diagram), UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(HandleDoubleClick), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Diagram), UIElement.PreviewMouseRightButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(HandleDoubleClick), handledEventsToo: true);
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(Diagram), UIElement.MouseRightButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(HandleDoubleClick), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		public Diagram()
		{
			InitializeFields();
			InitializeComponent();
			InitializeCommandBindings();
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				OnDisposing();
			}
		}

		public bool AddElement(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Add"))
			{
				try
				{
					if (AddElement(element))
					{
						PositionElement(element, position);
						return true;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool AddElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (PrimaryLayer.Children.Contains(element))
			{
				return true;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Add");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
			{
				PreviewElementAddedEventArgs e = new PreviewElementAddedEventArgs(element);
				OnPreviewElementAdded(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return AddElementNoPreview(element);
		}

		public bool AddElementNoPreview(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (PrimaryLayer.Children.Contains(element))
			{
				return true;
			}
			bool result = true;
			PrimaryLayer.AddElement(element);
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Add"))
			{
				try
				{
					transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
					{
						RemoveElementNoPreview(element);
					}, delegate
					{
						AddElementNoPreview(element);
					});
					if (GetElementService(element, typeof(IElementNotifiedService)) is IElementNotifiedService elementNotifiedService)
					{
						elementNotifiedService.OnElementAddedIntoDiagram(this);
					}
					if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
					{
						OnElementAdded(new ElementAddedEventArgs(element));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool AddNameLabelToElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return AddNameLabelToElement(element, string.Empty);
		}

		public bool AddNameLabelToElement(UIElement element, string labelUri)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(INameLabelService)) is INameLabelService nameLabelService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return nameLabelService.AddNameLabel(this, labelUri);
		}

		public void RemoveSelectedElements()
		{
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Remove");
			try
			{
				List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
				if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
				{
					foreach (UIElement item in new List<UIElement>(Selection))
					{
						PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs e = new PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs(item);
						OnPreviewElementRemoved(e);
						if (!e.Cancel)
						{
							list.Add(item);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (UIElement item2 in Selection)
					{
						list.Add(item2);
					}
				}
				foreach (UIElement item3 in list)
				{
					RemoveElementNoPreview(item3);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public bool RemoveElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!PrimaryLayer.Children.Contains(element))
			{
				return true;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Remove");
			try
			{
				if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
				{
					PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs e = new PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs(element);
					OnPreviewElementRemoved(e);
					if (e.Cancel)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return RemoveElementNoPreview(element);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public bool RemoveElementNoPreview(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			bool result = true;
			if (!PrimaryLayer.Children.Contains(element))
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (IsSelected(element))
			{
				Unselect(element);
			}
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Remove"))
			{
				try
				{
					modifyService.RemoveElement(this);
					if (GetElementService(element, typeof(IElementNotifiedService)) is IElementNotifiedService elementNotifiedService)
					{
						elementNotifiedService.OnElementRemovedFromDiagram(this);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool CanMoveElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return modifyService.CanMove(this);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetElementPosition(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return DiagramLayer.GetElementPosition(element);
		}

		public void PositionElement(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(newPosition.X) || double.IsNaN(newPosition.Y) || GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(GetElementPosition(element), newPosition) || !(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Move object");
			try
			{
				modifyService.PositionElement(this, newPosition);
				UpdateLayout();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public double GetElementWidth(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = element as FrameworkElement;
			if (!double.IsNaN(frameworkElement.Width))
			{
				return frameworkElement.Width;
			}
			return frameworkElement.ActualWidth;
		}

		public double GetElementHeight(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = element as FrameworkElement;
			if (!double.IsNaN(frameworkElement.Width))
			{
				return frameworkElement.Height;
			}
			return frameworkElement.ActualHeight;
		}

		public System.Windows.Size GetElementSize(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return new System.Windows.Size(GetElementWidth(element), GetElementHeight(element));
		}

		public bool CanResizeElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return modifyService.CanResize(this);
		}

		public void SetElementSize(UIElement element, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(size.Width) || double.IsInfinity(size.Width) || size.Width < 0.0 || double.IsNaN(size.Height) || double.IsInfinity(size.Height) || size.Height < 0.0 || !(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService) || !modifyService.CanResize(this))
			{
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Change size");
			try
			{
				modifyService.SizeElement(this, size);
				UpdateLayout();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public void SetElementWidth(UIElement element, double width)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			SetElementSize(element, new System.Windows.Size(width, GetElementHeight(element)));
		}

		public void SetElementHeight(UIElement element, double height)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			SetElementSize(element, new System.Windows.Size(GetElementWidth(element), height));
		}

		public bool ShouldShowQuickTool(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return modifyService.ShouldShowQuickTool(this);
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			UnselectAll();
			using (new TransactionContext(this))
			{
				foreach (UIElement item in DiagramElements.Cast<UIElement>().ToList())
				{
					RemoveElement(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void PrepareTransform(UIElement element)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = element.RenderTransform as TransformGroup;
			if (transformGroup == null)
			{
				transformGroup = (TransformGroup)(element.RenderTransform = new TransformGroup());
			}
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = null;
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = null;
			foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
			{
				if (child is RotateTransform)
				{
					rotateTransform = child as RotateTransform;
				}
				if (child is ScaleTransform)
				{
					scaleTransform = child as ScaleTransform;
				}
			}
			if (rotateTransform == null)
			{
				rotateTransform = new RotateTransform();
				rotateTransform.Angle = 0.0;
				transformGroup.Children.Add(rotateTransform);
			}
			if (scaleTransform == null)
			{
				scaleTransform = new ScaleTransform();
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = 1.0;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = 1.0;
				transformGroup.Children.Add(scaleTransform);
			}
			element.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
		}

		internal TransformGroup GetTransformGroup(UIElement element)
		{
			PrepareTransform(element);
			return element.RenderTransform as TransformGroup;
		}

		private RotateTransform GetRotateTransform(UIElement element)
		{
			return DefaultModifyService.GetRotateTransform(GetTransformGroup(element));
		}

		private ScaleTransform GetScaleTransform(UIElement element)
		{
			return DefaultModifyService.GetScaleTransform(GetTransformGroup(element));
		}

		public double GetElementRotation(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = GetRotateTransform(element);
			FlipAxis elementFlipAxis = GetElementFlipAxis(element);
			if (elementFlipAxis == FlipAxis.Horizontal || elementFlipAxis == FlipAxis.Vertical)
			{
				return 0.0 - rotateTransform.Angle;
			}
			return rotateTransform.Angle;
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetElementRotationCenter(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (element is Polygon || element is Polyline)
			{
				return new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			}
			if (element.GetValue(UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty) == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
			{
				element.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			}
			if (element.RenderTransformOrigin.X == 0.0 && element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y == 0.0)
			{
				element.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			}
			return element.RenderTransformOrigin;
		}

		public FlipAxis GetElementFlipAxis(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(element.RenderTransform is TransformGroup))
			{
				return FlipAxis.None;
			}
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = GetScaleTransform(element);
			if (scaleTransform.ScaleX < 0.0 && scaleTransform.ScaleY < 0.0)
			{
				return FlipAxis.Both;
			}
			if (scaleTransform.ScaleX > 0.0 && scaleTransform.ScaleY < 0.0)
			{
				return FlipAxis.Vertical;
			}
			if (scaleTransform.ScaleX < 0.0 && scaleTransform.ScaleY > 0.0)
			{
				return FlipAxis.Horizontal;
			}
			return FlipAxis.None;
		}

		public bool CanRotateElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return modifyService.CanRotate(this);
		}

		public bool RotateElement(UIElement element, double angle)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(angle) || double.IsInfinity(angle))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!modifyService.CanRotate(this))
			{
				return false;
			}
			using (new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Rotate"))
			{
				try
				{
					modifyService.RotateElement(this, angle);
					UpdateLayout();
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public bool RotateElement(UIElement element, double angle, System.Windows.Point center)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(angle) || double.IsInfinity(angle))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!modifyService.CanRotate(this))
			{
				return false;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Rotate");
			try
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementRotationCenter = GetElementRotationCenter(element);
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(element);
				double elementWidth = GetElementWidth(element);
				double elementHeight = GetElementHeight(element);
				Vector vector = new Vector(elementWidth * elementRotationCenter.X, elementHeight * elementRotationCenter.Y);
				System.Windows.Point point = elementPosition + new Vector(elementWidth / 2.0, elementHeight / 2.0);
				System.Windows.Point newPosition = System.Windows.Point.Subtract(new RotateTransform(angle, center.X, center.Y).Transform(point), vector);
				modifyService.PositionElement(this, newPosition);
				modifyService.RotateElement(this, angle);
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public bool CanFlipElement(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return modifyService.CanFlip(this);
		}

		public void FlipElement(UIElement element, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(GetElementService(element, typeof(IModifyService)) is IModifyService modifyService) || !modifyService.CanFlip(this))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (element.RenderTransformOrigin.X == 0.0 && element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y == 0.0)
			{
				element.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Flip");
			try
			{
				modifyService.FlipElement(this, flipAxis);
				UpdateLayout();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return Selection.Contains(element);
		}

		public void Select(UIElement toSelect)
		{
			if (toSelect == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("toSelect");
			}
			if (!IsSelected(toSelect) && DiagramElements.Contains(toSelect))
			{
				_selection.Add(toSelect);
				List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
				list.Add(toSelect);
				OnElementsSelected(new SelectionEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<UIElement>(list)));
			}
		}

		public void Select(IEnumerable<UIElement> toSelect)
		{
			if (toSelect == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("toSelect");
			}
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in toSelect)
			{
				if (!IsSelected(item) && DiagramElements.Contains(item))
				{
					_selection.Add(item);
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				OnElementsSelected(new SelectionEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<UIElement>(list)));
			}
		}

		public void Unselect(UIElement toUnselect)
		{
			if (toUnselect == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("toSelect");
			}
			if (IsSelected(toUnselect))
			{
				_selection.Remove(toUnselect);
				List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
				list.Add(toUnselect);
				OnElementsUnselected(new SelectionEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<UIElement>(list)));
			}
		}

		public void Unselect(IEnumerable<UIElement> toUnselect)
		{
			if (toUnselect == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("toSelect");
			}
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in toUnselect)
			{
				if (IsSelected(item))
				{
					_selection.Remove(item);
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				OnElementsUnselected(new SelectionEventArgs(new ReadOnlyCollection<UIElement>(list)));
			}
		}

		public void SelectAll()
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in DiagramElements)
			{
				if (!IsSelected(diagramElement) && !IsGrouped(diagramElement))
				{
					_selection.Add(diagramElement);
					list.Add(diagramElement);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				OnElementsSelected(new SelectionEventArgs(list));
			}
		}

		public void UnselectAll()
		{
			_ = _selection;
			if (_selection.Count != 0)
			{
				List<UIElement> selected = new List<UIElement>(_selection);
				_selection.Clear();
				OnElementsUnselected(new SelectionEventArgs(selected));
			}
		}

		public void AddAdorner(DiagramElementOverlay adorner)
		{
			if (adorner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("adorner");
			}
			_elementOverlays.Add(adorner);
			adorner.Editor = this;
			adorner.Display(OverlayLayer);
		}

		public void AddAdorners(IEnumerable<DiagramElementOverlay> adorners)
		{
			if (adorners == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("adorners");
			}
			foreach (DiagramElementOverlay adorner in adorners)
			{
				AddAdorner(adorner);
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAdorner(DiagramElementOverlay adorner)
		{
			if (adorner == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("adorner");
			}
			_elementOverlays.Remove(adorner);
			adorner.Hide(OverlayLayer);
			adorner.Editor = null;
		}

		public void RemoveAdorners(IEnumerable<DiagramElementOverlay> adorners)
		{
			if (adorners == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("adorners");
			}
			foreach (DiagramElementOverlay adorner in adorners)
			{
				RemoveAdorner(adorner);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DiagramElementOverlay> GetAdorners(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			return ElementOverlays.Where((DiagramElementOverlay adorner) => adorner.AdornedElement == element);
		}

		internal void LockAdornerUpdates()
		{
			_adornerUpdateLockCount++;
		}

		internal void UnlockAdornerUpdates()
		{
			if (_adornerUpdateLockCount == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			_adornerUpdateLockCount--;
			if (_adornerUpdateLockCount != 0 || _pendingAdornerUpdates == null || _pendingAdornerUpdates.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<DependencyObject> pendingAdornerUpdates = _pendingAdornerUpdates;
			_pendingAdornerUpdates = null;
			foreach (DependencyObject item in pendingAdornerUpdates)
			{
				UpdateAdorners(item);
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateAllAdorners()
		{
			UpdateAdorners(null);
		}

		internal void UpdateAdorners(DependencyObject modifiedObject)
		{
			if (_adornerUpdateLockCount > 0)
			{
				if (_pendingAdornerUpdates == null)
				{
					_pendingAdornerUpdates = new List<DependencyObject>();
					_pendingAdornerUpdates.Add(modifiedObject);
				}
				else if (!_pendingAdornerUpdates.Contains(modifiedObject))
				{
					_pendingAdornerUpdates.Add(modifiedObject);
				}
				return;
			}
			foreach (DiagramElementOverlay elementOverlay in ElementOverlays)
			{
				if (modifiedObject == null || elementOverlay.AdornedElement == modifiedObject)
				{
					elementOverlay.Update(OverlayLayer);
				}
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point SnapPointToGrid(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (!SnapToGrid)
			{
				return point;
			}
			double gridMinorSpacing = GridMinorSpacing;
			double num = point.X;
			double num2 = point.Y;
			double num3 = num % gridMinorSpacing;
			double num4 = num2 % gridMinorSpacing;
			if (num3 != 0.0)
			{
				num = ((!(Math.Abs(num3) >= GridMinorSpacing / 2.0)) ? (num - num3) : ((!(num3 > 0.0)) ? (num - (gridMinorSpacing + num3)) : (num + (gridMinorSpacing - num3))));
			}
			if (num4 != 0.0)
			{
				num2 = ((!(Math.Abs(num4) >= gridMinorSpacing / 2.0)) ? (num2 - num4) : ((!(num4 > 0.0)) ? (num2 - (gridMinorSpacing + num4)) : (num2 + (gridMinorSpacing - num4))));
			}
			return new System.Windows.Point(num, num2);
		}

		public Rect GetElementTranformedRect(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			Rect rect = ((!(GetElementService(element, typeof(ISelectionOverlayService)) is ISelectionOverlayService selectionOverlayService)) ? new Rect(element.RenderSize) : selectionOverlayService.GetAdornedRect(this));
			System.Windows.Point point = ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(element, rect.TopLeft);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(element, rect.TopRight);
			System.Windows.Point point3 = ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(element, rect.BottomLeft);
			System.Windows.Point point4 = ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(element, rect.BottomRight);
			Rect result = new Rect(point, point4);
			result.Union(new Rect(point2, point3));
			return result;
		}

		public System.Windows.Point ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (!(element is FrameworkElement frameworkElement))
			{
				return point;
			}
			if (!(frameworkElement.Parent is DiagramLayer diagramLayer))
			{
				return point;
			}
			diagramLayer.UpdateLayout();
			System.Windows.Point result = frameworkElement.TransformToAncestor(diagramLayer).Transform(point);
			result.X += diagramLayer.HorizontalOffset;
			result.Y += diagramLayer.VerticalOffset;
			return result;
		}

		public DiagramAnchor FindAnchor(Guid anchorId)
		{
			DiagramAnchor diagramAnchor = null;
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in DiagramElements)
			{
				if (GetElementService(diagramElement, typeof(IAnchorProvider)) is IAnchorProvider anchorProvider)
				{
					diagramAnchor = anchorProvider.FindAnchor(anchorId);
					if (diagramAnchor != null)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return diagramAnchor;
		}

		public System.Windows.Controls.Image ExportImage(ImagePrintOption printOption, bool keepZoom)
		{
			return ImagingHelper.GetImageFromDiagram(this, printOption, Background, keepZoom ? ZoomScale : 1.0);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			if (MouseWheelZoomOption == WheelZoomOption.Enabled || (MouseWheelZoomOption == WheelZoomOption.EnabledWithCtrlPressed && (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl))))
			{
				if (e.Delta > 0)
				{
					ZoomIn(WheelZoomStepFactor, GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
				}
				else
				{
					ZoomOut(WheelZoomStepFactor, GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseWheel(e);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			UIElement diagramElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			if (diagramElement == null)
			{
				if (e.Source is UIElement { IsKeyboardFocused: false } uIElement)
				{
					uIElement.Focus();
				}
			}
			else if (!diagramElement.IsKeyboardFocused && !diagramElement.IsKeyboardFocusWithin)
			{
				diagramElement.Focus();
			}
			base.OnPreviewMouseDown(e);
		}

		private static void SetZIndex(IEnumerable<UIElement> elements, IList<int> indexes)
		{
			if (elements == null || elements.Count() == 0 || indexes == null || indexes.Count() < elements.Count())
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (UIElement element in elements)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.Panel.SetZIndex(element, indexes[num++]);
			}
		}

		private void OrderElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> orderedElements, string description)
		{
			if (orderedElements == null || orderedElements.Count() == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<int> oldZIndexes = new List<int>();
			List<int> newZIndexes = new List<int>();
			int item = 1 - orderedElements.Count();
			foreach (UIElement orderedElement in orderedElements)
			{
				oldZIndexes.Add(System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(orderedElement));
				newZIndexes.Add(item);
				System.Windows.Controls.Panel.SetZIndex(orderedElement, item++);
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, description);
			try
			{
				if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
				{
					List<UIElement> contextElements = new List<UIElement>(orderedElements);
					transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
					{
						SetZIndex(contextElements, oldZIndexes);
					}, delegate
					{
						SetZIndex(contextElements, newZIndexes);
					});
				}
				if (!transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (UIElement orderedElement2 in orderedElements)
				{
					OnElementModified(new ElementModifiedEventArgs(orderedElement2));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		private List<UIElement> FilterOrderingElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> elementsToOrder)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			if (elementsToOrder != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<UIElement> collection = elementsToOrder.Where((UIElement element) => IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element));
				list.AddRange(collection);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public void BringToFront(IEnumerable<UIElement> elementsToOrder)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = FilterOrderingElements(elementsToOrder);
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> affectedUIElements = GetAffectedUIElements(list);
			if (affectedUIElements != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<UIElement> second = list.OrderBy((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
				IEnumerable<UIElement> orderedElements = affectedUIElements.Except(second).Concat(second);
				OrderElements(orderedElements, "Bring to Front");
			}
		}

		public void SendToBack(IEnumerable<UIElement> elementsToOrder)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = FilterOrderingElements(elementsToOrder);
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> affectedUIElements = GetAffectedUIElements(list);
			if (affectedUIElements != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<UIElement> enumerable = list.OrderBy((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
				IEnumerable<UIElement> orderedElements = enumerable.Concat(affectedUIElements.Except(enumerable));
				OrderElements(orderedElements, "Send to Back");
			}
		}

		public void BringForward(IEnumerable<UIElement> elementsToOrder)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = FilterOrderingElements(elementsToOrder);
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> affectedUIElements = GetAffectedUIElements(list);
			if (affectedUIElements == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> second = list.OrderBy((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
			int maxSelectedZindex = list.Max((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
			IEnumerable<UIElement> orderedElements;
			if (maxSelectedZindex == 0)
			{
				orderedElements = affectedUIElements.Except(list).Concat(second);
			}
			else
			{
				IEnumerable<UIElement> second2 = affectedUIElements.Where((UIElement element) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(element) > maxSelectedZindex + 1);
				orderedElements = affectedUIElements.Except(list).Except(second2).Concat(second)
					.Concat(second2);
			}
			OrderElements(orderedElements, "Bring Forward");
		}

		public void SendBackward(IEnumerable<UIElement> elementsToOrder)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = FilterOrderingElements(elementsToOrder);
			if (list.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> affectedUIElements = GetAffectedUIElements(list);
			if (affectedUIElements == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			IEnumerable<UIElement> enumerable = list.OrderBy((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
			int minSelectedZIndex = list.Min((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
			IEnumerable<UIElement> orderedElements;
			if (minSelectedZIndex == 1 - affectedUIElements.Count())
			{
				orderedElements = enumerable.Concat(affectedUIElements.Except(enumerable));
			}
			else
			{
				IEnumerable<UIElement> enumerable2 = affectedUIElements.Where((UIElement element) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(element) < minSelectedZIndex - 1);
				orderedElements = enumerable2.Concat(enumerable).Concat(affectedUIElements.Except(enumerable2).Except(enumerable));
			}
			OrderElements(orderedElements, "Send Backward");
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> GetAffectedUIElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> selectedElements)
		{
			List<UIElement> overlappedUIElements = GetOverlappedUIElements(selectedElements);
			if (overlappedUIElements.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			IOrderedEnumerable<UIElement> orderedEnumerable = from UIElement uie in overlappedUIElements.Concat(selectedElements)
				orderby System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie), DiagramElements.IndexOf(uie)
				select uie;
			int num = 1 - orderedEnumerable.Count();
			foreach (UIElement item in orderedEnumerable)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.Panel.SetZIndex(item, num++);
			}
			return orderedEnumerable.OrderBy((UIElement uie) => System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(uie));
		}

		private List<UIElement> GetOverlappedUIElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> selectedElements)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			if (selectedElements != null && selectedElements.Count() > 0)
			{
				List<Rect> list2 = new List<Rect>();
				List<UIElement> list3 = selectedElements.ToList();
				IEnumerable<UIElement> enumerable = DiagramElements.Cast<UIElement>().Except(selectedElements);
				while (list3.Count > 0)
				{
					list2.Clear();
					foreach (UIElement item in list3)
					{
						list2.Add(ClipRegionHelper.GetElementActualRegion(item));
					}
					list3.Clear();
					foreach (UIElement item2 in enumerable)
					{
						foreach (Rect item3 in list2)
						{
							if (item3.IntersectsWith(ClipRegionHelper.GetElementActualRegion(item2)) && !list.Contains(item2))
							{
								list.Add(item2);
								list3.Add(item2);
							}
						}
					}
					enumerable = enumerable.Except(list3);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDisposing()
		{
			if (this.Disposing != null)
			{
				this.Disposing(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementAdded(ElementAddedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (IsElementInPrimaryLayer(args.Element) && this.ElementAdded != null)
			{
				this.ElementAdded(this, args);
			}
		}

		{
			if (this.ElementRemoved != null)
			{
				this.ElementRemoved(this, args);
			}
		}

		public void RaiseElementRemoved(ElementRemovedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnElementRemoved(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementModified(ElementModifiedEventArgs args)
		{
			UpdateLayout();
			UpdateAdorners(args.Element);
			GetElementGroup(args.Element)?.UpdateGroupRectangle();
			if (IsElementInPrimaryLayer(args.Element) && this.ElementModified != null)
			{
				this.ElementModified(this, args);
			}
		}

		public void RaiseElementModified(ElementModifiedEventArgs args)
		{
			OnElementModified(args);
		}

		protected virtual void OnResolvingDrawingUri(ResolveDrawingUriEventArgs args)
		{
			if (this.ResolvingDrawingUri != null)
			{
				this.ResolvingDrawingUri(null, args);
			}
		}

		public UIElement ResolveDrawingUri(Symbol symbol, string drawingUri)
		{
			ResolveDrawingUriEventArgs e = new ResolveDrawingUriEventArgs(symbol, drawingUri);
			OnResolvingDrawingUri(e);
			if (e.Drawing == null)
			{
				e.Drawing = BuiltInDrawingFactory.ResolveDrawingUri(drawingUri);
			}
			return e.Drawing;
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementsSelected(SelectionEventArgs args)
		{
			if (!IsReadOnly)
			{
				foreach (UIElement item in args.Selected)
				{
					if (GetElementService(item, typeof(ISelectionOverlayService)) is ISelectionOverlayService selectionOverlayService)
					{
						selectionOverlayService.AddAdorners(this);
					}
				}
			}
			if (this.ElementsSelected != null)
			{
				this.ElementsSelected(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementsUnselected(SelectionEventArgs args)
		{
			List<DiagramElementOverlay> list = new List<DiagramElementOverlay>();
			foreach (DiagramElementOverlay elementOverlay in ElementOverlays)
			{
				if (elementOverlay.AdornedElement != null && args.Selected.Contains(elementOverlay.AdornedElement))
				{
					list.Add(elementOverlay);
				}
			}
			RemoveAdorners(list);
			if (this.ElementsUnselected != null)
			{
				this.ElementsUnselected(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewElementRemoved(PreviewElementRemovedEventArgs args)

			{
				this.PreviewElementRemoved(this, args);
			}
		}

				this.PreviewElementAdded(this, args);

		}

		public bool IsElementInPrimaryLayer(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element != null && DiagramElements.Contains(element))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public IEnumerable<UIElement> GroupMembers(DiagramGroup g)
		{
			return GroupMembers(g.Id);
		}

		public IEnumerable<UIElement> GroupMembers(Guid groupId)
		{
			if (!(groupId != Guid.Empty))
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in DiagramElements)
			{
				if (GetGroupId(diagramElement) == groupId)
				{
					yield return diagramElement;
				}
			}
		}

		private DiagramGroup FindGroup(Guid groupId)
		{
			foreach (DiagramGroup diagramGroup in DiagramGroups)
			{
				if (diagramGroup.Id == groupId)
				{
					return diagramGroup;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public bool IsGrouped(UIElement e)
		{
			return GetGroupId(e) != Guid.Empty;
		}

		public bool IsMemberOfGroup(UIElement e, DiagramGroup group)
		{
			return IsMemberOfGroup(e, group.Id);
		}

		public bool IsMemberOfGroup(UIElement e, Guid groupId)
		{
			Guid groupId2 = GetGroupId(e);
			if (groupId2 == Guid.Empty)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (groupId2 == groupId)
			{
				return true;
			}
			DiagramGroup diagramGroup = FindGroup(groupId2);
			if (diagramGroup == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsMemberOfGroup(diagramGroup, groupId);
		}

		public DiagramGroup GetElementGroup(UIElement e)
		{
			Guid groupId = GetGroupId(e);
			if (groupId == Guid.Empty)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return FindGroup(groupId);
		}

		public DiagramGroup GetUltimateParentGroup(UIElement e)
		{
			DiagramGroup diagramGroup = GetElementGroup(e);
			if (diagramGroup == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Guid groupId = GetGroupId(diagramGroup);
			while (groupId != Guid.Empty)
			{
				DiagramGroup diagramGroup2 = FindGroup(groupId);
				if (diagramGroup2 == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				diagramGroup = diagramGroup2;
				groupId = GetGroupId(diagramGroup);
			}
			return diagramGroup;
		}

		public DiagramGroup GroupElements(IEnumerable<UIElement> candidateMembers)
		{
			List<UIElement> members = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement candidateMember in candidateMembers)
			{
				DiagramGroup ultimateParentGroup = GetUltimateParentGroup(candidateMember);
				if (ultimateParentGroup == null)
				{
					members.Add(candidateMember);
				}
				else if (!members.Contains(ultimateParentGroup))
				{
					members.Add(ultimateParentGroup);
				}
			}
			DiagramGroup diagramGroup = null;
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Group"))
			{
				try
				{
					Guid groupId = Guid.NewGuid();
					diagramGroup = new DiagramGroup();
					diagramGroup.Id = groupId;
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (UIElement item in members)
					{
						if (!flag && IsSelected(item))
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						SetGroupId(item, diagramGroup.Id);
					}
					diagramGroup.UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(this, members);
					if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
					{
						transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
						{
							foreach (UIElement item2 in members)
							{
								SetGroupId(item2, Guid.Empty);
							}
						}, delegate
						{
							foreach (UIElement item3 in members)
							{
								SetGroupId(item3, groupId);
							}
						});
					}
					AddElement(diagramGroup);
					if (flag)
					{
						Unselect(members);
						Select(diagramGroup);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			return diagramGroup;
		}

		public void UngroupElements(DiagramGroup group)
		{
			List<UIElement> members = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in GroupMembers(group))
			{
				members.Add(item);
			}
			Guid newGroupId = GetGroupId(group);
			Guid oldGroupId = group.Id;
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "Ungroup");
			try
			{
				foreach (UIElement item2 in members)
				{
					SetGroupId(item2, newGroupId);
				}
				RemoveElement(group);
				DiagramGroup oldRegroupGroup = _regroupGroup;
				List<UIElement> oldRegroupMembers = _regroupMembers;
				_regroupGroup = group;
				_regroupMembers = members;
				if (!transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
				{
					return;
				}
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					_regroupGroup = oldRegroupGroup;
					_regroupMembers = oldRegroupMembers;
					foreach (UIElement item3 in members)
					{
						SetGroupId(item3, oldGroupId);
					}
				}, delegate
				{
					_regroupGroup = group;
					_regroupMembers = members;
					foreach (UIElement item4 in members)
					{
						SetGroupId(item4, newGroupId);
					}
				});
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		public DiagramGroup RegroupElements()
		{
			if (!CanRegroup)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DiagramGroup group = null;
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(TransactionManager, "CreateGroup"))
			{
				try
				{
					group = _regroupGroup;
					List<UIElement> members = _regroupMembers;
					_regroupGroup = null;
					_regroupMembers = null;
					Guid newGroupId = group.Id;
					Guid oldGroupId = ((members.Count > 0) ? GetGroupId(members[0]) : Guid.Empty);
					foreach (UIElement item in members)
					{
						SetGroupId(item, newGroupId);
					}
					if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
					{
						transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
						{
							foreach (UIElement item2 in members)
							{
								SetGroupId(item2, oldGroupId);
							}
							_regroupGroup = group;
							_regroupMembers = members;
						}, delegate
						{
							_regroupGroup = null;
							_regroupMembers = null;
							foreach (UIElement item3 in members)
							{
								SetGroupId(item3, newGroupId);
							}
						});
					}
					AddElement(group);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			return group;
		}

		private void UpdateLayersMinimumSize()
		{
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.MinimumSize = MinimumSize;
			}
		}

		private static void OnZoomScalePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is Diagram diagram)
			{
				diagram.OnZoomScaleChanged((double)args.OldValue, (double)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static object OnZoomScalePropertyCoerced(DependencyObject d, object baseValue)
		{
			Diagram diagram = d as Diagram;
			double num = (double)baseValue;
			if (double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				return 1.0;
			}
			if (num < diagram.MinimumZoom)
			{
				return diagram.MinimumZoom;
			}
			if (num > diagram.MaximumZoom)
			{
				return diagram.MaximumZoom;
			}
			return num;
		}

		private static void OnIsReadOnlyPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Diagram diagram = d as Diagram;
			if (diagram.IsReadOnly && diagram.Selection.Count > 0)
			{
				diagram.UnselectAll();
			}
		}

		private static void OnShowGridsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as Diagram).UnderlayLayer.UpdateGridLinesAndScaleLines();
		}

		private static void OnShowRulesPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(d as Diagram).UnderlayLayer.UpdateGridLinesAndScaleLines();
		}

		protected void InitializeFields()
		{
			_elementOverlaysRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<DiagramElementOverlay>(_elementOverlays);
			_selectionRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<UIElement>(_selection);
			TransactionManager = new TransactionManager();
		}

		protected sealed override Visual GetVisualChild(int index)
		{
			return index switch
			{
				0 => UnderlayLayer, 
				1 => PrimaryLayer, 
				2 => OverlayLayer, 
				3 => _horizontalScrollbar, 
				4 => _verticalScrollbar, 
				_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index"), 
			};
		}

		private void InitializeComponent()
		{
			UnderlayLayer = new DiagramUnderlayLayer
			{
				Diagram = this,
				MinimumSize = MinimumSize
			};
			PrimaryLayer = new DiagramPrimaryLayer
			{
				Diagram = this,
				MinimumSize = MinimumSize
			};
			OverlayLayer = new DiagramOverlayLayer
			{
				Diagram = this,
				MinimumSize = MinimumSize
			};
			UnderlayLayer.SetBinding(DiagramUnderlayLayer.ShowPageBorderProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(ShowPageBorderProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			UnderlayLayer.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Panel.BackgroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(BackgroundProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
			_verticalScrollbar = new System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollBar();
			_verticalScrollbar.Orientation = System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Vertical;
			_verticalScrollbar.Scroll += _verticalScrollbar_Scroll;
			_verticalScrollbar.LargeChange = 50.0;
			_verticalScrollbar.SmallChange = 20.0;
			_horizontalScrollbar = new System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollBar();
			_horizontalScrollbar.Orientation = System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal;
			_horizontalScrollbar.Scroll += _horizontalScrollbar_Scroll;
			_horizontalScrollbar.LargeChange = 50.0;
			_horizontalScrollbar.SmallChange = 20.0;
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.LayoutTransform = _zoomTransform;
			}
			AddLogicalChild(UnderlayLayer);
			AddLogicalChild(PrimaryLayer);
			AddLogicalChild(OverlayLayer);
			AddLogicalChild(_verticalScrollbar);
			AddLogicalChild(_horizontalScrollbar);
			AddVisualChild(UnderlayLayer);
			AddVisualChild(PrimaryLayer);
			AddVisualChild(OverlayLayer);
			AddVisualChild(_verticalScrollbar);
			AddVisualChild(_horizontalScrollbar);
		}

		private void _horizontalScrollbar_Scroll(object sender, System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.SetHorizontalOffset(e.NewValue, e.ScrollEventType != System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollEventType.ThumbTrack);
			}
		}

		private void _verticalScrollbar_Scroll(object sender, System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.SetVerticalOffset(e.NewValue, e.ScrollEventType != System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.ScrollEventType.ThumbTrack);
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateHorizontalScrollInfo(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (layer == PrimaryLayer)
			{
				_horizontalScrollbar.Minimum = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalMinimum;
				_horizontalScrollbar.Maximum = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalMaximum;
				_horizontalScrollbar.Value = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalOffset;
				_horizontalScrollbar.ViewportSize = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalViewport;
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateVerticalScrollInfo(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (layer == PrimaryLayer)
			{
				_verticalScrollbar.Minimum = PrimaryLayer.VerticalMinimum;
				_verticalScrollbar.Maximum = PrimaryLayer.VerticalMaximum;
				_verticalScrollbar.Value = PrimaryLayer.VerticalOffset;
				_verticalScrollbar.ViewportSize = PrimaryLayer.VerticalViewport;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateViewportSize()
		{
			double num = (double.IsNaN(_verticalScrollbar.Width) ? 0.0 : _verticalScrollbar.Width);
			double num2 = (double.IsNaN(_horizontalScrollbar.Height) ? 0.0 : _horizontalScrollbar.Height);
			System.Windows.Size size = new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(0.0, base.RenderSize.Width - num), Math.Max(0.0, base.RenderSize.Height - num2));
			RectangleGeometry clip = new RectangleGeometry(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, size.Width / ZoomScale, size.Height / ZoomScale));
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.Clip = clip;
				layer.VerticalViewport = size.Height / ZoomScale;
				layer.HorizontalViewport = size.Width / ZoomScale;
			}
			UnderlayLayer.UpdateGridLinesAndScaleLines();
			_horizontalScrollbar.Width = size.Width;
			_verticalScrollbar.Height = size.Height;
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			UpdateViewportSize();
			base.OnRenderSizeChanged(sizeInfo);
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			double num = (double.IsNaN(_verticalScrollbar.Width) ? 0.0 : _verticalScrollbar.Width);
			double num2 = (double.IsNaN(_horizontalScrollbar.Height) ? 0.0 : _horizontalScrollbar.Height);
			System.Windows.Size availableSize2 = new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(0.0, availableSize.Width - num), Math.Max(0.0, availableSize.Height - num2));
			PrimaryLayer.Measure(availableSize2);
			UnderlayLayer.Measure(availableSize2);
			OverlayLayer.Measure(availableSize2);
			_horizontalScrollbar.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(availableSize2.Width, num2));
			_verticalScrollbar.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(num, availableSize2.Height));
			System.Windows.Size validMeasureSize = RegionHelper.GetValidMeasureSize(availableSize);
			return new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(validMeasureSize.Width, num), Math.Max(validMeasureSize.Height, num2));
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = (double.IsNaN(_verticalScrollbar.Width) ? 0.0 : _verticalScrollbar.Width);
			double num2 = (double.IsNaN(_horizontalScrollbar.Height) ? 0.0 : _horizontalScrollbar.Height);
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, finalSize.Height - num2), _horizontalScrollbar.DesiredSize);
			_horizontalScrollbar.Arrange(finalRect);
			Rect finalRect2 = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(finalSize.Width - num, 0.0), _verticalScrollbar.DesiredSize);
			_verticalScrollbar.Arrange(finalRect2);
			Rect finalRect3 = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), UnderlayLayer.DesiredSize);
			UnderlayLayer.Arrange(finalRect3);
			Rect finalRect4 = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), PrimaryLayer.DesiredSize);
			PrimaryLayer.Arrange(finalRect4);
			Rect finalRect5 = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), OverlayLayer.DesiredSize);
			OverlayLayer.Arrange(finalRect5);
			System.Windows.Size validMeasureSize = RegionHelper.GetValidMeasureSize(finalSize);
			return new System.Windows.Size(Math.Max(validMeasureSize.Width, num), Math.Max(validMeasureSize.Height, num2));
		}

		public void SetHorizontalOffset(double scrollOffset)
		{
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.SetHorizontalOffset(scrollOffset);
			}
		}

		public void SetVerticalOffset(double scrollOffset)
		{
			foreach (DiagramLayer layer in Layers)
			{
				layer.SetVerticalOffset(scrollOffset);
			}
		}

		public void LineUp()

			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - 10.0);
		}

		public void LineDown()
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset + 10.0);
		}

		public void LineLeft()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset - 10.0);
		}

		public void LineRight()
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset + 10.0);
		}

		{
			LineDown();
		}

			LineLeft();
		}

		}

		}

			LineDown();
		}

		{

		}

		public void PageRight()
		{
			LineRight();
		}
