			LineUp();
		}

		public void ScrollPointIntoView(System.Windows.Point canvasPoint, double viewportMargin)
		{
			System.Windows.Point viewportPoint = CanvasPointToViewportPoint(canvasPoint);
			bool flag = false;
			if (viewportPoint.X < viewportMargin)
			{
				viewportPoint.X = viewportMargin;
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (viewportPoint.X > TrueViewportWidth - viewportMargin)
			{
				viewportPoint.X = TrueViewportWidth - viewportMargin;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (viewportPoint.Y < viewportMargin)
			{
				viewportPoint.Y = viewportMargin;
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (viewportPoint.Y > TrueViewportHeight - viewportMargin)
			{
				viewportPoint.Y = TrueViewportHeight - viewportMargin;
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				ScrollCanvasPointToViewportPoint(canvasPoint, viewportPoint);
			}
		}

		public void ScrollCanvasPointToViewportPoint(System.Windows.Point canvasPoint, System.Windows.Point viewportPoint)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = ViewportPointToCanvasPoint(viewportPoint);
			CanvasPointToViewportPoint(point);
			SetHorizontalOffset(HorizontalOffset - (point.X - canvasPoint.X));
			SetVerticalOffset(VerticalOffset - (point.Y - canvasPoint.Y));
		}

		public void ZoomIn(System.Windows.Point zoomCenter)
		{
			ZoomIn(ZoomStepFactor, zoomCenter);
		}

		public void ZoomIn(double zoomFactor, System.Windows.Point zoomCenter)
		{
			ZoomToScale(zoomFactor * ZoomScale, zoomCenter);
		}

		public void ZoomOut(System.Windows.Point zoomCenter)
		{
			ZoomOut(ZoomStepFactor, zoomCenter);
		}

		public void ZoomOut(double zoomFactor, System.Windows.Point zoomCenter)
		{
			ZoomToScale(ZoomScale / zoomFactor, zoomCenter);
		}

		public void ZoomActualSize()
		{
			double horizontalOffset = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalOffset;
			double verticalOffset = PrimaryLayer.VerticalOffset;
			ZoomToScale(1.0, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			SetHorizontalOffset(horizontalOffset);
			SetVerticalOffset(verticalOffset);
		}

		public void ZoomToScale(double zoomScale, System.Windows.Point zoomCenter)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = CanvasPointToViewportPoint(zoomCenter);
			System.Windows.Point position = Mouse.GetPosition(this);
			_ = point - position;
			ZoomScale = zoomScale;
			ScrollCanvasPointToViewportPoint(zoomCenter, point);
		}

		public void ZoomToRect(Rect rect)
		{
			if (rect.Width <= double.Epsilon || rect.Height <= double.Epsilon)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
			}
			double val = PrimaryLayer.HorizontalViewport / rect.Width;
			double val2 = PrimaryLayer.VerticalViewport / rect.Height;
			double zoomScale = ZoomScale * Math.Min(val, val2);
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point((rect.Left + rect.Right) / 2.0, (rect.Top + rect.Bottom) / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = CanvasPointToViewportPoint(point);
			ZoomScale = zoomScale;
			ViewportPointToCanvasPoint(point2);
			ScrollCanvasPointToViewportPoint(point, new System.Windows.Point(TrueViewportWidth / 2.0, TrueViewportHeight / 2.0));
		}

		public void ZoomFitToScreen()
		{
			ZoomFitToScreen(ZoomFullMargin);
		}

		public void ZoomFitToScreen(Thickness margin)
		{
			Rect boundingRect = PrimaryLayer.BoundingRect;
			ZoomToRect(new Rect(boundingRect.TopLeft - new Vector(margin.Left, margin.Top), boundingRect.BottomRight + new Vector(margin.Right, margin.Bottom)));
		}

		protected void OnZoomScaleChanged(double oldScale, double newScale)
		{
			try
			{
				LockAdornerUpdates();
				_zoomTransform.ScaleX = newScale;
				_zoomTransform.ScaleY = newScale;
				UpdateViewportSize();
			}
			finally
			{
				UnlockAdornerUpdates();
			}
			InvalidateMeasure();
			UpdateAllAdorners();
			if (this.ZoomScaleChanged != null)
			{
				this.ZoomScaleChanged(this, new ZoomScaleChangedEventArgs(oldScale, newScale));
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates()
		{
			return PrimaryLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
		}

		public System.Windows.Point VisualCoordinatesToCanvasCoordinates(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			return PrimaryLayer.VisualCoordinatesToLayerCoordinates(point);
		}

		public System.Windows.Point CanvasCoordinatesToVisualCoordinates(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			return PrimaryLayer.LayerCoordinatesToVisualCoordinates(point);
		}

		public System.Windows.Point ViewportPointToCanvasPoint(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point2 = point;
			point2.X += ZoomScale * PrimaryLayer.HorizontalOffset;
			point2.Y += ZoomScale * PrimaryLayer.VerticalOffset;
			if (_zoomTransform != null)
			{
				point2 = _zoomTransform.Inverse.Transform(point2);
			}
			return point2;
		}

		public System.Windows.Point CanvasPointToViewportPoint(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point2 = point;
			if (_zoomTransform != null)
			{
				point2 = _zoomTransform.Transform(point2);
			}
			point2.X -= ZoomScale * PrimaryLayer.HorizontalOffset;
			point2.Y -= ZoomScale * PrimaryLayer.VerticalOffset;
			return point2;
		}
	}
	public static class DiagramCommands
	{
		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomIn { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomOut { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomFitToScreen { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand ZoomActualSize { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand FlipHorizontal { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand FlipVertical { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand RotateLeft { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand RotateRight { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand Group { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand Ungroup { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand Regroup { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand Reroute { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand BringToFront { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand SendToBack { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand BringForward { get; private set; }

		public static RoutedUICommand SendBackward { get; private set; }

		static DiagramCommands()
		{
			ZoomIn = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Zoom_ZoomIn, "ZoomIn", string.Empty);
			ZoomOut = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Zoom_ZoomOut, "ZoomOut", string.Empty);
			ZoomFitToScreen = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Zoom_FitScreen, "ZoomFitToScreen", string.Empty);
			ZoomActualSize = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Zoom_ActualSize, "ZoomActualSize", string.Empty);
			FlipHorizontal = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_FlipAndRotate_FlipHorizontal, "FlipHorizontal", string.Empty);
			FlipVertical = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_FlipAndRotate_FlipVertical, "FlipVertical", string.Empty);
			RotateLeft = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_FlipAndRotate_RotateLeft, "RotateLeft", string.Empty);
			RotateRight = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_FlipAndRotate_RotateRight, "RotateRight", string.Empty);
			Group = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Grouping_Group, "Group", string.Empty);
			Ungroup = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Grouping_Ungroup, "Ungroup", string.Empty);
			Regroup = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Grouping_Regroup, "Regroup", string.Empty);
			Reroute = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Connector_Reroute, "Reroute", string.Empty);
			BringToFront = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Order_BringToFront, "BringToFront", string.Empty);
			SendToBack = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Order_SendToBack, "SendToBack", string.Empty);
			BringForward = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Order_BringForward, "BringForward", string.Empty);
			SendBackward = CreateCommand(Resources.Menu_Order_SendBackward, "SendBackward", string.Empty);
		}

		private static RoutedUICommand CreateCommand(string text, string name, string gesture)
		{
			RoutedUICommand routedUICommand = new RoutedUICommand(text, name, typeof(Diagram));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(gesture))
			{
				try
				{
					routedUICommand.InputGestures.Add(new KeyGestureConverter().ConvertFromString(gesture) as InputGesture);
				}
				catch (ArgumentNullException ex)
				{
					Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
				}
			}
			return routedUICommand;
		}
	}
	public static class DiagramCursors
	{
		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorZoomIn;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorZoomOut;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorZoomRegion;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorGrabHand;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorGrabHandGrasp;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorRotateInit;

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursorRotating;

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor ZoomIn
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorZoomIn == null)
				{
					_cursorZoomIn = LoadCursor(Resources.ZoomIn);
				}
				return _cursorZoomIn;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor ZoomOut
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorZoomOut == null)
				{
					_cursorZoomOut = LoadCursor(Resources.ZoomOut);
				}
				return _cursorZoomOut;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor ZoomRegion
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorZoomRegion == null)
				{
					_cursorZoomRegion = LoadCursor(Resources.ZoomRegion);
				}
				return _cursorZoomRegion;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor GrabHand
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorGrabHand == null)
				{
					_cursorGrabHand = LoadCursor(Resources.GrabHand);
				}
				return _cursorGrabHand;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor GrabHandGrasp
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorGrabHandGrasp == null)
				{
					_cursorGrabHandGrasp = LoadCursor(Resources.GrabHandGrasp);
				}
				return _cursorGrabHandGrasp;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor RotateInit
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorRotateInit == null)
				{
					_cursorRotateInit = LoadCursor(Resources.RotateInit);
				}
				return _cursorRotateInit;
			}
		}

		public static System.Windows.Input.Cursor Rotating
		{
			get
			{
				if (_cursorRotating == null)
				{
					_cursorRotating = LoadCursor(Resources.Rotating);
				}
				return _cursorRotating;
			}
		}

		private static System.Windows.Input.Cursor LoadCursor(byte[] cursorData)
		{
			System.Windows.Input.Cursor cursor = null;
			try
			{
				cursor = new System.Windows.Input.Cursor(new MemoryStream(cursorData));
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (cursor == null)
			{
				return System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;
			}
			return cursor;
		}
	}
	public class DiagramGroup : Shape, IServiceProvider, IModifyService, ISelectionOverlayService
	{
		private RectangleGeometry _rectangleGeometry = new RectangleGeometry();

		protected override Geometry DefiningGeometry => _rectangleGeometry;

		public UIElement AdornedElement => this;

		public Guid Id
		{
			get
			{
				return Diagram.GetId(this);
			}
			set
			{
				Diagram.SetId(this, value);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<UIElement> Members
		{
			get
			{
				Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
				if (parentDiagram != null)
				{
					yield break;
				}
				foreach (UIElement item in parentDiagram.GroupMembers(this))
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}

		public object GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (serviceType == typeof(IModifyService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool CanMove(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public bool CanRotate(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public bool CanResize(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public bool CanFlip(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return true;
		}

		public bool ShouldShowQuickTool(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public void PositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(this);
			Vector vector = newPosition - elementPosition;
			new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.GroupMembers(this))
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (Diagram.GetElementService(item, typeof(IAnchorConsumer)) is IAnchorConsumer anchorConsumer)
				{
					foreach (DiagramAnchor currentAnchor in anchorConsumer.CurrentAnchors)
					{
						if (diagram.IsMemberOfGroup(currentAnchor.Provider.Element, this))
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementPosition2 = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item);
					diagram.PositionElement(item, elementPosition2 + vector);
				}
			}
		}

		public void RotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(this);
			Vector vector = new Vector(diagram.GetElementWidth(this) / 2.0, diagram.GetElementHeight(this) / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point center = elementPosition + vector;
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.GroupMembers(this))
			{
				diagram.RotateElement(item, angle, center);
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = diagram.GetTransformGroup(this).Clone();
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = DefaultModifyService.GetRotateTransform(transformGroup);
			if (rotateTransform != null)
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				FlipAxis elementFlipAxis = diagram.GetElementFlipAxis(this);
				num = ((elementFlipAxis != FlipAxis.Horizontal && elementFlipAxis != FlipAxis.Vertical) ? (num + angle) : (num - angle));
				rotateTransform.Angle += num;
				base.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
				UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(diagram, diagram.GroupMembers(this));
			}
		}

		public void FlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(diagram, diagram.GroupMembers(this));
		}

		public void SizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			System.Windows.Size elementSize = diagram.GetElementSize(this);
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(this);
			double num = size.Width / elementSize.Width;
			double num2 = size.Height / elementSize.Height;
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.GroupMembers(this))
			{
				if (!(item is Label))
				{
					Vector vector = Diagram.GetElementPosition(item) - elementPosition;
					Vector vector2 = new Vector
					{
						X = vector.X * num,
						Y = vector.Y * num2
					};
					System.Windows.Point newPosition = elementPosition + vector2;
					diagram.PositionElement(item, newPosition);
					System.Windows.Size elementSize2 = diagram.GetElementSize(item);
					System.Windows.Size size2 = new System.Windows.Size
					{
						Width = elementSize2.Width * num,
						Height = elementSize2.Height * num2
					};
					diagram.SetElementSize(item, size2);
				}
			}
			UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(diagram, diagram.GroupMembers(this));
		}

		public void RemoveElement(Diagram diagram)
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.GroupMembers(this))
			{
				list.Add(item);
			}
			foreach (UIElement item2 in list)
			{
				if (Diagram.GetParentDiagram(item2) == diagram)
				{
					diagram.RemoveElement(item2);
				}
			}
			DefaultModifyService.RemoveElement(diagram, this);
		}

		public Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(AdornedElement));
		}

		public void AddAdorners(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (diagram != null)
			{
				diagram.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new RotationOverlay(this));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle));
				diagram.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle));
			}
		}

		public void UpdateGroupRectangle()
		{
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(parentDiagram, parentDiagram.GroupMembers(this));
				parentDiagram.GetElementGroup(this)?.UpdateGroupRectangle();
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateGroupRectangleInternal(Diagram diagram, IEnumerable<UIElement> members)
		{
			if (_rectangleGeometry == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = true;
			Rect rect = default(Rect);
			foreach (UIElement member in members)
			{
				Rect elementTranformedRect = diagram.GetElementTranformedRect(member);
				if (!flag)
				{
					rect.Union(elementTranformedRect);
					continue;
				}
				rect = elementTranformedRect;
				flag = false;
			}
			Rect originalRect = _rectangleGeometry.Rect;
			Rect newRect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, rect.Width, rect.Height);
			_rectangleGeometry.Rect = newRect;
			DefaultModifyService.PositionElement(diagram, this, rect.TopLeft);
			DefaultModifyService.SizeElement(diagram, this, rect.Size);
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Update Group");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(this))
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					_rectangleGeometry.Rect = originalRect;
				}, delegate
				{
					_rectangleGeometry.Rect = newRect;
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class DiagramLayer : System.Windows.Controls.Panel
	{
		private const double _extentMargin = 20.0;

		public static DependencyProperty TopProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("Top", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnMinimumSizePropertyChanged));

		public static DependencyProperty LeftProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("Left", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnMinimumSizePropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinimumSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("MinimumSize", typeof(System.Windows.Size), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new System.Windows.Size(10.0, 10.0), FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender, OnMinimumSizePropertyChanged));

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HorizontalOffsetKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HorizontalOffset", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnHorizontalOffsetPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HorizontalOffsetProperty = HorizontalOffsetKey.DependencyProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey VerticalOffsetKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("VerticalOffset", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnVerticalOffsetPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VerticalOffsetProperty = VerticalOffsetKey.DependencyProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey HorizontalViewportKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("HorizontalViewport", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnHorizontalViewportPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty HorizontalViewportProperty = HorizontalViewportKey.DependencyProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey VerticalViewportKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("VerticalViewport", typeof(double), typeof(DiagramLayer), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnVerticalViewportPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VerticalViewportProperty = VerticalViewportKey.DependencyProperty;

		protected static readonly DependencyPropertyKey BoundingRectKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("BoundingRect", typeof(Rect), typeof(DiagramLayer), new PropertyMetadata(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BoundingRectProperty = BoundingRectKey.DependencyProperty;

		private Diagram _diagram;

		public System.Windows.Size MinimumSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (System.Windows.Size)GetValue(MinimumSizeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(MinimumSizeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double HorizontalOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(HorizontalOffsetProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				SetValue(HorizontalOffsetKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double VerticalOffset
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(VerticalOffsetProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				SetValue(VerticalOffsetKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double HorizontalViewport
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(HorizontalViewportProperty);
			}
			internal set
			{
				SetValue(HorizontalViewportKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double VerticalViewport
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(VerticalViewportProperty);
			}
			internal set
			{
				SetValue(VerticalViewportKey, value);
			}
		}

		public Rect BoundingRect
		{
			get
			{
				return (Rect)GetValue(BoundingRectProperty);
			}
			protected set
			{
				SetValue(BoundingRectKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double HorizontalMaximum { get; private set; }

		public double HorizontalMinimum { get; private set; }

		public double VerticalMaximum { get; set; }

		public double VerticalMinimum { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public Diagram Diagram
		{
			get
			{
				if (_diagram == null)
				{
					DependencyObject parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(this);
					_diagram = parent as Diagram;
					while (_diagram == null && parent != null)
					{
						parent = LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(parent);
						_diagram = parent as Diagram;
					}
				}
				return _diagram;
			}
			set
			{
				_diagram = value;
			}
		}

		public static double GetTop(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(TopProperty);
		}

		public static void SetTop(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(TopProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetLeft(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(LeftProperty);
		}

		public static void SetLeft(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(LeftProperty, value);
		}

		private static void OnMinimumSizePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				diagramLayer.OnMinimumSizeChanged((System.Windows.Size)args.OldValue, (System.Windows.Size)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMinimumSizeChanged(System.Windows.Size oldValue, System.Windows.Size newValue)
		{
		}

		private static void OnHorizontalOffsetPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				diagramLayer.OnHorizontalOffsetChanged((double)args.OldValue, (double)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHorizontalOffsetChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
		}

		private static void OnVerticalOffsetPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				diagramLayer.OnVerticalOffsetChanged((double)args.OldValue, (double)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnVerticalOffsetChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
		}

		private static void OnHorizontalViewportPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DiagramLayer diagramLayer && args.NewValue != args.OldValue)
			{
				diagramLayer.RecalculateHorizontalExtent();
				diagramLayer.OnHorizontalViewportChanged((double)args.OldValue, (double)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHorizontalViewportChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
		}

		private static void OnVerticalViewportPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs args)
		{
			if (d is DiagramLayer diagramLayer && args.NewValue != args.OldValue)
			{
				diagramLayer.RecalculateVerticalExtent();
				diagramLayer.OnVerticalViewportChanged((double)args.OldValue, (double)args.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnVerticalViewportChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetElementPosition(UIElement element)
		{
			System.Windows.Point result = new System.Windows.Point(GetLeft(element), GetTop(element));
			if (double.IsNaN(result.X))
			{
				result.X = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(result.Y))
			{
				result.Y = 0.0;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SetHorizontalOffset(double scrollOffset)
		{
			SetHorizontalOffset(scrollOffset, recalculateExtent: true);
		}

		public void SetHorizontalOffset(double scrollOffset, bool recalculateExtent)
		{
			HorizontalOffset = scrollOffset;
			if (recalculateExtent)
			{
				RecalculateHorizontalExtent();
			}
		}

		public void SetVerticalOffset(double scrollOffset)
		{
			SetVerticalOffset(scrollOffset, recalculateExtent: true);
		}

		public void SetVerticalOffset(double scrollOffset, bool recalculateExtent)
		{
			VerticalOffset = scrollOffset;
			if (recalculateExtent)
			{
				RecalculateVerticalExtent();
			}
		}

		protected void RecalculateHorizontalExtent()
		{
			Rect boundingRect = BoundingRect;
			boundingRect.Union(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), MinimumSize));
			double num = boundingRect.Left;
			if (num + HorizontalOffset < 20.0)
			{
				num = HorizontalOffset - 20.0;
			}
			double num2 = boundingRect.Right - HorizontalViewport;
			if (num2 - HorizontalOffset < 20.0)
			{
				num2 = 20.0 + HorizontalOffset;
			}
			if (base.Width != HorizontalViewport + num2 - num)
			{
				base.Width = HorizontalViewport + num2 - num;
			}
			if (HorizontalMinimum != num || HorizontalMaximum != num2)
			{
				HorizontalMinimum = num;
				HorizontalMaximum = num2;
				if (Diagram != null)
				{
					Diagram.UpdateHorizontalScrollInfo(this);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void RecalculateVerticalExtent()
		{
			Rect boundingRect = BoundingRect;
			boundingRect.Union(new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), MinimumSize));
			double num = boundingRect.Top;
			if (num + VerticalOffset < 20.0)
			{
				num = VerticalOffset - 20.0;
			}
			double num2 = boundingRect.Bottom - VerticalViewport;
			if (num2 - VerticalOffset < 20.0)
			{
				num2 = 20.0 + VerticalOffset;
			}
			if (base.Height != VerticalViewport + num2 - num)
			{
				base.Height = VerticalViewport + num2 - num;
			}
			if (VerticalMinimum != num || VerticalMaximum != num2)
			{
				VerticalMinimum = num;
				VerticalMaximum = num2;
				if (Diagram != null)
				{
					Diagram.UpdateVerticalScrollInfo(this);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			Rect rect = Rect.Empty;
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(double.PositiveInfinity, double.PositiveInfinity));
				rect.Union(new Rect(GetElementPosition(child), child.DesiredSize));
			}
			System.Windows.Size validMeasureSize = RegionHelper.GetValidMeasureSize(availableSize);
			if (rect == Rect.Empty)
			{
				rect = new Rect(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), MinimumSize);
			}
			if (BoundingRect != rect)
			{
				BoundingRect = rect;
				RecalculateHorizontalExtent();
				RecalculateVerticalExtent();
			}
			return validMeasureSize;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(0.0 - HorizontalOffset, 0.0 - VerticalOffset);
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				System.Windows.Size desiredSize = child.DesiredSize;
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = GetElementPosition(child);
				elementPosition.X += point.X;
				elementPosition.Y += point.Y;
				child.Arrange(new Rect(elementPosition, desiredSize));
			}
			return RegionHelper.GetValidMeasureSize(finalSize);
		}

		public void AddElement(UIElement element)
		{
			base.Children.Add(element);
		}

		public void AddElement(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point initialPosition)
		{
			PositionElement(element, initialPosition);
			AddElement(element);
		}

		public void PositionElement(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			SetLeft(element, position.X);
			SetTop(element, position.Y);
			InvalidateArrange();
		}

		public void RemoveElement(UIElement element)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(element);
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates()
		{
			return VisualCoordinatesToLayerCoordinates(Mouse.GetPosition(this));
		}

		public System.Windows.Point VisualCoordinatesToLayerCoordinates(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Point(point.X + HorizontalOffset, point.Y + VerticalOffset);
		}

		public System.Windows.Point LayerCoordinatesToVisualCoordinates(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Point(point.X - HorizontalOffset, point.Y - VerticalOffset);
		}
	}
	public class DiagramOverlayLayer : DiagramLayer
	{
		public DiagramOverlayLayer()
		{
			base.IsHitTestVisible = true;
		}
	}
	public class DiagramPrimaryLayer : DiagramLayer
	{
		public DiagramPrimaryLayer()
		{
			base.IsHitTestVisible = true;
			base.Focusable = true;
			base.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
		}
	}
	public class DiagramUnderlayLayer : DiagramLayer
	{
		private const double MajorLineThickness = 1.0;

		private const double MinorLineThickness = 0.25;

		private const double MajorScaleLineLength = 10.0;

		private const double MinorScaleLineLength = 8.0;

		private const double LeastMinorSpacing = 5.0;

		private const double LeastMajorSpacing = 10.0;

		private const double RulerLabelWidth = 40.0;

		private const double RulerLabelHeight = 10.0;

		private const double LabelOffset = 2.0;

		private const double LeastRulerLabelSpacing = 5.0;

		private int _rulerLabelInterval;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _pageBorder;

		private readonly Canvas _gridHorizontalMajorCanvas = CreateGridLineCanvas(1.0);

		private readonly Canvas _gridHorizontalMinorCanvas = CreateGridLineCanvas(0.5);

		private readonly Canvas _gridVerticalMajorCanvas = CreateGridLineCanvas(1.0);

		private readonly Canvas _gridVerticalMinorCanvas = CreateGridLineCanvas(0.5);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMajorLeftCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(10.0, 0.0, 1.0, 10.0, 0.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMajorTopCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(0.0, 10.0, 1.0, 0.0, 10.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMajorRightCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(10.0, 0.0, 1.0, 10.0, 0.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMajorBottomCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(0.0, 10.0, 1.0, 0.0, 10.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMinorLeftCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(10.0, 0.0, 0.25, 8.0, 0.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMinorTopCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(0.0, 10.0, 0.25, 0.0, 8.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMinorRightCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(10.0, 0.0, 0.25, 8.0, 0.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerMinorBottomCanvas = CreateRulerLineCanvas(0.0, 10.0, 0.25, 0.0, 8.0);

		private readonly Canvas _rulerLeftCanvas = new Canvas
		{
			Width = 40.0
		};

		private readonly Canvas _rulerTopCanvas = new Canvas
		{
			Height = 10.0
		};

		private readonly Canvas _rulerRightCanvas = new Canvas
		{
			Width = 40.0
		};

		private readonly Canvas _rulerBottomCanvas = new Canvas
		{
			Height = 10.0
		};

		private readonly Canvas _labelsLeftRightVisual = new Canvas
		{
			Width = 40.0
		};

		private readonly Canvas _labelsTopBottomVisual = new Canvas
		{
			Height = 10.0
		};

		private readonly List<TextBlock> _labelsLeftRight = new List<TextBlock>();

		private readonly List<TextBlock> _labelsTopBottom = new List<TextBlock>();

		private readonly ScaleTransform _scaleTransformForLabels = new ScaleTransform();

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowPageBorderProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ShowPageBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(DiagramUnderlayLayer));

		public bool ShowPageBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowPageBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowPageBorderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DiagramUnderlayLayer()
		{
			base.IsHitTestVisible = false;
			_pageBorder = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			_pageBorder.Width = base.MinimumSize.Width;
			_pageBorder.Height = base.MinimumSize.Height;
			_pageBorder.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
			_pageBorder.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				Source = this,
				Path = new PropertyPath(ShowPageBorderProperty),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Converter = new BooleanToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			AddElement(_pageBorder, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_gridVerticalMajorCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_gridVerticalMinorCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMajorLeftCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMajorTopCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMajorRightCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMajorBottomCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMinorLeftCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMinorTopCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMinorRightCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerMinorBottomCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			_rulerLeftCanvas.Background = new VisualBrush(_labelsLeftRightVisual)
			{
				Stretch = Stretch.None
			};
			_rulerRightCanvas.Background = new VisualBrush(_labelsLeftRightVisual)
			{
				Stretch = Stretch.None
			};
			_rulerTopCanvas.Background = new VisualBrush(_labelsTopBottomVisual)
			{
				Stretch = Stretch.None
			};
			_rulerBottomCanvas.Background = new VisualBrush(_labelsTopBottomVisual)
			{
				Stretch = Stretch.None
			};
			AddElement(_rulerLeftCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerRightCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerTopCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			AddElement(_rulerBottomCanvas, new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
		}

		protected override void OnMinimumSizeChanged(System.Windows.Size oldValue, System.Windows.Size newValue)
		{
			_pageBorder.Width = newValue.Width;
			_pageBorder.Height = newValue.Height;
		}

		protected override void OnHorizontalOffsetChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			UpdateVerticalGridLines();
			UpdateVerticalScaleLines();
			UpdateTopBottomRulers();
			base.OnHorizontalOffsetChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected override void OnVerticalOffsetChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			UpdateHorizontalGridLines();
			UpdateHorizontalScaleLines();
			UpdateLeftRightRulers();
			base.OnVerticalOffsetChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected override void OnHorizontalViewportChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			UpdateVerticalGridLines();
			UpdateVerticalScaleLines();
			UpdateTopBottomRulers();
			base.OnHorizontalViewportChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected override void OnVerticalViewportChanged(double oldValue, double newValue)
		{
			UpdateHorizontalGridLines();
			UpdateHorizontalScaleLines();
			UpdateLeftRightRulers();
			base.OnVerticalViewportChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		public void UpdateGridLinesAndScaleLines()
		{
			if (!ShouldShowGridLines())
			{
				_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			else
			{
				_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				UpdateHorizontalGridLines();
				UpdateVerticalGridLines();
			}
			if (!ShouldShowScaleLines())
			{
				_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			else
			{
				_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				_rulerBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				UpdateHorizontalScaleLines();
				UpdateVerticalScaleLines();
				UpdateLeftRightRulers();
				UpdateTopBottomRulers();
			}
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private void UpdateHorizontalGridLines()
		{
			if (CheckDiagram() && ShouldShowGridLines())
			{
				Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridVerticalMajorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridVerticalMinorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements())
				{
					double num = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport;
					_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeGridLineCanvas(_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas, base.HorizontalViewport, 0.0, base.HorizontalViewport, diagram.GridMajorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements())
				{
					double num2 = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport;
					_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num2 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeGridLineCanvas(_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas, base.HorizontalViewport, 0.0, base.HorizontalViewport, diagram.GridMinorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVerticalGridLines()
		{
			if (CheckDiagram() && ShouldShowGridLines())
			{
				Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridHorizontalMajorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridHorizontalMinorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements())
				{
					double num = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridVerticalMajorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridVerticalMajorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
					InitializeGridLineCanvas(_gridVerticalMajorCanvas, 0.0, base.VerticalViewport, diagram.GridMajorSpacing, base.VerticalViewport);
				}
				else
				{
					_gridVerticalMajorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements())
				{
					double num2 = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_gridVerticalMinorCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num2 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_gridVerticalMinorCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
					InitializeGridLineCanvas(_gridVerticalMinorCanvas, 0.0, base.VerticalViewport, diagram.GridMinorSpacing, base.VerticalViewport);
				}
				else
				{
					_gridVerticalMinorCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateHorizontalScaleLines()
		{
			if (CheckDiagram() && ShouldShowScaleLines())
			{
				Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Height);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Height);
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && ShouldShowLeftRulers())
				{
					double num = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Width = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorLeftCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMajorLeftCanvas, _rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Width, diagram.GridMajorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMajorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && ShouldShowRightRulers())
				{
					double num2 = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Width = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerMajorRightCanvas.Width);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorRightCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num2 - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMajorRightCanvas, _rulerMajorRightCanvas.Width, diagram.GridMajorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMajorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements() && ShouldShowLeftRulers())
				{
					double num3 = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Width = 8.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorLeftCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num3 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMinorLeftCanvas, _rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Width, diagram.GridMinorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMinorLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements() && ShouldShowRightRulers())
				{
					double num4 = (DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder) - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Height = base.VerticalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Width = 8.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerMinorRightCanvas.Width);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorRightCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num4 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMinorRightCanvas, _rulerMinorRightCanvas.Width, diagram.GridMinorSpacing);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMinorRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVerticalScaleLines()
		{
			if (CheckDiagram() && ShouldShowScaleLines())
			{
				Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerMajorRightCanvas.Width);
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset);
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerMinorRightCanvas.Width);
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && ShouldShowTopRulers())
				{
					double num = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Height = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorTopCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMajorTopCanvas, diagram.GridMajorSpacing, _rulerMajorTopCanvas.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMajorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && ShouldShowBottomRulers())
				{
					double num2 = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Height = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMajorBottomCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num2 - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMajorBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Height);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMajorBottomCanvas, diagram.GridMajorSpacing, _rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMajorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements() && ShouldShowTopRulers())
				{
					double num3 = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Height = 8.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorTopCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num3 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMinorTopCanvas, diagram.GridMinorSpacing, _rulerMinorTopCanvas.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMinorTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowMinorElements() && ShouldShowBottomRulers())
				{
					double num4 = (DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder) - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Width = base.HorizontalViewport + diagram.GridMinorSpacing;
					_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Height = 8.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerMinorBottomCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num4 - diagram.GridMinorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerMinorBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Height);
					InitializeRulerLineCanvas(_rulerMinorBottomCanvas, diagram.GridMinorSpacing, _rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerMinorBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateLeftRightRulers()
		{
			if (!CheckDiagram() || !ShouldShowScaleLines())
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
			double top = DiagramLayer.GetTop(_pageBorder);
			DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
			DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerBottomCanvas.Height - 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
			if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && (ShouldShowLeftRulers() || ShouldShowRightRulers()))
			{
				double num = (top - base.VerticalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				int num2 = (int)(base.VerticalViewport / diagram.GridMajorSpacing) + 1;
				double height = (double)num2 * diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				_labelsLeftRightVisual.Height = height;
				IEnumerable<TextBlock> leftRightLabels = GetLeftRightLabels(num2);
				foreach (TextBlock item in _labelsLeftRightVisual.Children.Cast<TextBlock>().Except(leftRightLabels).ToList())
				{
					_labelsLeftRightVisual.Children.Remove(item);
				}
				double num3 = 0.0;
				double num4 = num;
				foreach (TextBlock item2 in leftRightLabels)
				{
					if (!_labelsLeftRightVisual.Children.Contains(item2))
					{
						_labelsLeftRightVisual.Children.Add(item2);
					}
					item2.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
					item2.Text = Convert.ToString((int)(base.VerticalOffset + num4 - top), NumberFormatInfo.InvariantInfo);
					Canvas.SetLeft(item2, 0.0);
					Canvas.SetTop(item2, num3);
					num3 += diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					num4 += diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				}
				_scaleTransformForLabels.ScaleX = 1.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
				_scaleTransformForLabels.ScaleY = 1.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
				if (ShouldShowLeftRulers())
				{
					_rulerLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerLeftCanvas.Height = height;
					_rulerLeftCanvas.Width = 40.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerLeftCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowRightRulers())
				{
					_rulerRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerRightCanvas.Height = height;
					_rulerRightCanvas.Width = 40.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerRightCanvas.Width - 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerRightCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_rulerLeftCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerRightCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateTopBottomRulers()
		{
			if (!CheckDiagram() || !ShouldShowScaleLines())
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram diagram = base.Diagram;
			double left = DiagramLayer.GetLeft(_pageBorder);
			DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerLeftCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
			DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerRightCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + base.HorizontalViewport - _rulerRightCanvas.Width - 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
			if (ShouldShowMajorElements() && (ShouldShowTopRulers() || ShouldShowBottomRulers()))
			{
				double num = (left - base.HorizontalOffset) % diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				int num2 = (int)(base.HorizontalViewport / diagram.GridMajorSpacing) + 1;
				double width = (double)num2 * diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				_labelsTopBottomVisual.Width = width;
				IEnumerable<TextBlock> topBottomLabels = GetTopBottomLabels(num2);
				foreach (TextBlock item in _labelsTopBottomVisual.Children.Cast<TextBlock>().Except(topBottomLabels).ToList())
				{
					_labelsTopBottomVisual.Children.Remove(item);
				}
				double num3 = 0.0;
				double num4 = num;
				foreach (TextBlock item2 in topBottomLabels)
				{
					if (!_labelsTopBottomVisual.Children.Contains(item2))
					{
						_labelsTopBottomVisual.Children.Add(item2);
					}
					item2.Width = diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					item2.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
					item2.Text = Convert.ToString((int)(base.HorizontalOffset + num4 - left), NumberFormatInfo.InvariantInfo);
					Canvas.SetLeft(item2, num3);
					Canvas.SetTop(item2, 0.0);
					num4 += diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
					num3 += diagram.GridMajorSpacing;
				}
				IEnumerator<TextBlock> enumerator3 = _labelsTopBottomVisual.Children.Cast<TextBlock>().GetEnumerator();
				if (ShouldShowEachRuler())
				{
					while (enumerator3.MoveNext())
					{
						enumerator3.Current.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					int num5 = 0;
					while (enumerator3.MoveNext())
					{
						enumerator3.Current.Visibility = ((num5 % (_rulerLabelInterval + 1) != 0) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
						num5++;
					}
				}
				_scaleTransformForLabels.ScaleX = 1.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
				_scaleTransformForLabels.ScaleY = 1.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
				if (ShouldShowTopRulers())
				{
					_rulerTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerTopCanvas.Width = width;
					_rulerTopCanvas.Height = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerTopCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerTopCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (ShouldShowBottomRulers())
				{
					_rulerBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_rulerBottomCanvas.Width = width;
					_rulerBottomCanvas.Height = 10.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
					DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_rulerBottomCanvas, base.HorizontalOffset + num - diagram.GridMajorSpacing / 2.0);
					DiagramLayer.SetTop(_rulerBottomCanvas, base.VerticalOffset + base.VerticalViewport - _rulerBottomCanvas.Height - 12.0 / diagram.ZoomScale);
				}
				else
				{
					_rulerBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_rulerTopCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				_rulerBottomCanvas.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
		}

		private bool CheckDiagram()
		{
			if (base.Diagram != null && !double.IsNaN(base.Diagram.GridMinorSpacing) && !double.IsNaN(base.Diagram.GridMajorSpacing) && base.Diagram.GridMinorSpacing > 0.0)
			{
				return base.Diagram.GridMajorSpacing > 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowGridLines()
		{
			if (base.Diagram != null)
			{
				return base.Diagram.ShowGrids;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowScaleLines()
		{
			if (base.Diagram != null)
			{
				return base.Diagram.ShowRulers != RulerOption.None;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowMajorElements()
		{
			return base.Diagram.ZoomScale * base.Diagram.GridMajorSpacing >= 10.0;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowMinorElements()
		{
			return base.Diagram.ZoomScale * base.Diagram.GridMinorSpacing >= 5.0;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowLeftRulers()
		{
			return (base.Diagram.ShowRulers & RulerOption.Left) == RulerOption.Left;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowRightRulers()
		{
			return (base.Diagram.ShowRulers & RulerOption.Right) == RulerOption.Right;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowTopRulers()
		{
			return (base.Diagram.ShowRulers & RulerOption.Top) == RulerOption.Top;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowBottomRulers()
		{
			return (base.Diagram.ShowRulers & RulerOption.Bottom) == RulerOption.Bottom;
		}

		private bool ShouldShowEachRuler()
		{
			int num = (int)(5.0 / (base.Diagram.ZoomScale * base.Diagram.GridMinorSpacing));
			if (num >= 1)
			{
				_rulerLabelInterval = num;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private IEnumerable<TextBlock> GetLeftRightLabels(int number)
		{
			return GetRulerLabels(_labelsLeftRight, number, _scaleTransformForLabels);
		}

		private IEnumerable<TextBlock> GetTopBottomLabels(int number)
		{
			return GetRulerLabels(_labelsTopBottom, number, _scaleTransformForLabels);
		}

		private void InitializeGridLineCanvas(Canvas canvas, double lineX2, double lineY2, double horizontalInternal, double verticalInternal)
		{
			if (canvas.Background is VisualBrush visualBrush)
			{
				visualBrush.Viewport = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, horizontalInternal, verticalInternal);
				if (visualBrush.Visual is Line line)
				{
					line.X2 = lineX2;
					line.Y2 = lineY2;
					line.StrokeThickness = (double)line.Tag / base.Diagram.ZoomScale;
				}
			}
		}

		private void InitializeRulerLineCanvas(Canvas canvas, double horizontalInternal, double verticalInternal)
		{
			if (canvas.Background is VisualBrush visualBrush)
			{
				visualBrush.Viewport = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, horizontalInternal, verticalInternal);
				if (visualBrush.Visual is Line line)
				{
					line.StrokeThickness = (double)line.Tag / base.Diagram.ZoomScale;
				}
			}
		}

		private static IEnumerable<TextBlock> GetRulerLabels(List<TextBlock> provider, int number, Transform renderTransform)
		{
			int num = number - provider.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				provider.Add(new TextBlock
				{
					Width = 40.0,
					Height = 10.0,
					FontWeight = FontWeights.ExtraLight,
					FontSize = 9.0,
					Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
					RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5),
					RenderTransform = renderTransform
				});
			}
			return provider.Take(number);
		}

		private static Canvas CreateGridLineCanvas(double lineThickness)
		{
			return new Canvas
			{
				Background = new VisualBrush
				{
					Visual = new Line
					{
						Opacity = 0.5,
						Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray,
						StrokeThickness = lineThickness,
						Tag = lineThickness
					},
					Stretch = Stretch.None,
					ViewportUnits = BrushMappingMode.Absolute,
					TileMode = TileMode.Tile
				}
			};
		}

		private static Canvas CreateRulerLineCanvas(double canvasWidth, double canvasHeight, double lineThickness, double x2, double y2)
		{
			return new Canvas
			{
				Width = canvasWidth,
				Height = canvasHeight,
				Background = new VisualBrush
				{
					Visual = new Line
					{
						Opacity = 0.5,
						Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
						StrokeThickness = lineThickness,
						X2 = x2,
						Y2 = y2,
						Tag = lineThickness
					},
					Stretch = Stretch.None,
					ViewportUnits = BrushMappingMode.Absolute,
					TileMode = TileMode.Tile
				}
			};
		}
	}
	public static class Extension
	{
		public static UIElement GetDiagramElement(this System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("e");
			}
			if (e.OriginalSource is UIElement d)
			{
				DependencyObject ancestorChild = null;
				DiagramLayer ancestor = GetAncestor<DiagramLayer>(d, out ancestorChild);
				UIElement layerElement = ancestorChild as UIElement;
				if (ancestor != null && layerElement != null)
				{
					if (typeof(DiagramPrimaryLayer).IsInstanceOfType(ancestor))
					{
						return layerElement;
					}
					if (typeof(DiagramOverlayLayer).IsInstanceOfType(ancestor))
					{
						Diagram diagram = ((DiagramOverlayLayer)ancestor).Diagram;
						if (diagram != null)
						{
							DiagramElementOverlay diagramElementOverlay = diagram.ElementOverlays.FirstOrDefault((DiagramElementOverlay o) => o.Adorners.Contains(layerElement));
							if (diagramElementOverlay != null)
							{
								return diagramElementOverlay.AdornedElement;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static T GetAncestor<T>(DependencyObject d, out DependencyObject ancestorChild) where T : class
		{
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = d;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = null;
			while (true)
			{
				dependencyObject2 = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(dependencyObject);
				if (dependencyObject2 == null)
				{
					ancestorChild = null;
					return null;
				}
				if (typeof(T).IsInstanceOfType(dependencyObject2))
				{
					break;
				}
				dependencyObject = dependencyObject2;
			}
			ancestorChild = dependencyObject;
			return dependencyObject2 as T;
		}
	}
	public class Label : Symbol
	{
		public static readonly DependencyPropertyKey LabeledElementPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LabeledElementProperty;

		private static readonly List<DependencyProperty> NoneSerializedProperties;

		public FrameworkElement LabeledElement
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Anchor == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(LabeledElementProperty);
			}
			private set
			{
				SetValue(LabeledElementPropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		static Label()
		{
			LabeledElementPropertyKey = DependencyProperty.RegisterReadOnly("LabeledElement", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(Label), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			LabeledElementProperty = LabeledElementPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			NoneSerializedProperties = new List<DependencyProperty>
			{
				FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty,
				System.Windows.Controls.Control.ForegroundProperty,
				System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontFamilyProperty,
				System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontSizeProperty,
				System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontStyleProperty,
				System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontWeightProperty
			};
			Diagram.CanResizeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanRotateProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.CanFlipProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Diagram.ShouldShowQuickToolProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			Symbol.DrawingUriProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new PropertyMetadata("Diagram.DisplayNameLabel"));
			Symbol.BindVisibilityToContentProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Label), new PropertyMetadata(true));
		}

		public Label()
		{
			SetBinding(FrameworkElement.DataContextProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.DataContext")
			});
			SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.ForegroundProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.Foreground")
			});
			SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontFamilyProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.FontFamily")
			});
			SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontSizeProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.FontSize")
			});
			SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontStyleProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.FontStyle")
			});
			SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontWeightProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
			{
				RelativeSource = RelativeSource.Self,
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay,
				Path = new PropertyPath("LabeledElement.FontWeight")
			});
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (NoneSerializedProperties.Contains(dp))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (dp == Symbol.DrawingUriProperty)
			{
				if (base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp))
				{
					return base.DrawingUri != "Diagram.DisplayNameLabel";
				}
				return false;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		protected override void OnAnchorChanged(DiagramAnchor oldAnchor, DiagramAnchor newAnchor)
		{
			base.OnAnchorChanged(oldAnchor, newAnchor);
			LabeledElement = ((newAnchor != null) ? (newAnchor.Provider.Element as FrameworkElement) : null);
		}

		protected override bool InternalAddNameLabel(Diagram diagram, string labelUri)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class Polyline : Shape, IServiceProvider, ISelectionOverlayService, IModifyService
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty FillRuleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PointsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsClosedProperty;

		private Geometry _polylineGeometry;

		public FillRule FillRule
		{
			get
			{
				return (FillRule)GetValue(FillRuleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FillRuleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public PointCollection Points
		{
			get
			{
				return (PointCollection)GetValue(PointsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PointsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsClosed
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsClosedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsClosedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected override Geometry DefiningGeometry => _polylineGeometry;

		public UIElement AdornedElement => this;

		static Polyline()
		{
			FillRuleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("FillRule", typeof(FillRule), typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FillRule.EvenOdd, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			PointsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Points", typeof(PointCollection), typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new PointCollection(), FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			IsClosedProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("IsClosed", typeof(bool), typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			FrameworkElement.WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnSizeChanged));
			FrameworkElement.HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.NaN, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnSizeChanged));
			Shape.StrokeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.SubPropertiesDoNotAffectRender));
			Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(3.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsRender));
			Diagram.ShouldShowQuickToolProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		private static bool IsWidthHeightValid(object value)
		{
			double num = (double)value;
			if (!double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				if (num >= 0.0)
				{
					return !double.IsPositiveInfinity(num);
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void OnSizeChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(d is Polyline polyline))
			{
				return;
			}
			Rect rectFromPoints = RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(polyline.Points);
			double num = (double)e.NewValue;
			DependencyProperty property = e.Property;
			Rect newRect;
			if (property == FrameworkElement.WidthProperty)
			{
				newRect = new Rect(rectFromPoints.TopLeft, new System.Windows.Size(num, rectFromPoints.Height));
			}
			else
			{
				if (property != FrameworkElement.HeightProperty)
				{
					return;
				}
				newRect = new Rect(rectFromPoints.TopLeft, new System.Windows.Size(rectFromPoints.Width, num));
			}
			polyline.Points = GeometryHelper.TransformPoints(polyline.Points, rectFromPoints, newRect);
		}

		private void CacheDefiningGeometry()
		{
			PointCollection points = Points;
			PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
			if (points == null)
			{
				_polylineGeometry = Geometry.Empty;
				return;
			}
			if (points.Count > 0)
			{
				Rect rectFromPoints = RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(points);
				pathFigure.StartPoint = CorrectPoint(points[0], rectFromPoints.TopLeft);
				if (points.Count > 1)
				{
					System.Windows.Point[] array = new System.Windows.Point[points.Count - 1];
					for (int i = 1; i < points.Count; i++)
					{
						array[i - 1] = CorrectPoint(points[i], rectFromPoints.TopLeft);
					}
					pathFigure.Segments.Add(new PolyLineSegment(array, isStroked: true));
				}
				pathFigure.IsClosed = IsClosed;
			}
			PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
			pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
			pathGeometry.FillRule = FillRule;
			if (pathGeometry.Bounds == Rect.Empty)
			{
				_polylineGeometry = Geometry.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				_polylineGeometry = pathGeometry;
			}
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size MeasureOverride(System.Windows.Size constraint)
		{
			CacheDefiningGeometry();
			return RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(Points).Size;
		}

		protected override System.Windows.Size ArrangeOverride(System.Windows.Size finalSize)
		{
			base.ArrangeOverride(finalSize);
			return RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(Points).Size;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point CorrectPoint(System.Windows.Point targetPoint, System.Windows.Point originalPoint)
		{
			Vector vector = targetPoint - originalPoint;
			return new System.Windows.Point(vector.X, vector.Y);
		}

		public object GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IModifyService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(AdornedElement));
		}

		public void AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			editor.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new RotationOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new PolylineReshapeOverlay(this));
		}

		public bool CanMove(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanMove(this);
		}

		public bool CanRotate(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanRotate(this);
		}

		public bool CanResize(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanResize(this);
		}

		public bool CanFlip(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanFlip(this);
		}

		public bool ShouldShowQuickTool(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public void PositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.PositionElement(diagram, this, newPosition);
		}

		public void RotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RotateElement(diagram, this, angle);
		}

		public void FlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.FlipElement(diagram, this, flipAxis);
		}

		public void SizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			Rect rectFromPoints = RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(Points);
			Points = GeometryHelper.TransformPoints(newRect: new Rect(rectFromPoints.TopLeft, size), collection: Points, oldRect: rectFromPoints);
			UpdateLayout();
		}

		public void RemoveElement(Diagram diagram)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RemoveElement(diagram, this);
		}
	}
	public class Symbol : ContentControl, IServiceProvider, ISelectionOverlayService, IContextMenuService, IAnchorProvider, IAnchorConsumer, IModifyService, INameLabelService, IElementNotifiedService
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty SymbolTypeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty BindVisibilityToContentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ProvidesUniversalConnectPointsProperty;

		private readonly SymbolAnchorCollection _symbolAnchors;

		private DiagramAnchor _dependencyAnchor;

		UIElement ISelectionOverlayService.AdornedElement => this;
