		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		public SymbolAnchorCollection SymbolAnchors => _symbolAnchors;

		public IEnumerable<DiagramAnchor> Anchors
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in _symbolAnchors)
				{
					yield return symbolAnchor;
				}
			}
		}

		public DiagramAnchor Anchor
		{
			get
			{
				EnsureAnchorInitiated();
				return _dependencyAnchor;
			}
			set
			{
				DiagramAnchor anchor = Anchor;
				if (anchor != value)
				{
					_dependencyAnchor = value;
					OnAnchorChanged(anchor, _dependencyAnchor);
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<DiagramAnchor> CurrentAnchors
		{
			get
			{
				if (Anchor != null)
				{
					yield return Anchor;
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<Label> Labels => from anchor in SymbolAnchors
			where anchor.Consumer != null && typeof(Label).IsInstanceOfType(anchor.Consumer)
			select (Label)anchor.Consumer;

		public System.Windows.Point AnchoredPosition => (Anchor?.AbsoluteProjectionPoint ?? new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0)) + GetAnchorOffset();

		public System.Windows.Point? DefaultLabelPosition
		{
			get
			{
				System.Windows.Point? result = null;
				UIElement trueContentElement = GetTrueContentElement();
				int num = ((trueContentElement != null) ? Diagram.GetMaximumDesignerNesting(trueContentElement) : 0);
				if (num > 0)
				{
					return FindDefaultLabelPosition(trueContentElement, num);
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		static Symbol()
		{
			SymbolTypeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("SymbolType", typeof(string), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty));
			DrawingUriProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DrawingUri", typeof(string), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, OnDrawingUriPropertyChanged));
			DefaultSizeProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("DefaultSize", typeof(System.Windows.Size), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(new System.Windows.Size(10.0, 10.0)));
			AnchorHorizontalAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnchorHorizontalAlignment", typeof(System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left));
			AnchorVerticalAlignmentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnchorVerticalAlignment", typeof(VerticalAlignment), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(VerticalAlignment.Top));
			AnchorIdProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnchorId", typeof(Guid), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(Guid.Empty));
			BindVisibilityToContentProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("BindVisibilityToContent", typeof(bool), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnBindVisibilityToContentPropertyChanged));
			ProvidesUniversalConnectPointsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ProvidesUniversalConnectPoints", typeof(UniversalConnectPointOption), typeof(Symbol), new PropertyMetadata((UniversalConnectPointOption)7));
			DiagramLayer.TopProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnTopPropertyChanged));
			DiagramLayer.LeftProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnLeftPropertyChanged));
			FrameworkElement.HeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnHeightChanged));
			FrameworkElement.WidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnWidthChanged));
			UIElement.RenderTransformProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OnTransformChanged));
			Diagram.ShouldShowQuickToolProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(Symbol), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public Symbol()
		{
			_symbolAnchors = new SymbolAnchorCollection(this);
			base.Loaded += Symbol_Loaded;
		}

		private static void OnTopPropertyChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as Symbol).NotifyAnchorOfPositionChanged();
		}

		private static void OnLeftPropertyChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as Symbol).NotifyAnchorOfPositionChanged();
		}

		private static void OnHeightChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is Symbol symbol) || (double)e.OldValue == 0.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in symbol._symbolAnchors)
			{
				symbolAnchor.VerifyDimension(symbol.Width, symbol.Width, (double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnWidthChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!(sender is Symbol symbol) || (double)e.OldValue == 0.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in symbol._symbolAnchors)
			{
				symbolAnchor.VerifyDimension((double)e.OldValue, (double)e.NewValue, symbol.Height, symbol.Height);
			}
		}

		private static void OnTransformChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			foreach (DiagramAnchor anchor in (sender as Symbol).Anchors)
			{
				(anchor as SymbolAnchor).VerifyTransformation(e.OldValue as TransformGroup, e.NewValue as TransformGroup);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in _symbolAnchors)
			{
				symbolAnchor.VerifyDimension(sizeInfo.PreviousSize.Width, sizeInfo.NewSize.Width, sizeInfo.PreviousSize.Height, sizeInfo.NewSize.Height);
			}
			base.OnRenderSizeChanged(sizeInfo);
		}

		private static void OnDrawingUriPropertyChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is Symbol symbol)
			{
				symbol.ReloadContent();
			}
		}

		private static void OnBindVisibilityToContentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((Symbol)d).SetVisibility();
		}

		public override bool ShouldSerializeContent()
		{
			return string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingUri);
		}

		protected override bool ShouldSerializeProperty(DependencyProperty dp)
		{
			if (dp == UIElement.RenderTransformProperty)
			{
				if (base.RenderTransform != null)
				{
					return base.RenderTransform.Value != System.Windows.Media.Matrix.Identity;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (dp == SymbolTypeProperty)
			{
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(SymbolType);
			}
			if (dp == DrawingUriProperty)
			{
				return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingUri);
			}
			if (dp == DefaultSizeProperty)
			{
				return DefaultSize != new System.Windows.Size(10.0, 10.0);
			}
			if (dp == AnchorHorizontalAlignmentProperty)
			{
				return AnchorHorizontalAlignment != System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			}
			if (dp == AnchorVerticalAlignmentProperty)
			{
				return AnchorVerticalAlignment != VerticalAlignment.Top;
			}
			if (dp == AnchorIdProperty)
			{
				return AnchorId != Guid.Empty;
			}
			return base.ShouldSerializeProperty(dp);
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeAnchor()
		{
			if (Anchor != null)
			{
				return Anchor is PointAnchor;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IModifyService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IContextMenuService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IAnchorProvider))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IAnchorConsumer))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(INameLabelService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IElementNotifiedService))
			{
				result = this;
			}
			return result;
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanMove(Diagram diagram)
		{
			UIElement trueContentElement = GetTrueContentElement();
			bool flag = true;
			if (trueContentElement != null)
			{
				flag = Diagram.GetCanMove(trueContentElement);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				flag = Diagram.GetCanMove(this);
			}
			return flag;
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanResize(Diagram diagram)
		{
			UIElement trueContentElement = GetTrueContentElement();
			bool flag = true;
			if (trueContentElement != null)
			{
				flag = Diagram.GetCanResize(trueContentElement);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				flag = Diagram.GetCanResize(this);
			}
			return flag;
		}

			{

			if (flag)
			{
				flag = Diagram.GetShouldShowQuickTool(this);
			}
			return flag;
		}

		void IModifyService.RemoveElement(Diagram diagram)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RemoveElement(diagram, this);
		}

		void IModifyService.PositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			InternalPositionElement(diagram, newPosition, updateAnchor: true);
		}

		void IModifyService.SizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			InternalSizeElement(diagram, size);
		}

		void IModifyService.RotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			InternalRotateElement(diagram, angle);
		}

		void IModifyService.FlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			InternalFlipElement(diagram, flipAxis);
		}

		protected virtual Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(this));
		}

		Rect ISelectionOverlayService.GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return GetAdornedRect(editor);
		}

		void ISelectionOverlayService.AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			if (editor != null)
			{
				editor.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
				editor.AddAdorner(new RotationOverlay(this));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle));
				editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle));
				editor.AddAdorner(new QuickToolbarOverlay(this, new DefaultFontToolService(this)));
			}
		}

		public virtual void AddMenuItems(System.Windows.Controls.ContextMenu contextMenu, System.Windows.Point clickedPoint)
		{
		}

		public DiagramAnchor FindAnchor(Guid anchorId)
		{
			foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in _symbolAnchors)
			{
				if (symbolAnchor.Id == anchorId)
				{
					return symbolAnchor;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public DiagramAnchor CreateAnchor(IAnchorConsumer consumer, System.Windows.Point absoluteAnchorPoint, double projectionDistance, double projectionAngle)
		{
			SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor = new SymbolAnchor
			{
				Provider = this,
				AbsoluteAnchorPoint = absoluteAnchorPoint,
				ProjectionDistance = projectionDistance,
				ProjectionAngle = projectionAngle,
				Consumer = consumer
			};
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				symbolAnchor.ProjectionAngle += parentDiagram.GetElementRotation(this);
			}
			AddAnchor(symbolAnchor);
			return symbolAnchor;
		}

		public void AddAnchor(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			if (anchor is SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor && !_symbolAnchors.Contains(symbolAnchor))
			{
				_symbolAnchors.Add(symbolAnchor);
				OnAnchorAdded(symbolAnchor);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAnchorAdded(SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor)
		{
		}

		public void RemoveAnchor(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			if (anchor is SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor && _symbolAnchors.Contains(symbolAnchor))
			{
				_symbolAnchors.Remove(symbolAnchor);
				OnAnchorRemoved(symbolAnchor);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAnchorRemoved(SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor)
		{
		}

		public bool ReconnectToAnchor(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (AnchorId == Guid.Empty)
			{
				Anchor = anchor;
			}
			else if (AnchorId == anchor.Id)
			{
				Anchor = anchor;
			}
			else
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void AnchorPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
		}

		public void AnchorProjectionPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor)
		{
			if (Anchor != anchor)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(this);
			System.Windows.Point anchoredPosition = AnchoredPosition;
			if (!GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(elementPosition, anchoredPosition))
			{
				Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
				if (parentDiagram != null)
				{
					InternalPositionElement(parentDiagram, anchoredPosition, updateAnchor: false);
				}
			}
		}

		bool INameLabelService.AddNameLabel(Diagram diagram, string labelUri)
		{
			return InternalAddNameLabel(diagram, labelUri);
		}

		protected virtual bool InternalAddNameLabel(Diagram diagram, string labelUri)
		{
			System.Windows.Point? defaultLabelPosition = DefaultLabelPosition;
			if (!defaultLabelPosition.HasValue)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(labelUri))
			{
				labelUri = diagram.DefaultSymbolLabelUri;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(labelUri))
			{
				return false;
			}
			System.Windows.Point point = diagram.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(this, defaultLabelPosition.Value);
			Label label = new Label
			{
				DrawingUri = labelUri
			};
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram))
			{
				label.Anchor = CreateAnchor(label, point, 0.0, 0.0);
				diagram.AddElementNoPreview(label);
				diagram.PositionElement(label, point);
				if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
				{
					transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
					{
					}, delegate
					{
						label.Anchor = CreateAnchor(label, Diagram.GetElementPosition(label), 0.0, 0.0);
					});
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public virtual void OnElementAddedIntoDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (base.Content == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingUri))
			{
				ReloadContent();
			}
		}

		public virtual void OnElementRemovedFromDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			foreach (Label item in new List<Label>(Labels))
			{
				diagram.Unselect(item);
				DefaultModifyService.RemoveElement(diagram, item);
			}
			if (!(GetService(typeof(IAnchorConsumer)) is IAnchorConsumer anchorConsumer))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DiagramAnchor item2 in new List<DiagramAnchor>(anchorConsumer.CurrentAnchors))
			{
				item2.Provider.RemoveAnchor(item2);
			}
		}

		public void SetToDefaultSize()
		{
			System.Windows.Size defaultSize = DefaultSize;
			if (defaultSize.Width > 0.0 && !double.IsNaN(defaultSize.Width) && !double.IsInfinity(defaultSize.Width))
			{
				base.Width = defaultSize.Width;
			}
			if (defaultSize.Height > 0.0 && !double.IsNaN(defaultSize.Height) && !double.IsInfinity(defaultSize.Height))
			{
				base.Height = defaultSize.Height;
			}
		}

		public void ReloadContent()
		{
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingUri))
			{
				ResetContent(parentDiagram.ResolveDrawingUri(this, DrawingUri));
			}
		}

		public void ResetContent(object content)
		{
			if (content is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				if (frameworkElement is Canvas child)
				{
					Viewbox viewbox = new Viewbox();
					System.Windows.Data.Binding binding = new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Width");
					binding.Source = this;
					viewbox.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, binding);
					System.Windows.Data.Binding binding2 = new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Height");
					binding2.Source = this;
					viewbox.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, binding2);
					viewbox.Child = child;
					viewbox.Stretch = Stretch.Fill;
					base.Content = viewbox;
				}
				else
				{
					base.Content = frameworkElement;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.Content = content;
			}
		}

		private void Symbol_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.Content == null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(DrawingUri))
			{
				ReloadContent();
			}
			EnsureAnchorInitiated();
			if (Anchor == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(this);
				if (double.IsNaN(elementPosition.X) || double.IsNaN(elementPosition.Y) || elementPosition.X == 0.0 || elementPosition.Y == 0.0)
				{
					parentDiagram.PositionElement(this, Anchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint);
				}
			}
		}

		private void EnsureAnchorInitiated()
		{
			if (_dependencyAnchor != null || !(AnchorId != Guid.Empty))
			{
				return;
			}
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				DiagramAnchor diagramAnchor = parentDiagram.FindAnchor(AnchorId);
				if (diagramAnchor != null)
				{
					_dependencyAnchor = diagramAnchor;
					OnAnchorChanged(null, _dependencyAnchor);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			SetVisibility();
			base.OnContentChanged(oldContent, newContent);
		}

		protected virtual void InternalSizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.SizeElement(diagram, this, size);
		}

		protected virtual void InternalRotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RotateElement(diagram, this, angle);
		}

		protected virtual void InternalFlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.FlipElement(diagram, this, flipAxis);
		}

		protected virtual void InternalPositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition, bool updateAnchor)
		{
			if (updateAnchor)
			{
				DefaultModifyService.PositionElement(diagram, this, newPosition);
			}
			else
			{
				using (new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Position", 257))
				{
					DefaultModifyService.PositionElement(diagram, this, newPosition);
				}
			}
			if (updateAnchor && Anchor != null)
			{
				Anchor.AbsoluteProjectionPoint = newPosition;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAnchorChanged(DiagramAnchor oldAnchor, DiagramAnchor newAnchor)
		{
			if (oldAnchor != null)
			{
				oldAnchor.Consumer = null;
			}
			if (newAnchor != null)
			{
				newAnchor.Consumer = this;
				AnchorId = newAnchor.Id;
				((IAnchorConsumer)this).AnchorProjectionPointChanged(newAnchor);
			}
		}

		protected Vector GetAnchorOffset()
		{
			Vector result = new Vector(0.0, 0.0);
			switch (AnchorHorizontalAlignment)
			{
			case System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Center:
				result.X -= base.RenderSize.Width / 2.0;
				break;
			case System.Windows.HorizontalAlignment.Right:
				result.X -= base.RenderSize.Width;
				break;
			}
			switch (AnchorVerticalAlignment)
			{
			case VerticalAlignment.Center:
				result.Y -= base.RenderSize.Height / 2.0;
				break;
			case VerticalAlignment.Bottom:
				result.Y -= base.RenderSize.Height;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetVisibility()
		{
			if (base.Content == null)
			{
				base.Visibility = (BindVisibilityToContent ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			else if (!(base.Content is UIElement source))
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			else if (BindVisibilityToContent)
			{
				SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding
				{
					Source = source,
					Path = new PropertyPath(UIElement.VisibilityProperty),
					Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
				});
			}
			else
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Point? FindDefaultLabelPosition(UIElement element, int maxDepth)
		{
			System.Windows.Point? result = null;
			int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(element);
			for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(element, i) as UIElement;
				if (uIElement is DefaultLabelPositionDesigner defaultLabelPositionDesigner)
				{
					GeneralTransform generalTransform = defaultLabelPositionDesigner.TransformToAncestor(this);
					result = generalTransform.Transform(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
					break;
				}
				if (maxDepth > 1)
				{
					result = FindDefaultLabelPosition(uIElement, maxDepth - 1);
					if (result.HasValue)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void NotifyAnchorOfPositionChanged()
		{
			foreach (SymbolAnchor symbolAnchor in _symbolAnchors)
			{
				symbolAnchor.SymbolPositionChanged();
			}
		}

		private UIElement GetTrueContentElement()
		{
			if (base.Content != null)
			{
				if (typeof(Viewbox).IsInstanceOfType(base.Content))
				{
					return ((Viewbox)base.Content).Child;
				}
				return base.Content as UIElement;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class Text : TextBlock, IServiceProvider, ISelectionOverlayService, IModifyService, IQuickToolService
	{
		private System.Windows.Controls.Panel toolPanel;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Image readonlyImage;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Image editableImage;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsReadOnlyProperty = System.Windows.Controls.Primitives.TextBoxBase.IsReadOnlyProperty.AddOwner(typeof(Text));

		public bool IsReadOnly
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsReadOnlyProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsReadOnlyProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public UIElement AdornedElement => this;

		public System.Windows.Controls.Panel QuickToolPanel
		{
			get
			{
				if (toolPanel == null)
				{
					IQuickToolService quickToolService = new DefaultFontToolService(this);
					toolPanel = quickToolService.QuickToolPanel;
					toolPanel.Children.Add(CreateReadOnlySwitch());
				}
				return toolPanel;
			}
		}

		public Text()
		{
			readonlyImage = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
			{
				Width = 16.0,
				Height = 16.0,
				Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(GetImageUri("Resources/NoPencil.png")))
			};
			editableImage = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
			{
				Width = 16.0,
				Height = 16.0,
				Source = new BitmapImage(new Uri(GetImageUri("Resources/Pencil.png")))
			};
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			base.OnRenderSizeChanged(sizeInfo);
			Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this).UpdateAdorners(this);
		}

		object IServiceProvider.GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(IModifyService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (serviceType == typeof(IQuickToolService))
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(AdornedElement));
		}

		public void AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			editor.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new RotationOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new QuickToolbarOverlay(this, this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new TextEditorOverlay(this));
		}

		public bool CanMove(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanMove(this);
		}

		public bool CanRotate(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanRotate(this);
		}

		public bool CanResize(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public bool CanFlip(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public bool ShouldShowQuickTool(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetShouldShowQuickTool(this);
		}

		public void PositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.PositionElement(diagram, this, newPosition);
		}

		public void RotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RotateElement(diagram, this, angle);
		}

		public void FlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
		}

		public void SizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
		}

		public void RemoveElement(Diagram diagram)
		{
			DefaultModifyService.RemoveElement(diagram, this);
		}

		private UIElement CreateReadOnlySwitch()
		{
			ToggleButton toggleButton = new ToggleButton();
			toggleButton.SetBinding(ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("IsReadOnly")
			{
				Source = this,
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
			});
			UpdateButtonStatus(toggleButton);
			toggleButton.Checked += button_StatusChanged;
			toggleButton.Unchecked += button_StatusChanged;
			return toggleButton;
		}

		private void UpdateButtonStatus(ToggleButton button)
		{
			if (IsReadOnly)
			{
				button.ToolTip = AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.Menu_Text_SetEditable;
				button.Content = readonlyImage;
			}
			else
			{
				button.ToolTip = AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Properties.Resources.Menu_Text_SetReadOnly;
				button.Content = editableImage;
			}
		}

		private void button_StatusChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ToggleButton button = sender as ToggleButton;
			UpdateButtonStatus(button);
			Diagram.GetParentDiagram(this).UpdateAdorners(this);
		}

		private static string GetImageUri(string fileName)
		{
			return "pack://application:,,,/" + Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetName().Name + ";component/" + fileName;
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.Transaction
{
	public abstract class ChangeRecordBase : IChangeRecord
	{
		public bool UndoDone { get; private set; }

		public bool Undo()
		{
			if (!UndoDone)
			{
				UndoDone = OnUndo();
			}
			return UndoDone;
		}

		public bool Redo()
		{
			if (UndoDone)
			{
				UndoDone = !OnRedo();
			}
			return !UndoDone;
		}

		protected abstract bool OnUndo();

		protected abstract bool OnRedo();
	}
	public class HistoryRecord
	{
		private readonly List<IChangeRecord> _changeRecords = new List<IChangeRecord>();

		public bool IsEmpty => _changeRecords.Count == 0;

		public string Description { get; set; }

		public int ChangeCount => _changeRecords.Count;

		public void MergeHistory(HistoryRecord recordToMerge)
		{
			if (recordToMerge == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (IChangeRecord changeRecord in recordToMerge._changeRecords)
			{
				_changeRecords.Add(changeRecord);
			}
		}

		public void AddRecord(IChangeRecord record)
		{
			if (record == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("record");
			}
			_changeRecords.Add(record);
		}

		public bool UndoChange(int index)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				result = _changeRecords[index].Undo();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool RedoChange(int index)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				result = _changeRecords[index].Redo();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public interface IChangeRecord
	{
		bool UndoDone { get; }

		bool Undo();

		bool Redo();
	}
	public static class TransactionFlags
	{
		public const int SuppressHistory = 1;

		public const int SuppressEvents = 2;
	}
	public interface ITransactionManager
	{
		bool DoingUndo { get; }

		bool DoingRedo { get; }

		int ActiveTransactionFlags { get; }

		bool SuppressEventsForTransaction { get; }

		bool SuppressHistoryForTransaction { get; }

		bool CanUndo { get; }

		bool CanRedo { get; }

		bool IsDirty { get; }

		void AddChangeRecord(IChangeRecord record);

		void StartTransaction(string description, int transactionFlags);

		void FinishTransaction();

		void AbortTransaction();

		int Undo(int undoCount);

		int Redo(int redoCount);

		void ResetIsDirty();

		void ClearHistory();
	}
	internal class ProxyChangeRecord : ChangeRecordBase
	{
		private Action _undoDelegate;

		private Action _redoDelegate;

		public ProxyChangeRecord(Action undoDelegate, Action redoDelegate)
		{
			if (undoDelegate == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("undoDelegate");
			}
			if (redoDelegate == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("redoDelegate");
			}
			_undoDelegate = undoDelegate;
			_redoDelegate = redoDelegate;
		}

		protected override bool OnUndo()
		{
			if (_undoDelegate != null)
			{
				_undoDelegate();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override bool OnRedo()
		{
			if (_redoDelegate != null)
			{
				_redoDelegate();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class TransactionContext : IDisposable
	{
		private ITransactionManager _transactionManager;

		private int _activeTransactionFlags;

		public bool ShouldRaiseEvents
		{
			get
			{
				if (_transactionManager == null)
				{
					return (_activeTransactionFlags & 2) != 2;
				}
				return !_transactionManager.SuppressEventsForTransaction;
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldCreateChangeRecord
		{
			get
			{
				if (_transactionManager == null)
				{
					return (_activeTransactionFlags & 1) != 1;
				}
				return !_transactionManager.SuppressHistoryForTransaction;
			}
		}

		public int ActiveTransactionFlags => _activeTransactionFlags;

		public bool IsUndoOrRedo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_transactionManager == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!_transactionManager.DoingRedo)
				{
					return _transactionManager.DoingUndo;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public TransactionContext(ITransactionManager manager)
			: this(manager, string.Empty)
		{
		}

		public TransactionContext(ITransactionManager manager, string description)
			: this(manager, description, manager?.ActiveTransactionFlags ?? 0)
		{
		}

		public TransactionContext(ITransactionManager manager, string description, int transactionFlags)
		{
			_transactionManager = manager;
			_activeTransactionFlags = transactionFlags;
			if (_transactionManager != null)
			{
				_transactionManager.StartTransaction(description, transactionFlags);
				_activeTransactionFlags = _transactionManager.ActiveTransactionFlags;
			}
		}

		public TransactionContext(Diagram diagram)
			: this(diagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty)
		{
		}

		public void AddChangeRecord(IChangeRecord changeRecord)
		{
			if (_transactionManager != null)
			{
				_transactionManager.AddChangeRecord(changeRecord);
			}
		}

		public void AddChangeRecord(Action undoDelegate, Action redoDelegate)
		{
			AddChangeRecord(new ProxyChangeRecord(undoDelegate, redoDelegate));
		}

		public void FinishTransaction()
		{
			if (_transactionManager != null)
			{
				_transactionManager.FinishTransaction();
			}
		}

		public void AbortTransaction()
		{
			if (_transactionManager != null)
			{
				_transactionManager.AbortTransaction();
			}
		}

		~TransactionContext()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: false);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				FinishTransaction();
			}
			_transactionManager = null;
		}
	}
	public class TransactionManager : ITransactionManager
	{
		private class TransactionInfo
		{
			public int TransactionFlags { get; set; }

			public string Description { get; set; }
		}

		private readonly Stack<HistoryRecord> _pendingHistoryStack = new Stack<HistoryRecord>();

		private readonly List<HistoryRecord> _undoStack = new List<HistoryRecord>();

		private readonly List<string> _undoStackDescriptions = new List<string>();

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _undoStackDescriptionsRO;

		private readonly List<HistoryRecord> _redoStack = new List<HistoryRecord>();

		private readonly List<string> _redoStackDescriptions = new List<string>();

		private ReadOnlyCollection<string> _redoStackDescriptionsRO;

		private HistoryRecord _savePointReference;

		private readonly Stack<TransactionInfo> _activeTransactionStack = new Stack<TransactionInfo>();

		public int TransactionNesting => _activeTransactionStack.Count;

		public bool TransactionActive => _activeTransactionStack.Count > 0;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> UndoStackDescriptions => _undoStackDescriptionsRO;

		public ReadOnlyCollection<string> RedoStackDescriptions => _redoStackDescriptionsRO;

		public bool UndoAndRedoRaiseEvents { get; set; }

		public bool IsDirty => _savePointReference != PeekUndoRecord();

		public int DefaultTransactionFlags { get; set; }

		public virtual bool CanUndo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_undoStack.Count > 0)
				{
					return _activeTransactionStack.Count == 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool CanRedo
		{
			get
			{
				if (_redoStack.Count > 0)
				{
					return _activeTransactionStack.Count == 0;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool DoingUndo { get; protected set; }

		public bool DoingRedo { get; protected set; }

		public int ActiveTransactionFlags
		{
			get
			{
				if (_activeTransactionStack.Count() <= 0)
				{
					return DefaultTransactionFlags;
				}
				return _activeTransactionStack.Peek().TransactionFlags;
			}
		}

		public bool SuppressEventsForTransaction => (ActiveTransactionFlags & 2) == 2;

		public bool SuppressHistoryForTransaction => (ActiveTransactionFlags & 1) == 1;

		public event EventHandler IsDirtyChanged;

		public event EventHandler HistoryRecordAdded;

		public event EventHandler Undone;

		public event EventHandler Redone;

		public TransactionManager()
		{
			InitializeFields();
		}

		public virtual void ClearRedoStack()
		{
			_redoStack.Clear();
			_redoStackDescriptions.Clear();
		}

		public virtual void ClearHistory()
		{
			_undoStack.Clear();
			_undoStackDescriptions.Clear();
			ClearRedoStack();
		}

		public void ResetIsDirty()
		{
			if (IsDirty)
			{
				_savePointReference = PeekUndoRecord();
				OnIsDirtyChanged();
			}
		}

		public virtual void StartTransaction(string description)
		{
			StartTransaction(description, ActiveTransactionFlags);
		}

		public virtual void StartTransaction(string description, int transactionFlags)
		{
			_activeTransactionStack.Push(new TransactionInfo
			{
				TransactionFlags = transactionFlags,
				Description = description
			});
			if (!SuppressHistoryForTransaction)
			{
				_pendingHistoryStack.Push(new HistoryRecord
				{
					Description = description
				});
			}
		}

		public virtual void FinishTransaction()
		{
			if (_activeTransactionStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!SuppressHistoryForTransaction && _pendingHistoryStack.Count > 0)
			{
				HistoryRecord historyRecord = _pendingHistoryStack.Pop();
				if (_pendingHistoryStack.Count == 0)
				{
					if (!historyRecord.IsEmpty)
					{
						bool isDirty = IsDirty;
						PushUndoRecord(historyRecord);
						ClearRedoStack();
						if (isDirty != IsDirty)
						{
							OnIsDirtyChanged();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_pendingHistoryStack.Peek().MergeHistory(historyRecord);
				}
			}
			_activeTransactionStack.Pop();
		}

		public virtual void AbortTransaction()
		{
			if (_activeTransactionStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!SuppressHistoryForTransaction && _pendingHistoryStack.Count > 0)
			{
				HistoryRecord historyRecord = _pendingHistoryStack.Pop();
				int changeCount = historyRecord.ChangeCount;
				if (changeCount > 0)
				{
					try
					{
						DoingUndo = true;
						for (int num = changeCount - 1; num >= 0; num--)
						{
							historyRecord.UndoChange(num);
						}
					}
					finally
					{
						DoingUndo = false;
					}
				}
			}
			_activeTransactionStack.Pop();
		}

		public virtual void AddChangeRecord(IChangeRecord changeRecord)
		{
			if (changeRecord == null || SuppressHistoryForTransaction)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_pendingHistoryStack.Count > 0)
			{
				_pendingHistoryStack.Peek().AddRecord(changeRecord);
				return;
			}
			bool isDirty = IsDirty;
			HistoryRecord historyRecord = new HistoryRecord();
			historyRecord.AddRecord(changeRecord);
			PushUndoRecord(historyRecord);
			ClearRedoStack();
			if (isDirty != IsDirty)
			{
				OnIsDirtyChanged();
			}
		}

		public virtual int Undo(int undoCount)
		{
			if (undoCount <= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < undoCount; i++)
			{
				if (!UndoOneRecord())
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return i;
		}

		public virtual int Redo(int redoCount)
		{
			if (redoCount <= 0)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < redoCount; i++)
			{
				if (!RedoOneRecord())
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return i;
		}

		protected virtual bool UndoOneRecord()
		{
			if (_undoStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (_activeTransactionStack.Count > 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool isDirty = IsDirty;
			try
			{
				DoingUndo = true;
				int num = 1;
				if (!UndoAndRedoRaiseEvents)
				{
					num |= 2;
				}
				StartTransaction("Undo", num);
				HistoryRecord historyRecord = PopUndoRecord();
				int changeCount = historyRecord.ChangeCount;
				if (changeCount <= 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				int num2 = changeCount - 1;
				while (num2 >= 0 && historyRecord.UndoChange(num2))
				{
					num2--;
				}
				if (num2 < 0)
				{
					flag = true;
					FinishTransaction();
				}
				else
				{
					DoingUndo = false;
					DoingRedo = true;
					while (TransactionNesting > 0)
					{
						AbortTransaction();
					}
					for (num2++; num2 < changeCount; num2++)
					{
						historyRecord.RedoChange(num2);
					}
					AbortTransaction();
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					PushRedoRecord(historyRecord);
					OnUndone();
				}
				else
				{
					PushUndoRecord(historyRecord);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				DoingUndo = false;
				DoingRedo = false;
			}
			if (isDirty != IsDirty)
			{
				OnIsDirtyChanged();
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected virtual bool RedoOneRecord()
		{
			if (_redoStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (_activeTransactionStack.Count > 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			bool isDirty = IsDirty;
			DoingRedo = true;
			try
			{
				int num = 1;
				if (!UndoAndRedoRaiseEvents)
				{
					num |= 2;
				}
				StartTransaction("Redo", num);
				HistoryRecord historyRecord = PopRedoRecord();
				int changeCount = historyRecord.ChangeCount;
				if (changeCount <= 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
				int i;
				for (i = 0; i < changeCount && historyRecord.RedoChange(i); i++)
				{
				}
				if (i == changeCount)
				{
					flag = true;
					FinishTransaction();
				}
				else
				{
					while (TransactionNesting > 1)
					{
						AbortTransaction();
					}
					DoingRedo = false;
					DoingUndo = true;
					for (i--; i >= 0; i--)
					{
						historyRecord.UndoChange(i);
					}
					AbortTransaction();
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					PushUndoRecord(historyRecord);
					OnRedone();
				}
				else
				{
					PushRedoRecord(historyRecord);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				DoingRedo = false;
				DoingUndo = false;
			}
			if (isDirty != IsDirty)
			{
				OnIsDirtyChanged();
			}
			return flag;
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsDirtyChanged()
		{
			if (this.IsDirtyChanged != null)
			{
				this.IsDirtyChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnUndone()
		{
			if (this.Undone != null)
			{
				this.Undone(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRedone()
		{
			if (this.Redone != null)
			{
				this.Redone(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHistoryRecordAdded()
		{
			if (this.HistoryRecordAdded != null)
			{
				this.HistoryRecordAdded(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected HistoryRecord PopUndoRecord()
		{
			if (_undoStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			HistoryRecord result = _undoStack[_undoStack.Count - 1];
			_undoStack.RemoveAt(_undoStack.Count - 1);
			_undoStackDescriptions.RemoveAt(_undoStackDescriptions.Count - 1);
			return result;
		}

		protected void PushUndoRecord(HistoryRecord record)
		{
			_undoStack.Add(record);
			_undoStackDescriptions.Add(record.Description);
		}

		protected HistoryRecord PeekUndoRecord()
		{
			if (_undoStack.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _undoStack[_undoStack.Count - 1];
		}

		protected HistoryRecord PopRedoRecord()
		{
			if (_redoStack.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			HistoryRecord result = _redoStack[_redoStack.Count - 1];
			_redoStack.RemoveAt(_redoStack.Count - 1);
			_redoStackDescriptions.RemoveAt(_redoStackDescriptions.Count - 1);
			return result;
		}

		protected void PushRedoRecord(HistoryRecord record)
		{
			_redoStack.Add(record);
			_redoStackDescriptions.Add(record.Description);
		}

		protected HistoryRecord PeekRedoRecord()
		{
			if (_redoStack.Count <= 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return _redoStack[_redoStack.Count - 1];
		}

		private void InitializeFields()
		{
			UndoAndRedoRaiseEvents = true;
			_undoStackDescriptionsRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_undoStackDescriptions);
			_redoStackDescriptionsRO = new ReadOnlyCollection<string>(_redoStackDescriptions);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.Tools
{
	public abstract class DiagramTool
	{
		private System.Windows.Input.Cursor _cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;

		private Diagram _mouseCaptureDiagram;

		protected List<Diagram> RegisteredDiagrams { get; private set; }

		protected System.Windows.Input.Cursor Cursor
		{
			get
			{
				return _cursor;
			}
			set
			{
				_cursor = value;
				foreach (Diagram registeredDiagram in RegisteredDiagrams)
				{
					registeredDiagram.PrimaryLayer.Cursor = _cursor;
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool IsMouseCaptured
		{
			get
			{
				if (_mouseCaptureDiagram != null)
				{
					return _mouseCaptureDiagram.IsMouseCaptured;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static bool IsCtrlDown
		{
			get
			{
				if (!Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl))
				{
					return Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl);
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public DiagramTool()
		{
			RegisteredDiagrams = new List<Diagram>();
		}

		public void RegisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			if (!RegisteredDiagrams.Contains(diagram))
			{
				RegisteredDiagrams.Add(diagram);
				diagram.PrimaryLayer.Cursor = Cursor;
				OnRegisterWithDiagram(diagram);
			}
		}

		public void UnregisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			if (RegisteredDiagrams.Contains(diagram))
			{
				if (diagram == _mouseCaptureDiagram)
				{
					ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
				RegisteredDiagrams.Remove(diagram);
				OnUnregisterWithDiagram(diagram);
			}
		}

		public void UnregisterWithAllDiagrams()
		{
			foreach (Diagram item in new List<Diagram>(RegisteredDiagrams))
			{
				UnregisterWithDiagram(item);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnRegisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			diagram.Disposing += diagram_Disposing;
			RegisterLayerEvents(diagram.PrimaryLayer);
			RegisterLayerEvents(diagram.OverlayLayer);
		}

		protected virtual void OnUnregisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			diagram.Disposing -= diagram_Disposing;
			UnregisterLayerEvents(diagram.PrimaryLayer);
			UnregisterLayerEvents(diagram.OverlayLayer);
		}

		protected virtual void RegisterLayerEvents(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			layer.PreviewKeyDown += diagramLayer_PreviewKeyDown;
			layer.PreviewKeyUp += diagramLayer_PreviewKeyUp;
			layer.MouseEnter += layer_MouseEnter;
			layer.MouseLeave += layer_MouseLeave;
			layer.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown += diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
			layer.PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp += diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp;
			layer.PreviewMouseMove += diagramLayer_PreviewMouseMove;
			layer.PreviewMouseRightButtonUp += diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonUp;
			layer.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown += diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
			layer.LostMouseCapture += diagramLayer_LostMouseCapture;
			layer.KeyDown += layer_KeyDown;
			layer.KeyUp += layer_KeyUp;
			layer.MouseLeftButtonDown += layer_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			layer.MouseMove += layer_MouseMove;
			layer.MouseLeftButtonUp += layer_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			layer.MouseRightButtonDown += layer_MouseRightButtonDown;
			layer.MouseRightButtonUp += layer_MouseRightButtonUp;
		}

		protected virtual void UnregisterLayerEvents(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			layer.PreviewKeyDown -= diagramLayer_PreviewKeyDown;
			layer.PreviewKeyUp -= diagramLayer_PreviewKeyUp;
			layer.MouseEnter -= layer_MouseEnter;
			layer.MouseLeave -= layer_MouseLeave;
			layer.PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown -= diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown;
			layer.PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp -= diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp;
			layer.PreviewMouseMove -= diagramLayer_PreviewMouseMove;
			layer.PreviewMouseRightButtonUp -= diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonUp;
			layer.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown -= diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
			layer.LostMouseCapture -= diagramLayer_LostMouseCapture;
			layer.KeyDown -= layer_KeyDown;
			layer.KeyUp -= layer_KeyUp;
			layer.MouseLeftButtonDown -= layer_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			layer.MouseMove -= layer_MouseMove;
			layer.MouseLeftButtonUp -= layer_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			layer.MouseRightButtonDown -= layer_MouseRightButtonDown;
			layer.MouseRightButtonUp -= layer_MouseRightButtonUp;
		}

		protected virtual void AbortOperation()
		{
			ReleaseMouseCapture();
		}

		private void diagram_Disposing(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UnregisterWithDiagram(sender as Diagram);
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewKeyDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewKeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewKeyUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseEnter(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseEnter(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseLeave(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseLeave(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_LostMouseCapture(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnLostMouseCapture(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewMouseRightButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewMouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewMouseMove(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void diagramLayer_PreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_KeyDown(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnKeyDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_KeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnKeyUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseMove(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseRightButtonDown(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		private void layer_MouseRightButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				OnMouseRightButtonUp(diagramLayer.Diagram, e);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewKeyDown(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewKeyUp(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseEnter(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseLeave(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnLostMouseCapture(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewMouseRightButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnKeyDown(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnKeyUp(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				AbortOperation();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseRightButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnMouseRightButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected void CaptureMouse(Diagram diagram)
		{
			_mouseCaptureDiagram = diagram;
			_mouseCaptureDiagram.PrimaryLayer.CaptureMouse();
		}

		protected void ReleaseMouseCapture()
		{
			if (_mouseCaptureDiagram != null && _mouseCaptureDiagram.PrimaryLayer.IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				_mouseCaptureDiagram.PrimaryLayer.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				_mouseCaptureDiagram = null;
			}
		}

		public static UIElement GetClickedElement(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			UIElement diagramElement = e.GetDiagramElement();
			if (diagramElement == null)
			{
				return e.Source as UIElement;
			}
			return diagramElement;
		}
	}
	public class ElementTool : ElementToolBase
	{
		private FrameworkElement _pendingElement;

		private Diagram _activeDiagram;

		public ElementTool()
		{
			base.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Cross;
		}

		protected override void OnUnregisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (_pendingElement != null && _activeDiagram == diagram)
			{
				AbortOperation();
			}
			base.OnUnregisterWithDiagram(diagram);
		}

		protected virtual void AddElementIntoDiagram(Diagram diagram, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			diagram.AddElementNoPreview(element);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_activeDiagram = diagram;
			_pendingElement = CreateElement();
			if (_pendingElement != null)
			{
				DiagramOverlayLayer overlayLayer = diagram.OverlayLayer;
				overlayLayer.AddElement(_pendingElement);
				_pendingElement.UpdateLayout();
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = overlayLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
				mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X -= diagram.GetElementWidth(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
				mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y -= diagram.GetElementHeight(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
				overlayLayer.PositionElement(position: diagram.SnapPointToGrid(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates), element: _pendingElement);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_pendingElement != null)
			{
				if (_activeDiagram != diagram)
				{
					_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingElement);
					_activeDiagram = diagram;
					_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_pendingElement);
				}
				DiagramOverlayLayer overlayLayer = _activeDiagram.OverlayLayer;
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = overlayLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
				mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X -= diagram.GetElementWidth(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
				mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y -= diagram.GetElementHeight(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
				overlayLayer.PositionElement(position: diagram.SnapPointToGrid(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates), element: _pendingElement);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
			if (_pendingElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingElement);
			System.Windows.Point mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = diagram.PrimaryLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
			mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X -= diagram.GetElementWidth(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
			mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y -= diagram.GetElementHeight(_pendingElement) / 2.0;
			mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = diagram.SnapPointToGrid(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates);
			PreviewElementToolEventArgs e2 = new PreviewElementToolEventArgs(_pendingElement);
			OnPreviewElementDrawn(e2);
			if (!e2.Cancel)
			{
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_pendingElement, mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_pendingElement, mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y);
				using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Add");
				try
				{
					AddElementIntoDiagram(diagram, _pendingElement);
					OnElementDrawn(new ElementToolEventArgs(_pendingElement));
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				}
			}
			_pendingElement = null;
		}

		protected override void AbortOperation()
		{
			if (_pendingElement != null && _activeDiagram != null)
			{
				_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingElement);
				_activeDiagram = null;
				_pendingElement = null;
			}
			base.AbortOperation();
		}
	}
	[ContentProperty("Protoype")]
	public abstract class ElementToolBase : DiagramTool
	{
		public FrameworkElement Protoype { get; set; }

		public event EventHandler<PreviewElementToolEventArgs> PreviewElementDrawn;

		public event EventHandler<ElementToolEventArgs> ElementDrawn;

		protected virtual FrameworkElement CreateElement()
		{
			if (Protoype != null)
			{
				return XamlReader.Parse(XamlWriter.Save(Protoype)) as FrameworkElement;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual void OnPreviewElementDrawn(PreviewElementToolEventArgs args)
		{
			if (this.PreviewElementDrawn != null)
			{
				this.PreviewElementDrawn(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnElementDrawn(ElementToolEventArgs args)
		{
			if (this.ElementDrawn != null)
			{
				this.ElementDrawn(this, args);
			}
		}
	}
	public class PanTool : DiagramTool
	{
		private enum ToolState
		{
			Idle,
			Dragging
		}

		private System.Windows.Point _dragStartPoint;

		private ToolState State { get; set; }

		public PanTool()
		{
			base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.GrabHand;
			State = ToolState.Idle;
		}

		protected override void AbortOperation()
		{
			base.AbortOperation();
			State = ToolState.Idle;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
			CaptureMouse(diagram);
			base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.GrabHandGrasp;
			_dragStartPoint = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			State = ToolState.Dragging;
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (State != ToolState.Dragging)
			{
				base.OnPreviewMouseMove(diagram, e);
			}
			else if (e.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				AbortOperation();
				base.OnPreviewMouseMove(diagram, e);
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				System.Windows.Point viewportPoint = diagram.CanvasPointToViewportPoint(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
				diagram.ScrollCanvasPointToViewportPoint(_dragStartPoint, viewportPoint);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (State != ToolState.Dragging)
			{
				base.OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
				return;
			}
			ReleaseMouseCapture();
			base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.GrabHand;
			State = ToolState.Idle;
		}
	}
	public class PolygonTool : PolylineTool
	{
		protected override void AddElementIntoDiagram(Diagram diagram, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			Polyline obj = element as Polyline;
			obj.IsClosed = true;
			obj.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.AddElementIntoDiagram(diagram, element);
		}
	}
	public class PolylineTool : ElementToolBase
	{
		private Polyline _pendingPolyline;

		private Line _pendingLine;

		private Diagram _activeDiagram;

		private System.Windows.Point _placementPoint;

		private System.Windows.Point _startPoint;

		public PolylineTool()
		{
			base.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Cross;
		}

		protected override void OnUnregisterWithDiagram(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (_pendingPolyline != null && _activeDiagram == diagram)
			{
				AbortOperation();
			}
			base.OnUnregisterWithDiagram(diagram);
		}

		protected virtual void AddElementIntoDiagram(Diagram diagram, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			diagram.AddElementNoPreview(element);
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_activeDiagram = diagram;
			DiagramOverlayLayer overlayLayer = diagram.OverlayLayer;
			System.Windows.Point mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = overlayLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
			mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = diagram.SnapPointToGrid(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates);
			if (_pendingPolyline == null)
			{
				_pendingPolyline = CreateElement() as Polyline;
				if (_pendingPolyline == null)
				{
					_pendingPolyline = new Polyline();
				}
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
				_pendingPolyline.Points = pointCollection;
				_placementPoint = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates;
				_startPoint = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates;
				overlayLayer.PositionElement(_pendingPolyline, _placementPoint);
				overlayLayer.AddElement(_pendingPolyline);
				_pendingLine = new Line
				{
					X1 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X,
					Y1 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y
				};
				_pendingLine.Stroke = _pendingPolyline.Stroke;
				_pendingLine.StrokeThickness = _pendingPolyline.StrokeThickness;
				_pendingLine.StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 1.0, 1.0 };
				_pendingLine.X2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X;
				_pendingLine.Y2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y;
				overlayLayer.AddElement(_pendingLine);
			}
			else
			{
				FixPosition(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates);
				PointCollection pointCollection2 = _pendingPolyline.Points.Clone();
				Vector vector = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates - _startPoint;
				pointCollection2.Add(new System.Windows.Point(vector.X, vector.Y));
				_pendingPolyline.Points = pointCollection2;
				_pendingLine.X1 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X;
				_pendingLine.Y1 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y;
				_pendingLine.X2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X;
				_pendingLine.Y2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnPreviewMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_pendingPolyline != null && _activeDiagram == diagram)
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = _activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates();
				mousePositionInLayerCoordinates = diagram.SnapPointToGrid(mousePositionInLayerCoordinates);
				_pendingLine.X2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.X;
				_pendingLine.Y2 = mousePositionInLayerCoordinates.Y;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseRightButtonDown(diagram, e);
			if (_pendingPolyline == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_pendingPolyline.Points.Count < 2)
			{
				AbortOperation();
				return;
			}
			_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingPolyline);
			_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingLine);
			PreviewElementToolEventArgs e2 = new PreviewElementToolEventArgs(_pendingLine);
			OnPreviewElementDrawn(e2);
			if (!e2.Cancel)
			{
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(_pendingPolyline, _placementPoint.X);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(_pendingPolyline, _placementPoint.Y);
				using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Add");
				try
				{
					AddElementIntoDiagram(diagram, _pendingPolyline);
					OnElementDrawn(new ElementToolEventArgs(_pendingPolyline));
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				}
			}
			_pendingPolyline = null;
		}

		protected override void AbortOperation()
		{
			if (_pendingPolyline != null && _activeDiagram != null)
			{
				_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingPolyline);
				_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_pendingLine);
				_activeDiagram = null;
				_pendingPolyline = null;
				_pendingLine = null;
			}
			base.AbortOperation();
		}

		private void FixPosition(System.Windows.Point clickedPoint)
		{
			if (clickedPoint.X < _placementPoint.X)
			{
				_placementPoint.X = clickedPoint.X;
			}
			if (clickedPoint.Y < _placementPoint.Y)
			{
				_placementPoint.Y = clickedPoint.Y;
			}
			_activeDiagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_pendingPolyline, _placementPoint);
		}
	}
	public class SelectTool : DiagramTool
	{
		private readonly Dictionary<Diagram, MultiSelectionRectangle> _multiSelectionRectangles = new Dictionary<Diagram, MultiSelectionRectangle>();

		private readonly Dictionary<Diagram, MovingPreviewRectangle> _movingPreviewRectangles = new Dictionary<Diagram, MovingPreviewRectangle>();

		private System.Windows.Point _startPoint;

		private bool _canStartMoving;

		private UIElement _mouseCaptureElement;

		protected override void AbortOperation()
		{
			_startPoint = default(System.Windows.Point);
			_canStartMoving = false;
			if (_multiSelectionRectangles.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (Diagram key in _multiSelectionRectangles.Keys)
				{
					if (key.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_multiSelectionRectangles[key]))
					{
						key.OverlayLayer.Children.Remove(_multiSelectionRectangles[key]);
					}
				}
				_multiSelectionRectangles.Clear();
			}
			if (_movingPreviewRectangles.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (Diagram key2 in _movingPreviewRectangles.Keys)
				{
					if (key2.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_movingPreviewRectangles[key2]))
					{
						key2.OverlayLayer.Children.Remove(_movingPreviewRectangles[key2]);
					}
				}
				_movingPreviewRectangles.Clear();
			}
			if (_mouseCaptureElement != null && _mouseCaptureElement.IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				_mouseCaptureElement.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			}
			base.AbortOperation();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!diagram.IsReadOnly)
			{
				_startPoint = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				UIElement uIElement = DiagramTool.GetClickedElement(diagram, e);
				if (uIElement is DiagramLayer)
				{
					uIElement = null;
				}
				if (uIElement != null)
				{
					if (DiagramTool.IsCtrlDown)
					{
						if (diagram.IsSelected(uIElement))
						{
							diagram.Unselect(uIElement);
						}
						else
						{
							diagram.Select(uIElement);
						}
					}
					else if (e.ClickCount == 1)
					{
						if (diagram.IsGrouped(uIElement))
						{
							DiagramGroup ultimateParentGroup = diagram.GetUltimateParentGroup(uIElement);
							if (diagram.IsSelected(ultimateParentGroup))
							{
								if (!diagram.IsSelected(uIElement))
								{
									IEnumerable<UIElement> selectedGroupMembers = GetSelectedGroupMembers(diagram, ultimateParentGroup);
									if (selectedGroupMembers.Count() != 0)
									{
										diagram.Unselect(selectedGroupMembers);
									}
									diagram.Select(uIElement);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								diagram.UnselectAll();
								diagram.Select(ultimateParentGroup);
							}
						}
						else if (!diagram.IsSelected(uIElement))
						{
							diagram.UnselectAll();
							diagram.Select(uIElement);
						}
						_canStartMoving = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					diagram.UnselectAll();
					MultiSelectionRectangle multiSelectionRectangle = new MultiSelectionRectangle();
					multiSelectionRectangle.StartPoint = _startPoint;
					multiSelectionRectangle.EndPoint = _startPoint;
					diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(multiSelectionRectangle);
					multiSelectionRectangle.UpdateLayout();
					_multiSelectionRectangles[diagram] = multiSelectionRectangle;
				}
				_mouseCaptureElement = e.Source as UIElement;
				if (_mouseCaptureElement != null)
				{
					_mouseCaptureElement.CaptureMouse();
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
		}

		private static IEnumerable<UIElement> GetSelectedGroupMembers(Diagram diagram, DiagramGroup group)
		{
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.GroupMembers(group))
			{
				if (item is DiagramGroup diagramGroup)
				{
					IEnumerable<UIElement> selectedGroupMembers = GetSelectedGroupMembers(diagram, diagramGroup);
					if (selectedGroupMembers.Count() == 0)
					{
						continue;
					}
					foreach (UIElement item2 in selectedGroupMembers)
					{
						yield return item2;
					}
				}
				else if (diagram.IsSelected(item))
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!diagram.IsReadOnly)
			{
				if (_multiSelectionRectangles.ContainsKey(diagram))
				{
					_multiSelectionRectangles[diagram].EndPoint = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (UIElement diagramElement in diagram.DiagramElements)
					{
						FrameworkElement frameworkElement = diagramElement as FrameworkElement;
						if (frameworkElement.IsMouseOver)
						{
							frameworkElement.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeAll;
						}
						else
						{
							frameworkElement.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.Arrow;
						}
					}
					if (_canStartMoving)
					{
						if (!_movingPreviewRectangles.ContainsKey(diagram))
						{
							MovingPreviewRectangle movingPreviewRectangle = new MovingPreviewRectangle(diagram);
							diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(movingPreviewRectangle);
							_movingPreviewRectangles[diagram] = movingPreviewRectangle;
						}
						System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
						_movingPreviewRectangles[diagram].MoveFromOriginal(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates - _startPoint);
					}
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseMove(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!diagram.IsReadOnly)
			{
				if (_multiSelectionRectangles.ContainsKey(diagram))
				{
					MultiSelectionRectangle multiSelectionRectangle = _multiSelectionRectangles[diagram];
					multiSelectionRectangle.EndPoint = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
					IEnumerable<UIElement> selectedElements = multiSelectionRectangle.GetSelectedElements(diagram);
					diagram.Select(selectedElements);
					diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(multiSelectionRectangle);
					_multiSelectionRectangles.Remove(diagram);
				}
				if (_canStartMoving)
				{
					if (_movingPreviewRectangles.ContainsKey(diagram))
					{
						System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
						MovingPreviewRectangle movingPreviewRectangle = _movingPreviewRectangles[diagram];
						movingPreviewRectangle.MoveFromOriginal(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates - _startPoint);
						using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Moving"))
						{
							try
							{
								foreach (UIElement item in GetNonGroupSelection(diagram))
								{
									if (diagram.CanMoveElement(item))
									{
										diagram.PositionElement(item, movingPreviewRectangle.GetElementNewPosition(item));
									}
								}
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
								transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
								throw;
							}
						}
						diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(movingPreviewRectangle);
						_movingPreviewRectangles.Remove(diagram);
					}
					_canStartMoving = false;
				}
				if (_mouseCaptureElement != null && _mouseCaptureElement.IsMouseCaptured)
				{
					_mouseCaptureElement.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> GetNonGroupSelection(Diagram diagram)
		{
			IList<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>(diagram.Selection);
			foreach (UIElement item in diagram.Selection)
			{
				DiagramGroup ultimateParentGroup = diagram.GetUltimateParentGroup(item);
				if (ultimateParentGroup != null && list.Contains(ultimateParentGroup))
				{
					list.Remove(ultimateParentGroup);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override void OnMouseRightButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			UIElement uIElement = DiagramTool.GetClickedElement(diagram, e);
			if (!diagram.IsReadOnly)
			{
				if (uIElement is DiagramLayer || uIElement is Diagram)
				{
					uIElement = null;
				}
				if (uIElement == null)
				{
					diagram.UnselectAll();
				}
				else if (diagram.IsGrouped(uIElement))
				{
					DiagramGroup ultimateParentGroup = diagram.GetUltimateParentGroup(uIElement);
					diagram.UnselectAll();
					diagram.Select(ultimateParentGroup);
				}
				else if (!diagram.IsSelected(uIElement))
				{
					diagram.UnselectAll();
					diagram.Select(uIElement);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class SymbolTool : ElementTool
	{
		public bool ShouldAttachNameLabel { get; set; }

		public SymbolTool()
		{
			ShouldAttachNameLabel = true;
		}

		protected override FrameworkElement CreateElement()
		{
			Symbol symbol = base.CreateElement() as Symbol;
			if (symbol == null)
			{
				symbol = new Symbol();
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(symbol.Width) || double.IsNaN(symbol.Height))
			{
				symbol.SetToDefaultSize();
			}
			return symbol;
		}

		protected override void AddElementIntoDiagram(Diagram diagram, FrameworkElement element)
		{
			base.AddElementIntoDiagram(diagram, element);
			if (ShouldAttachNameLabel)
			{
				diagram.AddNameLabelToElement(element);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ZoomTool : DiagramTool
	{
		private enum ToolState
		{
			Idle,
			Dragging
		}

		private const double _minimumDragDistance = 5.0;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _dragRectangle;

		private System.Windows.Point _dragFirstPoint;

		private System.Windows.Point _dragSecondPoint;

		private ToolState State { get; set; }

		public ZoomTool()
		{
			base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.ZoomIn;
			State = ToolState.Idle;
		}

		private void RemoveDragRectangle()
		{
			if (_dragRectangle != null)
			{
				if (_dragRectangle.Parent is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
				{
					diagramLayer.RemoveElement(_dragRectangle);
				}
				_dragRectangle = null;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLostMouseCapture(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			AbortOperation();
			base.OnLostMouseCapture(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(diagram, e);
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				CaptureMouse(diagram);
				_dragFirstPoint = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				State = ToolState.Dragging;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseMove(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseMove(diagram, e);
			if (e.Handled || State == ToolState.Idle)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.LeftButton != MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				AbortOperation();
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			double viewportMargin = 10.0;
			diagram.ScrollPointIntoView(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates, viewportMargin);
			_ = diagram.ActualWidth;
			_ = diagram.ActualHeight;
			double num = Math.Abs(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates.X - _dragFirstPoint.X);
			double num2 = Math.Abs(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates.Y - _dragFirstPoint.Y);
			double num3 = 5.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
			if (_dragRectangle == null && (num > num3 || num2 > num3))
			{
				_dragRectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
				{
					Height = 0.0,
					Width = 0.0,
					Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
					StrokeThickness = 1.0,
					StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 4.0, 4.0 }
				};
				diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_dragRectangle, _dragFirstPoint);
				base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.ZoomRegion;
			}
			if (_dragRectangle != null)
			{
				_dragSecondPoint = mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates;
				Rect rect = new Rect(_dragFirstPoint, _dragSecondPoint);
				diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_dragRectangle, rect.TopLeft);
				_dragRectangle.Width = rect.Width;
				_dragRectangle.Height = rect.Height;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(Diagram diagram, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(diagram, e);
			if (e.Handled || State == ToolState.Idle)
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (_dragRectangle != null)
			{
				RemoveDragRectangle();
				flag = true;
			}
			ReleaseMouseCapture();
			if (flag)
			{
				Rect rect = new Rect(_dragFirstPoint, _dragSecondPoint);
				double num = 5.0 / diagram.ZoomScale;
				if (rect.Width > num && rect.Height > num)
				{
					diagram.ZoomToRect(rect);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				if (DiagramTool.IsCtrlDown)
				{
					diagram.ZoomOut(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
				}
				else
				{
					diagram.ZoomIn(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
				}
			}
			base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.ZoomIn;
			State = ToolState.Idle;
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.LeftCtrl || e.Key == Key.RightCtrl)
			{
				base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.ZoomOut;
			}
			base.OnKeyDown(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyUp(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.LeftCtrl || e.Key == Key.RightCtrl)
			{
				base.Cursor = DiagramCursors.ZoomIn;
			}
			base.OnKeyUp(diagram, e);
		}

		protected override void AbortOperation()
		{
			State = ToolState.Idle;
			RemoveDragRectangle();
			base.AbortOperation();
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.Overlays
{
	public class BorderOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _solidBorder;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _transparentBorder;

		private const double _transparentMargin = 2.5;

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				if (_solidBorder != null)
				{
					yield return _solidBorder;
				}
				if (_transparentBorder != null)
				{
					yield return _transparentBorder;
				}
			}
		}

		public BorderOverlay(ISelectionOverlayService service)
			: base(service)
		{
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_solidBorder == null)
			{
				_transparentBorder = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
				_transparentBorder.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
				_transparentBorder.StrokeThickness = 5.0;
				_transparentBorder.IsHitTestVisible = true;
				_transparentBorder.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeAll;
				_transparentBorder.Width = base.AdornedWidth + 5.0;
				_transparentBorder.Height = base.AdornedHeight + 5.0;
				_transparentBorder.RenderTransform = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransform;
				_transparentBorder.RenderTransformOrigin = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
				layer.AddElement(_transparentBorder, base.AdornedTopLeft - new Vector(2.5, 2.5));
				_solidBorder = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
