				_solidBorder.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.DarkSlateGray;
				_solidBorder.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				_solidBorder.IsHitTestVisible = false;
				_solidBorder.Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeAll;
				_solidBorder.Width = base.AdornedWidth;
				_solidBorder.Height = base.AdornedHeight;
				_solidBorder.RenderTransform = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransform;
				_solidBorder.RenderTransformOrigin = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
				layer.AddElement(_solidBorder, base.AdornedTopLeft);
			}
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_solidBorder != null)
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_solidBorder);
			}
			if (_transparentBorder != null)
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_transparentBorder);
			}
			_solidBorder = null;
			_transparentBorder = null;
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_transparentBorder != null)
			{
				_transparentBorder.Width = base.AdornedWidth + 5.0;
				_transparentBorder.Height = base.AdornedHeight + 5.0;
				_transparentBorder.RenderTransform = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransform;
				_transparentBorder.RenderTransformOrigin = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
				layer.PositionElement(_transparentBorder, base.AdornedTopLeft - new Vector(2.5, 2.5));
			}
			if (_solidBorder != null)
			{
				_solidBorder.Width = base.AdornedWidth;
				_solidBorder.Height = base.AdornedHeight;
				_solidBorder.RenderTransform = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransform;
				_solidBorder.RenderTransformOrigin = base.AdornedElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
				layer.PositionElement(_solidBorder, base.AdornedTopLeft);
			}
		}
	}
	public abstract class DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private bool _displayed;

		public ISelectionOverlayService OverlayService { get; private set; }

		public Diagram Editor { get; set; }

		protected virtual bool CanDisplay => true;

		public abstract IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners { get; }

		public UIElement AdornedElement => OverlayService.AdornedElement;

		public double AdornedWidth
		{
			get
			{
				if (AdornedElement == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return OverlayService.GetAdornedRect(Editor).Width;
			}
		}

		public double AdornedHeight
		{
			get
			{
				if (AdornedElement == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return OverlayService.GetAdornedRect(Editor).Height;
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point AdornedTopLeft
		{
			get
			{
				UIElement adornedElement = AdornedElement;
				if (adornedElement == null)
				{
					return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				}
				Rect adornedRect = OverlayService.GetAdornedRect(Editor);
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(adornedElement);
				return new System.Windows.Point(elementPosition.X + adornedRect.X, elementPosition.Y + adornedRect.Y);
			}
		}

		public FlipAxis AdornedFlipAxis
		{
			get
			{
				UIElement adornedElement = AdornedElement;
				if (adornedElement == null)
				{
					return FlipAxis.None;
				}
				return Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(adornedElement);
			}
		}

		public double AdornedRotation
		{
			get
			{
				UIElement adornedElement = AdornedElement;
				if (adornedElement == null)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return Editor.GetElementRotation(adornedElement);
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point AdornedRotationCenter
		{
			get
			{
				UIElement adornedElement = AdornedElement;
				if (adornedElement == null)
				{
					return new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
				}
				return Editor.GetElementRotationCenter(adornedElement);
			}
		}

		protected DiagramElementOverlay(ISelectionOverlayService overlayService)
		{
			OverlayService = overlayService;
		}

		public void Display(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (!_displayed && CanDisplay)
			{
				DisplayUI(layer);
				_displayed = true;
			}
		}

		public void Update(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (!_displayed)
			{
				Display(layer);
			}
			else if (CanDisplay)
			{
				UpdateUI(layer);
			}
			else
			{
				Hide(layer);
			}
		}

		public void Hide(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_displayed)
			{
				HideUI(layer);
				_displayed = false;
			}
		}

		protected abstract void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer);

		protected abstract void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer);

		protected abstract void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer);

		public System.Windows.Point AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			UIElement adornedElement = AdornedElement;
			if (adornedElement == null)
			{
				return point;
			}
			return Editor.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(adornedElement, point);
		}
	}
	internal class MovingPreviewRectangle : ContentControl
	{
		private static readonly System.Windows.Size DefaultElementPreviewSize = new System.Windows.Size(50.0, 50.0);

		private System.Windows.Point _position;

		private readonly Dictionary<UIElement, System.Windows.Point> _oldPositions;

		private readonly Canvas _mirrorCanvas;

		private readonly Diagram _diagram;

		public System.Windows.Point Position
		{
			get
			{
				return _position;
			}
			set
			{
				_position = value;
				Reposition();
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point OldPosition { get; private set; }

		public MovingPreviewRectangle(Diagram diagram)
		{
			_diagram = diagram;
			_oldPositions = GetElementOldPositions();
			_mirrorCanvas = CreateMirrorCanvas(out var topLeft);
			Position = topLeft;
			OldPosition = topLeft;
			base.Content = _mirrorCanvas;
			base.Width = _mirrorCanvas.Width;
			base.Height = _mirrorCanvas.Height;
		}

		public void MoveFromOriginal(Vector distance)
		{
			Position = _diagram.SnapPointToGrid(OldPosition + distance);
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetElementOldPosition(UIElement element)
		{
			return _oldPositions[element];
		}

		public System.Windows.Point GetElementNewPosition(UIElement element)
		{
			return _oldPositions[element] + (Position - OldPosition);
		}

		private Canvas CreateMirrorCanvas(out System.Windows.Point topLeft)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(double.MaxValue, double.MaxValue);
			IEnumerable<UIElement> nonGroupSelection = GetNonGroupSelection();
			foreach (UIElement item in nonGroupSelection)
			{
				Rect elementTranformedRect = _diagram.GetElementTranformedRect(item);
				point.X = Math.Min(point.X, elementTranformedRect.Left);
				point.Y = Math.Min(point.Y, elementTranformedRect.Top);
			}
			topLeft = point;
			Canvas canvas = ImagingHelper.CreateVisualCanvasFrom(nonGroupSelection);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle element = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
			{
				Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray,
				StrokeThickness = 1.0,
				StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 4.0, 4.0 },
				Width = canvas.Width,
				Height = canvas.Height
			};
			Canvas.SetLeft(element, 0.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(element, 0.0);
			canvas.Children.Add(element);
			canvas.Opacity = 0.5;
			return canvas;
		}

		private Dictionary<UIElement, System.Windows.Point> GetElementOldPositions()
		{
			Dictionary<UIElement, System.Windows.Point> dictionary = new Dictionary<UIElement, System.Windows.Point>();
			foreach (UIElement item in GetNonGroupSelection())
			{
				dictionary.Add(item, Diagram.GetElementPosition(item));
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private IEnumerable<UIElement> GetNonGroupSelection()
		{
			IList<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>(_diagram.Selection);
			foreach (UIElement item in _diagram.Selection)
			{
				DiagramGroup ultimateParentGroup = _diagram.GetUltimateParentGroup(item);
				if (ultimateParentGroup != null && list.Contains(ultimateParentGroup))
				{
					list.Remove(ultimateParentGroup);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void Reposition()
		{
			_diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(this, Position);
		}
	}
	internal class MultiSelectionRectangle : ContentControl
	{
		private System.Windows.Point _startPoint;

		private System.Windows.Point _endPoint;

		private readonly System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _multiSelectionRectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
		{
			Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red,
			StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 4.0, 4.0 },
			StrokeThickness = 1.0
		};

		public System.Windows.Point StartPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return _startPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				_startPoint = value;
				Reposition();
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Point EndPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return _endPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				_endPoint = value;
				Reposition();
			}
		}

		public MultiSelectionRectangle()
		{
			base.Content = _multiSelectionRectangle;
		}

		private void Reposition()
		{
			if (!double.IsNaN(_startPoint.X) && !double.IsNaN(_startPoint.Y) && !double.IsNaN(_endPoint.X) && !double.IsNaN(_endPoint.Y))
			{
				Rect rect = new Rect(_startPoint, _endPoint);
				base.Width = rect.Width;
				base.Height = rect.Height;
				DiagramLayer.SetLeft(this, rect.Left);
				DiagramLayer.SetTop(this, rect.Top);
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<UIElement> GetSelectedElements(Diagram diagram)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			Rect thisRect = new Rect(_startPoint, _endPoint);
			IList<DiagramGroup> selectedGroups = new List<DiagramGroup>();
			foreach (DiagramGroup group in diagram.Groups)
			{
				if (group.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					Rect elementTranformedRect = diagram.GetElementTranformedRect(group);
					if (thisRect.Contains(elementTranformedRect))
					{
						selectedGroups.Add(group);
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (UIElement diagramElement in diagram.DiagramElements)
			{
				if (diagramElement.Visibility == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					Rect elementTranformedRect2 = diagram.GetElementTranformedRect(diagramElement);
					if (thisRect.Contains(elementTranformedRect2) && diagram.GetUltimateParentGroup(diagramElement) == null)
					{
						yield return diagramElement;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (DiagramGroup item in selectedGroups)
			{
				DiagramGroup elementGroup = diagram.GetElementGroup(item);
				if (elementGroup == null || !selectedGroups.Contains(elementGroup))
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class PolylineReshapeOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private Polyline polyline;

		private IList<Thumb> pointThumbs;

		private IList<Thumb> midThumbs;

		private System.Windows.Point oldPoint;

		private Line line1;

		private Line line2;

		private Rect currentRect;

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				foreach (Thumb pointThumb in pointThumbs)
				{
					yield return pointThumb;
				}
				foreach (Thumb midThumb in midThumbs)
				{
					yield return midThumb;
				}
			}
		}

		public PolylineReshapeOverlay(Polyline polyline)
			: base(polyline)
		{
			this.polyline = polyline;
			line1 = null;
			line2 = null;
			pointThumbs = new List<Thumb>();
			midThumbs = new List<Thumb>();
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			Rect finalRect = GetFinalRect();
			for (int i = 0; i < polyline.Points.Count; i++)
			{
				Thumb thumb = CreatePointThumb();
				pointThumbs.Add(thumb);
				layer.AddElement(thumb, CalcPointThumbPosition(finalRect, i, thumb));
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < (polyline.IsClosed ? polyline.Points.Count : (polyline.Points.Count - 1)); j++)
			{
				Thumb thumb2 = CreateMidThumb();
				midThumbs.Add(thumb2);
				layer.AddElement(thumb2, CalcMidThumbPosition(finalRect, j, thumb2));
			}
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < pointThumbs.Count; i++)
			{
				Thumb thumb = pointThumbs[i];
				thumb.DragStarted -= pointThumb_DragStarted;
				thumb.DragDelta -= pointThumb_DragDelta;
				thumb.DragCompleted -= pointThumb_DragCompleted;
				layer.RemoveElement(thumb);
			}
			pointThumbs.Clear();
			for (int j = 0; j < midThumbs.Count; j++)
			{
				Thumb thumb2 = midThumbs[j];
				thumb2.DragStarted -= midThumb_DragStarted;
				thumb2.DragDelta -= midThumb_DragDelta;
				thumb2.DragCompleted -= midThumb_DragCompleted;
				layer.RemoveElement(thumb2);
			}
			midThumbs.Clear();
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			Rect finalRect = GetFinalRect();
			for (int i = 0; i < polyline.Points.Count; i++)
			{
				Thumb thumb = pointThumbs[i];
				layer.PositionElement(thumb, CalcPointThumbPosition(finalRect, i, thumb));
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < (polyline.IsClosed ? polyline.Points.Count : (polyline.Points.Count - 1)); j++)
			{
				Thumb thumb2 = midThumbs[j];
				layer.PositionElement(thumb2, CalcMidThumbPosition(finalRect, j, thumb2));
			}
		}

		private Rect GetFinalRect()
		{
			return RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(polyline.Points);
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetPoint(int pointIndex)
		{
			if (pointIndex == -1)
			{
				pointIndex = polyline.Points.Count - 1;
			}
			else if (pointIndex == polyline.Points.Count)
			{
				pointIndex = 0;
			}
			return polyline.Points[pointIndex];
		}

		private System.Windows.Point CalcPointThumbCenter(Rect finalRect, int pointIndex, Thumb thumb)
		{
			return AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(CorrectPoint(GetPoint(pointIndex), finalRect.TopLeft));
		}

		private System.Windows.Point CalcMidThumbCenter(Rect finalRect, int pointIndex, Thumb thumb)
		{
			return AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(CorrectPoint(GetMidPoint(GetPoint(pointIndex), GetPoint(pointIndex + 1)), finalRect.TopLeft));
		}

		private System.Windows.Point CalcPointThumbPosition(Rect finalRect, int pointIndex, Thumb thumb)
		{
			return CalcPointThumbCenter(finalRect, pointIndex, thumb) - new Vector(thumb.Width / 2.0, thumb.Height / 2.0);
		}

		private System.Windows.Point CalcMidThumbPosition(Rect finalRect, int pointIndex, Thumb thumb)
		{
			return CalcMidThumbCenter(finalRect, pointIndex, thumb) - new Vector(thumb.Width / 2.0, thumb.Height / 2.0);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point CorrectPoint(System.Windows.Point targetPoint, System.Windows.Point originalPoint)
		{
			Vector vector = targetPoint - originalPoint;
			return new System.Windows.Point(vector.X, vector.Y);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetMidPoint(System.Windows.Point point1, System.Windows.Point point2)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Point((point1.X + point2.X) / 2.0, (point1.Y + point2.Y) / 2.0);
		}

		private Thumb CreatePointThumb()
		{
			Thumb thumb = ThumbHelper.CreateRegularDiamondThumb();
			thumb.Cursor = DiagramCursors.GrabHand;
			thumb.DragStarted += pointThumb_DragStarted;
			thumb.DragDelta += pointThumb_DragDelta;
			thumb.DragCompleted += pointThumb_DragCompleted;
			return thumb;
		}

		private Thumb CreateMidThumb()
		{
			Thumb thumb = ThumbHelper.CreateRegularDiamondThumb();
			thumb.Cursor = DiagramCursors.GrabHand;
			thumb.DragStarted += midThumb_DragStarted;
			thumb.DragDelta += midThumb_DragDelta;
			thumb.DragCompleted += midThumb_DragCompleted;
			return thumb;
		}

		private Line CreateLine(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
		{
			return new Line
			{
				X1 = startPoint.X,
				Y1 = startPoint.Y,
				X2 = endPoint.X,
				Y2 = endPoint.Y,
				Stroke = polyline.Stroke,
				StrokeThickness = polyline.StrokeThickness,
				StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 1.0, 1.0 }
			};
		}

		private void pointThumb_DragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			Thumb thumb = sender as Thumb;
			int num = pointThumbs.IndexOf(thumb);
			currentRect = GetFinalRect();
			oldPoint = CalcPointThumbCenter(currentRect, num, thumb);
			if (num != 0 || polyline.IsClosed)
			{
				System.Windows.Point startPoint = CalcPointThumbCenter(currentRect, num - 1, thumb);
				line1 = CreateLine(startPoint, oldPoint);
				base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(line1);
			}
			if (num != polyline.Points.Count - 1 || polyline.IsClosed)
			{
				System.Windows.Point endPoint = CalcPointThumbCenter(currentRect, num + 1, thumb);
				line2 = CreateLine(oldPoint, endPoint);
				base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(line2);
			}
		}

		private void pointThumb_DragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			Vector vector = new Vector(e.HorizontalChange, e.VerticalChange);
			System.Windows.Point point = oldPoint + vector;
			if (line1 != null)
			{
				line1.X2 = point.X;
				line1.Y2 = point.Y;
			}
			if (line2 != null)
			{
				line2.X1 = point.X;
				line2.Y1 = point.Y;
			}
		}

		private void pointThumb_DragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (line1 != null)
			{
				base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(line1);
			}
			if (line2 != null)
			{
				base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(line2);
			}
			line1 = null;
			line2 = null;
			Thumb item = sender as Thumb;
			int pointIndex = pointThumbs.IndexOf(item);
			Vector vector = new Vector(e.HorizontalChange, e.VerticalChange);
			double num = (0.0 - base.AdornedRotation) * Math.PI / 180.0;
			vector.X = Math.Cos(num) * vector.X - Math.Sin(num) * vector.Y;
			vector.Y = Math.Sin(num) * vector.X + Math.Cos(num) * vector.Y;
			System.Windows.Point oldPoint = this.oldPoint;
			System.Windows.Point newPoint = GetPoint(pointIndex) + vector;
			polyline.Points[pointIndex] = newPoint;
			polyline.InvalidateMeasure();
			polyline.InvalidateArrange();
			polyline.UpdateLayout();
			Rect finalRect = GetFinalRect();
			System.Windows.Point oldPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(polyline);
			System.Windows.Point newPosition = oldPosition + (finalRect.TopLeft - currentRect.TopLeft);
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(base.Editor.TransactionManager, polyline.IsClosed ? "Reshape polygon" : "Reshape polyline");
			base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, newPosition);
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					polyline.Points[pointIndex] = oldPoint;
					base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, oldPosition);
				}, delegate
				{
					polyline.Points[pointIndex] = newPoint;
					base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, newPosition);
				});
			}
		}

		private void midThumb_DragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			Thumb thumb = sender as Thumb;
			int num = midThumbs.IndexOf(thumb);
			currentRect = GetFinalRect();
			oldPoint = CalcMidThumbCenter(currentRect, num, thumb);
			System.Windows.Point startPoint = CalcPointThumbCenter(currentRect, num, thumb);
			line1 = CreateLine(startPoint, oldPoint);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(line1);
			System.Windows.Point endPoint = CalcPointThumbCenter(currentRect, num + 1, thumb);
			line2 = CreateLine(oldPoint, endPoint);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(line2);
		}

		private void midThumb_DragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			Vector vector = new Vector(e.HorizontalChange, e.VerticalChange);
			System.Windows.Point point = oldPoint + vector;
			line1.X2 = point.X;
			line1.Y2 = point.Y;
			line2.X1 = point.X;
			line2.Y1 = point.Y;
		}

		private void midThumb_DragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(line1);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(line2);
			line1 = null;
			line2 = null;
			Thumb item = sender as Thumb;
			int pointIndex = midThumbs.IndexOf(item);
			Vector vector = new Vector(e.HorizontalChange, e.VerticalChange);
			double num = (0.0 - base.AdornedRotation) * Math.PI / 180.0;
			vector.X = Math.Cos(num) * vector.X - Math.Sin(num) * vector.Y;
			vector.Y = Math.Sin(num) * vector.X + Math.Cos(num) * vector.Y;
			_ = oldPoint;
			System.Windows.Point newPoint = GetMidPoint(GetPoint(pointIndex), GetPoint(pointIndex + 1)) + vector;
			polyline.Points.Insert(pointIndex + 1, newPoint);
			polyline.InvalidateMeasure();
			polyline.InvalidateArrange();
			polyline.UpdateLayout();
			Thumb thumb = CreatePointThumb();
			pointThumbs.Insert(pointIndex + 1, thumb);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(thumb);
			Thumb thumb2 = CreateMidThumb();
			midThumbs.Insert(pointIndex + 1, thumb2);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(thumb2);
			Rect finalRect = GetFinalRect();
			System.Windows.Point oldPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(polyline);
			System.Windows.Point newPosition = oldPosition + (finalRect.TopLeft - currentRect.TopLeft);
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(base.Editor.TransactionManager, polyline.IsClosed ? "Reshape polygon" : "Reshape polyline");
			base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, newPosition);
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					polyline.Points.RemoveAt(pointIndex + 1);
					base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, oldPosition);
				}, delegate
				{
					polyline.Points.Insert(pointIndex + 1, newPoint);
					base.Editor.PositionElement(polyline, newPosition);
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class QuickToolbarOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private Expander expander;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Panel panel;

		private IQuickToolService toolService;

		private DiagramLayer layer;

		protected override bool CanDisplay => base.Editor.ShouldShowQuickTool(base.AdornedElement);

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				if (expander != null)
				{
					yield return expander;
				}
				if (panel != null)
				{
					yield return panel;
				}
			}
		}

		public QuickToolbarOverlay(ISelectionOverlayService overlayService, IQuickToolService toolService)
			: base(overlayService)
		{
			this.toolService = toolService;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point CalcExpanderHoverPoint(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			return AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth, 0.0 - layer.Diagram.GetElementHeight(expander)));
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			expander = new Expander
			{
				ExpandDirection = ExpandDirection.Right
			};
			expander.Expanded += expander_Expanded;
			expander.Collapsed += expander_Collapsed;
			layer.AddElement(expander);
			expander.UpdateLayout();
			layer.PositionElement(expander, CalcExpanderHoverPoint(layer));
			this.layer = layer;
		}

		private void expander_Expanded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (panel == null)
			{
				panel = toolService.QuickToolPanel;
				layer.AddElement(panel, Diagram.GetElementPosition(expander) + new Vector(expander.ActualWidth, 0.0));
			}
			panel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		private void expander_Collapsed(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			panel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			expander.Expanded -= expander_Expanded;
			expander.Collapsed -= expander_Collapsed;
			layer.RemoveElement(panel);
			layer.RemoveElement(expander);
			panel = null;
			expander = null;
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (expander != null)
			{
				layer.PositionElement(expander, CalcExpanderHoverPoint(layer));
			}
			if (panel != null)
			{
				layer.PositionElement(panel, Diagram.GetElementPosition(expander) + new Vector(expander.ActualWidth, 0.0));
			}
		}
	}
	public enum ResizingAdornerLocation
	{
		TopLeft,
		TopRight,
		BottomLeft,
		BottomRight,
		TopMiddle,
		BottomMiddle,
		LeftMiddle,
		RightMiddle
	}
	public class ResizerOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private Thumb _thumb;

		private bool _dragStarted;

		private UIElement _previewElement;

		private bool _horizontalFlipDone;

		private bool _verticalFlipDone;

		private System.Windows.Size oldSize;

		private FlipAxis oldFlipAxis;

		private double oldRotatedAngle;

		private System.Windows.Point oldPosition;

		protected RotateTransform RotateTransform { get; private set; }

		protected ScaleTransform ScaleTransform { get; private set; }

		protected override bool CanDisplay => base.Editor.CanResizeElement(base.AdornedElement);

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				if (_thumb != null)
				{
					yield return _thumb;
				}
			}
		}

		public Thumb ThumbControl
		{
			get
			{
				if (_thumb == null)
				{
					_thumb = CreateThumb();
					AddThumbEventHandlers();
				}
				return _thumb;
			}
		}

		public ResizingAdornerLocation Location { get; private set; }

		protected System.Windows.Input.Cursor ThumbCursor
		{
			get
			{
				_ = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeAll;
				System.Windows.Input.Cursor[] array = new System.Windows.Input.Cursor[8]
				{
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNWSE,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNS,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNESW,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeWE,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNWSE,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNS,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeNESW,
					System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeWE
				};
				List<ResizingAdornerLocation> list = new List<ResizingAdornerLocation>(new ResizingAdornerLocation[8]
				{
					ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft,
					ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle
				});
				double num = base.AdornedRotation % 360.0;
				if (num < 0.0)
				{
					num += 360.0;
				}
				int num2 = 0;
				if (num >= 22.5 && num < 67.5)
				{
					num2 = 1;
				}
				else if (num >= 67.5 && num < 112.5)
				{
					num2 = 2;
				}
				else if (num >= 112.5 && num < 157.5)
				{
					num2 = 3;
				}
				else if (num >= 157.5 && num < 202.5)
				{
					num2 = 4;
				}
				else if (num >= 202.5 && num < 247.5)
				{
					num2 = 5;
				}
				else if (num >= 237.5 && num < 292.5)
				{
					num2 = 6;
				}
				else if (num >= 292.5 && num < 337.5)
				{
					num2 = 7;
				}
				int num3 = list.IndexOf(Location) + num2;
				num3 %= 8;
				return array[num3];
			}
		}

		protected Visibility ThumbVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				Visibility result = Visibility.Visible;
				switch (Location)
				{
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft:
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight:
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft:
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight:
					result = CornerAdornerVisibility;
					break;
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle:
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle:
					result = TopAndBottomAdornerVisibility;
					break;
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle:
				case ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle:
					result = LeftAndRightAdornerVisibility;
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		protected System.Windows.Point ThumbTopLeft
		{
			get
			{
				RotateTransform.Angle = base.AdornedRotation;
				return base.Editor.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(base.AdornedElement, GetLocationPoint(base.AdornedElement)) - new Vector(ThumbControl.Width / 2.0, ThumbControl.Height / 2.0);
			}
		}

		protected Visibility CornerAdornerVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dragStarted)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (base.AdornedElement == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				Diagram editor = base.Editor;
				if (editor == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (editor.ZoomScale * base.AdornedWidth * 3.0 <= editor.MinElementWidthToHideAdorner || editor.ZoomScale * base.AdornedHeight * 3.0 <= editor.MinElementHeightToHideAdorner)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		protected Visibility TopAndBottomAdornerVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dragStarted)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (base.AdornedElement == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				Diagram editor = base.Editor;
				if (editor == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (editor.ZoomScale * base.AdornedWidth <= editor.MinElementWidthToHideAdorner)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		protected Visibility LeftAndRightAdornerVisibility
		{
			get
			{
				if (_dragStarted)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				if (base.AdornedElement == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				Diagram editor = base.Editor;
				if (editor == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (editor.ZoomScale * base.AdornedHeight <= editor.MinElementHeightToHideAdorner)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}

		protected double ScaleFactor
		{
			get
			{
				double num = 1.0 / base.Editor.ZoomScale;
				double num2 = 0.4;
				double num3 = 2.5;
				if (num < num2)
				{
					num = num2;
				}
				else if (num > num3)
				{
					num = num3;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public ResizerOverlay(ISelectionOverlayService service, ResizingAdornerLocation location)
			: base(service)
		{
			Location = location;
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			Thumb thumbControl = ThumbControl;
			if (thumbControl != null)
			{
				thumbControl.Cursor = ThumbCursor;
				thumbControl.Visibility = ThumbVisibility;
				layer.AddElement(thumbControl, ThumbTopLeft);
			}
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_thumb != null)
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_thumb);
				RemoveThumbEventHandlers();
			}
			_thumb = null;
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			Thumb thumbControl = ThumbControl;
			if (thumbControl != null)
			{
				RotateTransform.Angle = base.AdornedRotation;
				thumbControl.Cursor = ThumbCursor;
				thumbControl.Visibility = ThumbVisibility;
				layer.PositionElement(thumbControl, ThumbTopLeft);
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetLocationPoint(UIElement element)
		{
			double elementWidth = base.Editor.GetElementWidth(element);
			double elementHeight = base.Editor.GetElementHeight(element);
			return Location switch
			{
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth / 2.0, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth / 2.0, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, elementHeight / 2.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, elementHeight / 2.0), 
				_ => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), 
			};
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetLocationOppositePoint(UIElement element)
		{
			double elementWidth = base.Editor.GetElementWidth(element);
			double elementHeight = base.Editor.GetElementHeight(element);
			return Location switch
			{
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth / 2.0, 0.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth / 2.0, elementHeight), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, elementHeight / 2.0), 
				ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle => new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth, elementHeight / 2.0), 
				_ => new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0), 
			};
		}

		protected Thumb CreateThumb()
		{
			Thumb thumb = null;
			switch (Location)
			{
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight:
				thumb = ThumbHelper.CreateRegularEllipseThumb();
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle:
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle:
				thumb = ThumbHelper.CreateRegularRectangleThumb();
				break;
			}
			thumb.Focusable = true;
			thumb.IsHitTestVisible = true;
			thumb.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			RotateTransform = new RotateTransform();
			ScaleTransform = new ScaleTransform();
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(RotateTransform);
			transformGroup.Children.Add(ScaleTransform);
			thumb.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			return thumb;
		}

		private void Thumb_KeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape && _dragStarted)
			{
				FinishDragging();
			}
		}

		private void Thumb_MouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.LeftButton == MouseButtonState.Pressed)
			{
				double viewportMargin = 10.0;
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = base.Editor.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				base.Editor.ScrollPointIntoView(mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates, viewportMargin);
			}
		}

		private void Thumb_DragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			_dragStarted = true;
			_horizontalFlipDone = false;
			_verticalFlipDone = false;
			oldSize = base.Editor.GetElementSize(base.AdornedElement);
			oldFlipAxis = base.Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(base.AdornedElement);
			oldRotatedAngle = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(base.AdornedElement);
			oldPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(base.AdornedElement);
			_previewElement = ImagingHelper.CreatePreviewElement(base.AdornedElement, addBorder: false);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_previewElement, base.AdornedTopLeft);
		}

		private void Thumb_DragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_previewElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			UIElement previewElement = _previewElement;
			base.Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(previewElement);
			double elementRotation = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(previewElement);
			Vector dragVector = new Vector(e.HorizontalChange, e.VerticalChange);
			if (!Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl))
			{
				dragVector = SnapDragToGrid(dragVector);
			}
			switch (Location)
			{
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft:
				dragVector.X *= -1.0;
				dragVector.Y *= -1.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight:
				dragVector.Y *= -1.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft:
				dragVector.X *= -1.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle:
				dragVector.X = 0.0;
				dragVector.Y *= -1.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle:
				dragVector.X *= -1.0;
				dragVector.Y = 0.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle:
				dragVector.X = 0.0;
				break;
			case ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle:
				dragVector.Y = 0.0;
				break;
			}
			if ((base.AdornedFlipAxis & FlipAxis.Horizontal) == FlipAxis.Horizontal)
			{
				dragVector.X *= -1.0;
			}
			if ((base.AdornedFlipAxis & FlipAxis.Vertical) == FlipAxis.Vertical)
			{
				dragVector.Y *= -1.0;
			}
			System.Windows.Point point = base.Editor.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(previewElement, GetLocationOppositePoint(previewElement));
			double adornedWidth = base.AdornedWidth;
			double adornedHeight = base.AdornedHeight;
			double num = adornedWidth;
			double num2 = adornedHeight;
			double width = ThumbControl.DesiredSize.Width;
			if (adornedWidth + dragVector.X < 0.0 - width)
			{
				if (!_horizontalFlipDone)
				{
					base.Editor.FlipElement(previewElement, FlipAxis.Horizontal);
					base.Editor.RotateElement(previewElement, elementRotation * 2.0);
					_horizontalFlipDone = !_horizontalFlipDone;
				}
				num = 0.0 - dragVector.X - adornedWidth;
			}
			else if (adornedWidth + dragVector.X < width)
			{
				num = width;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_horizontalFlipDone)
				{
					base.Editor.FlipElement(previewElement, FlipAxis.Horizontal);
					base.Editor.RotateElement(previewElement, elementRotation * 2.0);
					_horizontalFlipDone = !_horizontalFlipDone;
				}
				num = adornedWidth + dragVector.X;
			}
			if (adornedHeight + dragVector.Y < 0.0 - width)
			{
				if (!_verticalFlipDone)
				{
					base.Editor.FlipElement(previewElement, FlipAxis.Vertical);
					base.Editor.RotateElement(previewElement, elementRotation * 2.0);
					_verticalFlipDone = !_verticalFlipDone;
				}
				num2 = 0.0 - dragVector.Y - adornedHeight;
			}
			else if (adornedHeight + dragVector.Y < width)
			{
				num2 = width;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_verticalFlipDone)
				{
					base.Editor.FlipElement(previewElement, FlipAxis.Vertical);
					base.Editor.RotateElement(previewElement, elementRotation * 2.0);
					_verticalFlipDone = !_verticalFlipDone;
				}
				num2 = adornedHeight + dragVector.Y;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(base.Editor.TransactionManager, "Resize", 257);
			try
			{
				base.Editor.SetElementWidth(previewElement, num);
				base.Editor.SetElementHeight(previewElement, num2);
				Vector vector = base.Editor.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(previewElement, GetLocationOppositePoint(previewElement)) - point;
				if (!GeometryHelper.IsRoughlyZero(vector))
				{
					base.Editor.PositionElement(previewElement, Diagram.GetElementPosition(previewElement) - vector);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
		}

		private void Thumb_DragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_previewElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Size elementSize = base.Editor.GetElementSize(_previewElement);
			FlipAxis elementFlipAxis = base.Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(_previewElement);
			double elementRotation = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(_previewElement);
			System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(_previewElement);
			if (oldSize == elementSize && oldFlipAxis == elementFlipAxis && oldRotatedAngle == elementRotation && oldPosition == elementPosition)
			{
				FinishDragging();
				return;
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(base.Editor.TransactionManager, "Resize");
			try
			{
				double elementRotation2 = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(base.AdornedElement);
				FlipAxis flipAxis = base.Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(_previewElement) ^ base.Editor.GetElementFlipAxis(base.AdornedElement);
				double angle = 0.0;
				switch (flipAxis)
				{
				case FlipAxis.Horizontal:
					angle = 2.0 * elementRotation2;
					break;
				case FlipAxis.Vertical:
					angle = 2.0 * elementRotation2;
					break;
				}
				base.Editor.SetElementSize(base.AdornedElement, base.Editor.GetElementSize(_previewElement));
				base.Editor.FlipElement(base.AdornedElement, flipAxis);
				base.Editor.RotateElement(base.AdornedElement, angle);
				base.Editor.PositionElement(base.AdornedElement, Diagram.GetElementPosition(_previewElement));
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
				throw;
			}
			finally
			{
				FinishDragging();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void AddThumbEventHandlers()
		{
			Thumb thumbControl = ThumbControl;
			if (thumbControl != null)
			{
				thumbControl.KeyUp += Thumb_KeyUp;
				thumbControl.MouseMove += Thumb_MouseMove;
				thumbControl.DragStarted += Thumb_DragStarted;
				thumbControl.DragDelta += Thumb_DragDelta;
				thumbControl.DragCompleted += Thumb_DragCompleted;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void RemoveThumbEventHandlers()
		{
			Thumb thumbControl = ThumbControl;
			if (thumbControl != null)
			{
				thumbControl.KeyUp -= Thumb_KeyUp;
				thumbControl.MouseMove -= Thumb_MouseMove;
				thumbControl.DragStarted -= Thumb_DragStarted;
				thumbControl.DragDelta -= Thumb_DragDelta;
				thumbControl.DragCompleted -= Thumb_DragCompleted;
			}
		}

		protected Vector SnapDragToGrid(Vector dragVector)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = base.Editor.ElementPointToDiagramCoordinates(base.AdornedElement, GetLocationPoint(base.AdornedElement));
			return base.Editor.SnapPointToGrid(point + dragVector) - point;
		}

		private void FinishDragging()
		{
			if (_dragStarted)
			{
				_dragStarted = false;
				if (_previewElement != null)
				{
					base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_previewElement);
					_previewElement = null;
					Diagram editor = base.Editor;
					editor.Unselect(base.AdornedElement);
					editor.Select(base.AdornedElement);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class RotationOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private class RotationCenterShape : Shape
		{
			private PathGeometry _geometry;

			private static readonly System.Windows.Size Size = new System.Windows.Size(10.0, 10.0);

			protected override Geometry DefiningGeometry
			{
				get
				{
					if (_geometry == null)
					{
						ConstructGeometry();
					}
					return _geometry;
				}
			}

			private void ConstructGeometry()
			{
				if (base.Stroke == null)
				{
					base.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green;
				}
				PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure.IsClosed = false;
				pathFigure.IsFilled = false;
				pathFigure.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, (0.0 - Size.Height) / 2.0);
				pathFigure.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, Size.Height / 2.0), isStroked: true));
				PathFigure pathFigure2 = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure2.IsClosed = false;
				pathFigure2.IsFilled = false;
				pathFigure2.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point((0.0 - Size.Width) / 2.0, 0.0);
				pathFigure2.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(Size.Width / 2.0, 0.0), isStroked: true));
				PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure2);
				_geometry = pathGeometry;
			}
		}

		private const double StickingAngle = 45.0;

		private const double StickingAngleTolerance = 5.0;

		private Thumb _rotateThumb;

		private Line _rotateBar;

		private RotationCenterShape _rotateCenter;

		private RotateTransform _rcRotateTransform;

		private UIElement _previewElement;

		private double _rotateAngle;

		protected override bool CanDisplay => base.Editor.CanRotateElement(base.AdornedElement);

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				if (_rotateThumb != null)
				{
					yield return _rotateThumb;
				}
				if (_rotateBar != null)
				{
					yield return _rotateBar;
				}
			}
		}

		public RotationOverlay(ISelectionOverlayService service)
			: base(service)
		{
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_rotateThumb == null)
			{
				_rotateThumb = ThumbHelper.CreateRegularRotateThumb();
				_rotateThumb.Cursor = DiagramCursors.RotateInit;
				_rotateThumb.IsHitTestVisible = true;
				_rotateThumb.Focusable = true;
				_rotateThumb.Width = 10.0;
				_rotateThumb.Height = 10.0;
				_rotateBar = new Line();
				_rotateBar.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.DarkSlateGray;
				_rotateBar.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				System.Windows.Point point = AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth / 2.0, 0.0));
				System.Windows.Point point2 = AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth / 2.0, -20.0));
				_rotateBar.X1 = 0.0;
				_rotateBar.Y1 = 0.0;
				_rotateBar.X2 = point.X - point2.X;
				_rotateBar.Y2 = point.Y - point2.Y;
				layer.AddElement(_rotateBar, point2);
				layer.AddElement(_rotateThumb, point2 - new Vector(_rotateThumb.Width / 2.0, _rotateThumb.Height / 2.0));
				_rotateThumb.KeyUp += _rotateThumb_KeyUp;
				_rotateThumb.DragStarted += RotateThumb_DragStarted;
				_rotateThumb.DragDelta += RotateThumb_DragDelta;
				_rotateThumb.DragCompleted += RotateThumb_DragCompleted;
				SetVisibility();
			}
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_rotateThumb != null)
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_rotateThumb);
				_rotateThumb.KeyUp -= _rotateThumb_KeyUp;
				_rotateThumb.DragStarted -= RotateThumb_DragStarted;
				_rotateThumb.DragDelta -= RotateThumb_DragDelta;
				_rotateThumb.DragCompleted -= RotateThumb_DragCompleted;
				_rotateThumb = null;
			}
			if (_rotateBar != null)
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_rotateBar);
				_rotateBar = null;
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (_rotateThumb != null)
			{
				System.Windows.Point point = AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth / 2.0, 0.0));
				System.Windows.Point point2 = AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth / 2.0, -20.0));
				_rotateBar.X1 = 0.0;
				_rotateBar.Y1 = 0.0;
				_rotateBar.X2 = point.X - point2.X;
				_rotateBar.Y2 = point.Y - point2.Y;
				layer.PositionElement(_rotateBar, point2);
				layer.PositionElement(_rotateThumb, point2 - new Vector(_rotateThumb.Width / 2.0, _rotateThumb.Height / 2.0));
				SetVisibility();
			}
		}

		private void _rotateThumb_KeyUp(object sender, System.Windows.Input.KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				FinishDragging();
			}
		}

		private void RotateThumb_DragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
			_previewElement = ImagingHelper.CreatePreviewElement(base.AdornedElement, addBorder: true);
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_previewElement, base.AdornedTopLeft);
			_rotateAngle = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(base.AdornedElement);
			_rotateThumb.Cursor = DiagramCursors.Rotating;
			_rcRotateTransform = new RotateTransform
			{
				Angle = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(base.AdornedElement)
			};
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_rcRotateTransform);
			_rotateCenter = new RotationCenterShape
			{
				RenderTransform = transformGroup
			};
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_rotateCenter, AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(base.AdornedWidth / 2.0, base.AdornedHeight / 2.0)));
		}

		private void RotateThumb_DragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_previewElement == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			double deltaDraggedAngle = GetDeltaDraggedAngle(base.Editor, GetRotationCenter(), 0.0, base.AdornedFlipAxis);
			deltaDraggedAngle = FixAngle(deltaDraggedAngle);
			using (TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(base.Editor.TransactionManager, "Rotating", 257))
			{
				try
				{
					base.Editor.RotateElement(_previewElement, deltaDraggedAngle - base.Editor.GetElementRotation(_previewElement));
				}
				catch
				{
					transactionContext.AbortTransaction();
					throw;
				}
			}
			_rcRotateTransform.Angle = base.Editor.GetElementRotation(_previewElement);
		}

		private void RotateThumb_DragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_previewElement != null)
			{
				if (!GeometryHelper.IsRoughlyZero(base.Editor.GetElementRotation(_previewElement) - _rotateAngle))
				{
					base.Editor.RotateElement(base.AdornedElement, base.Editor.GetElementRotation(_previewElement) - _rotateAngle);
				}
				FinishDragging();
			}
		}

		private double FixAngle(double angle)
		{
			angle = (angle % 360.0 + 360.0) % 360.0;
			double num = angle % 45.0;
			if (num <= 5.0)
			{
				return angle - num;
			}
			if (45.0 - num <= 5.0)
			{
				return angle + 45.0 - num;
			}
			return angle;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetRotationCenter()
		{
			System.Windows.Point elementRotationCenter = base.Editor.GetElementRotationCenter(base.AdornedElement);
			elementRotationCenter.X *= base.AdornedWidth;
			elementRotationCenter.Y *= base.AdornedHeight;
			return AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(elementRotationCenter);
		}

		private void FinishDragging()
		{
			_rotateThumb.Cursor = DiagramCursors.RotateInit;
			base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_rotateCenter);
			if (_previewElement != null)
			{
				base.Editor.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_previewElement);
				_previewElement = null;
				Diagram editor = base.Editor;
				editor.Unselect(base.AdornedElement);
				editor.Select(base.AdornedElement);
			}
		}

		private void SetVisibility()
		{
			Visibility visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			if (base.Editor.ZoomScale * base.AdornedWidth > base.Editor.MinElementWidthToHideAdorner / 2.0)
			{
				visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
			foreach (UIElement adorner in Adorners)
			{
				adorner.Visibility = visibility;
			}
		}

		public static double GetDeltaDraggedAngle(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point rotatedCenter, double currentAngle, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			return GetDeltaDraggedAngle(System.Windows.Point.Subtract(diagram.PrimaryLayer.GetMousePositionInLayerCoordinates(), rotatedCenter), currentAngle, flipAxis);
		}

		private static double GetDeltaDraggedAngle(Vector panelProjection, double currentAngle, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			num = ((panelProjection.X == 0.0) ? ((double)((panelProjection.Y > 0.0) ? 90 : 270)) : ((!(panelProjection.X > 0.0)) ? (Math.Atan(panelProjection.Y / panelProjection.X) * 180.0 / Math.PI + 180.0) : (Math.Atan(panelProjection.Y / panelProjection.X) * 180.0 / Math.PI)));
			if (flipAxis != FlipAxis.Vertical && flipAxis != FlipAxis.Both)
			{
				return (num + 90.0 - currentAngle) % 360.0;
			}
			return (num - 90.0 - currentAngle) % 360.0;
		}
	}
	public class TextEditorOverlay : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private System.Windows.Controls.TextBox editBox;

		private Text text;

		protected override bool CanDisplay => !text.IsReadOnly;

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				yield return editBox;
			}
		}

		public TextEditorOverlay(Text text)
			: base(text)
		{
			this.text = text;
			editBox = null;
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.TextBox CreateEditBox()
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.TextBox textBox = new System.Windows.Controls.TextBox();
			textBox.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
			textBox.Padding = new Thickness(0.0);
			textBox.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontFamilyProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("FontFamily")
			{
				Source = text
			});
			textBox.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.Control.FontSizeProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("FontSize")
			{
				Source = text
			});
			textBox.SetBinding(System.Windows.Controls.TextBox.TextProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Text")
			{
				Source = text,
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay,
				UpdateSourceTrigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged
			});
			textBox.SetBinding(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("RenderTransform")
			{
				Source = text
			});
			return textBox;
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			editBox = CreateEditBox();
			layer.AddElement(editBox, AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0)));
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			layer.RemoveElement(editBox);
			editBox = null;
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			layer.PositionElement(editBox, AdornedPointToDiagramCoordinates(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0)));
			if (text.IsReadOnly)
			{
				editBox.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			else
			{
				editBox.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.Helpers
{
	internal sealed class ClipRegionHelper
	{
		public static double ExtentBoundToSelectElement = 5.0;

		private ClipRegionHelper()
		{
		}

		public static Rect GetElementActualRegion(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(element);
			if (parentDiagram != null)
			{
				return parentDiagram.GetElementTranformedRect(element);
			}
			Rect elementRegion = GetElementRegion(element);
			if (elementRegion.IsEmpty)
			{
				return elementRegion;
			}
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			pointCollection.Add(elementRegion.TopLeft);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRegion.TopRight);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRegion.BottomRight);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRegion.BottomLeft);
			return RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(RegionHelper.GetActualPointCollection(element, pointCollection, elementRegion));
		}

		public static Rect GetElementRegion(UIElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			double x = (double.IsNaN(DiagramLayer.GetLeft(element)) ? 0.0 : DiagramLayer.GetLeft(element));
			double y = (double.IsNaN(DiagramLayer.GetTop(element)) ? 0.0 : DiagramLayer.GetTop(element));
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (element is FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
			{
				num = (double.IsNaN(frameworkElement.Width) ? frameworkElement.ActualWidth : frameworkElement.Width);
				num2 = (double.IsNaN(frameworkElement.Height) ? frameworkElement.ActualHeight : frameworkElement.Height);
			}
			if ((double.IsNaN(num) || double.IsInfinity(num) || num == 0.0 || double.IsNaN(num2) || double.IsInfinity(num2) || num2 == 0.0) && !element.DesiredSize.IsEmpty && element.DesiredSize != new System.Windows.Size(0.0, 0.0))
			{
				num = element.DesiredSize.Width;
				num2 = element.DesiredSize.Height;
			}
			if (x.Equals(double.NaN) || y.Equals(double.NaN) || num.Equals(double.NaN) || num2.Equals(double.NaN))
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			return new Rect(x, y, num, num2);
		}

		public static bool IsPointInElementRegion(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point point, UIElement element)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("canvas");
			}
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			if (Diagram.GetElementService(element, typeof(IHitTestService)) is IHitTestService hitTestService)
			{
				return hitTestService.IsPointInElementRegion(point);
			}
			System.Windows.Point point2 = diagram.PrimaryLayer.TransformToDescendant(element).Transform(point - new Vector(diagram.PrimaryLayer.HorizontalOffset, diagram.PrimaryLayer.VerticalOffset));
			GraphicsPath graphicsPath = CreateElementGraphicsPath(element);
			if (ExtentBoundToSelectElement <= 0.0)
			{
				return graphicsPath.IsVisible((float)point2.X, (float)point2.Y);
			}
			RectangleF rect = new RectangleF((float)(point2.X - ExtentBoundToSelectElement), (float)(point2.Y - ExtentBoundToSelectElement), (float)(2.0 * ExtentBoundToSelectElement), (float)(2.0 * ExtentBoundToSelectElement));
			return new Region(graphicsPath).IsVisible(rect);
		}

		private static GraphicsPath CreateElementGraphicsPath(UIElement element)
		{
			GraphicsPath areaPath = new GraphicsPath();
			if (element.GetType() == typeof(Ellipse))
			{
				Ellipse ellipse = element as Ellipse;
				areaPath.AddEllipse(0f, 0f, (float)ellipse.Width, (float)ellipse.Height);
			}
			else if (element.GetType() == typeof(Polygon))
			{
				BuildGraphicsPathWithPoints((element as Polygon).Points, ref areaPath);
			}
			else if (element.GetType() == typeof(Polyline))
			{
				BuildGraphicsPathWithPoints((element as Polyline).Points, ref areaPath);
			}
			else
			{
				Rect elementRegion = GetElementRegion(element);
				areaPath.AddRectangle(new RectangleF(0f, 0f, (float)elementRegion.Width, (float)elementRegion.Height));
			}
			areaPath.CloseFigure();
			return areaPath;
		}

		private static void BuildGraphicsPathWithPoints(PointCollection points, ref GraphicsPath areaPath)
		{
			if (points == null || points.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 3;
			if (points.Count >= 3)
			{
				num = points.Count;
			}
			PointF[] array = new PointF[num];
			for (int i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
			{
				array[i] = new PointF((float)points[i].X, (float)points[i].Y);
			}
			if (points.Count < 3)
			{
				for (int j = points.Count; j < 3; j++)
				{
					array[j] = new PointF((float)points[0].X, (float)points[0].Y);
				}
			}
			areaPath.AddPolygon(array);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetActualCenterPoint(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			Rect elementActualRegion = GetElementActualRegion(element);
			return new System.Windows.Point(elementActualRegion.Left + elementActualRegion.Width / 2.0 - DiagramLayer.GetLeft(element), elementActualRegion.Top + elementActualRegion.Height / 2.0 - DiagramLayer.GetTop(element));
		}
	}
	public enum GeometryOrientation
	{
		Indeterminate,
		Horizontal,
		Vertical
	}
	internal static class GeometryHelper
	{
		private const double algorithmPrecision = 1E-10;

		public static bool IsInsideRect(System.Windows.Point point, Rect rect)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			if (rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The Rect cannot be Empty.", "rect");
			}
			double x = point.X;
			double y = point.Y;
			double x2 = rect.TopLeft.X;
			double y2 = rect.TopLeft.Y;
			double x3 = rect.BottomRight.X;
			double y3 = rect.BottomRight.Y;
			if (x > x2 && x < x3 && y > y2)
			{
				return y < y3;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsOnRect(System.Windows.Point point, Rect rect)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			if (rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The Rect cannot be Empty.", "rect");
			}
			if (IsPointOnLineSegment(rect.TopLeft, rect.TopRight, point))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsPointOnLineSegment(rect.TopLeft, rect.BottomLeft, point))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsPointOnLineSegment(rect.TopRight, rect.BottomRight, point))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsPointOnLineSegment(rect.BottomLeft, rect.BottomRight, point))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsOutsideRect(System.Windows.Point point, Rect rect)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			if (rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The Rect cannot be Empty.", "rect");
			}
			double x = point.X;
			double y = point.Y;
			double left = rect.Left;
			double top = rect.Top;
			double right = rect.Right;
			double bottom = rect.Bottom;
			if (x < left || x > right || y < top || y > bottom)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point ClosestPointOnLineFromPoint(Line line, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			if (line == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("line");
			}
			return ClosestPointOnLineFromPoint(line.X1, line.Y1, line.X2, line.Y2, point.X, point.Y);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point ClosestPointOnLineFromPoint(System.Windows.Point sp, System.Windows.Point ep, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(sp.X) || double.IsNaN(sp.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "sp");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(ep.X) || double.IsNaN(ep.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "ep");
			}
			return ClosestPointOnLineFromPoint(sp.X, sp.Y, ep.X, ep.Y, point.X, point.Y);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point ClosestPointOnLineFromPoint(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, double px, double py)
		{
			double num = x2 - x1;
			double num2 = y2 - y1;
			double num3 = px - x1;
			double num4 = py - y1;
			double num5 = num * num3 + num2 * num4;
			double y3;
			if (num5 <= 0.0)
			{
				y3 = y1;
				return new System.Windows.Point(x1, y3);
			}
			double num6 = num * num + num2 * num2;
			if (num6 <= num5)
			{
				y3 = y2;
				return new System.Windows.Point(x2, y3);
			}
			double num7 = num5 / num6;
			double x3 = x1 + num7 * num;
			y3 = y1 + num7 * num2;
			return new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point ClosestPointOnRectangleFromPoint(Rect rect, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The Rect cannot be Empty.", "rect");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "point");
			}
			return ClosestPointOnRectangleFromPoint(rect.Left, rect.Top, rect.Right, rect.Bottom, point.X, point.Y);
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point ClosestPointOnRectangleFromPoint(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, double px, double py)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(x1))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "x1");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(y1))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "y1");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(x2))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "x2");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(y2))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "y2");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(px))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "px");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(py))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The value cannot be NaN.", "py");
			}
			double num = Math.Min(Math.Abs(px - x1), Math.Abs(px - x2));
			double num2 = Math.Min(Math.Abs(py - y1), Math.Abs(py - y2));
			double num3 = ((Math.Abs(px - x1) < Math.Abs(px - x2)) ? x1 : x2);
			double num4 = ((Math.Abs(py - y1) < Math.Abs(py - y2)) ? y1 : y2);
			double x3 = ((num < num2) ? num3 : px);
			double y3 = ((num < num2) ? py : num4);
			return new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point? IntersectSegments(System.Windows.Point segment1P, System.Windows.Point segment1Q, System.Windows.Point segment2P, System.Windows.Point segment2Q)
		{
			double num = segment1P.X;
			double num2 = segment1P.Y;
			double num3 = segment1Q.X;
			double num4 = segment1Q.Y;
			double num5 = segment2P.X;
			double num6 = segment2P.Y;
			double num7 = segment2Q.X;
			double num8 = segment2Q.Y;
			if (num > num3)
			{
				double num9 = num;
				num = num3;
				num3 = num9;
			}
			if (num2 > num4)
			{
				double num10 = num2;
				num2 = num4;
				num4 = num10;
			}
			if (num5 > num7)
			{
				double num11 = num5;
				num5 = num7;
				num7 = num11;
			}
			if (num6 > num8)
			{
				double num12 = num6;
				num6 = num8;
				num8 = num12;
			}
			GeometryOrientation geometryOrientation = GeometryOrientation.Indeterminate;
			bool flag = Math.Abs(num3 - num) <= 1E-10;
			bool flag2 = Math.Abs(num4 - num2) <= 1E-10;
			if (flag && !flag2)
			{
				geometryOrientation = GeometryOrientation.Vertical;
			}
			else if (flag2 && !flag)
			{
				geometryOrientation = GeometryOrientation.Horizontal;
			}
			GeometryOrientation geometryOrientation2 = GeometryOrientation.Indeterminate;
			bool flag3 = Math.Abs(num7 - num5) <= 1E-10;
			bool flag4 = Math.Abs(num8 - num6) <= 1E-10;
			if (flag3 && !flag4)
			{
				geometryOrientation2 = GeometryOrientation.Vertical;
			}
			else if (flag4 && !flag3)
			{
				geometryOrientation2 = GeometryOrientation.Horizontal;
			}
			System.Windows.Point? result = null;
			if (geometryOrientation == GeometryOrientation.Vertical && geometryOrientation2 == GeometryOrientation.Horizontal)
			{
				if (num >= num5 - 1E-10 && num <= num7 + 1E-10 && num6 >= num2 - 1E-10 && num6 <= num4 + 1E-10)
				{
					result = new System.Windows.Point(num, num6);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (geometryOrientation != GeometryOrientation.Horizontal || geometryOrientation2 != GeometryOrientation.Vertical)
				{
					return IntersectTwoSegmentsOnPoint(segment1P, segment1Q, segment2P, segment2Q);
				}
				if (num5 >= num - 1E-10 && num5 <= num3 + 1E-10 && num2 >= num6 - 1E-10 && num2 <= num8 + 1E-10)
				{
					result = new System.Windows.Point(num5, num2);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point? IntersectTwoSegmentsOnPoint(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c, System.Windows.Point d)
		{
			System.Windows.Point? result = null;
			if (!IsIntersectedInterior(a, b, c, d))
			{
				return result;
			}
			double num = a.X * (d.Y - c.Y) + b.X * (c.Y - d.Y) + d.X * (b.Y - a.Y) + c.X * (a.Y - b.Y);
			if (!IsRoughlyZero(num))
			{
				double num2 = (a.X * (d.Y - c.Y) + c.X * (a.Y - d.Y) + d.X * (c.Y - a.Y)) / num;
				double num3 = (0.0 - (a.X * (c.Y - b.Y) + b.X * (a.Y - c.Y) + c.X * (b.Y - a.Y))) / num;
				if (num2 >= -1E-10 && num2 <= 1.0000000001 && num3 >= -1E-10 && num3 <= 1.0000000001)
				{
					result = new System.Windows.Point(a.X + num2 * (b.X - a.X), a.Y + num2 * (b.Y - a.Y));
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal static PointCollection IntersectionsPolyLineRay(System.Windows.Point startPoint, PolyLineSegment polyline, Line ray)
		{
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			System.Windows.Point a = startPoint;
			foreach (System.Windows.Point point2 in polyline.Points)
			{
				System.Windows.Point? point = IntersectTwoSegmentsOnPoint(a, point2, new System.Windows.Point(ray.X1, ray.Y1), new System.Windows.Point(ray.X2, ray.Y2));
				a = point2;
				if (point.HasValue)
				{
					pointCollection.Add(point.Value);
				}
			}
			return pointCollection;
		}

		internal static bool IsIntersected(Line line, Rect rect)
		{
			if (line == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("line");
			}
			if (rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				return false;
			}
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(line.X1, line.Y1);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point(line.X2, line.Y2);
			if ((!IsInsideRect(point, rect) || !IsInsideRect(point2, rect)) && !IsIntersectedInterior(point, point2, rect.TopLeft, rect.TopRight) && !IsIntersectedInterior(point, point2, rect.TopRight, rect.BottomRight) && !IsIntersectedInterior(point, point2, rect.BottomRight, rect.BottomLeft))
			{
				return IsIntersectedInterior(point, point2, rect.BottomLeft, rect.TopLeft);
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool IsIntersectedInterior(Line segment1, Line segment2)
		{
			if (segment1 == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("segment1");
			}
			if (segment2 == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("segment2");
			}
			return IsIntersectedInterior(new System.Windows.Point(segment1.X1, segment1.Y1), new System.Windows.Point(segment1.X2, segment1.Y2), new System.Windows.Point(segment2.X1, segment2.Y1), new System.Windows.Point(segment2.X2, segment2.Y2));
		}

		internal static bool IsIntersectedInterior(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c, System.Windows.Point d)
		{
			if ((IsLeft(a, b, c) ^ IsLeft(a, b, d)) || (IsCollinear(a, b, c) ^ IsCollinear(a, b, d)))
			{
				if (!(IsLeft(c, d, a) ^ IsLeft(c, d, b)))
				{
					return IsCollinear(c, d, a) ^ IsCollinear(c, d, b);
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static double ManhattanDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Abs(p.X - q.X) + Math.Abs(p.Y - q.Y);
		}

		public static double EuclideanDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(p.X - q.X, 2.0) + Math.Pow(p.Y - q.Y, 2.0));
		}

		public static double HorizontalDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Abs(p.X - q.X);
		}

		public static double VerticalDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Abs(p.Y - q.Y);
		}

		public static double MaxDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Max(Math.Abs(p.X - q.X), Math.Abs(p.Y - q.Y));
		}

		public static double MinDistance(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(p.X) || double.IsNaN(p.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "p");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(q.X) || double.IsNaN(q.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "q");
			}
			return Math.Min(Math.Abs(p.X - q.X), Math.Abs(p.Y - q.Y));
		}

		public static bool IsLeft(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(a.X) || double.IsNaN(a.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "a");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(b.X) || double.IsNaN(b.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "b");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(c.X) || double.IsNaN(c.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "c");
			}
			return Area2(a, b, c) > 0.0;
		}

		public static bool IsLeftOn(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(a.X) || double.IsNaN(a.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "a");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(b.X) || double.IsNaN(b.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "b");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(c.X) || double.IsNaN(c.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "c");
			}
			return Area2(a, b, c) >= 0.0;
		}

		public static bool IsCollinear(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(a.X) || double.IsNaN(a.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "a");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(b.X) || double.IsNaN(b.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "b");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(c.X) || double.IsNaN(c.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "c");
			}
			return Area2(a, b, c) == 0.0;
		}

		public static bool IsPointOnLineSegment(System.Windows.Point from, System.Windows.Point to, System.Windows.Point point)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(from.X) || double.IsNaN(from.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "a");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(to.X) || double.IsNaN(to.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "b");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(point.X) || double.IsNaN(point.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "c");
			}
			if (Area2(from, to, point) == 0.0 && point.X <= Math.Max(from.X, to.X) && point.X >= Math.Min(from.X, to.X) && point.Y <= Math.Max(from.Y, to.Y) && point.Y >= Math.Min(from.Y, to.Y))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static double Area2(System.Windows.Point a, System.Windows.Point b, System.Windows.Point c)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(a.X) || double.IsNaN(a.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "a");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(b.X) || double.IsNaN(b.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "b");
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(c.X) || double.IsNaN(c.Y))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The coordinates of the Point cannot be NaN.", "c");
			}
			return (b.X - a.X) * (a.Y - c.Y) - (c.X - a.X) * (a.Y - b.Y);
		}

		public static PointCollection FlattenPathData(PointCollection collection)
		{
			try
			{
				if (collection == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException("collection");
				}
				if (collection.Count <= 3)
				{
					return collection;
				}
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				pointCollection.Add(collection[0]);
				for (int i = 1; i < collection.Count - 1; i++)
				{
					System.Windows.Point point = collection[i - 1];
					System.Windows.Point value = collection[i];
					System.Windows.Point point2 = collection[i + 1];
					if ((AreRoughlyEqual(value.X, point2.X) && !AreRoughlyEqual(value.Y, point2.Y) && AreRoughlyEqual(value.Y, point.Y) && !AreRoughlyEqual(value.X, point.X)) || (AreRoughlyEqual(value.Y, point2.Y) && !AreRoughlyEqual(value.X, point2.X) && AreRoughlyEqual(value.X, point.X) && !AreRoughlyEqual(value.Y, point.Y)))
					{
						pointCollection.Add(value);
					}
				}
				pointCollection.Add(collection[collection.Count - 1]);
				return pointCollection;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}

		public static GeometryOrientation CalculateOrientation(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			GeometryOrientation result = GeometryOrientation.Indeterminate;
			bool flag = AreRoughlyEqual(p.X, q.X);
			bool flag2 = AreRoughlyEqual(p.Y, q.Y);
			if (flag && !flag2)
			{
				result = GeometryOrientation.Vertical;
			}
			else if (flag2 && !flag)
			{
				result = GeometryOrientation.Horizontal;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool AreRoughlyEqual(double number, double target)
		{
			double num = number - target;
			if (double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				if (!double.IsNaN(number) || !double.IsNaN(target))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (num >= -1E-10)
			{
				return num <= 1E-10;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool AreRoughlyEqual(System.Windows.Point p, System.Windows.Point q)
		{
			if (AreRoughlyEqual(p.X, q.X))
			{
				return AreRoughlyEqual(p.Y, q.Y);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsRoughlyZero(double value)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(value))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (value >= -1E-10)
			{
				return value <= 1E-10;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsRoughlyZero(Vector value)
		{
			if (IsRoughlyZero(value.X))
			{
				return IsRoughlyZero(value.Y);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsRoughlyBetween(double value, double boundA, double boundB)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsNaN(boundA) || double.IsNaN(boundB))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(boundA < boundB))
			{
				if (value >= boundB - 1E-10)
				{
					return value <= boundA + 1E-10;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (value >= boundA - 1E-10)
			{
				return value <= boundB + 1E-10;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static Rect GetSquareRectFromCenter(double pointX, double pointY, double dbSideLength)
		{
			if (dbSideLength < 0.0)
			{
				dbSideLength = Math.Abs(dbSideLength);
			}
			return new Rect(pointX - dbSideLength, pointY - dbSideLength, dbSideLength * 2.0, dbSideLength * 2.0);
		}

		public static Rect GetSquareRectFromCenter(System.Windows.Point pointCenter, double dbSideLength)
		{
			return GetSquareRectFromCenter(pointCenter.X, pointCenter.Y, dbSideLength);
		}

		public static Rect GetRectFromPoints(IEnumerable<System.Windows.Point> pointCollection)
		{
			if (pointCollection == null)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			double num = double.MaxValue;
			double num2 = double.MinValue;
			double num3 = double.MaxValue;
			double num4 = double.MinValue;
			foreach (System.Windows.Point item in pointCollection)
			{
				num = ((item.X <= num) ? item.X : num);
				num3 = ((item.Y <= num3) ? item.Y : num3);
				num2 = ((item.X >= num2) ? item.X : num2);
				num4 = ((item.Y >= num4) ? item.Y : num4);
			}
			if (num2 >= num && num4 >= num3)
			{
				return new Rect(num, num3, num2 - num, num4 - num3);
			}
			return Rect.Empty;
		}

		public static PointCollection TransformPoints(PointCollection collection, Rect oldRect, Rect newRect)
		{
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection(collection);
			for (int i = 0; i < pointCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point value = default(System.Windows.Point);
				if (oldRect.Width != 0.0)
				{
					value.X = (pointCollection[i].X - oldRect.X) / oldRect.Width * newRect.Width + newRect.X;
				}
				if (oldRect.Height != 0.0)
				{
					value.Y = (pointCollection[i].Y - oldRect.Y) / oldRect.Height * newRect.Height + newRect.Y;
				}
				pointCollection[i] = value;
			}
			return pointCollection;
		}
