		public static double AngleBetweenPoints(System.Windows.Point first, System.Windows.Point second)
		{
			double num = first.X - second.X;
			double num2 = first.Y - second.Y;
			if (num == 0.0 && num2 == 0.0)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				if (num2 > 0.0)
				{
					return 90.0;
				}
				return 180.0;
			}
			if (num2 == 0.0)
			{
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				return 180.0;
			}
			if (!(num > 0.0))
			{
				return 180.0 + Math.Atan(num2 / num) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
			}
			return Math.Atan(num2 / num) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
		}
	}
	internal static class ImagingHelper
	{
		private static readonly Thickness ImageMargin = new Thickness(10.0);

		private const double DefaultDpi = 96.0;

		internal static System.Windows.Controls.Image GetImageFromDiagram(Diagram diagram, ImagePrintOption printOption, System.Windows.Media.Brush background, double zoomScale)
		{
			if (diagram == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("diagram");
			}
			System.Windows.Controls.Image image = null;
			switch (printOption)
			{
			case ImagePrintOption.Selection:
			{
				Canvas canvas2 = CreateVisualCanvasFrom(diagram.Selection);
				image = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = GetBitmapSourceFrom(canvas2, background, zoomScale, ImageMargin),
					Width = GetScaledWidth(canvas2.Width, zoomScale),
					Height = GetScaledHeight(canvas2.Height, zoomScale)
				};
				break;
			}
			case ImagePrintOption.All:
			{
				Canvas canvas = CreateVisualCanvasFrom(diagram.DiagramElements.Cast<UIElement>());
				image = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = GetBitmapSourceFrom(canvas, background, zoomScale, ImageMargin),
					Width = GetScaledWidth(canvas.Width, zoomScale),
					Height = GetScaledHeight(canvas.Height, zoomScale)
				};
				break;
			}
			case ImagePrintOption.Viewport:
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
				{
					Width = diagram.PrimaryLayer.HorizontalViewport,
					Height = diagram.PrimaryLayer.VerticalViewport,
					Fill = new VisualBrush(diagram.PrimaryLayer)
					{
						Stretch = Stretch.Uniform
					}
				};
				image = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
				{
					Source = GetBitmapSourceFrom(rectangle, background, zoomScale, ImageMargin),
					Width = GetScaledWidth(rectangle.Width, zoomScale),
					Height = GetScaledHeight(rectangle.Width, zoomScale)
				};
				break;
			}
			}
			if (image != null)
			{
				image.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(image.Width, image.Height));
				image.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Size(image.Width, image.Height)));
			}
			return image;
		}

		internal static RenderTargetBitmap GetBitmapSourceFrom(IEnumerable<UIElement> elements, System.Windows.Media.Brush background, double zoomScale)
		{
			if (elements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("elements");
			}
			return GetBitmapSourceFrom(CreateVisualCanvasFrom(elements), background, zoomScale, ImageMargin);
		}

		internal static Canvas CreateVisualCanvasFrom(IEnumerable<UIElement> elements)
		{
			if (elements == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("elements");
			}
			List<UIElement> list = elements.Where((UIElement e) => e.Visibility == Visibility.Visible).ToList();
			double num = double.MaxValue;
			double num2 = double.MaxValue;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			Dictionary<UIElement, Rect> dictionary = new Dictionary<UIElement, Rect>();
			foreach (UIElement item in list)
			{
				Rect elementActualRegion = ClipRegionHelper.GetElementActualRegion(item);
				dictionary.Add(item, elementActualRegion);
				num = Math.Min(num, elementActualRegion.Left);
				num2 = Math.Min(num2, elementActualRegion.Top);
			}
			foreach (Rect value in dictionary.Values)
			{
				num3 = Math.Max(num3, value.Right - num);
				num4 = Math.Max(num4, value.Bottom - num2);
			}
			Canvas canvas = new Canvas
			{
				Width = num3,
				Height = num4
			};
			foreach (UIElement item2 in list)
			{
				Rect rect = dictionary[item2];
				UIElement imagingElement = GetImagingElement(item2, rect.Size);
				Canvas.SetLeft(imagingElement, rect.Left - num);
				Canvas.SetTop(imagingElement, rect.Top - num2);
				canvas.Children.Add(imagingElement);
			}
			return canvas;
		}

		internal static UIElement CreatePreviewElement(UIElement element, bool addBorder)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = element as FrameworkElement;
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement2 = null;
			try
			{
				frameworkElement2 = XamlReader.Parse(XamlWriter.Save(frameworkElement)) as FrameworkElement;
			}
			catch (XamlParseException)
			{
				Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(frameworkElement);
				System.Windows.Media.Brush background = ((parentDiagram == null) ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White : parentDiagram.Background);
				double zoomScale = parentDiagram?.ZoomScale ?? 1.0;
				Transform renderTransform = frameworkElement.RenderTransform;
				frameworkElement.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity;
				RenderTargetBitmap bitmapSourceFrom = GetBitmapSourceFrom(frameworkElement, background, zoomScale, new Thickness(0.0));
				frameworkElement.RenderTransform = renderTransform;
				frameworkElement2 = new Viewbox
				{
					Child = new System.Windows.Controls.Image
					{
						Source = bitmapSourceFrom
					}
				};
			}
			if (addBorder)
			{
				Grid result = new Grid
				{
					Opacity = 0.5,
					Width = frameworkElement.Width,
					Height = frameworkElement.Height,
					RenderTransform = frameworkElement.RenderTransform,
					RenderTransformOrigin = frameworkElement.RenderTransformOrigin,
					Children = 
					{
						(UIElement)new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
						{
							Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.DarkSlateGray,
							StrokeThickness = 1.0,
							StrokeDashArray = new DoubleCollection { 4.0, 4.0 }
						},
						(UIElement)frameworkElement2
					}
				};
				frameworkElement2.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty);
				frameworkElement2.ClearValue(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty);
				frameworkElement2.ClearValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty);
				return result;
			}
			frameworkElement2.Opacity = 0.5;
			frameworkElement2.Width = frameworkElement.Width;
			frameworkElement2.Height = frameworkElement.Height;
			frameworkElement2.RenderTransform = frameworkElement.RenderTransform;
			frameworkElement2.RenderTransformOrigin = frameworkElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
			return frameworkElement2;
		}

		private static RenderTargetBitmap GetBitmapSourceFrom(FrameworkElement element, System.Windows.Media.Brush background, double zoomScale, Thickness margin)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("element");
			}
			Viewbox viewbox = new Viewbox
			{
				Width = element.Width * zoomScale,
				Height = element.Height * zoomScale,
				Child = element
			};
			Border border = new Border
			{
				Background = background,
				Width = viewbox.Width + margin.Left + margin.Right,
				Height = viewbox.Height + margin.Top + margin.Bottom,
				Child = viewbox
			};
			border.Measure(new System.Windows.Size(border.Width, border.Height));
			border.Arrange(new Rect(new System.Windows.Size(border.Width, border.Height)));
			RenderTargetBitmap renderTargetBitmap = new RenderTargetBitmap((int)border.ActualWidth, (int)border.ActualHeight, 96.0, 96.0, PixelFormats.Default);
			renderTargetBitmap.Render(border);
			return renderTargetBitmap;
		}

		private static double GetScaledWidth(double width, double zoomScale)
		{
			return width * zoomScale + ImageMargin.Left + ImageMargin.Right;
		}

		private static double GetScaledHeight(double height, double zoomScale)
		{
			return height * zoomScale + ImageMargin.Top + ImageMargin.Bottom;
		}

		private static UIElement GetImagingElement(UIElement element, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle element2 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle
			{
				Width = size.Width * 3.0,
				Height = size.Height * 3.0,
				Fill = new VisualBrush(element)
				{
					Stretch = Stretch.None
				}
			};
			Canvas.SetLeft(element2, 0.0 - size.Width);
			Canvas.SetTop(element2, 0.0 - size.Height);
			Canvas obj = new Canvas
			{
				Width = size.Width,
				Height = size.Height
			};
			System.Windows.Controls.Panel.SetZIndex(obj, System.Windows.Controls.Panel.GetZIndex(element));
			obj.Children.Add(element2);
			return obj;
		}
	}
	internal sealed class RegionHelper
	{
		private RegionHelper()
		{
		}

		public static Rect GetElementActualRegion(FrameworkElement element, Rect elementRect)
		{
			if (elementRect.Equals(Rect.Empty))
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			pointCollection.Add(elementRect.TopLeft);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRect.TopRight);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRect.BottomRight);
			pointCollection.Add(elementRect.BottomLeft);
			return GetRectFromPoints(GetActualPointCollection(element, pointCollection, elementRect));
		}

		public static Rect GetRectFromPoints(PointCollection pointCollection)
		{
			if (pointCollection == null)
			{
				return Rect.Empty;
			}
			double num = double.MaxValue;
			double num2 = double.MinValue;
			double num3 = double.MaxValue;
			double num4 = double.MinValue;
			foreach (System.Windows.Point item in pointCollection)
			{
				num = ((item.X <= num) ? item.X : num);
				num3 = ((item.Y <= num3) ? item.Y : num3);
				num2 = ((item.X >= num2) ? item.X : num2);
				num4 = ((item.Y >= num4) ? item.Y : num4);
			}
			return new Rect(num, num3, Math.Abs(num2 - num), Math.Abs(num4 - num3));
		}

		public static PointCollection GetActualPointCollection(UIElement element, PointCollection points, Rect elementRect)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (points == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			System.Windows.Point coordinatePoint = new System.Windows.Point(DiagramLayer.GetLeft(element) + element.RenderTransformOrigin.X * elementRect.Width, DiagramLayer.GetTop(element) + element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y * elementRect.Height);
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
			foreach (System.Windows.Point point in points)
			{
				pointCollection.Add(GetActualPoint(element, point, coordinatePoint));
			}
			return pointCollection;
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetActualPoint(UIElement element, System.Windows.Point point, System.Windows.Point coordinatePoint)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return point;
			}
			if (element.RenderTransform.Equals(null))
			{
				return point;
			}
			if (coordinatePoint.X.Equals(double.NaN) || coordinatePoint.Y.Equals(double.NaN))
			{
				return point;
			}
			System.Windows.Point point2 = element.RenderTransform.Transform(new System.Windows.Point(point.X - coordinatePoint.X, point.Y - coordinatePoint.Y));
			return new System.Windows.Point(point2.X + coordinatePoint.X, point2.Y + coordinatePoint.Y);
		}

		public static double GetDistanceFromTwoPoints(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
		{
			return Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(endPoint.X - startPoint.X, 2.0) + Math.Pow(endPoint.Y - startPoint.Y, 2.0));
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetMiddlePoint(System.Windows.Point startPoint, System.Windows.Point endPoint)
		{
			return new System.Windows.Point((startPoint.X + endPoint.X) / 2.0, (startPoint.Y + endPoint.Y) / 2.0);
		}

		public static double GetDistancePointToLine(System.Windows.Point point, System.Windows.Point linePoint1, System.Windows.Point linePoint2)
		{
			double distanceFromTwoPoints = GetDistanceFromTwoPoints(point, linePoint1);
			double distanceFromTwoPoints2 = GetDistanceFromTwoPoints(point, linePoint2);
			double distanceFromTwoPoints3 = GetDistanceFromTwoPoints(linePoint1, linePoint2);
			double num = (distanceFromTwoPoints + distanceFromTwoPoints2 + distanceFromTwoPoints3) / 2.0;
			return Math.Sqrt(num * (num - distanceFromTwoPoints) * (num - distanceFromTwoPoints2) * (num - distanceFromTwoPoints3)) * 2.0 / distanceFromTwoPoints3;
		}

		public static bool GetPointDirectionToLine(System.Windows.Point point, System.Windows.Point linePoint1, System.Windows.Point linePoint2)
		{
			double num = linePoint2.Y - linePoint1.Y;
			double num2 = linePoint1.X - linePoint2.X;
			double num3 = linePoint2.X * linePoint1.Y - linePoint1.X * linePoint2.Y;
			return num * point.X + num2 * point.Y + num3 >= 0.0;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Size GetValidMeasureSize(System.Windows.Size availableSize)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(availableSize.Width) || double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width) || double.IsNaN(availableSize.Height) || double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height))
			{
				return new System.Windows.Size(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			return availableSize;
		}
	}
	internal static class ThumbHelper
	{
		private class HalfDiamondConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection();
				if (value == null || value == DependencyProperty.UnsetValue)
				{
					return pointCollection;
				}
				double num = (double)value;
				pointCollection.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new System.Windows.Point(num, 0.0));
				pointCollection.Add(new System.Windows.Point(num, num));
				return pointCollection;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		private static double _defaultThumbWidth = 10.0;

		private static readonly ControlTemplate _ellipseThumbTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Thumb))
		{
			VisualTree = CreateEllipseThumbFactory()
		};

		private static readonly ControlTemplate _rectangleThumbTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Thumb))
		{
			VisualTree = CreateRectangleThumbFactory()
		};

		private static readonly ControlTemplate _diamondThumbTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Thumb))
		{
			VisualTree = CreateDiamondThumbFactory()
		};

		private static readonly ControlTemplate _pinNodeThumbTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Thumb))
		{
			VisualTree = CreatePinNodeThumbFactory()
		};

		internal static readonly ControlTemplate _transparentThumbTemplate = new ControlTemplate(typeof(Thumb))
		{
			VisualTree = CreateTransparentThumbFactory()
		};

		public static Thumb CreateRegularEllipseThumb()
		{
			return CreateEllipseThumb(_defaultThumbWidth, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue);
		}

		public static Thumb CreateRegularRectangleThumb()
		{
			return CreateRectangleThumb(_defaultThumbWidth * 0.8, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue);
		}

		public static Thumb CreateRegularRotateThumb()
		{
			return CreateEllipseThumb(_defaultThumbWidth, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGreen);
		}

		public static Thumb CreateRegularDiamondThumb()
		{
			return CreateDiamondThumb(_defaultThumbWidth * 0.8, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Yellow);
		}

		public static Thumb CreateHalfDiamondThumb(double angle)
		{
			return CreateHalfDiamondThumb(_defaultThumbWidth, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Yellow, angle);
		}

		public static Thumb CreateRegularPinNodeThumb()
		{
			return CreatePinNodeThumb(_defaultThumbWidth * 1.0, _defaultThumbWidth * 1.0, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGreen);
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreateEllipseThumbFactory()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Ellipse));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop
			{
				Offset = 0.5
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Background.Color")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Thumb), 1)
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new System.Windows.Media.LinearGradientBrush
			{
				StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.0),
				EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 1.0),
				GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection
				{
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 0.0),
					gradientStop,
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 1.0)
				}
			}, dp: Shape.FillProperty);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreateRectangleThumbFactory()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop
			{
				Offset = 0.5
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Background.Color")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Thumb), 1)
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new System.Windows.Media.LinearGradientBrush
			{
				StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.0),
				EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 1.0),
				GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection
				{
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 0.0),
					gradientStop,
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 1.0)
				}
			}, dp: Shape.FillProperty);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreateDiamondThumbFactory()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty, new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty, new RotateTransform(45.0));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop
			{
				Offset = 0.5
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Background.Color")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Thumb), 1)
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new System.Windows.Media.LinearGradientBrush
			{
				StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0),
				EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 1.0),
				GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection
				{
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 0.0),
					gradientStop,
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 1.0)
				}
			}, dp: Shape.FillProperty);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreateHalfDiamondThumbFactory(double angle)
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Polygon));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.RenderTransformOriginProperty, new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(UIElement.RenderTransformProperty, new RotateTransform(45.0 + angle));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new TemplateBindingExtension
			{
				Property = FrameworkElement.WidthProperty,
				Converter = new HalfDiamondConverter()
			}, dp: Polygon.PointsProperty);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop
			{
				Offset = 0.5
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Background.Color")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Thumb), 1)
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new System.Windows.Media.LinearGradientBrush
			{
				StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0),
				EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 1.0),
				GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection
				{
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 0.0),
					gradientStop,
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 1.0)
				}
			}, dp: Shape.FillProperty);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreateTransparentThumbFactory()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Ellipse));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.FillProperty, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}

		private static FrameworkElementFactory CreatePinNodeThumbFactory()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(Ellipse));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeThicknessProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderThicknessProperty));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(Shape.StrokeProperty, new TemplateBindingExtension(System.Windows.Controls.Control.BorderBrushProperty));
			GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop
			{
				Offset = 0.5
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Background.Color")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Thumb), 1)
			});
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(value: new System.Windows.Media.LinearGradientBrush
			{
				StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.0),
				EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 1.0),
				GradientStops = new GradientStopCollection
				{
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 0.0),
					gradientStop,
					new GradientStop(Colors.White, 1.0)
				}
			}, dp: Shape.FillProperty);
			return frameworkElementFactory;
		}
	}
	internal static class TransformHelper
	{
		public static TransformGroup GetElementRenderTransformGroupClone(FrameworkElement childElement)
		{
			if (childElement.RenderTransform == null || childElement.RenderTransform.Equals(Transform.Identity))
			{
				return new TransformGroup();
			}
			if (!(childElement.RenderTransform is TransformGroup))
			{
				return new TransformGroup
				{
					Children = { childElement.RenderTransform.Clone() }
				};
			}
			return childElement.RenderTransform.Clone() as TransformGroup;
		}

		public static TransformGroup GetAbsoluteRenderTransform(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return new TransformGroup();
			}
			System.Windows.Point absoluteOriginPoint = GetAbsoluteOriginPoint(element);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			TransformGroup elementRenderTransformGroupClone = GetElementRenderTransformGroupClone(element);
			if (GetTransform(elementRenderTransformGroupClone, typeof(RotateTransform)) is RotateTransform rotateTransform)
			{
				rotateTransform.CenterX = absoluteOriginPoint.X;
				rotateTransform.CenterY = absoluteOriginPoint.Y;
				transformGroup.Children.Add(rotateTransform);
			}
			if (GetTransform(elementRenderTransformGroupClone, typeof(ScaleTransform)) is ScaleTransform scaleTransform)
			{
				scaleTransform.CenterX = absoluteOriginPoint.X;
				scaleTransform.CenterY = absoluteOriginPoint.Y;
				transformGroup.Children.Add(scaleTransform);
			}
			return transformGroup;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetAbsoluteOriginPoint(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			if (element == null)
			{
				return default(System.Windows.Point);
			}
			System.Windows.Point result = default(System.Windows.Point);
			result.X = GetTopLeft(element).X + GetOriginPoint(element).X;
			result.Y = GetTopLeft(element).Y + GetOriginPoint(element).Y;
			result.X = (double.IsNaN(result.X) ? 0.0 : result.X);
			result.Y = (double.IsNaN(result.Y) ? 0.0 : result.Y);
			return result;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetTopLeft(UIElement frameworkElement)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				num = DiagramLayer.GetLeft(frameworkElement);
				num2 = DiagramLayer.GetTop(frameworkElement);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(num) && !double.IsNaN(num2))
			{
				return new System.Windows.Point(num, num2);
			}
			return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point GetOriginPoint(FrameworkElement frameworkElement)
		{
			if (frameworkElement != null)
			{
				Diagram parentDiagram = Diagram.GetParentDiagram(frameworkElement);
				if (parentDiagram != null)
				{
					System.Windows.Point elementRotationCenter = parentDiagram.GetElementRotationCenter(frameworkElement);
					double elementWidth = parentDiagram.GetElementWidth(frameworkElement);
					double elementHeight = parentDiagram.GetElementHeight(frameworkElement);
					return new System.Windows.Point(elementWidth * elementRotationCenter.X, elementHeight * elementRotationCenter.Y);
				}
				System.Windows.Point renderTransformOrigin = frameworkElement.RenderTransformOrigin;
				double num = frameworkElement.ActualWidth;
				double num2 = frameworkElement.ActualHeight;
				if (num == 0.0 || num2 == 0.0)
				{
					num = frameworkElement.Width;
					num2 = frameworkElement.Height;
				}
				return new System.Windows.Point(num * renderTransformOrigin.X, num2 * renderTransformOrigin.Y);
			}
			return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public static TransformGroup GetElementLayoutTransformGroupClone(FrameworkElement childElement)
		{
			if (childElement.LayoutTransform == null || childElement.LayoutTransform.Equals(Transform.Identity))
			{
				return new TransformGroup();
			}
			if (!(childElement.LayoutTransform is TransformGroup))
			{
				return new TransformGroup
				{
					Children = { childElement.LayoutTransform.Clone() }
				};
			}
			return childElement.LayoutTransform.Clone() as TransformGroup;
		}

		public static TransformGroup GetElementRenderTransform(FrameworkElement element)
		{
			TransformGroup result = new TransformGroup();
			if (element.RenderTransform == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (!(element.RenderTransform is TransformGroup transformGroup))
			{
				return result;
			}
			result = transformGroup.Clone();
			foreach (Transform child in result.Children)
			{
				if (child is RotateTransform { CenterX: 0.0, CenterY: 0.0 } rotateTransform && (element.RenderTransformOrigin.X != 0.0 || element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y != 0.0))
				{
					rotateTransform.CenterX = element.RenderTransformOrigin.X * element.ActualWidth;
					rotateTransform.CenterY = element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y * element.ActualHeight;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static TransformGroup GetInitializedTransformGroup(double CenterX, double CenterY)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			RotateTransform value = new RotateTransform
			{
				Angle = 0.0,
				CenterX = CenterX,
				CenterY = CenterY
			};
			transformGroup.Children.Add(value);
			ScaleTransform value2 = new ScaleTransform
			{
				ScaleX = 1.0,
				ScaleY = 1.0,
				CenterX = CenterX,
				CenterY = CenterY
			};
			transformGroup.Children.Add(value2);
			return transformGroup;
		}

		public static System.Windows.Point RotatePoint(System.Windows.Point targetPoint, System.Windows.Point originPoint, double angle)
		{
			return new RotateTransform(angle, originPoint.X, originPoint.Y).Transform(targetPoint);
		}

		public static Rect GetRectAccordingDefaultCoordinate(System.Windows.Point topLeft, System.Windows.Point topRight, System.Windows.Point bottomRight, System.Windows.Point bottomLeft, System.Windows.Point centerPoint, double angle)
		{
			PointCollection pointCollection = new PointCollection(4);
			pointCollection.Add(topLeft);
			pointCollection.Add(topRight);
			pointCollection.Add(bottomRight);
			pointCollection.Add(bottomLeft);
			pointCollection[0] = RotatePoint(pointCollection[0], centerPoint, angle);
			pointCollection[1] = RotatePoint(pointCollection[1], centerPoint, angle);
			pointCollection[2] = RotatePoint(pointCollection[2], centerPoint, angle);
			pointCollection[3] = RotatePoint(pointCollection[3], centerPoint, angle);
			return RegionHelper.GetRectFromPoints(pointCollection);
		}

		public static Transform GetTransform(TransformGroup transformGroup, Type type)
		{
			if (transformGroup == null)
			{
				return Transform.Identity;
			}
			foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
			{
				if (child.GetType() == type)
				{
					return child;
				}
			}
			return Transform.Identity;
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.ElementServices
{
	internal static class BuiltinElementServices
	{
		public static object GetServiceForElement(UIElement element, Type serviceType)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (serviceType == typeof(ISelectionOverlayService))
			{
				result = GetSelectionOverlayServiceForElement(element);
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IContextMenuService))
			{
				result = GetContextMenuServiceForElement(element);
			}
			else if (serviceType == typeof(IModifyService))
			{
				result = GetModifyServiceForElement(element);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static IModifyService GetModifyServiceForElement(UIElement element)
		{
			return new DefaultModifyService(element);
		}

		public static ISelectionOverlayService GetSelectionOverlayServiceForElement(UIElement element)
		{
			return new BuiltinRegularAdornerService(element);
		}

		public static IContextMenuService GetContextMenuServiceForElement(UIElement element)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class BuiltinBorderService : ISelectionOverlayService
	{
		private UIElement uie;

		public UIElement AdornedElement => uie;

		public BuiltinBorderService(UIElement adornedElement)
		{
			uie = adornedElement;
		}

		public Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(AdornedElement));
		}

		public void AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			editor.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
		}
	}
	public class BuiltinRegularAdornerService : ISelectionOverlayService
	{
		private UIElement uie;

		public UIElement AdornedElement => uie;

		public BuiltinRegularAdornerService(UIElement adornedElement)
		{
			uie = adornedElement;
		}

		public Rect GetAdornedRect(Diagram editor)
		{
			return new Rect(editor.GetElementSize(AdornedElement));
		}

		public void AddAdorners(Diagram editor)
		{
			editor.AddAdorner(new BorderOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new RotationOverlay(this));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopLeft));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopRight));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomLeft));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomRight));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.TopMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.BottomMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.LeftMiddle));
			editor.AddAdorner(new ResizerOverlay(this, ResizingAdornerLocation.RightMiddle));
		}
	}
	public class DefaultFontToolService : IQuickToolService
	{
		private TextBlock text;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Control control;

		private System.Windows.Controls.Panel fontPanel;

		private System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox familySelector;

		private System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox sizeSelector;

		private System.Windows.Media.FontFamily FontFamily
		{
			get
			{
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text.FontFamily;
				}
				if (control != null)
				{
					return control.FontFamily;
				}
				return null;
			}
			set
			{
				if (text != null)
				{
					text.FontFamily = value;
				}
				else if (control != null)
				{
					control.FontFamily = value;
				}
			}
		}

		private double FontSize
		{
			get
			{
				if (text != null)
				{
					return text.FontSize;
				}
				if (control != null)
				{
					return control.FontSize;
				}
				return double.NaN;
			}
			set
			{
				if (text != null)
				{
					text.FontSize = value;
				}
				else if (control != null)
				{
					control.FontSize = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Controls.Panel QuickToolPanel
		{
			get
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.Panel result = FontPanel;
				FamilySelector.Text = FontFamily.Source;
				SizeSelector.Text = FontSize.ToString();
				return result;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.Panel FontPanel
		{
			get
			{
				if (fontPanel == null)
				{
					fontPanel = new StackPanel
					{
						Orientation = System.Windows.Controls.Orientation.Horizontal
					};
					fontPanel.Children.Add(FamilySelector);
					fontPanel.Children.Add(SizeSelector);
				}
				return fontPanel;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox FamilySelector
		{
			get
			{
				if (familySelector == null)
				{
					familySelector = new System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox
					{
						Width = 80.0,
						IsEditable = true,
						IsReadOnly = true
					};
					familySelector.DropDownOpened += familySelector_DropDownOpened;
					familySelector.SelectionChanged += familySelector_SelectionChanged;
				}
				return familySelector;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox SizeSelector
		{
			get
			{
				if (sizeSelector == null)
				{
					sizeSelector = new System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox
					{
						Width = 40.0,
						IsEditable = true,
						IsReadOnly = false
					};
					for (double num = 8.0; num <= 20.0; num += 2.0)
					{
						sizeSelector.Items.Add(num);
					}
					sizeSelector.SelectionChanged += fontSize_SelectionChanged;
					sizeSelector.LostFocus += fontSize_LostFocus;
				}
				return sizeSelector;
			}
		}

		public DefaultFontToolService(TextBlock text)
		{
			if (text == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("text");
			}
			this.text = text;
		}

		public DefaultFontToolService(System.Windows.Controls.Control control)
		{
			if (control == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("control");
			}
			this.control = control;
		}

		static DefaultFontToolService()
		{
			CreateFontFamilyItems();
		}

		private static IList<ComboBoxItem> CreateFontFamilyItems()
		{
			IList<ComboBoxItem> list = new List<ComboBoxItem>();
			foreach (System.Windows.Media.FontFamily systemFontFamily in Fonts.SystemFontFamilies)
			{
				ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem = new ComboBoxItem();
				comboBoxItem.Content = systemFontFamily.Source;
				comboBoxItem.FontFamily = systemFontFamily;
				list.Add(comboBoxItem);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void familySelector_DropDownOpened(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox comboBox = sender as System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox;
			foreach (System.Windows.Media.FontFamily systemFontFamily in Fonts.SystemFontFamilies)
			{
				ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem = new ComboBoxItem();
				comboBoxItem.Content = systemFontFamily.Source;
				comboBoxItem.FontFamily = systemFontFamily;
				comboBox.Items.Add(comboBoxItem);
			}
		}

		private void familySelector_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.AddedItems.Count != 0 && e.AddedItems[0] is ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem)
			{
				FontFamily = comboBoxItem.FontFamily;
			}
		}

		private void fontSize_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.AddedItems.Count != 0)
			{
				double fontSize = (double)e.AddedItems[0];
				FontSize = fontSize;
			}
		}

		private void fontSize_LostFocus(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox comboBox = sender as System.Windows.Controls.ComboBox;
			if (comboBox.Text.Length != 0)
			{
				if (double.TryParse(comboBox.Text, out var result))
				{
					FontSize = result;
				}
				else
				{
					comboBox.Text = FontSize.ToString();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class DefaultModifyService : IModifyService
	{
		private readonly UIElement _element;

		public DefaultModifyService(UIElement e)
		{
			_element = e;
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanMove(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanMove(_element);
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanResize(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanResize(_element);
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanRotate(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanRotate(_element);
		}

		bool IModifyService.CanFlip(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetCanFlip(_element);
		}

		bool IModifyService.ShouldShowQuickTool(Diagram diagram)
		{
			return Diagram.GetShouldShowQuickTool(_element);
		}

		void IModifyService.RemoveElement(Diagram diagram)
		{
			RemoveElement(diagram, _element);
		}

		void IModifyService.PositionElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			PositionElement(diagram, _element, newPosition);
		}

		void IModifyService.RotateElement(Diagram diagram, double angle)
		{
			RotateElement(diagram, _element, angle);
		}

		void IModifyService.FlipElement(Diagram diagram, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			FlipElement(diagram, _element, flipAxis);
		}

		void IModifyService.SizeElement(Diagram diagram, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			SizeElement(diagram, _element, size);
		}

		public static void PositionElement(Diagram diagram, UIElement element, System.Windows.Point newPosition)
		{
			System.Windows.Point oldPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(element);
			if (GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(oldPosition, newPosition))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent(element) is DiagramLayer diagramLayer)
			{
				diagramLayer.PositionElement(element, newPosition);
			}
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Position Element");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					diagram.PositionElement(element, oldPosition);
				}, delegate
				{
					diagram.PositionElement(element, newPosition);
				});
			}
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				diagram.RaiseElementModified(new ElementModifiedEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		public static void RemoveElement(Diagram diagram, UIElement element)
		{
			diagram.PrimaryLayer.RemoveElement(element);
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Remove Element");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					diagram.AddElementNoPreview(element);
				}, delegate
				{
					diagram.RemoveElementNoPreview(element);
				});
			}
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents)
			{
				diagram.RaiseElementRemoved(new ElementRemovedEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		public static void SizeElement(Diagram diagram, UIElement element, System.Windows.Size size)
		{
			System.Windows.Size oldSize = new System.Windows.Size(diagram.GetElementWidth(element), diagram.GetElementHeight(element));
			double height = size.Height;
			double width = size.Width;
			FrameworkElement obj = element as FrameworkElement;
			obj.Width = width;
			obj.Height = height;
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Size Element");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					diagram.SetElementSize(element, oldSize);
				}, delegate
				{
					diagram.SetElementSize(element, size);
				});
			}
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				diagram.RaiseElementModified(new ElementModifiedEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		public static void RotateElement(Diagram diagram, UIElement element, double angle)
		{
			if (angle == 0.0)
			{
				return;
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = diagram.GetTransformGroup(element).Clone();
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = GetRotateTransform(transformGroup);
			if (rotateTransform == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			double actionAngle = 0.0;
			FlipAxis elementFlipAxis = diagram.GetElementFlipAxis(element);
			if (elementFlipAxis == FlipAxis.Horizontal || elementFlipAxis == FlipAxis.Vertical)
			{
				actionAngle -= angle;
			}
			else
			{
				actionAngle += angle;
			}
			rotateTransform.Angle += actionAngle;
			element.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Rotate Element");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					diagram.RotateElement(element, 0.0 - actionAngle);
				}, delegate
				{
					diagram.RotateElement(element, actionAngle);
				});
			}
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				diagram.RaiseElementModified(new ElementModifiedEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		public static void FlipElement(Diagram diagram, UIElement element, FlipAxis flipAxis)
		{
			if (element.RenderTransformOrigin.X == 0.0 && element.RenderTransformOrigin.Y == 0.0)
			{
				element.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = diagram.GetTransformGroup(element).Clone();
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = GetScaleTransform(transformGroup);
			if (scaleTransform == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (flipAxis)
			{
			case FlipAxis.Horizontal:
				scaleTransform.ScaleX *= -1.0;
				break;
			case FlipAxis.Vertical:
				scaleTransform.ScaleY *= -1.0;
				break;
			case FlipAxis.Both:
				scaleTransform.ScaleX *= -1.0;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY *= -1.0;
				break;
			}
			element.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(diagram.TransactionManager, "Flip Element");
			if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				FlipAxis originalFlipAxis = diagram.GetElementFlipAxis(element);
				transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
				{
					diagram.FlipElement(element, originalFlipAxis);
				}, delegate
				{
					diagram.FlipElement(element, flipAxis);
				});
			}
			if (transactionContext.ShouldRaiseEvents && diagram.IsElementInPrimaryLayer(element))
			{
				diagram.RaiseElementModified(new ElementModifiedEventArgs(element));
			}
		}

		internal static RotateTransform GetRotateTransform(TransformGroup transformGroup)
		{
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = null;
			foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
			{
				rotateTransform = child as RotateTransform;
				if (rotateTransform != null)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return rotateTransform;
		}

		internal static ScaleTransform GetScaleTransform(TransformGroup transformGroup)
		{
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = null;
			foreach (Transform child in transformGroup.Children)
			{
				scaleTransform = child as ScaleTransform;
				if (scaleTransform != null)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			return scaleTransform;
		}
	}
	public interface IAnchorConsumer
	{
		UIElement Element { get; }

		IEnumerable<DiagramAnchor> CurrentAnchors { get; }

		bool ReconnectToAnchor(DiagramAnchor anchor);

		void AnchorPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor);

		void AnchorProjectionPointChanged(DiagramAnchor anchor);
	}
	public interface IAnchorProvider
	{
		UIElement Element { get; }

		IEnumerable<DiagramAnchor> Anchors { get; }

		DiagramAnchor FindAnchor(Guid anchorId);

	public class DefaultLabelPositionDesigner : Shape
	{
		private PathGeometry _geometry;

		private const double _boxHeight = 10.0;

		private const double _boxWidth = 10.0;

		public bool? ShowDesigner { get; set; }

		protected override Geometry DefiningGeometry
		{
			get
			{
				if (_geometry == null)
				{
					ConstructGeometry();
				}
				return _geometry;
			}
		}

		protected void ConstructGeometry()
		{
			bool? flag = ShowDesigner;
			if (!flag.HasValue)
			{
				flag = DesignerProperties.GetIsInDesignMode(this);
			}
			if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value)
			{
				PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
				pathGeometry.Figures.Add(pathFigure);
				_geometry = pathGeometry;
				return;
			}
			if (base.Stroke == null)
			{
				base.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green;
			}
			PathFigure pathFigure2 = new PathFigure();
			pathFigure2.IsClosed = true;
			pathFigure2.IsFilled = false;
			pathFigure2.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(-5.0, -5.0);
			PolyLineSegment polyLineSegment = new PolyLineSegment();
			polyLineSegment.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(5.0, -5.0));
			polyLineSegment.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(5.0, 5.0));
			polyLineSegment.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-5.0, 5.0));
			pathFigure2.Segments.Add(polyLineSegment);
			PathFigure pathFigure3 = new PathFigure();
			pathFigure3.IsClosed = false;
			pathFigure3.IsFilled = false;
			pathFigure3.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(-5.0, -5.0);
			pathFigure3.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(5.0, 5.0), isStroked: true));
			PathFigure pathFigure4 = new PathFigure();
			pathFigure4.IsClosed = false;
			pathFigure4.IsFilled = false;
			pathFigure4.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(-5.0, 5.0);
			pathFigure4.Segments.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(5.0, -5.0), isStroked: true));
			PathGeometry pathGeometry2 = new PathGeometry();
			pathGeometry2.Figures.Add(pathFigure2);
			pathGeometry2.Figures.Add(pathFigure3);
			pathGeometry2.Figures.Add(pathFigure4);
			_geometry = pathGeometry2;
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.BuiltInDrawings

	public static class BuiltInDrawingFactory
	{
		public const string DisplayNameLabelUri = "Diagram.DisplayNameLabel";

		public static UIElement ResolveDrawingUri(string drawingUri)
		{
			UIElement result = null;
			if (drawingUri == "Diagram.DisplayNameLabel")
			{
				result = new DisplayNameLabel();
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class DisplayNameLabel : System.Windows.Controls.UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private class StringToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				if (value.ToString() == string.Empty)
				{
					return Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		internal TextBlock tb;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public DisplayNameLabel()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			BindVisibility();
			base.OnInitialized(e);
		}

		private void BindVisibility()
		{
			PriorityBinding priorityBinding = new PriorityBinding
			{
				FallbackValue = Visibility.Hidden
			};
			priorityBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("DisplayName")
			{
				Converter = new StringToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			priorityBinding.Bindings.Add(new System.Windows.Data.Binding("Name")
			{
				Converter = new StringToVisibilityConverter()
			});
			SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, priorityBinding);
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing;component/diagrameditor/builtindrawings/displaynamelabel.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			if (connectionId == 1)
			{
				tb = (TextBlock)target;
			}
			else
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
			}
		}
	}

namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.DiagramEditor.Anchors
{
	public abstract class DiagramAnchor
	{
		private Guid _id;

		private double _projectionDistance;

		private double _projectionAngle;

		private IAnchorConsumer _consumer;

		public Guid Id
		{
			get
			{
				if (_id == Guid.Empty)
				{
					_id = Guid.NewGuid();
				}
				return _id;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_id == Guid.Empty)
				{
					_id = value;
					return;
				}
				throw new ArgumentException("Property Id is not allowed to change.");
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public IAnchorProvider Provider { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public IAnchorConsumer Consumer
		{
			get
			{
				return _consumer;
			}
			set
			{
				_consumer = value;
				if (_consumer != null)
				{
					_consumer.ReconnectToAnchor(this);
				}
			}
		}

		public abstract System.Windows.Point AbsoluteAnchorPoint { get; set; }

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public System.Windows.Point AbsoluteProjectionPoint
		{
			get
			{
				double projectionAngle = ProjectionAngle;
				double projectionDistance = ProjectionDistance;
				System.Windows.Point absoluteAnchorPoint = AbsoluteAnchorPoint;
				if (!GeometryHelper.IsRoughlyZero(projectionDistance))
				{
					absoluteAnchorPoint.X += projectionDistance * Math.Cos(projectionAngle * Math.PI / 180.0);
					absoluteAnchorPoint.Y += projectionDistance * Math.Sin(projectionAngle * Math.PI / 180.0);
				}
				return absoluteAnchorPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				System.Windows.Point absoluteAnchorPoint = AbsoluteAnchorPoint;
				System.Windows.Point point = value;
				double num = point.X - absoluteAnchorPoint.X;
				double num2 = point.Y - absoluteAnchorPoint.Y;
				_projectionDistance = Math.Sqrt(num * num + num2 * num2);
				if (num == 0.0 && num2 == 0.0)
				{
					_projectionAngle = 0.0;
				}
				else if (num == 0.0)
				{
					if (num2 > 0.0)
					{
						_projectionAngle = 90.0;
					}
					else
					{
						_projectionAngle = 270.0;
					}
				}
				else if (num2 == 0.0)
				{
					if (num > 0.0)
					{
						_projectionAngle = 0.0;
					}
					else
					{
						_projectionAngle = 180.0;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_projectionAngle = ((num > 0.0) ? (Math.Atan(num2 / num) * 180.0 / Math.PI) : (180.0 + Math.Atan(num2 / num) * 180.0 / Math.PI));
				}
				OnAbsoluteProjectionPointChanged();
			}
		}

		public double ProjectionDistance
		{
			get
			{
				return _projectionDistance;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_projectionDistance != value)
				{
					_projectionDistance = value;
					OnAbsoluteProjectionPointChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public double ProjectionAngle
		{
			get
			{
				return _projectionAngle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_projectionAngle != value)
				{
					_projectionAngle = value;
					OnAbsoluteProjectionPointChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public bool ShouldSerializeProjectionDistance()
		{
			return !GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(ProjectionDistance, 0.0);
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public bool ShouldSerializeProjectionAngle()
		{
			return !GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(ProjectionAngle, 0.0);
		}

		protected virtual void OnAbsoluteAnchorPointChanged()
		{
			if (Consumer != null)
			{
				Consumer.AnchorPointChanged(this);
			}
			OnAbsoluteProjectionPointChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnAbsoluteProjectionPointChanged()
		{
			if (Consumer != null)
			{
				Consumer.AnchorProjectionPointChanged(this);
			}
		}
	}
	public class PointAnchor : DiagramAnchor
	{
		private System.Windows.Point _absoluteAnchorPoint = new System.Windows.Point(double.NaN, double.NaN);

		public override System.Windows.Point AbsoluteAnchorPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return _absoluteAnchorPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				_absoluteAnchorPoint = value;
				OnAbsoluteAnchorPointChanged();
			}
		}

		public PointAnchor()
		{
		}

		public PointAnchor(System.Windows.Point absoluteAnchorPoint)
		{
			_absoluteAnchorPoint = absoluteAnchorPoint;
		}
	}
	public class SymbolAnchor : DiagramAnchor
	{
		private System.Windows.Point? _relativeAnchorPoint;

		public System.Windows.Point RelativeAnchorPoint
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_relativeAnchorPoint.HasValue)
				{
					return new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				}
				return _relativeAnchorPoint.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				bool flag = true;
				if (_relativeAnchorPoint.HasValue && GeometryHelper.AreRoughlyEqual(_relativeAnchorPoint.Value, value))
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				_relativeAnchorPoint = value;
				if (flag)
				{
					OnAbsoluteAnchorPointChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public override System.Windows.Point AbsoluteAnchorPoint
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.Provider.Element is Symbol frameworkElement)
				{
					System.Windows.Point topLeft = TransformHelper.GetTopLeft(frameworkElement);
					System.Windows.Point originPoint = TransformHelper.GetOriginPoint(frameworkElement);
					return new System.Windows.Point(topLeft.X + originPoint.X + RelativeAnchorPoint.X, topLeft.Y + originPoint.Y + RelativeAnchorPoint.Y);
				}
				return RelativeAnchorPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Provider is Symbol frameworkElement)
				{
					System.Windows.Point topLeft = TransformHelper.GetTopLeft(frameworkElement);
					System.Windows.Point originPoint = TransformHelper.GetOriginPoint(frameworkElement);
					value.X = value.X - topLeft.X - originPoint.X;
					value.Y = value.Y - topLeft.Y - originPoint.Y;
					RelativeAnchorPoint = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool ShouldSerializeRelativeAnchorPoint()
		{
			return _relativeAnchorPoint.HasValue;
		}

		public void VerifyTransformation(TransformGroup oldTransform, TransformGroup newTransform)
		{
			UpdatePointsFromTransform(oldTransform, newTransform);
		}

		private void UpdatePointsFromTransform(TransformGroup oldTransform, TransformGroup newTransform)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			System.Windows.Point absoluteOriginPoint = TransformHelper.GetAbsoluteOriginPoint(base.Provider as FrameworkElement);
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0, absoluteOriginPoint.X, absoluteOriginPoint.Y);
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform2 = TransformHelper.GetTransform(oldTransform, typeof(ScaleTransform)) as ScaleTransform;
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform3 = TransformHelper.GetTransform(newTransform, typeof(ScaleTransform)) as ScaleTransform;
			scaleTransform2 = ((scaleTransform2 == null) ? new ScaleTransform() : scaleTransform2);
			scaleTransform3 = ((scaleTransform3 == null) ? new ScaleTransform() : scaleTransform3);
			double num = scaleTransform3.ScaleX / scaleTransform2.ScaleX;
			double num2 = scaleTransform3.ScaleY / scaleTransform2.ScaleY;
			if (num != 1.0 || num2 != 1.0)
			{
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = num;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = num2;
				flag = true;
			}
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = new RotateTransform(0.0, absoluteOriginPoint.X, absoluteOriginPoint.Y);
			RotateTransform rotateTransform2 = TransformHelper.GetTransform(oldTransform, typeof(RotateTransform)) as RotateTransform;
			RotateTransform rotateTransform3 = TransformHelper.GetTransform(newTransform, typeof(RotateTransform)) as RotateTransform;
			rotateTransform2 = ((rotateTransform2 == null) ? new RotateTransform() : rotateTransform2);
			rotateTransform3 = ((rotateTransform3 == null) ? new RotateTransform() : rotateTransform3);
			double num3 = rotateTransform3.Angle - rotateTransform2.Angle;
			num3 %= 360.0;
			if (!(scaleTransform3.ScaleX < 0.0) ^ !(scaleTransform3.ScaleY < 0.0))
			{
				num3 *= -1.0;
			}
			if (num3 != 0.0)
			{
				rotateTransform.Angle = num3;
				flag = true;
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(rotateTransform);
			transformGroup.Children.Add(scaleTransform);
			if (flag)
			{
				System.Windows.Point absoluteProjectionPoint = base.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
				AbsoluteAnchorPoint = transformGroup.Transform(AbsoluteAnchorPoint);
				base.AbsoluteProjectionPoint = transformGroup.Transform(absoluteProjectionPoint);
			}
		}

		public void VerifyDimension(double oldWidth, double newWidth, double oldHeight, double newHeight)
		{
			System.Windows.Point absoluteAnchorPoint = AbsoluteAnchorPoint;
			_ = base.AbsoluteProjectionPoint;
			FrameworkElement element = base.Provider as FrameworkElement;
			double num = newWidth / oldWidth;
			double num2 = newHeight / oldHeight;
			num = (double.IsNaN(num) ? 1.0 : num);
			num2 = (double.IsNaN(num2) ? 1.0 : num2);
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = new ScaleTransform(num, num2);
			if (!scaleTransform.Value.IsIdentity)
			{
				System.Windows.Point absoluteOriginPoint = TransformHelper.GetAbsoluteOriginPoint(element);
				TransformGroup absoluteRenderTransform = TransformHelper.GetAbsoluteRenderTransform(element);
				System.Windows.Point point = absoluteRenderTransform.Inverse.Transform(absoluteAnchorPoint);
				scaleTransform.CenterX = absoluteOriginPoint.X;
				scaleTransform.CenterY = absoluteOriginPoint.Y;
				point = scaleTransform.Transform(point);
				point = absoluteRenderTransform.Transform(point);
				AbsoluteAnchorPoint = point;
			}
		}

		internal void SymbolPositionChanged()
		{
			OnAbsoluteAnchorPointChanged();
		}
	}
	public class SymbolAnchorCollection : ObservableCollection<SymbolAnchor>
	{
		public Symbol Owner { get; private set; }

		public SymbolAnchorCollection(Symbol owner)
		{
			Owner = owner;
		}

		protected override void InsertItem(int index, SymbolAnchor item)
		{
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				item.Provider = Owner;
			}
			base.InsertItem(index, item);
		}

		protected override void SetItem(int index, SymbolAnchor item)
		{
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				item.Provider = Owner;
			}
			base.SetItem(index, item);
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.AspenPlus.MolecularDrawing.Adorners
{
	public class BondElementAdorner : DiagramElementOverlay
	{
		private readonly BondElement _bondElement;

		private readonly Polygon _polygon;

		public override IEnumerable<UIElement> Adorners
		{
			get
			{
				yield return _polygon;
			}
		}

		public BondElementAdorner(ISelectionOverlayService overlayService)
			: base(overlayService)
		{
			_bondElement = overlayService.AdornedElement as BondElement;
			_bondElement.AnchorPositionChanged += _bondElement_AnchorPositionChanged;
			_polygon = new Polygon
			{
				Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.YellowGreen,
				Opacity = 0.5,
				IsHitTestVisible = false
			};
		}

		private void _bondElement_AnchorPositionChanged(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshPolygon();
		}

		protected override void DisplayUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (!layer.Children.Contains(_polygon))
			{
				layer.AddElement(_polygon);
			}
			RefreshPolygon();
		}

		protected override void HideUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			if (layer.Children.Contains(_polygon))
			{
				layer.RemoveElement(_polygon);
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateUI(DiagramLayer layer)
		{
			RefreshPolygon();
		}

		private void RefreshPolygon()
		{
			double num = _bondElement.LinesIntervalThickness + _bondElement.StrokeThickness;
			if (_bondElement.Bond != null)
			{
				num = ((_bondElement.Bond.Type == BondType.Single) ? (_bondElement.LinesIntervalThickness + _bondElement.StrokeThickness) : ((_bondElement.Bond.Type != BondType.Double) ? (_bondElement.LinesIntervalThickness * 4.0 + _bondElement.StrokeThickness * 3.0) : (_bondElement.LinesIntervalThickness * 3.0 + _bondElement.StrokeThickness * 2.0)));
			}
			System.Windows.Point centerStartPoint = _bondElement.CenterStartPoint;
			System.Windows.Point centerEndPoint = _bondElement.CenterEndPoint;
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint = GetSidePoint(centerStartPoint, centerEndPoint, -90.0, num / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint2 = GetSidePoint(centerStartPoint, centerEndPoint, 90.0, num / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint3 = GetSidePoint(centerEndPoint, centerStartPoint, -90.0, num / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point sidePoint4 = GetSidePoint(centerEndPoint, centerStartPoint, 90.0, num / 2.0);
			Rect rect = new Rect(sidePoint, sidePoint2);
			rect.Union(new Rect(sidePoint3, sidePoint4));
			Vector vector = new Vector(rect.Left, rect.Top);
			_polygon.Points = new PointCollection
			{
				sidePoint - vector,
				sidePoint4 - vector,
				sidePoint3 - vector,
				sidePoint2 - vector
			};
			if (base.Editor != null)
			{
				base.Editor.PositionElement(_polygon, rect.TopLeft);
			}
		}

		private static System.Windows.Point GetSidePoint(System.Windows.Point sp, System.Windows.Point ep, double angle, double interval)
		{
			Vector vector = ep - sp;
			if (vector.Length == 0.0)
			{
				return sp;
			}
			double num = Math.Abs(interval / Math.Sin(Math.Abs(angle) * Math.PI / 180.0));
			Vector vector2 = new RotateTransform(angle).Value.Transform(vector);
			vector2 = vector2 * num / vector2.Length;
			return sp + vector2;
		}
	}
	public class RotationAdorner
	{
		private static readonly System.Windows.Input.Cursor RotationCursor = new System.Windows.Input.Cursor(new MemoryStream(Resources.Rotating));

		private const double StepRotationAnlge = 5.0;

		private readonly MolecularDrawingCanvas _drawingCanvas;

		private readonly Diagram _diagram;

		private readonly List<AtomElement> _atomElements;

		private readonly UIElement _rotationShape;

		private Ellipse _outerEllipse;

		private Ellipse _innerEllipse;

		private readonly TrippleText _rotationText;

		private readonly double _rotationShapeThickness = 2.0;

		private readonly System.Windows.Media.Brush _rotationShapeStroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;

		private readonly System.Windows.Size _rotationShapeSize = new System.Windows.Size(40.0, 40.0);

		private System.Windows.Point _clickDownPoint;

		private System.Windows.Point _rotationCenterPoint;

		private System.Windows.Point _oldRotaionShapePosition;

		private readonly Dictionary<AtomElement, System.Windows.Point> _oldPositions;

		public bool IsMouseOver
		{
			get
			{
				if ((_outerEllipse != null && _outerEllipse.IsMouseCaptured) || (_innerEllipse != null && _innerEllipse.IsMouseCaptured))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public RotationAdorner(MolecularDrawingCanvas drawingCanvas)
		{
			if (drawingCanvas == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("drawingCanvas");
			}
			_drawingCanvas = drawingCanvas;
			_diagram = _drawingCanvas.Diagram;
			_atomElements = new List<AtomElement>();
			_oldPositions = new Dictionary<AtomElement, System.Windows.Point>();
			_rotationShape = BuildRotationShape();
			_rotationText = new TrippleText
			{
				FontSize = 12.0,
				TopRightText = "O"
			};
		}

		public void Update()
		{
			_atomElements.Clear();
			foreach (UIElement item2 in _diagram.Selection)
			{
				if (item2 is AtomElement item && !_atomElements.Contains(item))
				{
					_atomElements.Add(item);
				}
				if (item2 is BondElement bondElement)
				{
					if (!_atomElements.Contains(bondElement.StartAtomElement))
					{
						_atomElements.Add(bondElement.StartAtomElement);
					}
					if (!_atomElements.Contains(bondElement.EndAtomElement))
					{
						_atomElements.Add(bondElement.EndAtomElement);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_atomElements.Count >= 2)
			{
				if (!_diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rotationShape))
				{
					_diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_rotationShape);
				}
				_diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_rotationShape, GetSelectionCenter() - new Vector(_rotationShapeSize.Width / 2.0, _rotationShapeSize.Height / 2.0));
			}
		}

		public void Hide()
		{
			if (_diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rotationShape))
			{
				_diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_rotationShape);
			}
		}

		private UIElement BuildRotationShape()
		{
			Canvas canvas = new Canvas();
			canvas.Width = _rotationShapeSize.Width;
			canvas.Height = _rotationShapeSize.Height;
			canvas.RenderTransformOrigin = new System.Windows.Point(0.5, 0.5);
			canvas.RenderTransform = new RotateTransform();
			canvas.MouseLeftButtonDown += canvas_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			canvas.MouseMove += canvas_MouseMove;
			canvas.MouseLeftButtonUp += canvas_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			Ellipse ellipse = new Ellipse
			{
				Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent,
				Width = _rotationShapeSize.Width,
				Height = _rotationShapeSize.Height,
				Cursor = RotationCursor
			};
			ellipse.MouseLeftButtonDown += outerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			ellipse.MouseMove += outerEllipse_MouseMove;
			ellipse.MouseLeftButtonUp += outerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			_outerEllipse = ellipse;
			canvas.Children.Add(ellipse);
			Ellipse element = new Ellipse
			{
				Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent,
				Width = 30.0,
				Height = 30.0,
				Stroke = _rotationShapeStroke,
				StrokeThickness = _rotationShapeThickness,
				IsHitTestVisible = false
			};
			Canvas.SetLeft(element, 5.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(element, 5.0);
			canvas.Children.Add(element);
			Ellipse ellipse2 = new Ellipse
			{
				Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent,
				Width = 20.0,
				Height = 20.0,
				Cursor = System.Windows.Input.Cursors.SizeAll
			};
			ellipse2.MouseLeftButtonDown += innerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			ellipse2.MouseMove += innerEllipse_MouseMove;
			ellipse2.MouseLeftButtonUp += innerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			Canvas.SetLeft(ellipse2, 10.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(ellipse2, 10.0);
			_innerEllipse = ellipse2;
			canvas.Children.Add(ellipse2);
			canvas.Children.Add(new Line
			{
				Stroke = _rotationShapeStroke,
				StrokeThickness = _rotationShapeThickness,
				X1 = 15.0,
				Y1 = 20.0,
				X2 = 25.0,
				Y2 = 20.0,
				IsHitTestVisible = false
			});
			canvas.Children.Add(new Line
			{
				Stroke = _rotationShapeStroke,
				StrokeThickness = _rotationShapeThickness,
				X1 = 20.0,
				Y1 = 15.0,
				X2 = 20.0,
				Y2 = 25.0,
				IsHitTestVisible = false
			});
			return canvas;
		}

		private void canvas_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void canvas_MouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void canvas_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void outerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as Ellipse).CaptureMouse();
			_clickDownPoint = _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_rotationCenterPoint = GetRotationCenter();
			_oldPositions.Clear();
			foreach (AtomElement atomElement in _atomElements)
			{
				_oldPositions.Add(atomElement, Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement));
			}
			((RotateTransform)_rotationShape.RenderTransform).Angle = 0.0;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void outerEllipse_MouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((sender as Ellipse).IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				double rotationAngle = GetRotationAngle(_rotationCenterPoint, _clickDownPoint, mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates);
				using (new TransactionContext(_diagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty, 257))
				{
					RotateElements(_atomElements, _oldPositions, _rotationCenterPoint, rotationAngle);
				}
				_rotationText.PrimaryText = rotationAngle.ToString();
				if (!_diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rotationText))
				{
					_diagram.OverlayLayer.AddElement(_rotationText);
				}
				_diagram.OverlayLayer.PositionElement(_rotationText, mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates + new Vector(10.0, 10.0));
				((RotateTransform)_rotationShape.RenderTransform).Angle = rotationAngle;
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void outerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Ellipse ellipse = sender as Ellipse;
			if (!ellipse.IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_diagram.OverlayLayer.Children.Contains(_rotationText))
			{
				_diagram.OverlayLayer.RemoveElement(_rotationText);
			}
			double angle = GetRotationAngle(_rotationCenterPoint, _clickDownPoint, _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates());
			if (!AngleHelper.IsRoughlyZero(angle))
			{
				using (new TransactionContext(_diagram.TransactionManager, string.Empty, 257))
				{
					RotateElements(_atomElements, _oldPositions, _rotationCenterPoint, angle);
				}
				using TransactionContext transactionContext = new TransactionContext(_diagram);
				List<AtomElement> historyElements = new List<AtomElement>(_atomElements);
				System.Windows.Point historyCenterPoint = _rotationCenterPoint;
				if (transactionContext.ShouldCreateChangeRecord)
				{
					transactionContext.AddChangeRecord(delegate
					{
						RotateElements(historyElements, historyCenterPoint, 0.0 - angle);
					}, delegate
					{
						RotateElements(historyElements, historyCenterPoint, angle);
					});
				}
			}
			ellipse.ReleaseMouseCapture();
			((RotateTransform)_rotationShape.RenderTransform).Angle = 0.0;
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void innerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			(sender as Ellipse).CaptureMouse();
			_clickDownPoint = _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
			_oldRotaionShapePosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(_rotationShape);
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void innerEllipse_MouseMove(object sender, System.Windows.Input.MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((sender as Ellipse).IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				_diagram.PositionElement(_rotationShape, GetAppropriatePosition(_oldRotaionShapePosition + (mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates - _clickDownPoint)));
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void innerEllipse_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Ellipse ellipse = sender as Ellipse;
			if (ellipse.IsMouseCaptured)
			{
				System.Windows.Point mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates = _diagram.GetMousePositionInCanvasCoordinates();
				_diagram.PositionElement(_rotationShape, GetAppropriatePosition(_oldRotaionShapePosition + (mousePositionInCanvasCoordinates - _clickDownPoint)));
				ellipse.ReleaseMouseCapture();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetSelectionCenter()
		{
			Rect rect = Rect.Empty;
			foreach (AtomElement atomElement in _atomElements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point elementPosition = Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement);
				if (rect == Rect.Empty)
				{
					rect = new Rect(elementPosition, elementPosition);
				}
				else
				{
					rect.Union(elementPosition);
				}
			}
			return rect.TopLeft + (rect.BottomRight - rect.TopLeft) / 2.0;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetRotationCenter()
		{
			return Diagram.GetElementPosition(_rotationShape) + new Vector(_rotationShapeSize.Width / 2.0, _rotationShapeSize.Height / 2.0);
		}

		private double GetRotationAngle(System.Windows.Point centerPoint, System.Windows.Point fromPoint, System.Windows.Point toPoint)
		{
			double angleBetween = AngleHelper.GetAngleBetween(fromPoint - centerPoint, toPoint - centerPoint);
			if (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl))
			{
				angleBetween -= angleBetween % 0.01;
			}
			else
			{
				angleBetween = (angleBetween + 360.0) % 360.0;
				double num = angleBetween % 5.0;
				angleBetween = ((!(num <= 2.5)) ? (angleBetween - num + 5.0) : (angleBetween - num));
			}
			return angleBetween % 360.0;
		}

		private void RotateElements(List<AtomElement> elements, Dictionary<AtomElement, System.Windows.Point> oldPositions, System.Windows.Point centerPoint, double angle)
		{
			System.Windows.Media.Matrix value = new RotateTransform(angle).Value;
			foreach (AtomElement element in elements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point newPosition = centerPoint + value.Transform(oldPositions[element] - centerPoint);
				_diagram.PositionElement(element, newPosition);
			}
		}

		private void RotateElements(List<AtomElement> elements, System.Windows.Point centerPoint, double angle)
		{
			System.Windows.Media.Matrix value = new RotateTransform(angle).Value;
			foreach (AtomElement element in elements)
			{
				System.Windows.Point newPosition = centerPoint + value.Transform(Diagram.GetElementPosition(element) - centerPoint);
				_diagram.PositionElement(element, newPosition);
			}
		}

		private System.Windows.Point GetAppropriatePosition(System.Windows.Point position)
		{
			if (Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl))
			{
				return position;
			}
			Vector vector = new Vector(_rotationShapeSize.Width / 2.0, _rotationShapeSize.Height / 2.0);
			System.Windows.Point position2 = position + vector;
			AtomElement atomElement = _drawingCanvas.FindAtomElementAt(position2);
			if (atomElement != null)
			{
				return Diagram.GetElementPosition(atomElement) - vector;
			}
			return position;
		}
	}
}
